Chapter􀀉1􀀉—􀀉The􀀉Blistering Tomorrow􀀉

Gage􀀉pushed􀀉open􀀉the􀀉door􀀉of􀀉his􀀉shack.􀀉The􀀉hinges􀀉screamed􀀉—􀀉rusted􀀉
metal􀀉on􀀉rusted􀀉metal􀀉—􀀉and􀀉the􀀉air􀀉outside􀀉hit􀀉him􀀉before􀀉the􀀉light􀀉did.􀀉
Chemical.􀀉Sharp.􀀉The􀀉kind􀀉of􀀉smell􀀉that􀀉coated􀀉the􀀉back􀀉of􀀉your􀀉throat􀀉and􀀉
stayed􀀉there􀀉for􀀉hours.􀀉

He􀀉looked􀀉back􀀉into􀀉the􀀉dark.􀀉His􀀉sister􀀉Camilla􀀉was􀀉cross-legged􀀉on􀀉the􀀉
floor,􀀉hunched􀀉over􀀉a􀀉scrap􀀉of􀀉copper􀀉she’d􀀉pulled􀀉from􀀉the􀀉sump􀀉the􀀉night􀀉
before.􀀉Her􀀉fingers􀀉moved􀀉with􀀉a􀀉precision􀀉he’d􀀉never􀀉have􀀉—􀀉tracing􀀉a􀀉
faded􀀉serial􀀉number,􀀉reading􀀉the􀀉metal􀀉the􀀉way􀀉other􀀉kids􀀉read􀀉faces.􀀉She􀀉
was􀀉fifteen􀀉and􀀉already􀀉better􀀉with􀀉machines􀀉than􀀉anyone􀀉he􀀉knew.􀀉

“Stay􀀉inside,􀀉Camilla,”􀀉he􀀉muttered,􀀉his􀀉voice􀀉a􀀉low􀀉rasp.􀀉“The􀀉sun’s􀀉got􀀉a􀀉
bite􀀉today.􀀉Don’t􀀉go􀀉near􀀉the􀀉gaps.”􀀉

She􀀉didn’t􀀉look􀀉up.􀀉She􀀉never􀀉did􀀉when􀀉her􀀉hands􀀉were􀀉working.􀀉But􀀉Gage􀀉
stood􀀉in􀀉the􀀉doorway􀀉an􀀉extra􀀉second􀀉anyway,􀀉watching􀀉her,􀀉the􀀉way􀀉you’d􀀉
watch􀀉a􀀉fire􀀉you􀀉were􀀉afraid􀀉might􀀉go􀀉out.􀀉She􀀉was􀀉the􀀉reason􀀉his􀀉legs􀀉still􀀉
moved􀀉in􀀉the􀀉morning.􀀉Not􀀉the􀀉shovel,􀀉not􀀉the􀀉shift,􀀉not􀀉the􀀉threat􀀉of􀀉
being􀀉added􀀉to􀀉the􀀉disposal􀀉list.􀀉Her.􀀉

He􀀉stepped􀀉out􀀉and􀀉pulled􀀉the􀀉door􀀉shut􀀉behind􀀉him.􀀉

The􀀉heat􀀉hit􀀉him􀀉like􀀉a􀀉physical􀀉blow􀀉as􀀉he􀀉stepped􀀉onto􀀉the􀀉ridge.􀀉The􀀉sun􀀉
was􀀉a􀀉swollen􀀉needle􀀉of􀀉fire􀀉stabbing􀀉through􀀉the􀀉exhausted􀀉sky.􀀉He􀀉
adjusted􀀉his􀀉respirator􀀉—􀀉a􀀉heavy􀀉mask􀀉of􀀉rubber􀀉and􀀉brass􀀉that􀀉groaned􀀉
with􀀉every􀀉breath,􀀉barely􀀉straining􀀉the􀀉coal􀀉dust􀀉from􀀉the􀀉air.􀀉

The􀀉walk􀀉to􀀉the􀀉mine􀀉was􀀉a􀀉procession􀀉of􀀉the􀀉damned.􀀉Gage􀀉joined􀀉the􀀉
shuffling􀀉column􀀉of􀀉men􀀉and􀀉boys􀀉filing􀀉down􀀉the􀀉ridge􀀉path􀀉—􀀉a􀀉trail􀀉
beaten􀀉into􀀉the􀀉grey􀀉silt􀀉by􀀉a􀀉thousand􀀉boots􀀉a􀀉day.􀀉No􀀉one􀀉talked.􀀉Talking􀀉
cost􀀉moisture,􀀉and􀀉moisture􀀉was􀀉currency􀀉here.􀀉A􀀉man􀀉could􀀉trade􀀉a􀀉cup􀀉of􀀉


filtered􀀉water􀀉for􀀉two􀀉days􀀉of􀀉salt􀀉rations􀀉—􀀉and􀀉still􀀉lose􀀉every􀀉drop􀀉in􀀉the􀀉
first􀀉hour􀀉of􀀉sun.􀀉So􀀉they􀀉walked􀀉in􀀉silence,􀀉a􀀉river􀀉of􀀉hunched􀀉shoulders􀀉
and􀀉downcast􀀉eyes,􀀉the􀀉only􀀉sound􀀉the􀀉rhythmic􀀉scrape􀀉of􀀉iron􀀉tools􀀉
dragging􀀉through􀀉grit.􀀉

On􀀉either􀀉side􀀉of􀀉the􀀉path,􀀉the􀀉shack-rows􀀉of􀀉Miner􀀉Town􀀉stretched􀀉out􀀉
like􀀉the􀀉ribs􀀉of􀀉a􀀉half-buried􀀉skeleton.􀀉Each􀀉dwelling􀀉was􀀉a􀀉patchwork􀀉of􀀉
salvaged􀀉metal􀀉—􀀉car􀀉doors􀀉flattened􀀉into􀀉walls,􀀉oil􀀉drums􀀉split􀀉and􀀉
hammered􀀉into􀀉roofing,􀀉gaps􀀉sealed􀀉with􀀉whatever􀀉the􀀉last􀀉storm􀀉hadn’t􀀉
blown􀀉away.􀀉Some􀀉families􀀉had􀀉hung􀀉strips􀀉of􀀉old􀀉tarpaulin􀀉as􀀉curtains,􀀉
their􀀉colors􀀉long􀀉since􀀉faded􀀉to􀀉a􀀉uniform􀀉grey.􀀉Others􀀉had􀀉given􀀉up􀀉on􀀉
privacy􀀉altogether,􀀉and􀀉through􀀉the􀀉open􀀉gaps􀀉Gage􀀉could􀀉see􀀉the􀀉domestic􀀉
rituals􀀉of􀀉dawn:􀀉off-shift􀀉miners􀀉boiling􀀉sump􀀉water􀀉through􀀉cloth􀀉filters,􀀉
children􀀉sitting􀀉motionless􀀉in􀀉corners􀀉to􀀉conserve􀀉energy,􀀉the􀀉broken􀀉
staring􀀉at􀀉nothing􀀉with􀀉the􀀉glassy􀀉patience􀀉of􀀉the􀀉already􀀉defeated.􀀉

He􀀉passed􀀉the􀀉communal􀀉pump􀀉—􀀉a􀀉rusted􀀉iron􀀉standpipe􀀉that􀀉served􀀉
three􀀉hundred􀀉families.􀀉A􀀉queue􀀉had􀀉already􀀉formed:􀀉off-shift􀀉miners􀀉and􀀉
children􀀉with􀀉dented􀀉buckets,􀀉waiting􀀉for􀀉the􀀉twenty-minute􀀉window􀀉
when􀀉the􀀉underground􀀉spring􀀉produced􀀉enough􀀉pressure􀀉to􀀉push􀀉water􀀉to􀀉
the􀀉surface.􀀉The􀀉water􀀉came􀀉out􀀉with􀀉an􀀉orange􀀉tint􀀉and􀀉a􀀉faint􀀉chemical􀀉
bite,􀀉but􀀉it􀀉was􀀉the􀀉only􀀉water􀀉source􀀉in􀀉Miner􀀉Town􀀉that􀀉wasn’t􀀉owned􀀉by􀀉
Trinity.􀀉Gage􀀉noticed􀀉Mara􀀉in􀀉the􀀉queue􀀉—􀀉thin􀀉as􀀉wire,􀀉eyes􀀉fixed􀀉on􀀉the􀀉
spigot􀀉with􀀉the􀀉desperate􀀉focus􀀉of􀀉someone􀀉who􀀉had􀀉learned􀀉that􀀉looking􀀉
away􀀉meant􀀉losing􀀉your􀀉place.􀀉

This􀀈is􀀈what􀀈they’ve􀀈made􀀈of􀀈us,􀀉Gage􀀉thought.􀀉Not􀀈slaves.􀀈Slaves􀀈at􀀈least􀀈
know􀀈someone􀀈is􀀈holding􀀈the􀀈chain.􀀈We’re􀀈just…􀀈parts.􀀈Interchangeable􀀈parts􀀈
in􀀈a􀀈machine􀀈so􀀈vast􀀈it􀀈didn’t􀀈notice􀀈when􀀈one􀀈of􀀈them􀀈broke.􀀈

He’d􀀉had􀀉that􀀉thought􀀉before􀀉—􀀉had􀀉it􀀉so􀀉many􀀉times􀀉it􀀉was􀀉worn􀀉smooth􀀉
like􀀉a􀀉river􀀉stone􀀉—􀀉but􀀉it􀀉still􀀉cut.􀀉Some􀀉thoughts􀀉were􀀉like􀀉that.􀀉They􀀉
didn’t􀀉get􀀉duller􀀉with􀀉repetition;􀀉they􀀉just􀀉sank􀀉deeper.􀀉


He􀀉glared􀀉at􀀉the􀀉horizon.􀀉The􀀉Trinity􀀉Cities􀀉shimmered􀀉at􀀉the􀀉edge􀀉of􀀉the􀀉
world􀀉—􀀉silver􀀉domes􀀉behind􀀉walls􀀉of􀀉clean􀀉air,􀀉so􀀉distant􀀉they􀀉looked􀀉like􀀉
something􀀉imagined.􀀉Every􀀉second􀀉of􀀉their􀀉coolness􀀉was􀀉paid􀀉for􀀉by􀀉the􀀉
bodies􀀉walking􀀉this􀀉ridge.􀀉Gage􀀉didn’t􀀉need􀀉to􀀉imagine􀀉what􀀉life􀀉looked􀀉like􀀉
inside􀀉those􀀉domes.􀀉He􀀉just􀀉needed􀀉to􀀉remember􀀉that􀀉the􀀉people􀀉in􀀉them􀀉
had􀀉never􀀉once􀀉looked􀀉back.􀀉

High􀀉above􀀉the􀀉chemical􀀉stench,􀀉the􀀉Managers􀀉of􀀉the􀀉harvest􀀉were􀀉
beginning􀀉their􀀉cycles􀀉with􀀉clinical,􀀉unhurried􀀉precision.􀀉In􀀉a􀀉world􀀉defined􀀉
by􀀉silence􀀉rather􀀉than􀀉the􀀉squeal􀀉of􀀉rusted􀀉iron,􀀉a􀀉woman􀀉named􀀉Elara􀀉
began􀀉her􀀉day􀀉at􀀉precisely􀀉04:00.􀀉There􀀉was􀀉no􀀉orange􀀉haze􀀉here;􀀉the􀀉light􀀉
was􀀉a􀀉permanent,􀀉soothing􀀉lavender,􀀉specifically􀀉calibrated􀀉to􀀉keep􀀉the􀀉
human􀀉heart􀀉rate􀀉at􀀉a􀀉perfect,􀀉efficient􀀉sixty-five􀀉beats􀀉per􀀉minute.􀀉While􀀉
Gage􀀉struggled􀀉to􀀉pull􀀉turpentine-thick􀀉oxygen􀀉through􀀉a􀀉failing􀀉filter,􀀉
Elara􀀉existed􀀉in􀀉a􀀉state􀀉of􀀉uninterrupted􀀉stillness.􀀉To􀀉her,􀀉the􀀉Blistering􀀉
Tomorrow􀀉of􀀉Miner􀀉Town􀀉was􀀉not􀀉a􀀉nightmare􀀉of􀀉heat􀀉but􀀉a􀀉resource􀀉
management􀀉problem􀀉to􀀉be􀀉monitored􀀉from􀀉the􀀉safety􀀉of􀀉a􀀉screen􀀉—􀀉a􀀉vast􀀉
apparatus􀀉of􀀉energy􀀉where􀀉the􀀉people􀀉below􀀉were􀀉merely􀀉audited􀀉units,􀀉
their􀀉humanity􀀉filtered􀀉out􀀉as􀀉effectively􀀉as􀀉the􀀉Spires􀀉filtered􀀉out􀀉the􀀉soot.􀀉

Gage􀀉coughed􀀉—􀀉a􀀉wet􀀉rattle􀀉that􀀉dragged􀀉him􀀉back􀀉to􀀉the􀀉ridge.􀀉He􀀉wasn’t􀀉
up􀀉there.􀀉He􀀉was􀀉down􀀉here,􀀉with􀀉the􀀉column􀀉and􀀉the􀀉silt,􀀉the􀀉shovel􀀉biting􀀉
into􀀉his􀀉shoulder.􀀉

He􀀉coughed􀀉—􀀉dry,􀀉racking􀀉—􀀉until􀀉the􀀉heavy􀀉shovel􀀉shifted􀀉on􀀉his􀀉
shoulder,􀀉the􀀉shaft􀀉digging􀀉into􀀉his􀀉collarbone.􀀉The􀀉weight􀀉was􀀉a􀀉constant􀀉
tether􀀉to􀀉reality,􀀉to􀀉the􀀉frame􀀉built􀀉on􀀉the􀀉sacrifice􀀉of􀀉his􀀉father􀀉Thorton􀀉
and􀀉his􀀉grandfather􀀉Pappy.􀀉

As􀀉he􀀉regained􀀉his􀀉footing,􀀉he􀀉felt􀀉what􀀉his􀀉family􀀉had􀀉taught􀀉him􀀉to􀀉call􀀉
the􀀉Pappy􀀉Debt􀀉—􀀉the􀀉weight􀀉of􀀉every􀀉meal􀀉his􀀉grandfather􀀉had􀀉given􀀉up􀀉so􀀉
Gage􀀉could􀀉grow.􀀉It􀀉felt􀀉heavier􀀉than􀀉the􀀉iron􀀉in􀀉his􀀉hands.􀀉He􀀉swallowed􀀉
hard,􀀉throat􀀉coated􀀉with􀀉the􀀉chalky􀀉dust􀀉of􀀉the􀀉Ration􀀉Brick􀀉he’d􀀉choked􀀉
down􀀉for􀀉breakfast􀀉—􀀉a􀀉dry􀀉block􀀉of􀀉compressed􀀉nutrients􀀉designed􀀉to􀀉fuel􀀉


a􀀉body􀀉while􀀉leaving􀀉the􀀉mind􀀉too􀀉hollow􀀉to􀀉dream.􀀉He􀀉could􀀉feel􀀉the􀀉
phantom􀀉weight􀀉of􀀉the􀀉starch􀀉cubes􀀉Thorton􀀉and􀀉Pappy􀀉had􀀉surrendered􀀉
for􀀉him,􀀉their􀀉faces􀀉growing􀀉sallow􀀉each􀀉evening􀀉as􀀉they􀀉slid􀀉their􀀉portions􀀉
onto􀀉his􀀉plate.􀀉They􀀉had􀀉traded􀀉their􀀉health􀀉for􀀉his􀀉bones,􀀉and􀀉now􀀉he􀀉
traded􀀉those􀀉bones􀀉to􀀉pay􀀉back􀀉a􀀉loan􀀉of􀀉survival􀀉that􀀉could􀀉never􀀉be􀀉
settled.􀀉

That􀀉guilt􀀉was􀀉nearly􀀉as􀀉suffocating􀀉as􀀉the􀀉turpentine􀀉air􀀉—􀀉until􀀉the􀀉
thought􀀉was􀀉severed􀀉by􀀉the􀀉earth􀀉itself.􀀉

The􀀉ground􀀉groaned􀀉with􀀉a􀀉deep,􀀉subterranean􀀉bass􀀉that􀀉he􀀉felt􀀉vibrate􀀉
through􀀉his􀀉ribs.􀀉The􀀉Stubborn􀀉Carbon􀀉deep􀀉below􀀉emitted􀀉a􀀉piezoelectric􀀉
pulse􀀉—􀀉the􀀉kind􀀉of􀀉energy􀀉crystals􀀉release􀀉when􀀉they’re􀀉squeezed􀀉or􀀉struck􀀉

—􀀉that􀀉he􀀉felt􀀉in􀀉his􀀉marrow,􀀉a􀀉relentless􀀉vibration􀀉that􀀉traveled􀀉up􀀉his􀀉legs􀀉
and􀀉settled􀀉in􀀉his􀀉chest􀀉like􀀉an􀀉iron􀀉bell􀀉being􀀉struck.􀀉
The􀀉memory􀀉of􀀉starch􀀉loaves􀀉and􀀉sallow􀀉faces􀀉vanished.􀀉Cold􀀉focus􀀉took􀀉its􀀉
place.􀀉The􀀉hum􀀉was􀀉the􀀉starting􀀉bell,􀀉and􀀉it􀀉narrowed􀀉his􀀉world􀀉until􀀉only􀀉
the􀀉immediate􀀉path􀀉remained.􀀉

The􀀉mine􀀉mouth􀀉loomed􀀉ahead􀀉like􀀉the􀀉dark􀀉throat􀀉of􀀉a􀀉hungry􀀉god.􀀉
Guarding􀀉the􀀉entrance􀀉were􀀉the􀀉Urban􀀉Servants,􀀉encased􀀉in􀀉scrap-armor􀀉
that􀀉rattled􀀉with􀀉dull􀀉resonance.􀀉As􀀉Gage􀀉walked􀀉past,􀀉he􀀉heard􀀉the􀀉
rhythmic􀀉hiss􀀉of􀀉their􀀉pressurized􀀉seals􀀉—􀀉the􀀉Ritual􀀉of􀀉the􀀉Mask,􀀉the􀀉
sound􀀉of􀀉men􀀉recycled􀀉into􀀉tools􀀉of􀀉enforcement.􀀉Behind􀀉the􀀉predator-
snout􀀉visors,􀀉he􀀉saw􀀉only􀀉polished􀀉vacancy.􀀉

Gage􀀉knew􀀉what􀀉they􀀉were.􀀉Recycled􀀉products􀀉of􀀉a􀀉failed􀀉Initiation.􀀉Off-
duty,􀀉a􀀉guard􀀉like􀀉Kael􀀉didn’t􀀉return􀀉to􀀉a􀀉family.􀀉He􀀉returned􀀉to􀀉the􀀉
Barracks􀀉of􀀉the􀀉Blind,􀀉a􀀉series􀀉of􀀉concrete􀀉honeycomb􀀉cells,􀀉where􀀉the􀀉hiss􀀉
of􀀉equalizing􀀉pressure􀀉as􀀉his􀀉mask􀀉came􀀉away􀀉was􀀉the􀀉only􀀉conversation􀀉
he’d􀀉known􀀉for􀀉years.􀀉Beneath􀀉the􀀉mask:􀀉a􀀉map􀀉of􀀉smooth,􀀉featureless􀀉scar􀀉
tissue􀀉and􀀉recessed􀀉ports􀀉where􀀉sensory-deprivation􀀉drugs􀀉were􀀉fed􀀉directly􀀉
into􀀉his􀀉jugular.􀀉No􀀉twinkling􀀉joys,􀀉no􀀉childhood􀀉drawings􀀉of􀀉forests,􀀉no􀀉


memories􀀉of􀀉sisters􀀉in􀀉Kael’s􀀉world.􀀉Just􀀉a􀀉sterile􀀉white􀀉void􀀉devoid􀀉of􀀉any􀀉
object􀀉that􀀉might􀀉trigger􀀉a􀀉human􀀉thought.􀀉A􀀉hollowed-out􀀉circuit􀀉waiting􀀉
for􀀉a􀀉command.􀀉

The􀀉guard􀀉nearest􀀉the􀀉gate􀀉scanned􀀉the􀀉queue􀀉of􀀉miners􀀉with􀀉the􀀉cold,􀀉
controlled􀀉curiosity􀀉of􀀉a􀀉foreman􀀉deciding􀀉exactly􀀉where􀀉to􀀉swing􀀉the􀀉lash􀀉
to􀀉ensure􀀉the􀀉maximum􀀉yield􀀉for􀀉the􀀉masters􀀉in􀀉their􀀉towers.􀀉

Gage􀀉waited􀀉in􀀉line,􀀉watching􀀉the􀀉men􀀉ahead􀀉of􀀉him􀀉disappear􀀉one􀀉by􀀉one􀀉
into􀀉the􀀉black.􀀉Each􀀉one􀀉walked􀀉the􀀉same􀀉way􀀉—􀀉head􀀉down,􀀉shoulders􀀉
rolled􀀉inward,􀀉as􀀉if􀀉trying􀀉to􀀉make􀀉themselves􀀉smaller.􀀉As􀀉if􀀉being􀀉smaller􀀉
might􀀉make􀀉the􀀉mountain􀀉notice􀀉them􀀉less.􀀉He􀀉wondered􀀉how􀀉many􀀉of􀀉
them􀀉still􀀉remembered􀀉being􀀉something􀀉other􀀉than􀀉this.􀀉How􀀉many􀀉still􀀉
had􀀉a􀀉name􀀉in􀀉their􀀉own􀀉heads,􀀉a􀀉face􀀉they􀀉saw􀀉when􀀉they􀀉closed􀀉their􀀉eyes􀀉
at􀀉night􀀉that􀀉wasn’t􀀉covered􀀉in􀀉soot.􀀉

He􀀉thought􀀉of􀀉Thorton.􀀉His􀀉father􀀉hadn’t􀀉walked􀀉like􀀉these􀀉men.􀀉Even􀀉
after􀀉the􀀉worst􀀉shifts􀀉—􀀉the􀀉days􀀉when􀀉other􀀉men􀀉came􀀉back􀀉hollow-eyed􀀉
and􀀉silent􀀉—􀀉Thorton􀀉had􀀉walked􀀉into􀀉the􀀉mine􀀉the􀀉next􀀉morning􀀉with􀀉his􀀉
chin􀀉level.􀀉Not􀀉defiant,􀀉exactly.􀀉More􀀉like􀀉a􀀉man􀀉who􀀉refused􀀉to􀀉let􀀉the􀀉
mountain􀀉see􀀉him􀀉flinch.􀀉Gage􀀉had􀀉asked􀀉him􀀉once􀀉why􀀉he􀀉bothered,􀀉when􀀉
no􀀉one􀀉was􀀉watching􀀉and􀀉no􀀉one􀀉cared.􀀉

“Because􀀈I’m􀀈watching,”􀀈Thorton􀀉had􀀉said.􀀉“And􀀈I􀀈care.”􀀈

Gage􀀉lifted􀀉his􀀉chin.􀀉It􀀉was􀀉a􀀉small􀀉thing.􀀉It􀀉changed􀀉nothing􀀉about􀀉the􀀉
long􀀉shift􀀉ahead,􀀉nothing􀀉about􀀉the􀀉sludge􀀉or􀀉the􀀉hum􀀉or􀀉the􀀉guards.􀀉But􀀉it􀀉
was􀀉his,􀀉and􀀉the􀀉mountain􀀉couldn’t􀀉have􀀉it.􀀉

Gage􀀉gripped􀀉his􀀉shovel􀀉and􀀉stepped􀀉into􀀉the􀀉black.􀀉He􀀉was􀀉no􀀉longer􀀉a􀀉son􀀉
carrying􀀉a􀀉debt;􀀉he􀀉was􀀉a􀀉fire􀀉waiting􀀉for􀀉a􀀉spark.􀀉This􀀉was􀀉the􀀉new􀀉order:􀀉
break􀀉the􀀉world􀀉for􀀉the􀀉masters􀀉in􀀉their􀀉lavender􀀉towers,􀀉or􀀉rot􀀉in􀀉the􀀉sun.􀀉
As􀀉he􀀉disappeared􀀉into􀀉the􀀉tunnel,􀀉the􀀉stinging􀀉trail􀀉of􀀉his􀀉first􀀉lesson􀀉
pulsed􀀉on􀀉his􀀉brow􀀉—􀀉a􀀉compass􀀉pointing􀀉toward􀀉the􀀉Trinity􀀉domes􀀉on􀀉the􀀉
horizon􀀉—􀀉and􀀉their􀀉destruction.􀀉


Chapter􀀉2􀀉—􀀉The􀀉End􀀉of􀀉Innocence􀀉

Gage􀀉watched􀀉an􀀉old􀀉Soot-Wraith􀀉cough􀀉a􀀉thick􀀉grey􀀉slurry􀀉into􀀉the􀀉dust,􀀉
eyes􀀉empty􀀉as􀀉a􀀉dry􀀉well.􀀉Material􀀉Failure􀀉—􀀉the􀀉point􀀉where􀀉a􀀉human􀀉
structure,􀀉subjected􀀉to􀀉the􀀉crushing􀀉load􀀉of􀀉the􀀉mines􀀉and􀀉the􀀉hum􀀉of􀀉the􀀉
earth,􀀉simply􀀉dissolved.􀀉In􀀉Miner􀀉Town,􀀉men􀀉like􀀉this􀀉were􀀉called􀀉The􀀉
Hollows:􀀉people􀀉squeezed􀀉of􀀉every􀀉useful􀀉calorie􀀉until􀀉only􀀉the􀀉instinct􀀉to􀀉
shovel􀀉remained.􀀉Gage􀀉knew􀀉the􀀉road.􀀉The􀀉only􀀉question􀀉was􀀉how􀀉long􀀉he􀀉
had􀀉before􀀉he􀀉reached􀀉it.􀀉

Gage􀀉tightened􀀉his􀀉grip.􀀉The􀀉blade􀀉scraped􀀉against􀀉jagged􀀉rock,􀀉the􀀉screech􀀉
vibrating􀀉through􀀉his􀀉arms.􀀉He􀀉looked􀀉down􀀉at􀀉his􀀉massive􀀉hands,􀀉caked􀀉in􀀉
black􀀉sludge.􀀉No􀀉amount􀀉of􀀉scrubbing􀀉could􀀉lift􀀉the􀀉dark􀀉grit􀀉from􀀉under􀀉
his􀀉fingernails􀀉—􀀉a􀀉permanent􀀉stain.􀀉

That􀀉grit􀀉was􀀉the􀀉thread􀀉that􀀉pulled􀀉him􀀉back.􀀉The􀀉same􀀉stain,􀀉the􀀉same􀀉
screech,􀀉had􀀉defined􀀉him􀀉long􀀉before􀀉his􀀉family􀀉started􀀉calling􀀉him􀀉their􀀉
engine.􀀉

He􀀉reached􀀉into􀀉his􀀉pocket􀀉and􀀉felt􀀉the􀀉cold􀀉piece􀀉of􀀉stamped􀀉metal􀀉
Camilla􀀉had􀀉pulled􀀉from􀀉the􀀉sump􀀉months􀀉ago.􀀉She’d􀀉given􀀉it􀀉to􀀉him􀀉after􀀉
she’d􀀉decoded􀀉what􀀉was􀀉on􀀉it􀀉—􀀉said􀀉he􀀉should􀀉keep􀀉it􀀉where􀀉he􀀉could􀀉feel􀀉
it.􀀉He􀀉couldn’t􀀉parse􀀉the􀀉letters􀀉—􀀉to􀀉him,􀀉they􀀉were􀀉sharp,􀀉interlocking􀀉
teeth􀀉of􀀉dark􀀉grooves􀀉—􀀉but􀀉he􀀉remembered􀀉her􀀉voice􀀉trembling􀀉when􀀉she􀀉
read􀀉them􀀉aloud:􀀉Those􀀈left􀀈unprotected􀀈offer􀀈the􀀈greatest􀀈return􀀈for􀀈those􀀈who􀀈
wish􀀈to􀀈take.􀀈

He􀀉didn’t􀀉need􀀉to􀀉be􀀉a􀀉scholar􀀉to􀀉understand.􀀉He􀀉lived􀀉it􀀉every􀀉time􀀉the􀀉sun􀀉
rose.􀀉

Gage􀀉carried􀀉a􀀉tough􀀉exterior􀀉built􀀉before􀀉he􀀉ever􀀉swung􀀉a􀀉pick􀀉—􀀉Pappy􀀉
and􀀉Thorton􀀉had􀀉spent􀀉years􀀉building􀀉it􀀉for􀀉him,􀀉sacrifice􀀉by􀀉sacrifice,􀀉
before􀀉the􀀉mine􀀉ever􀀉got􀀉its􀀉hands􀀉on􀀉him.􀀉He􀀉had􀀉Pappy􀀉to􀀉thank􀀉for􀀉the􀀉


blood􀀉in􀀉his􀀉veins,􀀉and􀀉Thorton􀀉to􀀉thank􀀉for􀀉survival.􀀉They􀀉had􀀉realized􀀉
early􀀉that􀀉Gage􀀉was􀀉the􀀉family’s􀀉whole􀀉bet.􀀉A􀀉boy􀀉that􀀉big􀀉couldn’t􀀉be􀀉built􀀉
on􀀉standard􀀉grey􀀉mush.􀀉Throughout􀀉his􀀉teenage􀀉years,􀀉Thorton􀀉and􀀉Pappy􀀉
practiced􀀉a􀀉silent􀀉ritual:􀀉every􀀉evening,􀀉they􀀉slid􀀉half􀀉their􀀉starch􀀉cubes􀀉onto􀀉
Gage’s􀀉plate􀀉while􀀉Rita’s􀀉back􀀉was􀀉turned.􀀉

They􀀉weren’t􀀉just􀀉feeding􀀉him;􀀉they􀀉were􀀉building􀀉a􀀉machine.􀀉While􀀉the􀀉
Grey􀀉Wash􀀉ground􀀉most􀀉men􀀉into􀀉husks􀀉by􀀉thirty,􀀉Thorton􀀉and􀀉Pappy􀀉bet􀀉
everything􀀉on􀀉Gage’s􀀉endurance.􀀉They􀀉over-engineered􀀉his􀀉bones􀀉through􀀉
the􀀉slow􀀉theft􀀉of􀀉their􀀉own􀀉vitality.􀀉Every􀀉starch􀀉cube􀀉was􀀉a􀀉calorie􀀉of􀀉
defiance􀀉—􀀉a􀀉spark􀀉of􀀉stolen􀀉heat􀀉meant􀀉to􀀉ensure􀀉Gage􀀉wouldn’t􀀉red-line􀀉
before􀀉he􀀉reached􀀉the􀀉deep.􀀉He􀀉wasn’t􀀉just􀀉their􀀉son􀀉or􀀉grandson;􀀉he􀀉was􀀉
the􀀉future􀀉they􀀉were􀀉trying􀀉to􀀉build.􀀉

But􀀉before􀀉the􀀉mine,􀀉there􀀉had􀀉been􀀉the􀀉dream.􀀉As􀀉a􀀉child,􀀉Gage􀀉was􀀉
slender,􀀉possessed􀀉by􀀉curiosity.􀀉While􀀉most􀀉children􀀉played􀀉with􀀉rubble,􀀉he􀀉
drew􀀉dream􀀉worlds􀀉over􀀉the􀀉walls􀀉of􀀉Miner􀀉Town􀀉with􀀉coal􀀉chalk􀀉—􀀉
pushing􀀉it􀀉so􀀉hard􀀉it􀀉felt􀀉like􀀉carving􀀉a􀀉door􀀉through􀀉stone.􀀉

That􀀉same􀀉screech􀀉—􀀉coal􀀉on􀀉rock􀀉—􀀉had􀀉been􀀉the􀀉soundtrack􀀉of􀀉his􀀉
youth.􀀉While􀀉he􀀉drew􀀉forests􀀉and􀀉oceans,􀀉places􀀉he􀀉only􀀉knew􀀉from􀀉stories,􀀉
the􀀉black􀀉dust􀀉got􀀉stuck􀀉under􀀉his􀀉fingernails.􀀉His􀀉dreams􀀉were􀀉sinking􀀉into􀀉
his􀀉skin.􀀉

Every􀀉evening,􀀉he􀀉sat􀀉in􀀉Pappy’s􀀉lap􀀉against􀀉the􀀉flickering􀀉flames,􀀉listening􀀉
to􀀉tales􀀉of􀀉the􀀉world􀀉that􀀉once􀀉was.􀀉Thorton􀀉would􀀉frown􀀉at􀀉Pappy􀀉
whenever􀀉the􀀉old􀀉man􀀉started􀀉planting􀀉fairyland􀀉stories􀀉in􀀉the􀀉boy’s􀀉head.􀀉
Thorton􀀉knew􀀉the􀀉reality􀀉of􀀉the􀀉shovel;􀀉he􀀉knew􀀉it􀀉was􀀉designed􀀉to􀀉bury􀀉
any􀀉dream􀀉of􀀉a􀀉different􀀉life.􀀉

But􀀉Pappy􀀉was􀀉stubborn􀀉in􀀉the􀀉way􀀉only􀀉old􀀉men􀀉can􀀉be􀀉—􀀉stubborn􀀉with􀀉
a􀀉gentleness􀀉that􀀉made􀀉it􀀉impossible􀀉to􀀉argue􀀉with.􀀉He􀀉would􀀉wait􀀉for􀀉
Thorton􀀉to􀀉look􀀉away,􀀉then􀀉lean􀀉close􀀉to􀀉Gage􀀉and􀀉pick􀀉up􀀉exactly􀀉where􀀉


he’d􀀉left􀀉off,􀀉his􀀉voice􀀉dropping􀀉to􀀉a􀀉conspiratorial􀀉whisper.􀀉“Where􀀉were􀀉
we,􀀉boy?􀀉Ah,􀀉yes.􀀉The􀀉rivers.”􀀉

The􀀉rivers􀀉were􀀉Gage’s􀀉favorite.􀀉Pappy􀀉described􀀉them􀀉as􀀉roads􀀉made􀀉of􀀉
water􀀉—􀀉water􀀉so􀀉clean􀀉you􀀉could􀀉see􀀉the􀀉stones􀀉at􀀉the􀀉bottom,􀀉water􀀉that􀀉
moved􀀉of􀀉its􀀉own􀀉will,􀀉finding􀀉its􀀉way􀀉downhill􀀉without􀀉being􀀉pumped􀀉or􀀉
rationed􀀉or􀀉fought􀀉over.􀀉Gage􀀉would􀀉close􀀉his􀀉eyes􀀉and􀀉try􀀉to􀀉imagine􀀉it:􀀉a􀀉
whole􀀉road􀀉of􀀉the􀀉stuff,􀀉flowing􀀉freely,􀀉belonging􀀉to􀀉no􀀉one.􀀉It􀀉was􀀉the􀀉most􀀉
impossible􀀉thing􀀉Pappy􀀉ever􀀉told􀀉him,􀀉more􀀉impossible􀀉than􀀉the􀀉forests􀀉or􀀉
the􀀉oceans􀀉or􀀉the􀀉animals􀀉with􀀉names􀀉he􀀉couldn’t􀀉pronounce.􀀉Because􀀉in􀀉
Miner􀀉Town,􀀉water􀀉was􀀉life􀀉measured􀀉in􀀉tin􀀉cups.􀀉The􀀉idea􀀉that􀀉it􀀉once􀀉
flowed􀀉endlessly,􀀉wasted􀀉and􀀉wild,􀀉felt􀀉less􀀉like􀀉history􀀉and􀀉more􀀉like􀀉a􀀉cruel􀀉
joke.􀀉

“Did􀀉people􀀉swim􀀉in􀀉them?”􀀉Gage􀀉asked􀀉once.􀀉

“Swim,􀀉fish,􀀉drink,􀀉play.􀀉Children􀀉would􀀉jump􀀉from􀀉the􀀉banks􀀉on􀀉hot􀀉days􀀉
and􀀉let􀀉the􀀉current􀀉carry􀀉them.”􀀉

“Weren’t􀀉they􀀉afraid?”􀀉

“Of􀀉what?”􀀉

“Of􀀉using􀀉it􀀉all􀀉up.”􀀉

Pappy􀀉had􀀉gone􀀉quiet􀀉at􀀉that.􀀉He􀀉held􀀉Gage􀀉tighter􀀉and􀀉stared􀀉into􀀉the􀀉fire􀀉
for􀀉a􀀉long􀀉time.􀀉When􀀉he􀀉finally􀀉spoke,􀀉his􀀉voice􀀉was􀀉different􀀉—􀀉heavier.􀀉
“No,􀀉boy.􀀉They􀀉weren’t􀀉afraid.􀀉That􀀉was􀀉the􀀉problem.”􀀉

Gage􀀉carried􀀉those􀀉stories􀀉like􀀉contraband,􀀉hidden􀀉in􀀉a􀀉part􀀉of􀀉himself􀀉the􀀉
mine􀀉couldn’t􀀉reach.􀀉He􀀉never􀀉told􀀉his􀀉friends􀀉about􀀉the􀀉rivers.􀀉Some􀀉
things􀀉were􀀉too􀀉fragile􀀉to􀀉share;􀀉saying􀀉them􀀉out􀀉loud􀀉in􀀉the􀀉coal􀀉dust􀀉felt􀀉
like􀀉it􀀉would􀀉break􀀉them.􀀉

Gage􀀉and􀀉his􀀉best􀀉pals,􀀉Otto􀀉Baxter􀀉and􀀉Ben􀀉Parker,􀀉hung􀀉out􀀉in􀀉a􀀉backyard􀀉
made􀀉of􀀉oily􀀉silt.􀀉They􀀉didn’t􀀉just􀀉build􀀉tunnels;􀀉they􀀉rehearsed􀀉escapes.􀀉


They􀀉watched􀀉the􀀉Urban􀀉Servants􀀉from􀀉a􀀉distance􀀉—􀀉guards􀀉who􀀉wore􀀉the􀀉
Trinity’s􀀉colors.􀀉These􀀉men􀀉were􀀉a􀀉different􀀉kind􀀉of􀀉victim:􀀉fed􀀉better,􀀉but􀀉
their􀀉loyalty􀀉bought􀀉with􀀉drugs􀀉and􀀉sensory􀀉deprivation􀀉that􀀉stripped􀀉away􀀉
empathy.􀀉By􀀉dusk,􀀉the􀀉oily􀀉silt􀀉filled􀀉every􀀉crack􀀉in􀀉their􀀉skin􀀉—􀀉the􀀉mine􀀉
marking􀀉them􀀉as􀀉its􀀉own􀀉even􀀉while􀀉they􀀉dreamed􀀉of􀀉breaking􀀉out.􀀉

His􀀉half-sister,􀀉Camilla,􀀉would􀀉try􀀉to􀀉sneak􀀉into􀀉their􀀉backyard􀀉missions.􀀉
Gage􀀉would􀀉push􀀉her􀀉back:􀀉“We’re􀀉not􀀉looking􀀉for􀀉weaklings􀀉for􀀉this􀀉
dangerous􀀉mission!”􀀉She􀀉was􀀉two􀀉years􀀉younger,􀀉Rita’s􀀉daughter􀀉—􀀉Rita􀀉
who􀀉had􀀉stepped􀀉in􀀉after􀀉Gage’s􀀉mother􀀉died􀀉giving􀀉him􀀉life.􀀉Camilla􀀉was􀀉
his􀀉half-sister,􀀉but􀀉she􀀉had􀀉never􀀉loved􀀉him􀀉by􀀉halves.􀀉

While􀀉Gage􀀉carved􀀉charcoal􀀉forests􀀉into􀀉stone,􀀉Pappy􀀉noticed􀀉Camilla􀀉
doing􀀉something􀀉far􀀉more􀀉dangerous:􀀉taking􀀉the􀀉world􀀉apart􀀉to􀀉see􀀉how􀀉it􀀉
worked.􀀉She􀀉sat􀀉in􀀉the􀀉oily􀀉silt,􀀉hunched􀀉over􀀉a􀀉broken􀀉copper􀀉lamp􀀉pulled􀀉
from􀀉scrapped􀀉history.􀀉In􀀉a􀀉world􀀉where􀀉the􀀉Trinity􀀉hoarded􀀉every􀀉spark􀀉of􀀉
energy,􀀉copper􀀉was􀀉more􀀉than􀀉metal;􀀉it􀀉was􀀉a􀀉relic􀀉of􀀉a􀀉time􀀉when􀀉light􀀉
wasn’t􀀉a􀀉luxury.􀀉

Camilla􀀉didn’t􀀉draw􀀉with􀀉coal.􀀉She􀀉used􀀉a􀀉shard􀀉of􀀉glass􀀉to􀀉scrape􀀉the􀀉
corrosion􀀉off􀀉the􀀉copper,􀀉her􀀉movements􀀉steady􀀉and􀀉tireless.􀀉She􀀉had􀀉what􀀉
Pappy􀀉called􀀉the􀀉quiet􀀉hands􀀉—􀀉hands􀀉that􀀉didn’t􀀉just􀀉move􀀉weight,􀀉but􀀉
understood􀀉the􀀉tension􀀉within􀀉it.􀀉

“Gage􀀉has􀀉the􀀉strength,”􀀉Pappy􀀉whispered.􀀉“The􀀉family􀀉has􀀉poured􀀉
everything􀀉into􀀉building􀀉him.􀀉But􀀉a􀀉body􀀉without􀀉a􀀉mind􀀉to􀀉guide􀀉it􀀉is􀀉a􀀉
thing􀀉that􀀉breaks􀀉itself.”􀀉

Pappy􀀉beckoned􀀉Camilla􀀉over􀀉and􀀉produced􀀉an􀀉oil-stained􀀉manual􀀉for􀀉an􀀉
old-world􀀉generator􀀉—􀀉forbidden􀀉sight􀀉in􀀉a􀀉town􀀉meant􀀉to􀀉be􀀉blind.􀀉He􀀉
pointed􀀉to􀀉a􀀉bold􀀉word􀀉on􀀉their􀀉rusted􀀉water􀀉tank,􀀉then􀀉traced􀀉the􀀉same􀀉
four􀀉letters􀀉in􀀉the􀀉manual.􀀉

“That’s􀀉not􀀉just􀀉a􀀉shape,􀀉little􀀉bird,”􀀉Pappy􀀉told􀀉her,􀀉eyes􀀉reflecting􀀉the􀀉
flickering􀀉coal-fire.􀀉“It’s􀀉four􀀉sounds.􀀉F.􀀉U.􀀉E.􀀉L.􀀉Together􀀉they􀀉make􀀉‘fuel’􀀉


—􀀉the􀀉word􀀉for􀀉fire􀀉trapped􀀉inside.􀀉Tonight􀀉we􀀉learn􀀉these􀀉four.􀀉Tomorrow􀀉
we􀀉learn􀀉four􀀉more.􀀉If􀀉you􀀉can’t􀀉read􀀉the􀀉labels,􀀉you’ll􀀉never􀀉know􀀉where􀀉
the􀀉heat􀀉is􀀉hiding.􀀉And􀀉you’ll􀀉walk􀀉past􀀉one􀀉of􀀉them􀀉someday,􀀉and􀀉it’ll􀀉
catch􀀉you.”􀀉
He􀀉placed􀀉his􀀉gnarled􀀉hand􀀉over􀀉her􀀉small􀀉one􀀉on􀀉the􀀉copper􀀉coil.􀀉“Gage􀀉
has􀀉all􀀉the􀀉strength􀀉the􀀉family􀀉could􀀉give􀀉him,􀀉little􀀉bird.􀀉But􀀉strength􀀉alone􀀉
breaks􀀉itself.􀀉Copper􀀉doesn’t􀀉fight􀀉what􀀉runs􀀉through􀀉it;􀀉it􀀉directs􀀉it.􀀉That’s􀀉
what􀀉you’ll􀀉be􀀉for􀀉him.”􀀉

Gage􀀉watched􀀉the􀀉firelight􀀉dance􀀉on􀀉copper􀀉from􀀉the􀀉shadows.􀀉He􀀉didn’t􀀉
have􀀉words􀀉for􀀉what􀀉he􀀉was􀀉seeing,􀀉but􀀉he􀀉understood􀀉what􀀉Pappy􀀉was􀀉
doing.􀀉He􀀉wasn’t􀀉just􀀉teaching􀀉Camilla􀀉to􀀉read􀀉—􀀉he􀀉was􀀉giving􀀉her􀀉the􀀉
eyes􀀉Gage􀀉would􀀉need􀀉someday.􀀉

In􀀉Miner􀀉Town,􀀉though,􀀉realizations􀀉were􀀉luxuries􀀉the􀀉clock􀀉always􀀉ran􀀉out􀀉
on.􀀉

The􀀉end􀀉of􀀉innocence􀀉arrived􀀉on􀀉the􀀉day􀀉of􀀉the􀀉Coalball􀀉game.􀀉Peace􀀉didn’t􀀉
break􀀉with􀀉a􀀉shout;􀀉it􀀉broke􀀉with􀀉the􀀉hollow􀀉thud􀀉of􀀉a􀀉coal􀀉block􀀉hitting􀀉
forbidden􀀉dirt.􀀉

They’d􀀉been􀀉playing􀀉near􀀉the􀀉exhausted􀀉mine􀀉fields􀀉in􀀉the􀀉late-afternoon􀀉
window􀀉—􀀉the􀀉only􀀉stretch􀀉of􀀉the􀀉day􀀉when􀀉the􀀉sun􀀉let􀀉kids􀀉be􀀉kids􀀉—􀀉
Gage,􀀉Otto,􀀉and􀀉Ben􀀉lost􀀉in􀀉the􀀉rare􀀉joy􀀉of􀀉the􀀉game.􀀉Camilla􀀉sat􀀉nearby,􀀉
tracing􀀉the􀀉grooves􀀉of􀀉a􀀉discarded􀀉copper􀀉coil.􀀉To􀀉any􀀉observer,􀀉they􀀉were􀀉
just􀀉children;􀀉to􀀉the􀀉Trinity,􀀉they􀀉were􀀉simply􀀉unprocessed􀀉resources.􀀉

A􀀉stray􀀉throw,􀀉fueled􀀉by􀀉Gage’s􀀉unrefined􀀉strength,􀀉sent􀀉the􀀉block􀀉soaring􀀉
into􀀉the􀀉guard􀀉camp.􀀉

The􀀉silence􀀉was􀀉immediate.􀀉Four􀀉guards􀀉swarmed􀀉out􀀉behind􀀉predator-
snout􀀉masks.􀀉Otto􀀉and􀀉Ben􀀉bolted􀀉for􀀉the􀀉shacks.􀀉Camilla􀀉stayed.􀀉


When􀀉a􀀉guard􀀉reached􀀉for􀀉Gage,􀀉she􀀉lunged􀀉—􀀉bit􀀉the􀀉guard’s􀀉hand􀀉with􀀉
everything􀀉she􀀉had.􀀉The􀀉retaliation􀀉was􀀉a􀀉blur􀀉of􀀉leather􀀉and􀀉grit.􀀉The􀀉whip􀀉
wound􀀉around􀀉her􀀉leg,􀀉spinning􀀉her􀀉into􀀉the􀀉coal􀀉dust.􀀉

By􀀉the􀀉time􀀉Pappy􀀉and􀀉Rita􀀉arrived,􀀉gasping,􀀉Camilla􀀉lay􀀉unconscious􀀉in􀀉
the􀀉coal􀀉dust.􀀉Rita􀀉dropped􀀉to􀀉her􀀉knees􀀉beside􀀉her􀀉daughter,􀀉hands􀀉
moving􀀉over􀀉the􀀉bruised􀀉body.􀀉A􀀉guard􀀉had􀀉Gage􀀉pinned􀀉by􀀉the􀀉throat.􀀉
Pappy􀀉stepped􀀉forward􀀉and􀀉fell􀀉to􀀉his􀀉knees􀀉in􀀉front􀀉of􀀉the􀀉guard.􀀉“Please􀀉

—􀀉forgive􀀉him.􀀉He’s􀀉just􀀉a􀀉boy.”􀀉
“Anything􀀉you􀀉say,􀀉sir,”􀀉the􀀉guard􀀉said􀀉through􀀉his􀀉mask,􀀉the􀀉words􀀉thin􀀉
and􀀉metallic.􀀉“But􀀉every􀀉game􀀉needs􀀉a􀀉dog􀀉to􀀉fetch􀀉the􀀉ball.”􀀉

He􀀉threw􀀉the􀀉coal􀀉block􀀉forty􀀉yards􀀉into􀀉the􀀉blistering􀀉sun.􀀉Then􀀉he􀀉drew􀀉a􀀉
knife􀀉and􀀉rested􀀉the􀀉flat􀀉of􀀉it􀀉against􀀉Gage’s􀀉cheek,􀀉almost􀀉gentle.􀀉

“Sixty􀀉seconds,􀀉old􀀉man.􀀉Every􀀉second􀀉after,􀀉I􀀉take􀀉a􀀉piece􀀉of􀀉his􀀉face.􀀉Could􀀉
be􀀉we􀀉play􀀉a􀀉long􀀉game.”􀀉

Pappy􀀉ran.􀀉Gage􀀉watched,􀀉his􀀉internal􀀉world􀀉fracturing􀀉as􀀉he􀀉saw􀀉the􀀉source􀀉
of􀀉his􀀉strength􀀉—􀀉the􀀉man􀀉who􀀉had􀀉starved􀀉himself􀀉to􀀉build􀀉Gage’s􀀉body􀀉

—􀀉stumble􀀉into􀀉the􀀉heat.􀀉Pappy􀀉was􀀉already􀀉broken􀀉—􀀉years􀀉of􀀉grey􀀉rot􀀉in􀀉
his􀀉lungs,􀀉a􀀉heart􀀉that􀀉had􀀉been􀀉failing􀀉for􀀉two􀀉winters􀀉—􀀉and􀀉the􀀉sprint􀀉
was􀀉more􀀉than􀀉his􀀉body􀀉could􀀉carry.􀀉He􀀉ran􀀉until􀀉his􀀉heart􀀉felt􀀉like􀀉it􀀉would􀀉
burst,􀀉until􀀉the􀀉sun􀀉pressed􀀉him􀀉into􀀉the􀀉grit.􀀉He􀀉collapsed􀀉inches􀀉from􀀉the􀀉
goal,􀀉the􀀉coal􀀉falling􀀉from􀀉his􀀉lifeless􀀉hand.􀀉The􀀉guards􀀉laughed.􀀉
The􀀉guard􀀉with􀀉the􀀉knife􀀉didn’t􀀉kill􀀉Gage.􀀉He􀀉crouched􀀉in􀀉front􀀉of􀀉him,􀀉
the􀀉blade􀀉catching􀀉the􀀉light.􀀉

“Promise􀀉is􀀉a􀀉promise,􀀉boy.􀀉Old􀀉man􀀉missed􀀉the􀀉goal.􀀉Long􀀉game􀀉wins.”􀀉

He􀀉used􀀉the􀀉steel􀀉to􀀉trace􀀉a􀀉stinging􀀉trail􀀉across􀀉Gage’s􀀉brow􀀉—􀀉the􀀉first􀀉
lines􀀉of􀀉the􀀉Map􀀉of􀀉Miner􀀉Town.􀀉As􀀉blood􀀉dripped􀀉into􀀉his􀀉eyes,􀀉Gage􀀉
looked􀀉at􀀉his􀀉fallen􀀉Pappy􀀉and􀀉his􀀉broken􀀉sister.􀀉The􀀉boy􀀉who􀀉drew􀀉forests􀀉
was􀀉dead.􀀉He􀀉had􀀉finally􀀉found􀀉his􀀉Why,􀀉and􀀉he􀀉swore􀀉a􀀉silent,􀀉bloody􀀉vow:􀀉


he􀀉would􀀉carry􀀉this􀀉map􀀉until􀀉he􀀉burned􀀉down􀀉the􀀉gates􀀉of􀀉the􀀉Trinity􀀉and􀀉
everything􀀉they􀀉protected.􀀉


Chapter􀀉3􀀉—􀀉The􀀉Initiation􀀉

Three􀀉days.􀀉In􀀉the􀀉brutal􀀉calculus􀀉of􀀉Miner􀀉Town,􀀉three􀀉days􀀉was􀀉the􀀉
precise􀀉duration􀀉required􀀉to􀀉wither􀀉the􀀉human􀀉spirit􀀉and􀀉begin􀀉the􀀉
biological􀀉harvest.􀀉Every􀀉child􀀉feared􀀉it􀀉from􀀉the􀀉moment􀀉they􀀉understood􀀉
the􀀉weight􀀉of􀀉the􀀉mountain􀀉—􀀉a􀀉dark􀀉transition􀀉called􀀉the􀀉Initiation.􀀉

“The􀀉beginning􀀉of􀀉a􀀉glorious􀀉future,”􀀉the􀀉sector􀀉warden􀀉they􀀉called􀀉Icepick􀀉
would􀀉drone􀀉at􀀉every􀀉ceremony,􀀉his􀀉voice􀀉as􀀉cold􀀉as􀀉the􀀉deep-vein􀀉coal􀀉he􀀉
coveted.􀀉Standing􀀉on􀀉his􀀉platform,􀀉he􀀉looked􀀉down􀀉at􀀉the􀀉intake􀀉not􀀉as􀀉
children,􀀉but􀀉as􀀉raw􀀉materials.􀀉“This􀀉is􀀉the􀀉day􀀉you􀀉earn􀀉your􀀉keep.􀀉The􀀉day􀀉
you􀀉stop􀀉being􀀉a􀀉mouth􀀉to􀀉feed􀀉and􀀉start􀀉being􀀉a􀀉hand􀀉that􀀉gives.”􀀉

Gage􀀉stood􀀉in􀀉the􀀉dust-choked􀀉queue􀀉with􀀉Otto􀀉and􀀉Ben,􀀉feeling􀀉the􀀉iron􀀉
weight􀀉of􀀉the􀀉moment􀀉pressing􀀉into􀀉his􀀉shoulders.􀀉He􀀉turned􀀉his􀀉head􀀉one􀀉
last􀀉time􀀉and􀀉caught􀀉his􀀉family􀀉in􀀉the􀀉dim􀀉light.􀀉He􀀉didn’t􀀉just􀀉look􀀉at􀀉them􀀉

—􀀉he􀀉paused􀀉at􀀉the􀀉threshold,􀀉his􀀉gaze􀀉lingering􀀉on􀀉Camilla􀀉as􀀉if􀀉
memorizing􀀉the􀀉lines􀀉of􀀉her􀀉face􀀉for􀀉a􀀉much􀀉longer􀀉journey.􀀉The􀀉scar􀀉on􀀉his􀀉
brow􀀉seemed􀀉to􀀉pulse.􀀉
Behind􀀉him,􀀉beyond􀀉the􀀉queue,􀀉Camilla􀀉tugged􀀉at􀀉Rita’s􀀉sleeve.􀀉“Why􀀉did􀀉
Gage􀀉look􀀉at􀀉us􀀉like􀀉he􀀉was􀀉saying􀀉goodbye,􀀉Ma?􀀉Isn’t􀀉he􀀉coming􀀉back􀀉after􀀉
three􀀉days?”􀀉

“Of􀀉course􀀉he’ll􀀉be􀀉back,”􀀉Rita􀀉replied,􀀉though􀀉her􀀉voice􀀉lacked􀀉truth.􀀉She’d􀀉
seen􀀉too􀀉many􀀉kids􀀉march􀀉into􀀉that􀀉tunnel􀀉only􀀉to􀀉return􀀉as􀀉Hollows.􀀉“He􀀉
just􀀉won’t􀀉be􀀉the􀀉same.”􀀉

Legend􀀉had􀀉it􀀉that􀀉Icepick􀀉himself􀀉designed􀀉the􀀉ceremony􀀉to􀀉ensure􀀉every􀀉
recruit􀀉was􀀉broken􀀉before􀀉they􀀉ever􀀉set􀀉foot􀀉in􀀉the􀀉active􀀉shafts.􀀉As􀀉the􀀉
massive􀀉iron􀀉gates􀀉slid􀀉open􀀉with􀀉a􀀉soul-jarring􀀉screech,􀀉a􀀉heavy􀀉air􀀉
enveloped􀀉the􀀉kids.􀀉Yet􀀉once􀀉swallowed􀀉by􀀉darkness,􀀉the􀀉initial􀀉fear􀀉briefly􀀉
evaporated,􀀉replaced􀀉by􀀉a􀀉calculated􀀉deception.􀀉For􀀉a􀀉few􀀉hours,􀀉the􀀉


youngsters􀀉wandered􀀉freely.􀀉To􀀉them,􀀉there􀀉was􀀉no􀀉surveillance,􀀉no􀀉guards,􀀉
and􀀉most􀀉importantly,􀀉no􀀉hunger.􀀉Each􀀉recruit􀀉carried􀀉a􀀉vacuum-sealed􀀉
pouch􀀉of􀀉food􀀉—􀀉Spire-made􀀉tech􀀉that􀀉felt􀀉like􀀉a􀀉king’s􀀉fortune􀀉in􀀉a􀀉land􀀉of􀀉
scarcity.􀀉

Gage􀀉tore􀀉into􀀉the􀀉heavy􀀉plastic,􀀉and􀀉a􀀉cloud􀀉of􀀉thick􀀉white􀀉steam􀀉billowed􀀉
out,􀀉smelling􀀉so􀀉rich􀀉it􀀉made􀀉his􀀉head􀀉spin.􀀉Warm,􀀉buttery􀀉potatoes􀀉and􀀉the􀀉
savory􀀉salt􀀉of􀀉poultry.􀀉To􀀉a􀀉boy􀀉who’d􀀉spent􀀉his􀀉life􀀉eating􀀉grey􀀉starch􀀉mush,􀀉
it􀀉felt􀀉like􀀉holding􀀉an􀀉edible􀀉cloud􀀉in􀀉his􀀉blackened􀀉fingers.􀀉They􀀉ate􀀉like􀀉
starving􀀉animals,􀀉washing􀀉it􀀉down􀀉with􀀉cool,􀀉clear􀀉water􀀉that􀀉felt􀀉like􀀉a􀀉lost􀀉
memory􀀉of􀀉rain􀀉—􀀉though􀀉something􀀉at􀀉the􀀉back􀀉of􀀉the􀀉throat􀀉warned􀀉
them,􀀉a􀀉thin􀀉metallic􀀉tang􀀉like􀀉a􀀉copper􀀉coin􀀉held􀀉under􀀉the􀀉tongue.􀀉

Gage􀀉lowered􀀉the􀀉cup.􀀉The􀀉light􀀉caught􀀉a􀀉rainbow􀀉shimmer􀀉on􀀉the􀀉surface􀀉

—􀀉a􀀉slick􀀉that􀀉hadn’t􀀉been􀀉there􀀉before.􀀉He􀀉could􀀉feel􀀉it􀀉now,􀀉a􀀉hum􀀉behind􀀉
his􀀉eyes,􀀉his􀀉pulse􀀉climbing􀀉for􀀉no􀀉reason.􀀉A􀀉chemical􀀉warning􀀉he’d􀀉
swallowed􀀉too􀀉late.􀀉The􀀉food􀀉hadn’t􀀉been􀀉a􀀉gift.􀀉It􀀉had􀀉been􀀉a􀀉delivery􀀉—􀀉
though􀀉it􀀉would􀀉be􀀉hours􀀉before􀀉he’d􀀉know􀀉what􀀉it􀀉had􀀉delivered:􀀉a􀀉
rainbow􀀉fuel􀀉designed􀀉to􀀉lock􀀉the􀀉nervous􀀉system􀀉into􀀉permanent􀀉
wakefulness􀀉until􀀉the􀀉mind􀀉fractured.􀀉Icepick􀀉wasn’t􀀉feeding􀀉children.􀀉He􀀉
was􀀉priming􀀉them.􀀉
As􀀉Ben􀀉laughed,􀀉face􀀉smeared􀀉with􀀉grease,􀀉Gage􀀉couldn’t􀀉shake􀀉Pappy’s􀀉
voice.􀀉This􀀉was􀀉exactly􀀉how􀀉the􀀉Great􀀉Blind􀀉Eye􀀉had􀀉begun􀀉—􀀉not􀀉with􀀉a􀀉
bang,􀀉but􀀉with􀀉a􀀉bribe.􀀉Pappy􀀉had􀀉told􀀉him􀀉stories􀀉of􀀉the􀀉old􀀉world􀀉trading􀀉
the􀀉sky􀀉itself􀀉for􀀉a􀀉few􀀉more􀀉hours􀀉of􀀉fake􀀉winter,􀀉and􀀉now,􀀉looking􀀉at􀀉the􀀉
sterile􀀉plastic􀀉in􀀉his􀀉hands,􀀉Gage􀀉saw􀀉the􀀉pattern􀀉repeating.􀀉The􀀉Trinity􀀉
wasn’t􀀉feeding􀀉them􀀉out􀀉of􀀉mercy;􀀉they􀀉were􀀉fattening􀀉the􀀉Engines􀀉before􀀉
the􀀉harvest.􀀉He􀀉looked􀀉at􀀉the􀀉silver􀀉needles􀀉on􀀉the􀀉horizon􀀉and􀀉realized􀀉that􀀉
for􀀉the􀀉elite􀀉in􀀉their􀀉isolated􀀉ecospheres,􀀉this􀀉Initiation􀀉was􀀉just􀀉a􀀉high-stakes􀀉
strategy􀀉game,􀀉and􀀉he,􀀉Ben,􀀉and􀀉Otto􀀉were􀀉finally􀀉on􀀉the􀀉board.􀀉

High􀀉above,􀀉Elara􀀉adjusted􀀉her􀀉interface.􀀉She􀀉watched􀀉three􀀉heat-signatures􀀉
huddled􀀉against􀀉a􀀉salt-stained􀀉wall.􀀉To􀀉her,􀀉Gage,􀀉Ben,􀀉and􀀉Otto􀀉weren’t􀀉


children􀀉experiencing􀀉their􀀉first􀀉full􀀉stomach;􀀉they􀀉were􀀉newly􀀉activated􀀉
units􀀉on􀀉her􀀉ledger.􀀉She􀀉noted􀀉the􀀉Engine􀀉sharing􀀉his􀀉polymer􀀉pouch􀀉—􀀉a􀀉
deviation􀀉in􀀉resource􀀉management􀀉—􀀉with􀀉a􀀉flick􀀉of􀀉her􀀉finger.􀀉

After􀀉the􀀉meal,􀀉the􀀉youngsters􀀉gathered􀀉to􀀉compare􀀉pouches􀀉and􀀉trade􀀉
rumors􀀉—􀀉what􀀉each􀀉had􀀉heard􀀉about􀀉the􀀉Initiation,􀀉which􀀉older􀀉sibling􀀉
had􀀉survived,􀀉which􀀉had􀀉come􀀉back􀀉as􀀉a􀀉Hollow.􀀉But􀀉as􀀉hours􀀉bled􀀉
together,􀀉horror􀀉settled􀀉in.􀀉Gage􀀉realized􀀉they’d􀀉been􀀉awake􀀉for􀀉nearly􀀉
eighteen􀀉hours􀀉without􀀉rest.􀀉By􀀉the􀀉second􀀉evening,􀀉panic􀀉took􀀉root.􀀉Their􀀉
hands􀀉ached,􀀉their􀀉backs􀀉felt􀀉like􀀉stiffened􀀉hide,􀀉but􀀉their􀀉minds􀀉remained􀀉
wide,􀀉terrifyingly􀀉awake.􀀉They􀀉tried􀀉racing􀀉through􀀉the􀀉tunnels􀀉to􀀉exhaust􀀉
their􀀉bodies􀀉—􀀉only􀀉blistered􀀉feet􀀉and􀀉more􀀉pain.􀀉Still􀀉no􀀉sleep.􀀉

By􀀉the􀀉twenty-fourth􀀉hour,􀀉the􀀉gathering􀀉broke􀀉apart.􀀉Conversation􀀉came􀀉
in􀀉fragments􀀉now􀀉—􀀉sentences􀀉that􀀉started􀀉in􀀉one􀀉mouth􀀉and􀀉ended􀀉in􀀉
another􀀉that􀀉had􀀉forgotten􀀉what􀀉was􀀉being􀀉said.􀀉The􀀉kids􀀉who􀀉had􀀉been􀀉
trading􀀉rumors􀀉a􀀉few􀀉hours􀀉earlier􀀉could􀀉not􀀉remember􀀉what􀀉each􀀉other􀀉
had􀀉told􀀉them.􀀉Some􀀉sat􀀉against􀀉the􀀉walls􀀉staring;􀀉others􀀉walked􀀉the􀀉tunnel􀀉
running􀀉their􀀉fingers􀀉over􀀉the􀀉stone􀀉like􀀉they􀀉were􀀉searching􀀉for􀀉something.􀀉
The􀀉voices􀀉echoed􀀉differently􀀉now.􀀉Words􀀉ran􀀉into􀀉themselves.􀀉

By􀀉thirty􀀉hours,􀀉the􀀉hallucinations􀀉began.􀀉A􀀉girl􀀉Gage􀀉didn’t􀀉know􀀉stood􀀉in􀀉
the􀀉middle􀀉of􀀉the􀀉chamber􀀉and􀀉shouted􀀉at􀀉someone􀀉who􀀉wasn’t􀀉there,􀀉
raising􀀉her􀀉arms􀀉to􀀉defend􀀉herself􀀉from􀀉a􀀉blow􀀉that􀀉never􀀉came.􀀉Another􀀉
boy􀀉laughed􀀉at􀀉the􀀉ceiling􀀉for􀀉nearly􀀉an􀀉hour􀀉without􀀉stopping.􀀉Gage􀀉saw􀀉
the􀀉salt􀀉patterns􀀉on􀀉the􀀉walls􀀉move􀀉—􀀉slow􀀉at􀀉first,􀀉then􀀉quickening􀀉into􀀉
something􀀉almost􀀉alive.􀀉He􀀉looked􀀉away.􀀉When􀀉he􀀉looked􀀉back,􀀉the􀀉
patterns􀀉were􀀉still􀀉moving.􀀉

By􀀉thirty-six,􀀉the􀀉bodies􀀉started􀀉to􀀉fail􀀉in􀀉different􀀉directions.􀀉Some􀀉kids􀀉
collapsed􀀉where􀀉they􀀉stood􀀉and􀀉lay􀀉motionless,􀀉eyes􀀉open,􀀉breathing􀀉
shallow,􀀉unresponsive􀀉when􀀉shaken.􀀉Others􀀉struck􀀉themselves􀀉—􀀉pulling􀀉at􀀉
their􀀉own􀀉hair,􀀉biting􀀉their􀀉hands,􀀉running􀀉into􀀉the􀀉walls􀀉just􀀉to􀀉feel􀀉
something􀀉other􀀉than􀀉the􀀉wakefulness.􀀉Otto􀀉held􀀉Ben􀀉back􀀉from􀀉one􀀉of􀀉


these.􀀉Gage􀀉tried􀀉to􀀉call􀀉out􀀉and􀀉found􀀉his􀀉voice􀀉gone,􀀉swallowed􀀉by􀀉the􀀉
same􀀉sandstone􀀉that􀀉was􀀉already􀀉collecting􀀉at􀀉the􀀉back􀀉of􀀉his􀀉throat.􀀉

By􀀉the􀀉forty-eight-hour􀀉mark,􀀉time􀀉didn’t􀀉flow;􀀉it􀀉fractured􀀉into􀀉jagged􀀉
shards.􀀉Gage􀀉tried􀀉to􀀉lift􀀉his􀀉arm,􀀉but􀀉it􀀉felt􀀉like􀀉a􀀉stone􀀉pillar􀀉fused􀀉to􀀉his􀀉
shoulder.􀀉Every􀀉time􀀉he􀀉blinked,􀀉it􀀉was􀀉as􀀉if􀀉sandpaper􀀉scraped􀀉his􀀉eyeballs􀀉

—􀀉the􀀉Trinity􀀉had􀀉removed􀀉the􀀉off-switch􀀉from􀀉his􀀉brain.􀀉
Then􀀉came􀀉the􀀉hum.􀀉Deep􀀉in􀀉the􀀉walls,􀀉the􀀉Stubborn􀀉Carbon􀀉began􀀉its􀀉
predatory􀀉song􀀉—􀀉a􀀉piezoelectric􀀉pulse􀀉that􀀉vibrated􀀉in􀀉perfect,􀀉cruel􀀉
synchronization􀀉with􀀉the􀀉rainbow􀀉fuel􀀉in􀀉his􀀉veins.􀀉

The􀀉world􀀉began􀀉to􀀉melt.􀀉Walls􀀉seemed􀀉to􀀉breathe,􀀉expanding􀀉and􀀉
contracting􀀉like􀀉the􀀉ribcage􀀉of􀀉a􀀉giant􀀉beast.􀀉His􀀉vision􀀉trailed􀀉in􀀉sickening􀀉
lag,􀀉leaving􀀉ghostly􀀉shadows􀀉that􀀉took􀀉a􀀉split􀀉second􀀉to􀀉snap􀀉into􀀉place.􀀉

Gage􀀉turned􀀉his􀀉sandpaper􀀉eyes􀀉toward􀀉the􀀉shadows􀀉where􀀉the􀀉Urban􀀉
Servants􀀉stood􀀉like􀀉statues.􀀉He􀀉couldn’t􀀉form􀀉the􀀉words􀀉anymore,􀀉but􀀉he􀀉
remembered􀀉something􀀉he’d􀀉thought􀀉about􀀉them􀀉at􀀉the􀀉gate􀀉—􀀉that􀀉they􀀉
had􀀉been􀀉here􀀉before,􀀉that􀀉they􀀉had􀀉been􀀉him,􀀉that􀀉this􀀉room􀀉was􀀉where􀀉
they􀀉had􀀉been􀀉made.􀀉

In􀀉this􀀉shimmering􀀉void,􀀉the􀀉rainbow􀀉fuel􀀉stripped􀀉away􀀉the􀀉barriers􀀉
between􀀉Gage’s􀀉memory􀀉and􀀉the􀀉present.􀀉In􀀉the􀀉corner􀀉of􀀉his􀀉vision,􀀉he􀀉saw􀀉
Pappy􀀉—􀀉not􀀉the􀀉vibrant􀀉storyteller,􀀉but􀀉the􀀉hollowed􀀉man􀀉from􀀉the􀀉sun-
scorched􀀉run.􀀉Every􀀉calorie􀀉he’d􀀉consumed􀀉felt􀀉like􀀉lead􀀉in􀀉his􀀉gut.􀀉It􀀉wasn’t􀀉
just􀀉food;􀀉it􀀉was􀀉a􀀉stolen􀀉life.􀀉

“I􀀉am􀀉the􀀉Engine,”􀀉Gage􀀉whispered,􀀉his􀀉voice􀀉a􀀉jagged􀀉rasp.􀀉“But􀀉the􀀉fuel…􀀉
the􀀉fuel􀀉is􀀉their􀀉blood.”􀀉

The􀀉hallucination􀀉tried􀀉to􀀉pull􀀉him􀀉under,􀀉but􀀉the􀀉Map􀀉on􀀉his􀀉brow􀀉flared􀀉
with􀀉phantom􀀉heat.􀀉The􀀉scar􀀉was􀀉a􀀉compass.􀀉He􀀉pictured􀀉Camilla􀀉in􀀉the􀀉
shack,􀀉her􀀉quiet􀀉hands􀀉scraping􀀉rot􀀉off􀀉a􀀉copper􀀉lamp.􀀉If􀀉he􀀉let􀀉his􀀉mind􀀉
melt􀀉here,􀀉she􀀉would􀀉be􀀉left􀀉alone.􀀉The􀀉Pappy􀀉Debt􀀉couldn’t􀀉be􀀉repaid􀀉by􀀉


dying􀀉in􀀉a􀀉hole;􀀉it􀀉could􀀉only􀀉be􀀉settled􀀉by􀀉becoming􀀉the􀀉fire􀀉that􀀉burned􀀉
the􀀉Trinity’s􀀉ledger􀀉to􀀉ash.􀀉

“Not􀀉yet,”􀀉he􀀉growled.􀀉“Not􀀉before􀀉I􀀉burn􀀉the􀀉gates.”􀀉

Beside􀀉him,􀀉Otto􀀉sat􀀉perfectly􀀉still.􀀉The􀀉same􀀉drug􀀉was􀀉in􀀉his􀀉blood,􀀉the􀀉
same􀀉hum􀀉in􀀉the􀀉walls􀀉—􀀉but􀀉where􀀉Gage􀀉was􀀉fighting􀀉to􀀉stay􀀉above􀀉the􀀉
surface,􀀉Otto􀀉had􀀉gone􀀉inward.􀀉

He􀀉remembered􀀉the􀀉red􀀉Porsche􀀉replica􀀉he’d􀀉salvaged􀀉as􀀉a􀀉boy􀀉—􀀉the􀀉toy􀀉
he’d􀀉called􀀉his􀀉Otto-mobile􀀉before􀀉he􀀉was􀀉old􀀉enough􀀉to􀀉know􀀉that􀀉wasn’t􀀉
its􀀉name.􀀉The􀀉name􀀉had􀀉stuck􀀉to􀀉the􀀉boy􀀉long􀀉after􀀉the􀀉toy􀀉was􀀉lost.􀀉

He􀀉had􀀉been􀀉six􀀉the􀀉first􀀉time􀀉he􀀉understood􀀉what􀀉brave-and-stupid􀀉meant.􀀉
He􀀉had􀀉seen􀀉four􀀉older􀀉boys􀀉cornering􀀉an􀀉old􀀉man􀀉in􀀉the􀀉alley􀀉behind􀀉the􀀉
ration􀀉cart.􀀉The􀀉old􀀉man􀀉was􀀉small,􀀉frail,􀀉easy􀀉to􀀉break.􀀉Otto,􀀉who􀀉was􀀉
small􀀉and􀀉frail􀀉and􀀉easy􀀉to􀀉break􀀉himself,􀀉had􀀉charged􀀉at􀀉the􀀉four􀀉of􀀉them􀀉
with􀀉a􀀉piece􀀉of􀀉pipe.􀀉

He􀀉woke􀀉up􀀉in􀀉his􀀉own􀀉shack􀀉three􀀉days􀀉later,􀀉with􀀉five􀀉stitches􀀉in􀀉his􀀉scalp􀀉
and􀀉his􀀉right􀀉hand􀀉wrapped􀀉in􀀉rags.􀀉He􀀉couldn’t􀀉remember􀀉being􀀉carried􀀉
home.􀀉He􀀉couldn’t􀀉remember􀀉anything􀀉between􀀉the􀀉alley􀀉and􀀉the􀀉rags.􀀉

On􀀉the􀀉third􀀉night􀀉of􀀉his􀀉recovery,􀀉half-asleep,􀀉he􀀉saw􀀉a􀀉blue􀀉light􀀉moving􀀉
past􀀉his􀀉shack􀀉—􀀉faint,􀀉the􀀉kind􀀉of􀀉glow􀀉you􀀉might􀀉miss􀀉if􀀉you􀀉blinked.􀀉He􀀉
followed􀀉it.􀀉

It􀀉led􀀉him􀀉to􀀉an􀀉empty􀀉back-row􀀉shed􀀉where􀀉the􀀉old􀀉man􀀉was􀀉sitting􀀉cross-
legged􀀉on􀀉the􀀉floor,􀀉looking􀀉nothing􀀉like􀀉the􀀉figure􀀉who􀀉had􀀉been􀀉beaten􀀉in􀀉
the􀀉alley.􀀉Stronger.􀀉More􀀉upright.􀀉A􀀉pale􀀉blue􀀉light􀀉at􀀉the􀀉edge􀀉of􀀉his􀀉skin􀀉
like􀀉the􀀉last􀀉shimmer􀀉of􀀉something􀀉the􀀉world􀀉had􀀉forgotten􀀉how􀀉to􀀉make.􀀉
A􀀉butterfly􀀉rested􀀉on􀀉his􀀉shoulder,􀀉wings􀀉opening􀀉and􀀉closing􀀉slowly.􀀉

“My􀀉name􀀉is􀀉Shu􀀉Chan,”􀀉the􀀉old􀀉man􀀉said.􀀉“Where􀀉I􀀉come􀀉from􀀉is􀀉dust􀀉
now.􀀉I􀀉was􀀉the􀀉last􀀉guard􀀉of􀀉a􀀉place􀀉that􀀉no􀀉longer􀀉exists.􀀉I􀀉have􀀉been􀀉
waiting􀀉for􀀉someone􀀉to􀀉find􀀉me.”􀀉He􀀉touched􀀉Otto’s􀀉forehead􀀉with􀀉one􀀉


finger,􀀉and􀀉for􀀉a􀀉heartbeat􀀉Otto􀀉stood􀀉inside􀀉a􀀉stillness􀀉so􀀉deep􀀉that􀀉the􀀉
world􀀉made􀀉sense.􀀉

Otto􀀉spent􀀉the􀀉next􀀉two􀀉years􀀉in􀀉that􀀉shed,􀀉learning􀀉what􀀉Shu􀀉Chan􀀉called􀀉
the􀀉long-lost􀀉art􀀉—􀀉a􀀉way􀀉of􀀉holding􀀉the􀀉body􀀉that􀀉turned􀀉breath􀀉and􀀉
skeleton􀀉into􀀉a􀀉fortress􀀉no􀀉chemical􀀉and􀀉no􀀉blade􀀉could􀀉enter.􀀉Shu􀀉Chan􀀉
called􀀉the􀀉discipline􀀉karate,􀀉a􀀉word􀀉from􀀉the􀀉buried􀀉world.􀀉He􀀉called􀀉the􀀉
light􀀉at􀀉his􀀉skin􀀉the􀀉breath;􀀉he􀀉called􀀉the􀀉butterfly􀀉the􀀉proof.􀀉

“I􀀉did􀀉not􀀉pick􀀉you,􀀉Otto,”􀀉Shu􀀉Chan􀀉said􀀉the􀀉night􀀉before􀀉he􀀉died.􀀉“You􀀉
picked􀀉me,􀀉that􀀉day􀀉in􀀉the􀀉alley.􀀉I􀀉had􀀉been􀀉waiting􀀉a􀀉long􀀉time􀀉for􀀉a􀀉
successor.􀀉I􀀉had􀀉begun􀀉to􀀉think􀀉no􀀉one􀀉would􀀉come.”􀀉

Otto􀀉had􀀉gone􀀉home􀀉that􀀉night􀀉and􀀉slept􀀉with􀀉a􀀉smile,􀀉knowing􀀉he􀀉finally􀀉
meant􀀉something􀀉to􀀉someone􀀉whose􀀉meaning􀀉could􀀉not􀀉be􀀉taken􀀉from􀀉
him.􀀉

Now,􀀉in􀀉the􀀉black􀀉eternity􀀉of􀀉the􀀉Initiation􀀉chamber,􀀉the􀀉smile􀀉was􀀉a􀀉ghost􀀉

—􀀉but􀀉the􀀉breath􀀉Shu􀀉Chan􀀉had􀀉taught􀀉him􀀉was􀀉not.􀀉He􀀉held􀀉it.􀀉He􀀉held􀀉
it.􀀉He􀀉held􀀉it.􀀉
Gage􀀉watched􀀉him,􀀉drawing􀀉strength􀀉from􀀉that􀀉silence.􀀉They􀀉were􀀉no􀀉
longer􀀉children􀀉being􀀉unmade;􀀉they􀀉were􀀉the􀀉friction􀀉that􀀉would􀀉eventually􀀉
seize􀀉the􀀉machine.􀀉Something􀀉had􀀉hardened􀀉in􀀉Gage􀀉that􀀉the􀀉drug􀀉couldn’t􀀉
dissolve.􀀉The􀀉Engine􀀉was􀀉finally􀀉idling􀀉in􀀉sync􀀉with􀀉the􀀉earth,􀀉waiting􀀉for􀀉
the􀀉moment􀀉to􀀉strike.􀀉


Chapter􀀉4􀀉—􀀉Emily’s􀀉Not Talking􀀉

Gage’s􀀉right􀀉leg􀀉was􀀉a􀀉dead􀀉weight,􀀉a􀀉numb􀀉log􀀉that􀀉refused􀀉his􀀉brain’s􀀉
commands.􀀉Some􀀉primal􀀉part􀀉of􀀉him􀀉wanted􀀉to􀀉crawl􀀉into􀀉shadow􀀉the􀀉way􀀉
wounded􀀉animals􀀉do,􀀉but􀀉sixty􀀉hours􀀉of􀀉forced􀀉wakefulness􀀉had􀀉turned􀀉his􀀉
muscles􀀉into􀀉shredded􀀉rope.􀀉Deep􀀉in􀀉his􀀉marrow,􀀉the􀀉piezoelectric􀀉hum􀀉
vibrated􀀉through􀀉the􀀉floor􀀉—􀀉a􀀉relentless􀀉interference􀀉trying􀀉to􀀉untune􀀉his􀀉
soul.􀀉

He􀀉looked􀀉at􀀉his􀀉shaking􀀉hands􀀉and􀀉thought􀀉of􀀉Camilla.􀀉Back􀀉in􀀉the􀀉shack,􀀉
she’d􀀉be􀀉hunched􀀉over􀀉some􀀉piece􀀉of􀀉scrap,􀀉her􀀉quiet􀀉hands􀀉finding􀀉logic􀀉in􀀉
a􀀉broken􀀉machine.􀀉He􀀉was􀀉the􀀉force;􀀉she􀀉was􀀉the􀀉insight.􀀉If􀀉he􀀉was􀀉going􀀉to􀀉
survive􀀉this􀀉Initiation,􀀉he􀀉couldn’t􀀉just􀀉be􀀉the􀀉Engine􀀉anymore.􀀉He􀀉had􀀉to􀀉
find􀀉a􀀉way􀀉to􀀉see􀀉the􀀉world􀀉through􀀉her􀀉eyes.􀀉

Beside􀀉him,􀀉Ben􀀉and􀀉Otto􀀉were􀀉sprawled􀀉with􀀉their􀀉cheeks􀀉pressed􀀉to􀀉the􀀉
grit,􀀉breathing􀀉shallow􀀉and􀀉ragged.􀀉A􀀉figure􀀉jerked􀀉through􀀉the􀀉orange􀀉haze.􀀉
Rosa.􀀉She􀀉moved􀀉with􀀉a􀀉twitchy,􀀉insect-like􀀉rhythm,􀀉her􀀉skin􀀉pale􀀉as􀀉
translucent􀀉parchment.􀀉

“Rosa?”􀀉Gage􀀉croaked,􀀉swallowing􀀉hard􀀉against􀀉a􀀉throat􀀉that􀀉felt􀀉lined􀀉with􀀉
dry􀀉wool.􀀉“You􀀉still􀀉awake?􀀉God􀀉knows􀀉what􀀉Icepick􀀉put􀀉in􀀉that􀀉food…”􀀉

She􀀉stared􀀉at􀀉him􀀉with􀀉a􀀉fixed􀀉grin􀀉that􀀉didn’t􀀉reach􀀉her􀀉bloodshot􀀉eyes.􀀉
“Yeah.􀀉Emily’s􀀉not􀀉talking􀀉to􀀉me,􀀉though.􀀉She’s􀀉being􀀉quiet􀀉today.”􀀉

As􀀉Rosa􀀉turned,􀀉Gage’s􀀉breath􀀉hitched.􀀉Tied􀀉to􀀉her􀀉back􀀉with􀀉frayed􀀉
mining􀀉rope􀀉was􀀉Emily’s􀀉body,􀀉slumped􀀉and􀀉heavy.􀀉As􀀉Rosa􀀉swayed,􀀉
Emily’s􀀉limbs􀀉flailed􀀉in􀀉a􀀉boneless􀀉arc􀀉—􀀉a􀀉broken􀀉puppet􀀉dancing􀀉to􀀉a􀀉
madwoman’s􀀉tune.􀀉


“She􀀉kept􀀉stumbling􀀉into􀀉the􀀉walls􀀉until􀀉she􀀉just…􀀉drifted􀀉off,”􀀉Rosa􀀉
whispered.􀀉“Look􀀉at􀀉the􀀉grey􀀉dust􀀉in􀀉her􀀉hair.􀀉It􀀉almost􀀉looks􀀉like􀀉a􀀉crown􀀉
of􀀉silver,􀀉doesn’t􀀉it?”􀀉

She􀀉bent􀀉down􀀉and􀀉pulled􀀉a􀀉rusted􀀉pickaxe􀀉from􀀉the􀀉corner􀀉of􀀉the􀀉chamber􀀉

—􀀉left􀀉behind􀀉by􀀉some􀀉kid􀀉who􀀉hadn’t􀀉made􀀉it􀀉out.􀀉“Gage…􀀉you􀀉look􀀉like􀀉
you’re􀀉struggling.􀀉Do􀀉you􀀉want􀀉me􀀉to􀀉help􀀉you􀀉rest􀀉like􀀉Emily?”􀀉
Rosa􀀉lunged.􀀉The􀀉combined􀀉weight􀀉slammed􀀉into􀀉Gage’s􀀉chest.􀀉He􀀉clawed􀀉
at􀀉the􀀉air,􀀉fingers􀀉like􀀉wet􀀉noodles,􀀉the􀀉rusted􀀉blade􀀉inches􀀉from􀀉his􀀉throat.􀀉

“Ben! Otto!”􀀉

No􀀉answer.􀀉Just􀀉Rosa’s􀀉breath􀀉on􀀉his􀀉face,􀀉sour􀀉and􀀉metallic,􀀉and􀀉the􀀉blade􀀉
descending.􀀉He􀀉could􀀉see􀀉the􀀉rust-flakes􀀉on􀀉the􀀉edge.􀀉He􀀉could􀀉see􀀉Rosa’s􀀉
pale􀀉eye,􀀉calm􀀉in􀀉the􀀉way􀀉that􀀉nothing􀀉human􀀉is􀀉calm.􀀉The􀀉pickaxe􀀉touched􀀉
his􀀉throat􀀉—􀀉a􀀉kiss􀀉of􀀉cold􀀉iron􀀉—􀀉and􀀉broke􀀉the􀀉skin.􀀉

Camilla,􀀈he􀀉thought.􀀉The􀀉first􀀉thing􀀉in􀀉his􀀉head,􀀉the􀀉only􀀉thing.􀀉He’s􀀈going􀀈
to􀀈leave􀀈her􀀈alone.􀀈

Then,􀀉from􀀉the􀀉corner􀀉of􀀉his􀀉eye,􀀉a􀀉figure􀀉lunged.􀀉The􀀉pressure􀀉vanished.􀀉A􀀉
single􀀉sharp􀀉crack􀀉—􀀉bone􀀉striking􀀉bone,􀀉calculated.􀀉Rosa’s􀀉head􀀉snapped􀀉
sideways􀀉as􀀉she􀀉was􀀉tossed􀀉off􀀉him.􀀉Gage􀀉rolled􀀉over,􀀉gasping􀀉for􀀉air􀀉that􀀉
tasted􀀉of􀀉copper.􀀉A􀀉line􀀉from􀀉Pappy’s􀀉stories􀀉pierced􀀉the􀀉fog:􀀉You􀀈can’t􀀈pipe􀀈
the􀀈deep􀀈carbon,􀀈boy.􀀈You􀀈have􀀈to􀀈break􀀈it.􀀈Looking􀀉at􀀉Rosa’s􀀉crumpled􀀉form,􀀉
Gage􀀉understood􀀉the􀀉Initiation.􀀉The􀀉Trinity􀀉didn’t􀀉want􀀉loyalty;􀀉they􀀉
wanted􀀉to􀀉see􀀉if􀀉these􀀉kids􀀉would􀀉snap􀀉or􀀉turn􀀉into􀀉diamonds􀀉under􀀉
pressure.􀀉

Otto􀀉lay􀀉trembling􀀉nearby,􀀉his􀀉hand􀀉still􀀉curled􀀉into􀀉the􀀉mathematical􀀉strike􀀉
that􀀉could􀀉only􀀉have􀀉come􀀉from􀀉Shu􀀉Chan’s􀀉training.􀀉

The􀀉truth􀀉hit􀀉Gage:􀀉the􀀉Initiation􀀉wasn’t􀀉a􀀉test.􀀉It􀀉was􀀉a􀀉culling.􀀉Icepick􀀉was􀀉
weeding􀀉out􀀉the􀀉weak,􀀉turning􀀉survivors􀀉into􀀉bots􀀉too􀀉broken􀀉to􀀉dream􀀉of􀀉
mutiny.􀀉


Gage􀀉was􀀉on􀀉his􀀉feet􀀉before􀀉he􀀉knew􀀉it,􀀉fists􀀉balled,􀀉heading􀀉for􀀉the􀀉tunnel􀀉
mouth.􀀉“I’m􀀉going􀀉to􀀉find􀀉Icepick.􀀉I’m􀀉going􀀉to—”􀀉

“And􀀉do􀀉what?”􀀉Ben’s􀀉voice􀀉was􀀉barely􀀉a􀀉rasp,􀀉but􀀉it􀀉stopped􀀉Gage􀀉mid-
stride.􀀉Ben􀀉hadn’t􀀉moved􀀉from􀀉the􀀉ground.􀀉He􀀉was􀀉lying􀀉on􀀉his􀀉side,􀀉arms􀀉
wrapped􀀉around􀀉his􀀉ribs,􀀉too􀀉weak􀀉to􀀉stand.􀀉But􀀉his􀀉eyes􀀉were􀀉open􀀉and􀀉
fixed􀀉on􀀉Gage.􀀉“You􀀉can’t􀀉even􀀉walk􀀉straight.􀀉You􀀉go􀀉down􀀉that􀀉tunnel􀀉and􀀉
they’ll􀀉break􀀉you􀀉like􀀉they􀀉broke􀀉Emily.”􀀉

Otto􀀉was􀀉sitting􀀉against􀀉the􀀉wall,􀀉staring􀀉at􀀉the􀀉hand􀀉that􀀉had􀀉struck􀀉Rosa.􀀉
His􀀉fingers􀀉were􀀉trembling.􀀉He􀀉hadn’t􀀉spoken􀀉since􀀉the􀀉blow􀀉landed􀀉—􀀉the􀀉
precision􀀉that􀀉Master􀀉Chan􀀉had􀀉drilled􀀉into􀀉him􀀉used􀀉not􀀉to􀀉protect,􀀉but􀀉to􀀉
hurt􀀉a􀀉girl􀀉who􀀉was􀀉already􀀉broken.􀀉The􀀉silence􀀉coming􀀉off􀀉him􀀉was􀀉worse􀀉
than􀀉Ben’s􀀉shouts.􀀉

“Otto.”􀀉Ben􀀉turned􀀉his􀀉head􀀉on􀀉the􀀉grit.􀀉“You􀀉saved􀀉him.􀀉That’s􀀉what􀀉that􀀉
was.􀀉Don’t􀀉make􀀉it􀀉anything􀀉else.”􀀉

Otto􀀉didn’t􀀉answer.􀀉But􀀉his􀀉hand􀀉stopped􀀉trembling.􀀉

Ben􀀉looked􀀉between􀀉them􀀉—􀀉the􀀉Engine􀀉about􀀉to􀀉charge􀀉blindly􀀉into􀀉the􀀉
dark,􀀉the􀀉Fighter􀀉about􀀉to􀀉collapse􀀉into􀀉himself􀀉—􀀉and􀀉said􀀉the􀀉only􀀉thing􀀉
that􀀉mattered:􀀉“We􀀉stay􀀉together.􀀉We􀀉think.􀀉Then􀀉we􀀉move.”􀀉

Gage􀀉stood􀀉in􀀉the􀀉tunnel􀀉mouth􀀉for􀀉three􀀉long􀀉breaths.􀀉Then􀀉he􀀉came􀀉back􀀉
and􀀉sat􀀉down.􀀉

Gage􀀉knew􀀉they􀀉were􀀉running􀀉out􀀉of􀀉time.􀀉If􀀉they􀀉didn’t􀀉sleep,􀀉they􀀉would􀀉
murder􀀉each􀀉other􀀉or􀀉die􀀉of􀀉exhaustion.􀀉He􀀉forced􀀉himself􀀉to􀀉think􀀉like􀀉
Camilla􀀉—􀀉to􀀉decode􀀉what􀀉was􀀉killing􀀉them􀀉before􀀉it􀀉finished􀀉the􀀉job.􀀉

He􀀉remembered􀀉the􀀉night􀀉before􀀉the􀀉gates􀀉opened.􀀉Camilla􀀉had􀀉been􀀉
unusually􀀉silent,􀀉her􀀉quiet􀀉hands􀀉working􀀉with􀀉frantic􀀉precision􀀉over􀀉a􀀉pile􀀉
of􀀉scavenged􀀉medical􀀉scrap.􀀉Before􀀉he􀀉left,􀀉she’d􀀉slipped􀀉a􀀉thin,􀀉cold􀀉object􀀉
into􀀉the􀀉hidden􀀉seam􀀉of􀀉his􀀉sleeve.􀀉


“For􀀉the􀀉fires􀀉you􀀉cannot􀀉see,􀀉Gage,”􀀉she’d􀀉whispered.􀀉“Don’t􀀉pull􀀉it􀀉out􀀉
unless􀀉you􀀉have􀀉to.􀀉If􀀉anything􀀉tastes􀀉wrong,􀀉or􀀉your􀀉body􀀉starts􀀉to􀀉lie􀀉to􀀉
you,􀀉test􀀉what􀀉they􀀉fed􀀉you.􀀉Test􀀉all􀀉of􀀉it.􀀉And􀀉let􀀉it􀀉breathe􀀉between􀀉tests.”􀀉

Now,􀀉with􀀉his􀀉mind􀀉fracturing􀀉under􀀉the􀀉sixty-hour􀀉strain􀀉—􀀉Rosa’s􀀉blade􀀉
still􀀉warm􀀉on􀀉his􀀉neck,􀀉Emily’s􀀉body􀀉tied􀀉to􀀉her􀀉back􀀉like􀀉a􀀉doll􀀉—􀀉Gage􀀉
understood.􀀉The􀀉chamber􀀉wasn’t􀀉a􀀉test􀀉of􀀉endurance.􀀉It􀀉was􀀉a􀀉slow􀀉
execution.􀀉He􀀉had􀀉told􀀉himself􀀉the􀀉tang􀀉was􀀉rationing,􀀉the􀀉wakefulness􀀉
fear,􀀉the􀀉hum􀀉just􀀉nerves.􀀉Camilla􀀉had􀀉said􀀉don’t􀀉pull􀀉it􀀉out􀀉unless􀀉you􀀉have􀀉
to.􀀉He􀀉had􀀉to.􀀉

He􀀉ripped􀀉the􀀉seam􀀉open.􀀉He􀀉pulled􀀉out􀀉a􀀉thin􀀉sliver􀀉of􀀉something􀀉—􀀉
colder􀀉than􀀉skin,􀀉translucent,􀀉the􀀉color􀀉of􀀉nothing􀀉in􀀉particular.􀀉He􀀉didn’t􀀉
know􀀉what􀀉to􀀉call􀀉it􀀉yet;􀀉he􀀉only􀀉knew􀀉it􀀉was􀀉hers,􀀉and􀀉that􀀉meant􀀉it􀀉had􀀉
cost􀀉her􀀉something􀀉to􀀉make.􀀉

He􀀉pressed􀀉the􀀉strip􀀉into􀀉the􀀉mashed􀀉potatoes.􀀉It􀀉stayed􀀉clear􀀉—􀀉no􀀉
reaction,􀀉nothing􀀉to􀀉reset.􀀉He􀀉touched􀀉it􀀉to􀀉the􀀉dried􀀉chicken.􀀉Nothing.􀀉
Finally,􀀉he􀀉dipped􀀉it􀀉into􀀉the􀀉water.􀀉

The􀀉reaction􀀉was􀀉instantaneous.􀀉The􀀉polymer􀀉turned􀀉a􀀉violent,􀀉bruised􀀉
purple􀀉that􀀉seemed􀀉to􀀉throb􀀉with􀀉its􀀉own􀀉malice.􀀉Camilla􀀉had􀀉taught􀀉him􀀉
the􀀉basics􀀉before􀀉the􀀉gates􀀉opened.􀀉Three􀀉colors,􀀉three􀀉kinds􀀉of􀀉bad.􀀉Green􀀉
for􀀉rot.􀀉Yellow􀀉for􀀉poison.􀀉Purple􀀉for􀀉the􀀉kind􀀉that􀀉went􀀉for􀀉your􀀉head􀀉—􀀉
the􀀉worst􀀉kind,􀀉she’d􀀉said,􀀉because􀀉it􀀉made􀀉you􀀉stop􀀉being􀀉you.􀀉The􀀉water􀀉
wasn’t􀀉just􀀉dirty.􀀉It􀀉was􀀉drugged.􀀉

As􀀉he􀀉pulled􀀉it􀀉out,􀀉the􀀉color􀀉began􀀉to􀀉ripple􀀉—􀀉like􀀉an􀀉oil􀀉slick􀀉shifting􀀉on􀀉
a􀀉hot􀀉sidewalk,􀀉the􀀉purple􀀉retreated􀀉from􀀉the􀀉edges􀀉but􀀉stalled,􀀉settling􀀉into􀀉
a􀀉dull,􀀉bruised􀀉grey􀀉that􀀉wouldn’t􀀉fade􀀉further.􀀉Camilla􀀉had􀀉warned􀀉him:􀀉
the􀀉strip􀀉needed􀀉to􀀉breathe􀀉between􀀉tests,􀀉needed􀀉time􀀉to􀀉bleed􀀉the􀀉last􀀉
reaction􀀉out􀀉before􀀉it􀀉could􀀉read􀀉clean.􀀉He’d􀀉rushed􀀉through􀀉the􀀉first􀀉two􀀉
without􀀉waiting􀀉because􀀉neither􀀉had􀀉triggered􀀉a􀀉response.􀀉Now􀀉the􀀉sensor􀀉


was􀀉stuck􀀉—􀀉saturated􀀉with􀀉the􀀉water’s􀀉signature,􀀉stubbornly􀀉holding􀀉its􀀉
color.􀀉It􀀉would􀀉clear􀀉eventually.􀀉But􀀉not􀀉yet.􀀉

“The􀀉water,”􀀉Gage􀀉rasped.􀀉It􀀉wasn’t􀀉the􀀉food􀀉priming􀀉them;􀀉it􀀉was􀀉the􀀉very􀀉
thing􀀉they􀀉used􀀉to􀀉wash􀀉the􀀉bitterness􀀉away.􀀉Camilla􀀉hadn’t􀀉just􀀉given􀀉him􀀉
a􀀉tool;􀀉she􀀉had􀀉given􀀉him􀀉her􀀉Eyes.􀀉

He􀀉looked􀀉at􀀉Ben,􀀉already􀀉drifting􀀉into􀀉ecstatic􀀉madness,􀀉shouting􀀉about􀀉a􀀉
chicken􀀉sandwich.􀀉Otto􀀉had􀀉to􀀉knock􀀉Ben􀀉out􀀉to􀀉stop􀀉the􀀉shrieks.􀀉As􀀉Ben’s􀀉
body􀀉slumped􀀉into􀀉the􀀉grit,􀀉his􀀉consciousness􀀉slid􀀉away􀀉from􀀉the􀀉mine􀀉and􀀉
into􀀉the􀀉sanctuary􀀉his􀀉parents􀀉had􀀉built􀀉—􀀉an􀀉invisible􀀉fortress􀀉against􀀉the􀀉
culling􀀉of􀀉the􀀉soul.􀀉

In􀀉his􀀉dream,􀀉Ben􀀉saw􀀉his􀀉father’s􀀉face.􀀉Oscar􀀉had􀀉always􀀉told􀀉him􀀉that􀀉the􀀉
Trinity􀀉didn’t􀀉just􀀉harvest􀀉coal;􀀉they􀀉harvested􀀉the􀀉capacity􀀉to􀀉care.􀀉He􀀉
knew􀀉the􀀉system􀀉wanted􀀉human􀀉engines􀀉—􀀉tools􀀉driven􀀉by􀀉grinding􀀉fear.􀀉
To􀀉survive􀀉without􀀉becoming􀀉a􀀉bot,􀀉you􀀉had􀀉to􀀉maintain􀀉the􀀉one􀀉thing􀀉the􀀉
ledger􀀉couldn’t􀀉calculate:􀀉Empathy.􀀉

The􀀉memory􀀉shifted􀀉to􀀉the􀀉day􀀉Leroy􀀉arrived.􀀉For􀀉years,􀀉Leroy􀀉had􀀉been􀀉the􀀉
family’s􀀉enemy􀀉—􀀉the􀀉one􀀉who􀀉stole􀀉their􀀉bread􀀉when􀀉the􀀉rations􀀉failed,􀀉the􀀉
one􀀉who􀀉left􀀉bruises􀀉on􀀉Oscar’s􀀉face􀀉whenever􀀉Oscar􀀉wouldn’t􀀉surrender􀀉
what􀀉little􀀉he’d􀀉put􀀉aside.􀀉Leroy􀀉had􀀉crossed􀀉into􀀉something􀀉worse􀀉than􀀉a􀀉
man,􀀉a􀀉creature􀀉that􀀉ran􀀉on􀀉the􀀉calculus􀀉of􀀉scrap􀀉or􀀉be􀀉scrapped.􀀉Ben􀀉had􀀉
spent􀀉his􀀉childhood􀀉watching􀀉his􀀉father􀀉come􀀉home􀀉with􀀉a􀀉split􀀉lip􀀉and􀀉
saying􀀉nothing􀀉about􀀉it.􀀉

And􀀉then,􀀉one􀀉morning,􀀉there􀀉Leroy􀀉was􀀉—􀀉bleeding􀀉out􀀉on􀀉their􀀉
doorstep,􀀉a􀀉jagged􀀉knife􀀉wound􀀉leaking􀀉his􀀉life􀀉into􀀉the􀀉dirt.􀀉

Ben􀀉remembered􀀉the􀀉cold􀀉logic􀀉that􀀉had􀀉briefly􀀉flared􀀉in􀀉his􀀉chest:􀀉Let􀀈him􀀈
die.􀀈One􀀈less􀀈mouth.􀀈One􀀈less􀀈threat.􀀈The􀀉culling􀀉instinct,􀀉the􀀉protocol􀀉that􀀉
stripped􀀉humanity􀀉to􀀉ensure􀀉survival.􀀉


But􀀉his􀀉sister􀀉Gretchen’s􀀉voice􀀉cut􀀉through:􀀉The􀀉system􀀉made􀀉him􀀉this􀀉way,􀀉
Ben.􀀉If􀀉we􀀉let􀀉him􀀉die􀀉for􀀉what􀀉it􀀉turned􀀉him􀀉into,􀀉we’re􀀉doing􀀉Trinity’s􀀉
work􀀉for􀀉it.􀀉

That􀀉was􀀉the􀀉moment􀀉empathy􀀉became􀀉a􀀉weapon􀀉of􀀉rebellion.􀀉By􀀉choosing􀀉
to􀀉save􀀉Leroy,􀀉they􀀉refused􀀉to􀀉be􀀉culled.􀀉They􀀉fed􀀉him􀀉their􀀉own􀀉rations􀀉—􀀉
starving􀀉their􀀉bellies􀀉to􀀉prove􀀉their􀀉spirits􀀉couldn’t􀀉be􀀉bought.􀀉

Leroy􀀉eventually􀀉changed.􀀉Over􀀉the􀀉months􀀉that􀀉followed,􀀉he􀀉taught􀀉Ben􀀉
how􀀉to􀀉read􀀉a􀀉mine􀀉wall􀀉for􀀉stress􀀉fractures,􀀉how􀀉to􀀉ration􀀉water􀀉so􀀉it􀀉lasted􀀉
three􀀉shifts􀀉instead􀀉of􀀉two,􀀉how􀀉to􀀉move􀀉through􀀉the􀀉shaft􀀉rows􀀉without􀀉
drawing􀀉a􀀉guard’s􀀉eye.􀀉He􀀉became􀀉the􀀉older􀀉brother􀀉Ben􀀉never􀀉had􀀉—􀀉a􀀉
man􀀉redeemed􀀉by􀀉the􀀉very􀀉people􀀉he’d􀀉tormented.􀀉

It􀀉lasted􀀉eleven􀀉months.􀀉A􀀉guard􀀉cornered􀀉Gretchen􀀉near􀀉the􀀉water􀀉pump􀀉
one􀀉evening􀀉—􀀉not􀀉for􀀉any􀀉infraction,􀀉just􀀉because􀀉he􀀉could.􀀉Leroy􀀉saw􀀉it􀀉
happen􀀉and􀀉did􀀉the􀀉only􀀉thing􀀉his􀀉old􀀉instincts􀀉knew􀀉how􀀉to􀀉do.􀀉He􀀉put􀀉
the􀀉guard􀀉on􀀉the􀀉ground􀀉with􀀉a􀀉single􀀉blow.􀀉

The􀀉guard􀀉survived.􀀉Leroy􀀉didn’t.􀀉Andre’s􀀉men􀀉came􀀉for􀀉him􀀉before􀀉dawn.􀀉
Ben􀀉heard􀀉the􀀉scuffle􀀉through􀀉the􀀉wall􀀉—􀀉the􀀉thud􀀉of􀀉boots,􀀉a􀀉single􀀉shout􀀉
cut􀀉short,􀀉and􀀉then􀀉the􀀉sound􀀉of􀀉something􀀉heavy􀀉being􀀉dragged􀀉across􀀉the􀀉
grit.􀀉By􀀉the􀀉time􀀉he􀀉reached􀀉the􀀉door,􀀉the􀀉path􀀉outside􀀉was􀀉empty􀀉except􀀉
for􀀉a􀀉dark􀀉smear􀀉that􀀉led􀀉toward􀀉the􀀉disposal􀀉tunnels􀀉and􀀉stopped􀀉at􀀉the􀀉
dark.􀀉

Ben􀀉never􀀉saw􀀉Leroy􀀉again.􀀉The􀀉system􀀉took􀀉him􀀉quietly,􀀉the􀀉way􀀉it􀀉took􀀉
everything􀀉that􀀉drew􀀉its􀀉attention.􀀉Just􀀉an􀀉absence􀀉where􀀉a􀀉man􀀉had􀀉been,􀀉
and􀀉a􀀉lesson􀀉that􀀉branded􀀉itself􀀉into􀀉his􀀉chest:􀀉Leroy􀀉had􀀉used􀀉violence􀀉to􀀉
protect􀀉someone􀀉he􀀉loved,􀀉and􀀉the􀀉machine􀀉had􀀉swallowed􀀉him􀀉whole.􀀉Not􀀉
because􀀉the􀀉violence􀀉was􀀉wrong􀀉—􀀉it􀀉wasn’t.􀀉But􀀉because􀀉in􀀉this􀀉system,􀀉a􀀉
fist􀀉was􀀉just􀀉an􀀉excuse􀀉for􀀉the􀀉machine􀀉to􀀉do􀀉what􀀉it􀀉had􀀉always􀀉wanted􀀉to􀀉
do.􀀉The􀀉system􀀉didn’t􀀉punish􀀉cruelty.􀀉It􀀉punished􀀉disruption.􀀉And􀀉
violence,􀀉no􀀉matter􀀉how􀀉righteous,􀀉was􀀉the􀀉loudest􀀉disruption􀀉there􀀉was.􀀉


That􀀉was􀀉the􀀉night􀀉Ben􀀉decided.􀀉Not􀀉a􀀉vow􀀉spoken􀀉aloud􀀉—􀀉just􀀉a􀀉quiet􀀉
closing􀀉of􀀉a􀀉door􀀉inside􀀉himself.􀀉He􀀉would􀀉find􀀉a􀀉way􀀉to􀀉fight􀀉that􀀉didn’t􀀉
give􀀉the􀀉machine􀀉an􀀉excuse􀀉to􀀉take􀀉another􀀉Leroy.􀀉

Gage􀀉watched􀀉Ben’s􀀉twitching􀀉face.􀀉Ben’s􀀉mind􀀉was􀀉somewhere􀀉else􀀉—􀀉
back􀀉on􀀉a􀀉doorstep􀀉with􀀉Leroy􀀉bleeding􀀉into􀀉the􀀉dirt,􀀉back􀀉in􀀉the􀀉moment􀀉
Oscar􀀉had􀀉taught􀀉him􀀉what􀀉to􀀉do􀀉when􀀉the􀀉man􀀉who􀀉had􀀉hurt􀀉you􀀉arrived􀀉
dying􀀉at􀀉your􀀉door.􀀉Ben􀀉was􀀉holding􀀉onto􀀉that.􀀉Ben􀀉was􀀉holding􀀉onto􀀉it􀀉
because􀀉he􀀉could􀀉feel􀀉the􀀉chamber􀀉trying􀀉to􀀉take􀀉it􀀉away.􀀉

And􀀉in􀀉that􀀉moment􀀉Gage􀀉understood.􀀉The􀀉chamber􀀉didn’t􀀉just􀀉want􀀉their􀀉
muscles.􀀉It􀀉wanted􀀉to􀀉erase􀀉the􀀉human􀀉moments􀀉from􀀉their􀀉history.􀀉It􀀉
wanted􀀉to􀀉delete􀀉the􀀉empathy􀀉that􀀉allowed􀀉them􀀉to􀀉act􀀉as􀀉a􀀉group.􀀉Oscar􀀉
had􀀉been􀀉right􀀉—􀀉the􀀉Trinity􀀉harvested􀀉the􀀉capacity􀀉to􀀉care,􀀉and􀀉the􀀉
Initiation􀀉was􀀉the􀀉threshing􀀉floor.􀀉

If􀀉Otto􀀉was􀀉the􀀉alignment􀀉and􀀉Gage􀀉was􀀉the􀀉force,􀀉Ben􀀉was􀀉the􀀉Binding􀀉
Agent􀀉—􀀉the􀀉one􀀉whose􀀉resolve􀀉to􀀉abstain􀀉from􀀉violence,􀀉whose􀀉stubborn􀀉
insistence􀀉on􀀉seeing􀀉a􀀉neighbor􀀉instead􀀉of􀀉a􀀉competitor,􀀉was􀀉the􀀉only􀀉thing􀀉
keeping􀀉them􀀉from􀀉turning􀀉on􀀉each􀀉other􀀉in􀀉the􀀉dark.􀀉Without􀀉that􀀉glue,􀀉
Gage’s􀀉strength􀀉was􀀉just􀀉a􀀉weapon􀀉looking􀀉for􀀉a􀀉target,􀀉and􀀉Otto’s􀀉precision􀀉
was􀀉just􀀉a􀀉machine􀀉with􀀉no􀀉purpose.􀀉

“He’s􀀉not􀀉just􀀉dreaming􀀉of􀀉a􀀉sandwich,”􀀉Gage􀀉whispered􀀉to􀀉Otto,􀀉his􀀉own􀀉
mind􀀉clearing.􀀉“He’s􀀉holding􀀉the􀀉line.􀀉If􀀉he􀀉breaks,􀀉we􀀉all􀀉become􀀉bots.”􀀉

Gage’s􀀉fingers􀀉traced􀀉the􀀉Logic􀀉Strip.􀀉He􀀉remembered􀀉Camilla’s􀀉return􀀉
from􀀉the􀀉Heights􀀉—􀀉two􀀉full􀀉days􀀉gone,􀀉quiet􀀉hands􀀉stained􀀉with􀀉industrial􀀉
grease,􀀉breath􀀉a􀀉panicked􀀉rattle.􀀉She􀀉hadn’t􀀉just􀀉found􀀉the􀀉strip;􀀉she’d􀀉
hunted􀀉it􀀉in􀀉the􀀉treacherous􀀉waste-chutes􀀉of􀀉the􀀉Mid-Spires.􀀉She’d􀀉read􀀉
about􀀉the􀀉strips􀀉in􀀉one􀀉of􀀉Pappy’s􀀉hidden􀀉Scripts􀀉—􀀉a􀀉faded􀀉medical􀀉
pamphlet􀀉from􀀉the􀀉buried􀀉world.􀀉The􀀉Scripts􀀉had􀀉taught􀀉her􀀉the􀀉legend;􀀉
the􀀉salvage􀀉taught􀀉her􀀉the􀀉rest.􀀉She􀀉hadn’t􀀉told􀀉him􀀉about􀀉the􀀉automated􀀉
culling-sweeps􀀉that􀀉turned􀀉scavengers􀀉into􀀉grit,􀀉or􀀉how􀀉she’d􀀉spent􀀉hours􀀉


buried􀀉under􀀉pressurized􀀉medical􀀉scrap􀀉to􀀉evade􀀉the􀀉Technocrats’􀀉thermal􀀉
sensors.􀀉She􀀉had􀀉risked􀀉her􀀉own􀀉deletion􀀉by􀀉the􀀉system􀀉to􀀉pull􀀉this􀀉single􀀉
sliver􀀉of􀀉clarity􀀉from􀀉the􀀉elites’􀀉trash.􀀉

She􀀉had􀀉been􀀉preparing􀀉for􀀉his􀀉Initiation􀀉almost􀀉since􀀉the􀀉day􀀉she􀀉could􀀉
read.􀀉Pappy􀀉had􀀉warned􀀉her,􀀉in􀀉fragments,􀀉about􀀉what􀀉they􀀉did􀀉to􀀉the􀀉
children􀀉inside:􀀉that􀀉the􀀉food􀀉was􀀉the􀀉trick,􀀉that􀀉the􀀉chamber􀀉didn’t􀀉kill􀀉the􀀉
body􀀉but􀀉emptied􀀉the􀀉head,􀀉that􀀉anyone􀀉who􀀉went􀀉in􀀉came􀀉out􀀉less.􀀉Years􀀉
of􀀉watching􀀉Hollows􀀉on􀀉the􀀉surface􀀉had􀀉confirmed􀀉his􀀉fears.􀀉By􀀉the􀀉time􀀉
Gage’s􀀉seventeenth􀀉birthday􀀉approached,􀀉she􀀉had􀀉been􀀉collecting􀀉tools􀀉and􀀉
warnings􀀉for􀀉him􀀉like􀀉armor,􀀉scrap􀀉by􀀉scrap,􀀉against􀀉the􀀉day􀀉the􀀉gates􀀉would􀀉
open􀀉and􀀉he􀀉would􀀉walk􀀉in􀀉unprepared.􀀉

Holding􀀉it􀀉now􀀉in􀀉the􀀉dark,􀀉Gage􀀉didn’t􀀉see􀀉a􀀉diagnostic􀀉tool.􀀉He􀀉felt􀀉the􀀉
weight􀀉of􀀉her􀀉sacrifice􀀉—􀀉a􀀉piece􀀉of􀀉her􀀉insight􀀉bought􀀉with􀀉her􀀉own􀀉safety.􀀉
She􀀉had􀀉faced􀀉the􀀉system’s􀀉most􀀉lethal􀀉protocols􀀉so􀀉he􀀉wouldn’t􀀉face􀀉them􀀉
blind.􀀉

Gage􀀉stood,􀀉legs􀀉shaking􀀉but􀀉mind􀀉finally􀀉seeing􀀉clearly.􀀉They􀀉knew􀀉the􀀉
weapon􀀉now􀀉—􀀉the􀀉water􀀉and􀀉the􀀉food,􀀉dosed􀀉by􀀉a􀀉lottery􀀉that􀀉turned􀀉
every􀀉meal􀀉into􀀉a􀀉coin􀀉flip.􀀉They􀀉could􀀉stop􀀉drinking.􀀉They􀀉could􀀉ration􀀉
what􀀉was􀀉clean.􀀉And􀀉they􀀉were􀀉still􀀉standing􀀉—􀀉not􀀉because􀀉they􀀉were􀀉the􀀉
strongest􀀉kids􀀉in􀀉the􀀉tunnel,􀀉but􀀉because􀀉Ben􀀉had􀀉stopped􀀉him􀀉from􀀉
charging􀀉Icepick,􀀉and􀀉steadied􀀉Otto􀀉when􀀉his􀀉hand􀀉still􀀉shook,􀀉and􀀉held􀀉
them􀀉to􀀉the􀀉one􀀉sentence􀀉that􀀉mattered:􀀉We􀀉stay􀀉together.􀀉We􀀉think.􀀉Then􀀉
we􀀉move.􀀉Gage􀀉didn’t􀀉have􀀉a􀀉name􀀉for􀀉what􀀉that􀀉was􀀉worth.􀀉He􀀉just􀀉knew􀀉
it􀀉was􀀉the􀀉only􀀉thing􀀉they􀀉had􀀉that􀀉the􀀉system􀀉hadn’t􀀉managed􀀉to􀀉take.􀀉


Chapter􀀉5􀀉—􀀉The􀀉Lottery􀀉

Gage􀀉looked􀀉at􀀉his􀀉reflection􀀉in􀀉the􀀉polished􀀉curve􀀉of􀀉a􀀉discarded􀀉nutrient􀀉
tin􀀉and􀀉failed􀀉to􀀉recognize􀀉the􀀉Engine􀀉staring􀀉back.􀀉His􀀉eyes􀀉were􀀉a􀀉
roadmap􀀉of􀀉angry􀀉red􀀉lines,􀀉his􀀉skin􀀉bearing􀀉a􀀉paleness􀀉that􀀉mirrored􀀉his􀀉last􀀉
memory􀀉of􀀉his􀀉father’s􀀉corpse.􀀉He􀀉didn’t􀀉recognize􀀉the􀀉face􀀉staring􀀉back􀀉—􀀉
sixty􀀉hours􀀉had􀀉hollowed􀀉him􀀉into􀀉a􀀉skeletal􀀉shadow,􀀉his􀀉Engine-frame􀀉
already􀀉eating􀀉itself􀀉alive.􀀉

Time􀀉was􀀉a􀀉vanishing􀀉resource.􀀉Despite􀀉identifying􀀉the􀀉poison􀀉in􀀉the􀀉water,􀀉
the􀀉sleep􀀉deprivation􀀉hadn’t􀀉lifted.􀀉Something􀀉was􀀉still􀀉off;􀀉quitting􀀉the􀀉
water􀀉hadn’t􀀉cleared􀀉the􀀉chemical􀀉concussion􀀉from􀀉their􀀉systems.􀀉Then􀀉
Gage􀀉heard􀀉it:􀀉the􀀉heavy􀀉snoring􀀉of􀀉someone􀀉in􀀉deep􀀉sleep.􀀉He􀀉found􀀉a􀀉
teenager􀀉sleeping􀀉soundly􀀉in􀀉the􀀉grit􀀉—􀀉a􀀉physical􀀉blow􀀉to􀀉his􀀉fracturing􀀉
mind,􀀉seeing􀀉someone􀀉off􀀉the􀀉clock􀀉when􀀉he􀀉and􀀉his􀀉friends􀀉were􀀉being􀀉
unmade.􀀉Ben􀀉and􀀉Otto􀀉could􀀉only􀀉stare,􀀉wondering􀀉what􀀉the􀀉boy􀀉had􀀉
figured􀀉out.􀀉Gage􀀉realized􀀉the􀀉boy􀀉hadn’t􀀉solved􀀉a􀀉puzzle;􀀉he’d􀀉simply􀀉been􀀉
spared􀀉by􀀉a􀀉game􀀉he􀀉didn’t􀀉know􀀉he􀀉was􀀉playing.􀀉

Nobody􀀉spoke􀀉for􀀉a􀀉long􀀉time.􀀉Gage􀀉watched􀀉the􀀉sleeping􀀉teenager’s􀀉chest􀀉
rise􀀉and􀀉fall􀀉—􀀉slow,􀀉even,􀀉peaceful.􀀉Maybe􀀉the􀀉kid􀀉was􀀉smarter􀀉than􀀉them.􀀉
Maybe􀀉he􀀉was􀀉tougher.􀀉It􀀉didn’t􀀉matter.􀀉He’d􀀉just􀀉drawn􀀉a􀀉clean􀀉meal􀀉from􀀉
the􀀉same􀀉pile􀀉that􀀉had􀀉poisoned􀀉them.􀀉

Ben􀀉was􀀉the􀀉first􀀉to􀀉understand􀀉what􀀉that􀀉meant􀀉—􀀉and􀀉the􀀉understanding􀀉
destroyed􀀉him.􀀉He’d􀀉spent􀀉his􀀉whole􀀉life􀀉believing􀀉in􀀉a􀀉bargain:􀀉that􀀉if􀀉you􀀉
held􀀉the􀀉line,􀀉the􀀉line􀀉held􀀉you􀀉back.􀀉That􀀉if􀀉you􀀉chose􀀉empathy,􀀉the􀀉world􀀉
made􀀉room􀀉for􀀉you.􀀉That􀀉care􀀉wasn’t􀀉just􀀉decoration􀀉—􀀉it􀀉was􀀉load-
bearing.􀀉But􀀉this􀀉wasn’t􀀉cruelty.􀀉It􀀉was􀀉arithmetic.􀀉A􀀉random􀀉shuffle􀀉that􀀉
didn’t􀀉care􀀉whether􀀉you􀀉were􀀉the􀀉glue􀀉or􀀉the􀀉thing􀀉that􀀉needed􀀉gluing.􀀉He’d􀀉
held􀀉Gage􀀉back􀀉from􀀉the􀀉tunnel.􀀉He’d􀀉talked􀀉Otto􀀉down􀀉from􀀉his􀀉guilt.􀀉


He’d􀀉been􀀉the􀀉Binding􀀉Agent􀀉for􀀉days􀀉—􀀉and􀀉none􀀉of􀀉it􀀉mattered,􀀉because􀀉
the􀀉system􀀉hadn’t􀀉been􀀉aiming􀀉at􀀉them.􀀉It􀀉had􀀉been􀀉rolling􀀉dice.􀀉

Ben􀀉snapped.􀀉His􀀉role􀀉as􀀉Binding􀀉Agent􀀉dissolved􀀉into􀀉jagged􀀉resentment.􀀉
He􀀉threw􀀉coal􀀉blocks􀀉at􀀉Gage􀀉and􀀉Otto,􀀉voice􀀉a􀀉dry􀀉shriek.􀀉“It’s􀀉all􀀉your􀀉
fault! Friendship􀀉is􀀉just􀀉a􀀉slower􀀉way􀀉to􀀉die!”􀀉

Gage􀀉didn’t􀀉lash􀀉out.􀀉He􀀉forced􀀉himself􀀉to􀀉adopt􀀉the􀀉insight􀀉he􀀉had􀀉seen􀀉in􀀉
Camilla’s􀀉eyes.􀀉Ben􀀉had􀀉snapped􀀉harder􀀉than􀀉Gage.􀀉Otto􀀉had􀀉collapsed􀀉
harder􀀉than􀀉Ben.􀀉The􀀉drug􀀉was􀀉supposed􀀉to􀀉be􀀉one􀀉drug􀀉—􀀉but􀀉they􀀉were􀀉
breaking􀀉at􀀉different􀀉speeds.􀀉Gage􀀉needed􀀉to􀀉know􀀉if􀀉their􀀉pouches􀀉had􀀉
been􀀉different􀀉from􀀉his.􀀉He􀀉pulled􀀉the􀀉Logic􀀉Strip􀀉from􀀉his􀀉sleeve,􀀉
remembering􀀉Camilla’s􀀉instruction􀀉that􀀉it􀀉needed􀀉to􀀉breathe􀀉between􀀉tests.􀀉
“One􀀉at􀀉a􀀉time,”􀀉he􀀉whispered,􀀉a􀀉mantra􀀉borrowed􀀉from􀀉her􀀉quiet􀀉hands.􀀉
He􀀉touched􀀉it􀀉to􀀉a􀀉crumb􀀉of􀀉Ben’s􀀉dried􀀉chicken.􀀉It􀀉flared􀀉violent􀀉neon􀀉
green.􀀉He􀀉waited􀀉for􀀉the􀀉sensor􀀉to􀀉clear,􀀉then􀀉pressed􀀉it􀀉into􀀉Otto’s􀀉mashed􀀉
potatoes.􀀉A􀀉sickly􀀉chemical􀀉yellow􀀉throbbed􀀉through􀀉the􀀉plastic.􀀉

The􀀉evidence􀀉was􀀉undeniable.􀀉It􀀉wasn’t􀀉a􀀉single􀀉contaminated􀀉batch.􀀉It􀀉was􀀉
the􀀉Lottery􀀉—􀀉a􀀉randomized􀀉culling􀀉designed􀀉by􀀉the􀀉Technocrats􀀉to􀀉ensure􀀉
they􀀉couldn’t􀀉share,􀀉couldn’t􀀉trust,􀀉couldn’t􀀉survive􀀉as􀀉a􀀉group.􀀉The􀀉
realization􀀉was􀀉a􀀉victory􀀉of􀀉insight,􀀉but􀀉it􀀉cost􀀉them􀀉their􀀉unity.􀀉For􀀉the􀀉first􀀉
time,􀀉they􀀉were􀀉unhappy􀀉in􀀉each􀀉other’s􀀉company,􀀉buckled􀀉up􀀉to􀀉last􀀉the􀀉
final􀀉hours􀀉in􀀉isolation.􀀉As􀀉Ben’s􀀉mind􀀉blue-screened􀀉into􀀉nightmare,􀀉a􀀉tiny􀀉
red􀀉flicker􀀉sparked􀀉in􀀉the􀀉ceiling􀀉—􀀉a􀀉lens􀀉belonging􀀉to􀀉Jung.􀀉

Jung􀀉was􀀉the􀀉Shadow,􀀉a􀀉Technocrat􀀉surveillance􀀉architect􀀉who􀀉watched􀀉
their􀀉behavior􀀉through􀀉every􀀉lens􀀉and􀀉microphone􀀉in􀀉the􀀉mountain􀀉—􀀉
reading􀀉patterns,􀀉tracking􀀉group􀀉dynamics,􀀉predicting􀀉who􀀉would􀀉break􀀉
next.􀀉To􀀉the􀀉system,􀀉they􀀉weren’t􀀉children;􀀉they􀀉were􀀉data􀀉points􀀉that􀀉
needed􀀉sorting􀀉before􀀉being􀀉recycled􀀉into􀀉the􀀉labor􀀉force􀀉or􀀉the􀀉Outflow.􀀉
The􀀉model􀀉had􀀉not􀀉predicted􀀉them.􀀉Three􀀉signatures􀀉should􀀉have􀀉
fragmented􀀉by􀀉now;􀀉instead􀀉they􀀉were􀀉holding.􀀉Until􀀉the􀀉cluster􀀉broke,􀀉the􀀉


data􀀉was􀀉unsortable.􀀉Jung􀀉was􀀉patient.􀀉The􀀉chemical􀀉would􀀉do􀀉its􀀉work􀀉
eventually.􀀉It􀀉always􀀉did.􀀉

Gage􀀉was􀀉eventually􀀉pulled􀀉into􀀉his􀀉own􀀉void􀀉as􀀉the􀀉Pappy􀀉Debt􀀉came􀀉for􀀉
payment.􀀉In􀀉the􀀉wavering􀀉dark,􀀉Pappy􀀉appeared􀀉—􀀉a􀀉horrific􀀉fusion􀀉of􀀉
bone􀀉and􀀉flesh,􀀉holding􀀉a􀀉knife􀀉against􀀉Gage’s􀀉face.􀀉“Let􀀉me􀀉complete􀀉the􀀉
sketch􀀉initiated􀀉a􀀉long􀀉time􀀉ago,”􀀉the􀀉apparition􀀉whispered,􀀉tracing􀀉the􀀉scar􀀉
lines􀀉across􀀉Gage’s􀀉brow.􀀉Gage􀀉felt􀀉the􀀉phantom􀀉blade􀀉cut􀀉through􀀉his􀀉flesh.􀀉

When􀀉the􀀉vision􀀉shattered,􀀉Gage􀀉took􀀉a􀀉jagged􀀉shard􀀉of􀀉metal􀀉and􀀉cut􀀉a􀀉
straight􀀉line􀀉into􀀉his􀀉own􀀉leg.􀀉The􀀉apparition􀀉would􀀉not􀀉finish􀀉the􀀉Map.􀀉
The􀀉drug􀀉would􀀉not􀀉finish􀀉him.􀀉The􀀉body􀀉still􀀉answered􀀉to􀀉him,􀀉and􀀉he􀀉
made􀀉it􀀉answer􀀉—􀀉once,􀀉on􀀉his􀀉own􀀉terms,􀀉in􀀉a􀀉place􀀉no􀀉one􀀉else􀀉had􀀉
marked.􀀉

Beside􀀉him,􀀉Otto􀀉had􀀉finally􀀉gone􀀉still􀀉—􀀉not􀀉the􀀉controlled􀀉stillness􀀉of􀀉his􀀉
Kata,􀀉but􀀉the􀀉heavy,􀀉slack􀀉collapse􀀉of􀀉a􀀉body􀀉that􀀉had􀀉simply􀀉run􀀉out􀀉of􀀉
fuel.􀀉The􀀉rainbow􀀉drug􀀉wasn’t􀀉releasing􀀉them;􀀉their􀀉bodies􀀉were􀀉running􀀉
out􀀉of􀀉the􀀉strength􀀉to􀀉fight􀀉it.􀀉Ben􀀉had􀀉been􀀉out􀀉since􀀉Otto’s􀀉fist􀀉put􀀉him􀀉
down.􀀉Now􀀉Otto􀀉had􀀉followed,􀀉his􀀉breathing􀀉shallow􀀉and􀀉ragged,􀀉his􀀉fists􀀉
still􀀉half-clenched􀀉even􀀉after􀀉he’d􀀉gone􀀉out.􀀉Gage􀀉was􀀉the􀀉last􀀉one􀀉standing􀀉

—􀀉the􀀉Engine􀀉that􀀉Thorton􀀉and􀀉Pappy􀀉had􀀉over-built􀀉for􀀉exactly􀀉this􀀉kind􀀉
of􀀉endurance.􀀉
He􀀉sat􀀉by􀀉his􀀉fallen􀀉friends,􀀉scratching􀀉a􀀉map􀀉of􀀉the􀀉tunnels􀀉into􀀉dirt􀀉from􀀉
memory,􀀉searching􀀉for􀀉the􀀉cracks􀀉in􀀉the􀀉system’s􀀉armor.􀀉He􀀉had􀀉made􀀉a􀀉
vow􀀉at􀀉the􀀉gates:􀀉not􀀉before􀀉he􀀉burned􀀉them.􀀉He􀀉intended􀀉to􀀉keep􀀉it.􀀉

As􀀉his􀀉eyelids􀀉finally􀀉grew􀀉heavy,􀀉Gage􀀉smiled.􀀉He􀀉had􀀉won􀀉the􀀉battle􀀉
against􀀉the􀀉drug,􀀉but􀀉the􀀉fight􀀉for􀀉his􀀉family􀀉—􀀉and􀀉for􀀉every􀀉family􀀉Trinity􀀉
had􀀉reduced􀀉to􀀉data􀀉—􀀉was􀀉only􀀉beginning.􀀉


Chapter􀀉6􀀉—􀀉To􀀉Hell􀀉and􀀉Back􀀉

Camilla􀀉paced􀀉the􀀉soot-choked􀀉street􀀉with􀀉frantic􀀉energy,􀀉her􀀉movements􀀉
mirroring􀀉the􀀉erratic􀀉pulse􀀉of􀀉a􀀉machine􀀉on􀀉the􀀉verge􀀉of􀀉seizure.􀀉Her􀀉eyes􀀉
were􀀉bloodshot,􀀉ringed􀀉with􀀉the􀀉dark􀀉hollows􀀉of􀀉someone􀀉who􀀉had􀀉barely􀀉
slept􀀉in􀀉three􀀉days.􀀉Every􀀉night􀀉she’d􀀉tossed􀀉on􀀉her􀀉cot,􀀉sleep􀀉coming􀀉in􀀉fits􀀉
between􀀉long􀀉stretches􀀉of􀀉staring􀀉at􀀉the􀀉metal􀀉ceiling,􀀉her􀀉mind􀀉cycling􀀉
through􀀉every􀀉version􀀉of􀀉what􀀉might􀀉be􀀉happening􀀉in􀀉the􀀉dark􀀉below.􀀉Now􀀉
it􀀉was􀀉the􀀉morning􀀉of􀀉the􀀉fourth􀀉day􀀉—􀀉the􀀉morning􀀉the􀀉Initiation􀀉was􀀉
supposed􀀉to􀀉end􀀉—􀀉and􀀉her􀀉eyes􀀉constantly􀀉darted􀀉toward􀀉the􀀉horizon􀀉
where􀀉the􀀉mine􀀉gates􀀉stood􀀉like􀀉massive􀀉iron􀀉teeth.􀀉Every􀀉breath􀀉felt􀀉like􀀉
inhaling􀀉ground􀀉glass.􀀉

Rita􀀉followed􀀉with􀀉sad􀀉eyes,􀀉moving􀀉with􀀉heavy􀀉weariness.􀀉She􀀉knew􀀉the􀀉
math􀀉of􀀉Miner􀀉Town􀀉too􀀉well;􀀉she’d􀀉seen􀀉too􀀉many􀀉teenagers􀀉march􀀉into􀀉
the􀀉Initiation􀀉only􀀉to􀀉have􀀉their􀀉families􀀉wait􀀉in􀀉the􀀉grit􀀉for􀀉a􀀉homecoming􀀉
that􀀉never􀀉arrived.􀀉

She􀀉was􀀉already􀀉hollowed􀀉out,􀀉her􀀉spirit􀀉eroded􀀉by􀀉relentless􀀉loss.􀀉The􀀉death􀀉
of􀀉Thorton,􀀉following􀀉only􀀉a􀀉few􀀉years􀀉after􀀉Trinity’s􀀉guards’􀀉cruelty􀀉had􀀉
killed􀀉Pappy􀀉on􀀉the􀀉Coalball􀀉field,􀀉had􀀉shattered􀀉her􀀉belief􀀉in􀀉anything􀀉just.􀀉
It􀀉seemed􀀉like􀀉only􀀉yesterday􀀉that􀀉Thorton􀀉would􀀉walk􀀉through􀀉the􀀉door􀀉
after􀀉a􀀉grueling􀀉shift,􀀉skin􀀉caked􀀉in􀀉the􀀉permanent􀀉grey􀀉rot􀀉of􀀉the􀀉pits.􀀉Even􀀉
when􀀉his􀀉body􀀉was􀀉broken,􀀉his􀀉spirit􀀉—􀀉fed􀀉by􀀉fierce􀀉love􀀉for􀀉his􀀉children􀀉—􀀉
remained􀀉a􀀉flickering􀀉candle.􀀉

They􀀉had􀀉lived􀀉a􀀉life􀀉of􀀉hard􀀉edges􀀉and􀀉deprivation,􀀉but􀀉Thorton􀀉always􀀉
brought􀀉home􀀉twinkling􀀉joys􀀉salvaged􀀉from􀀉scrap􀀉heaps:􀀉a􀀉copper􀀉wire􀀉that􀀉
caught􀀉the􀀉hearth’s􀀉glow,􀀉a􀀉stone􀀉smoothed􀀉by􀀉a􀀉river􀀉dead􀀉a􀀉century,􀀉a􀀉
rusted􀀉bolt􀀉with􀀉strange􀀉weight.􀀉These􀀉were􀀉raw􀀉materials􀀉for􀀉evening􀀉
investigative􀀉sessions,􀀉where􀀉they􀀉looked􀀉for􀀉logic􀀉in􀀉the􀀉world’s􀀉discarded􀀉
parts.􀀉


One􀀉evening,􀀉they􀀉sat􀀉around􀀉a􀀉piece􀀉of􀀉coal􀀉that􀀉looked􀀉different􀀉—􀀉it􀀉
didn’t􀀉shatter􀀉into􀀉brittle􀀉shards􀀉when􀀉Gage􀀉struck􀀉it.􀀉Instead,􀀉it􀀉sheared􀀉off􀀉
in􀀉dense,􀀉oily􀀉layers,􀀉revealing􀀉an􀀉iridescent􀀉sheen􀀉like􀀉oil􀀉on􀀉water.􀀉

“That’s􀀉not􀀉fern-leaf􀀉or􀀉peat,􀀉little􀀉bird,”􀀉Pappy􀀉had􀀉whispered􀀉to􀀉Camilla,􀀉
leaning􀀉into􀀉the􀀉flickering􀀉light􀀉of􀀉the􀀉coal-fire.􀀉“That’s􀀉Urban􀀉Anthracite.􀀉
During􀀉the􀀉Great􀀉Extraction,􀀉the􀀉cities􀀉weren’t􀀉just􀀉abandoned􀀉—􀀉they􀀉
were􀀉shredded.􀀉The􀀉Trinity􀀉machines􀀉ground􀀉down􀀉skyscrapers,􀀉plastics,􀀉
and􀀉the􀀉synthetic􀀉guts􀀉of􀀉the􀀉old􀀉world,􀀉burying􀀉them􀀉under􀀉a􀀉billion􀀉tons􀀉
of􀀉earth􀀉until􀀉they􀀉became􀀉something􀀉else.”􀀉

Gage􀀉had􀀉touched􀀉the􀀉smooth􀀉surface,􀀉feeling􀀉the􀀉compressed􀀉memory􀀉of􀀉a􀀉
dead􀀉world.􀀉Pappy􀀉had􀀉warned􀀉them􀀉that􀀉the􀀉coal􀀉wasn’t􀀉just􀀉rock􀀉—􀀉every􀀉
chunk􀀉carried􀀉the􀀉buried􀀉poisons􀀉of􀀉the􀀉old􀀉cities.􀀉Copper􀀉dust,􀀉chemical􀀉
solvents,􀀉things􀀉that􀀉had􀀉no􀀉business􀀉being􀀉breathed.􀀉“The􀀉mountain􀀉
doesn’t􀀉kill􀀉miners􀀉with􀀉rock,”􀀉Pappy􀀉had􀀉told􀀉them.􀀉“It􀀈kills􀀈them􀀈with􀀈
what􀀈it􀀈remembers.”􀀉

Thorton􀀉had􀀉been􀀉the􀀉human􀀉glue􀀉that􀀉held􀀉their􀀉fractured􀀉lives􀀉together.􀀉
But􀀉the􀀉mines􀀉harbored􀀉malice􀀉for􀀉anything􀀉not􀀉forged􀀉of􀀉cold􀀉metal.􀀉
Three􀀉days􀀉after􀀉Gage’s􀀉fifteenth􀀉birthday,􀀉the􀀉hum􀀉of􀀉the􀀉earth􀀉shifted􀀉to􀀉a􀀉
high-pitched􀀉scream􀀉—􀀉a􀀉critical􀀉valve􀀉seizing􀀉in􀀉Shaft􀀉4.􀀉While􀀉guards􀀉
retreated􀀉to􀀉upper􀀉vents,􀀉the􀀉miners􀀉remained􀀉trapped.􀀉Thorton􀀉grabbed􀀉a􀀉
rusted􀀉wrench􀀉and􀀉threw􀀉his􀀉frame􀀉against􀀉the􀀉release􀀉lever,􀀉holding􀀉the􀀉
line􀀉until􀀉the􀀉scream􀀉faded.􀀉The􀀉effort􀀉tore􀀉through􀀉his􀀉chest􀀉like􀀉a􀀉closing􀀉
fist.􀀉His􀀉heart􀀉gave􀀉out,􀀉and􀀉he􀀉collapsed􀀉into􀀉the􀀉sludge.􀀉In􀀉the􀀉cold􀀉ledger􀀉
of􀀉Miner􀀉Town,􀀉they􀀉didn’t􀀉record􀀉a􀀉hero;􀀉they􀀉recorded􀀉a􀀉broken􀀉part.􀀉

Thorton’s􀀉body􀀉went􀀉to􀀉the􀀉Outflow,􀀉into􀀉the􀀉disposal􀀉stream,􀀉into􀀉
nothing􀀉the􀀉family􀀉could􀀉touch.􀀉They􀀉got􀀉no􀀉notice􀀉that􀀉he􀀉had􀀉died.􀀉The􀀉
first􀀉night,􀀉Rita􀀉kept􀀉the􀀉lamp􀀉burning􀀉past􀀉dark,􀀉telling􀀉Camilla􀀉he􀀉must􀀉
have􀀉caught􀀉a􀀉long􀀉shift.􀀉The􀀉second􀀉day,􀀉she􀀉walked􀀉to􀀉the􀀉gates􀀉herself􀀉
and􀀉asked􀀉the􀀉guard􀀉who􀀉turned􀀉her􀀉away􀀉without􀀉a􀀉word.􀀉The􀀉third􀀉day,􀀉


she􀀉stopped􀀉asking.􀀉By􀀉then􀀉she􀀉knew.􀀉The􀀉system􀀉didn’t􀀉send􀀉word􀀉when􀀉
miners􀀉didn’t􀀉come􀀉home􀀉—􀀉silence􀀉was􀀉its􀀉way􀀉of􀀉saying􀀉it.􀀉

On􀀉the􀀉fourth􀀉day􀀉a􀀉guard􀀉came􀀉to􀀉her􀀉door􀀉with􀀉a􀀉wrench.􀀉The􀀉wrench􀀉he􀀉
had􀀉been􀀉holding􀀉had􀀉been􀀉logged􀀉to􀀉him􀀉at􀀉the􀀉start􀀉of􀀉the􀀉shift,􀀉and􀀉
Trinity􀀉was􀀉a􀀉careful􀀉auditor.􀀉An􀀉unaccounted􀀉tool􀀉was􀀉a􀀉hole􀀉in􀀉the􀀉
inventory.􀀉The􀀉guard􀀉handed􀀉it􀀉over􀀉without􀀉ceremony􀀉—􀀉the􀀉way􀀉one􀀉
returns􀀉a􀀉shipment.􀀉Rita􀀉signed􀀉for􀀉it.􀀉The􀀉husband􀀉was􀀉a􀀉part􀀉to􀀉discard;􀀉
the􀀉wrench􀀉was􀀉an􀀉asset􀀉to􀀉track.􀀉It􀀉was􀀉the􀀉only􀀉piece􀀉of􀀉him􀀉the􀀉algorithm􀀉
hadn’t􀀉erased.􀀉

That􀀉had􀀉been􀀉two􀀉years􀀉ago.􀀉Now,􀀉on􀀉the􀀉morning􀀉of􀀉the􀀉fourth􀀉day􀀉of􀀉
Gage’s􀀉Initiation,􀀉Rita􀀉stood􀀉at􀀉the􀀉towering􀀉gates􀀉clutching􀀉that􀀉wrench􀀉
like􀀉her􀀉only􀀉heirloom.􀀉The􀀉gates􀀉slid􀀉open􀀉with􀀉their􀀉soul-jarring􀀉screech.􀀉
Standing􀀉guard􀀉were􀀉the􀀉Urban􀀉Servants,􀀉faces􀀉hidden􀀉behind􀀉predator-
snout􀀉masks.􀀉

A􀀉collective􀀉gasp􀀉rippled􀀉through􀀉the􀀉crowd.􀀉A􀀉slender􀀉figure􀀉emerged,􀀉not􀀉
walking􀀉but􀀉crawling,􀀉one􀀉hand􀀉clutching􀀉the􀀉bleeding􀀉stump􀀉where􀀉her􀀉
other􀀉arm􀀉had􀀉been􀀉—􀀉Janine,􀀉a􀀉victim􀀉of􀀉the􀀉chemical􀀉concussion􀀉and􀀉the􀀉
hum.􀀉The􀀉guards􀀉didn’t􀀉move􀀉to􀀉help.􀀉They􀀉watched􀀉as􀀉she􀀉fainted􀀉and􀀉was􀀉
carried􀀉away,􀀉leaving􀀉a􀀉smear􀀉of􀀉red􀀉in􀀉the􀀉black􀀉grit.􀀉

Camilla􀀉shifted􀀉her􀀉weight,􀀉watching􀀉her􀀉mother’s􀀉hand􀀉close􀀉around􀀉the􀀉
wrench.􀀉Two􀀉years.􀀉The􀀉thought􀀉came􀀉unbidden,􀀉the􀀉way􀀉it􀀉always􀀉did􀀉
when􀀉she􀀉stood􀀉at􀀉the􀀉gates.􀀉The􀀉ration􀀉cards􀀉were􀀉how􀀉the􀀉system􀀉found􀀉
them􀀉—􀀉name,􀀉birthdate,􀀉family􀀉unit,􀀉all􀀉logged􀀉when􀀉the􀀉cards􀀉were􀀉
issued.􀀉The􀀉algorithm􀀉flagged􀀉kids􀀉when􀀉they􀀉hit􀀉seventeen,􀀉and􀀉the􀀉next􀀉
Initiation􀀉cycle􀀉pulled􀀉them􀀉in.􀀉Gage’s􀀉card􀀉had􀀉been􀀉flagged􀀉six􀀉months􀀉
ago.􀀉Hers􀀉would􀀉be􀀉flagged􀀉two􀀉years􀀉from􀀉now.􀀉Pappy􀀉had􀀉taught􀀉her􀀉this􀀉
kind􀀉of􀀉arithmetic􀀉—􀀉the􀀉kind􀀉where􀀉you􀀉counted􀀉the􀀉days􀀉until􀀉the􀀉system􀀉
came􀀉for􀀉someone􀀉you􀀉loved,􀀉and􀀉you􀀉used􀀉the􀀉days􀀉to􀀉prepare.􀀉


For􀀉a􀀉long,􀀉agonizing􀀉minute,􀀉the􀀉tunnel􀀉mouth􀀉remained􀀉empty.􀀉Rita􀀉
tightened􀀉her􀀉grip􀀉on􀀉Thorton’s􀀉wrench,􀀉her􀀉hope􀀉red-lining,􀀉her􀀉knuckles􀀉
white􀀉against􀀉the􀀉greasy􀀉iron.􀀉

Then􀀉a􀀉new􀀉shadow􀀉appeared􀀉—􀀉heavy,􀀉slow,􀀉rhythmic.􀀉Gage􀀉walked􀀉out􀀉
of􀀉the􀀉dark􀀉with􀀉a􀀉steady􀀉gait,􀀉his􀀉face􀀉changed􀀉in􀀉ways􀀉Camilla􀀉didn’t􀀉yet􀀉
have􀀉names􀀉for.􀀉He􀀉was􀀉unbreakable􀀉—􀀉six-foot-five,􀀉carrying􀀉Otto􀀉across􀀉
his􀀉shoulders􀀉and􀀉dragging􀀉a􀀉semi-conscious􀀉Ben􀀉by􀀉the􀀉collar􀀉of􀀉his􀀉shirt.􀀉
He􀀉wasn’t􀀉the􀀉broken􀀉marionette􀀉the􀀉guards􀀉expected;􀀉he􀀉was􀀉the􀀉Engine􀀉
that􀀉refused􀀉to􀀉red-line.􀀉

“They􀀉will􀀉live,”􀀉he􀀉said􀀉simply,􀀉his􀀉voice􀀉a􀀉low􀀉thrum􀀉that􀀉cut􀀉through􀀉the􀀉
sobbing􀀉of􀀉the􀀉other􀀉families.􀀉He􀀉hugged􀀉Camilla􀀉—􀀉held􀀉her􀀉longer􀀉than􀀉
he􀀉had􀀉in􀀉years􀀉—􀀉and􀀉in􀀉that􀀉embrace,􀀉she􀀉felt􀀉the􀀉transformation.􀀉The􀀉
brother􀀉who’d􀀉walked􀀉through􀀉those􀀉gates􀀉was􀀉gone.􀀉The􀀉man􀀉holding􀀉her􀀉
was􀀉possessed􀀉by􀀉a􀀉cold,􀀉calculating􀀉fire.􀀉

That􀀉night,􀀉the􀀉shack􀀉was􀀉full􀀉of􀀉bodies􀀉and􀀉silence.􀀉Rita􀀉had􀀉laid􀀉Ben􀀉and􀀉
Otto􀀉on􀀉pallets􀀉of􀀉flattened􀀉cardboard􀀉near􀀉the􀀉small􀀉heating􀀉element,􀀉a􀀉
cup􀀉of􀀉filtered􀀉water􀀉waiting􀀉beside􀀉each􀀉of􀀉them􀀉for􀀉when􀀉they􀀉woke.􀀉
Their􀀉breathing􀀉was􀀉shallow􀀉but􀀉steady􀀉—􀀉the􀀉sound􀀉of􀀉systems􀀉slowly􀀉
coming􀀉back􀀉online.􀀉Gage􀀉sat􀀉in􀀉the􀀉corner􀀉with􀀉his􀀉back􀀉against􀀉the􀀉
corrugated􀀉wall,􀀉watching􀀉them􀀉breathe,􀀉counting􀀉the􀀉seconds􀀉between􀀉
each􀀉inhale􀀉as􀀉if􀀉he􀀉could􀀉hold􀀉them􀀉in􀀉the􀀉world􀀉through􀀉sheer􀀉attention.􀀉

Camilla􀀉brought􀀉him􀀉a􀀉tin􀀉cup􀀉of􀀉lukewarm􀀉water􀀉—􀀉sump􀀉water,􀀉triple-
filtered􀀉through􀀉the􀀉charcoal􀀉rag,􀀉still􀀉faintly􀀉orange.􀀉She􀀉sat􀀉next􀀉to􀀉him,􀀉
close􀀉enough􀀉that􀀉their􀀉shoulders􀀉touched.􀀉For􀀉a􀀉long􀀉time,􀀉neither􀀉spoke.􀀉
The􀀉shack􀀉creaked􀀉around􀀉them,􀀉metal􀀉contracting􀀉in􀀉the􀀉cooling􀀉night􀀉air,􀀉
and􀀉somewhere􀀉in􀀉the􀀉shack-rows􀀉a􀀉baby􀀉was􀀉crying􀀉—􀀉a􀀉thin,􀀉reedy􀀉sound􀀉
that􀀉seemed􀀉to􀀉come􀀉from􀀉everywhere􀀉and􀀉nowhere.􀀉

“You􀀉smell􀀉like􀀉the􀀉deep,”􀀉Camilla􀀉said􀀉quietly.􀀉

“I􀀉smell􀀉like􀀉everything􀀉down􀀉there.”􀀉


“No.􀀉The􀀉deep􀀉has􀀉its􀀉own􀀉smell.􀀉Father􀀉used􀀉to􀀉come􀀉home􀀉with􀀉it􀀉after􀀉
the􀀉long􀀉shifts􀀉—􀀉it􀀉would􀀉still􀀉be􀀉on􀀉his􀀉coat􀀉when􀀉he􀀉sat􀀉down􀀉at􀀉the􀀉
table.􀀉It’s􀀉not􀀉just􀀉coal.􀀉It’s􀀉older􀀉than􀀉coal.􀀉It􀀉smells􀀉like􀀉the􀀉world􀀉before􀀉
people.”􀀉

Gage􀀉looked􀀉at􀀉her.􀀉In􀀉the􀀉dim􀀉light􀀉of􀀉the􀀉heating􀀉element,􀀉her􀀉face􀀉was􀀉
half􀀉shadow,􀀉half􀀉amber.􀀉She􀀉was􀀉fifteen􀀉and􀀉already􀀉had􀀉the􀀉eyes􀀉of􀀉
someone􀀉who􀀉had􀀉been􀀉awake􀀉too􀀉long.􀀉Not􀀉tired􀀉—􀀉awake.􀀉The􀀉kind􀀉of􀀉
awake􀀉that􀀉comes􀀉from􀀉watching􀀉the􀀉world􀀉with􀀉the􀀉certain􀀉knowledge􀀉
that􀀉it􀀉is􀀉trying􀀉to􀀉kill􀀉you.􀀉

“Little􀀉bird,”􀀉he􀀉said,􀀉and􀀉his􀀉voice􀀉caught.􀀉He’d􀀉never􀀉called􀀉her􀀉that􀀉—􀀉it􀀉
had􀀉always􀀉been􀀉Pappy’s􀀉word􀀉for􀀉her,􀀉never􀀉Gage’s.􀀉Camilla􀀉looked􀀉up􀀉at􀀉
him􀀉and􀀉felt􀀉something􀀉shift􀀉between􀀉them􀀉that􀀉couldn’t􀀉be􀀉undone.􀀉“I􀀉
saw􀀉things􀀉down􀀉there.􀀉Things􀀉I􀀉can’t…􀀉I􀀉don’t􀀉have􀀉the􀀉words􀀉for􀀉what􀀉
they􀀉did􀀉to􀀉us.”􀀉

“You􀀉don’t􀀉need􀀉words,”􀀉she􀀉said.􀀉“I􀀉can􀀉see􀀉it.”􀀉

“How?”􀀉

She􀀉pointed􀀉to􀀉his􀀉hands.􀀉They􀀉were􀀉shaking􀀉—􀀉a􀀉fine,􀀉rapid􀀉tremor􀀉that􀀉he􀀉
hadn’t􀀉noticed􀀉until􀀉she􀀉drew􀀉his􀀉attention􀀉to􀀉it.􀀉He􀀉clenched􀀉his􀀉fists,􀀉but􀀉
the􀀉shaking􀀉just􀀉moved􀀉up􀀉his􀀉forearms.􀀉

“Father’s􀀉hands􀀉used􀀉to􀀉do􀀉that,”􀀉she􀀉said.􀀉“After􀀉the􀀉bad􀀉shifts.􀀉He’d􀀉come􀀉
home􀀉and􀀉sit􀀉exactly􀀉where􀀉you’re􀀉sitting􀀉and􀀉hold􀀉his􀀉hands􀀉in􀀉his􀀉lap􀀉and􀀉
pretend􀀉they􀀉weren’t􀀉shaking.􀀉Ma􀀉would􀀉see􀀉it􀀉and􀀉go􀀉to􀀉the􀀉other􀀉room􀀉so􀀉
he􀀉wouldn’t􀀉know􀀉she􀀉was􀀉crying.”􀀉

“I’m􀀉not􀀉Father.”􀀉

“No.􀀉You’re􀀉not.”􀀉She􀀉paused.􀀉“Father􀀉accepted􀀉it.􀀉You􀀉won’t.􀀉That’s􀀉the􀀉
difference.􀀉That’s􀀉what􀀉scares􀀉me.”􀀉


She􀀉didn’t􀀉tell􀀉him􀀉the􀀉rest.􀀉That􀀉somewhere􀀉underneath􀀉the􀀉fear􀀉of􀀉his􀀉
defiance􀀉was􀀉another􀀉fear,􀀉smaller􀀉and􀀉more􀀉selfish􀀉—􀀉the􀀉fear􀀉of􀀉standing􀀉
where􀀉he􀀉was􀀉standing􀀉two􀀉years􀀉from􀀉now.􀀉The􀀉fear􀀉of􀀉being􀀉the􀀉one􀀉
whose􀀉hands􀀉shook􀀉in􀀉the􀀉lamplight􀀉while􀀉someone􀀉she􀀉loved􀀉counted􀀉her􀀉
breaths.􀀉She􀀉had􀀉watched􀀉him􀀉walk􀀉through􀀉the􀀉gates􀀉a􀀉brother􀀉and􀀉walk􀀉
back􀀉out􀀉something􀀉else,􀀉and􀀉a􀀉part􀀉of􀀉her􀀉had􀀉begun,􀀉quietly,􀀉to􀀉
understand􀀉why􀀉a􀀉body􀀉might􀀉want􀀉to􀀉hide􀀉before􀀉it􀀉could􀀉be􀀉sent􀀉down􀀉
there.􀀉

They􀀉sat􀀉in􀀉silence􀀉again.􀀉The􀀉baby􀀉in􀀉the􀀉shack-rows􀀉had􀀉stopped􀀉crying.􀀉
Somewhere,􀀉a􀀉dog􀀉—􀀉one􀀉of􀀉the􀀉mange-riddled􀀉strays􀀉that􀀉survived􀀉on􀀉coal􀀉
dust􀀉and􀀉scraps􀀉—􀀉howled􀀉at􀀉the􀀉chemical􀀉haze􀀉where􀀉the􀀉moon􀀉should􀀉
have􀀉been.􀀉

“When􀀉we􀀉were􀀉little,”􀀉Camilla􀀉said,􀀉“you􀀉drew􀀉a􀀉forest􀀉on􀀉the􀀉wall􀀉behind􀀉
the􀀉water􀀉tank.􀀉You􀀉remember?”􀀉

“No.”􀀉

“Liar.􀀉You􀀉drew􀀉it􀀉with􀀉the􀀉green􀀉chalk􀀉Pappy􀀉found􀀉in􀀉the􀀉old􀀉school􀀉
rubble.􀀉It􀀉had􀀉twelve􀀉trees.􀀉I􀀉counted􀀉them􀀉every􀀉night􀀉before􀀉I􀀉fell􀀉asleep.”􀀉

“It􀀉was􀀉just􀀉a􀀉drawing.”􀀉

“It􀀉was􀀉the􀀉only􀀉green􀀉thing􀀉in􀀉Miner􀀉Town,􀀉Gage.􀀉The􀀉only􀀉green􀀉thing􀀉
I’d􀀉ever􀀉seen􀀉that􀀉wasn’t􀀉corrosion􀀉on􀀉a􀀉pipe.”􀀉She􀀉turned􀀉to􀀉face􀀉him.􀀉“Ma􀀉
scrubbed􀀉it􀀉off􀀉the􀀉wall􀀉after􀀉Pappy􀀉died.􀀉She􀀉said􀀉it􀀉was􀀉attracting􀀉dust.􀀉I􀀉
hated􀀉her􀀉for􀀉a􀀉year.”􀀉

Gage􀀉felt􀀉something􀀉loosen􀀉in􀀉his􀀉chest􀀉—􀀉a􀀉knot􀀉he􀀉hadn’t􀀉known􀀉was􀀉
there.􀀉He􀀉looked􀀉at􀀉his􀀉shaking􀀉hands􀀉and􀀉then􀀉at􀀉his􀀉sister,􀀉this􀀉girl􀀉who􀀉
had􀀉memorized􀀉his􀀉childhood􀀉drawings􀀉while􀀉he􀀉was􀀉busy􀀉pretending􀀉she􀀉
didn’t􀀉exist.􀀉

“I’ll􀀉draw􀀉you􀀉another􀀉one,”􀀉he􀀉said.􀀉“When􀀉this􀀉is􀀉done.”􀀉


“Don’t􀀉draw􀀉me􀀉a􀀉forest,􀀉Gage.”􀀉Her􀀉voice􀀉hardened,􀀉and􀀉for􀀉a􀀉moment􀀉
she􀀉sounded􀀉like􀀉Pappy.􀀉“Draw􀀉me􀀉a􀀉way􀀉out.”􀀉

During􀀉the􀀉week􀀉of􀀉recovery,􀀉Gage􀀉turned􀀉the􀀉walls􀀉of􀀉his􀀉room􀀉into􀀉a􀀉live􀀉
billboard,􀀉scribbling􀀉a􀀉sprawling􀀉map􀀉with􀀉charcoal.􀀉He􀀉was􀀉no􀀉longer􀀉
tracing􀀉dream􀀉worlds;􀀉he􀀉was􀀉mapping􀀉a􀀉war.􀀉

Seeking􀀉the􀀉final􀀉piece,􀀉Gage􀀉sat􀀉with􀀉the􀀉Worker􀀉#4055􀀉tag􀀉hanging􀀉
between􀀉his􀀉fingers.􀀉The􀀉system􀀉didn’t􀀉retire􀀉numbers􀀉—􀀉it􀀉refilled􀀉them.􀀉
When􀀉Gage􀀉survived􀀉the􀀉Initiation􀀉and􀀉entered􀀉the􀀉intake􀀉line,􀀉the􀀉
processing􀀉clerk􀀉had􀀉stamped􀀉Thorton’s􀀉vacated􀀉slot􀀉onto􀀉his􀀉son’s􀀉chest􀀉
without􀀉a􀀉second􀀉glance.􀀉The􀀉position􀀉was􀀉the􀀉asset;􀀉Gage􀀉was􀀉just􀀉the􀀉next􀀉
body􀀉assigned􀀉to􀀉fill􀀉it.􀀉But􀀉what􀀉was􀀉once􀀉a􀀉symbol􀀉of􀀉his􀀉father’s􀀉disposal􀀉
had􀀉become􀀉a􀀉key􀀉to􀀉the􀀉machine’s􀀉destruction.􀀉He􀀉walked􀀉to􀀉Camilla.􀀉

“Cami,”􀀉he􀀉rasped,􀀉holding􀀉out􀀉the􀀉tag.􀀉“You􀀉read􀀉the􀀉system􀀉better􀀉than􀀉I􀀉
do.􀀉Tell􀀉me􀀉what􀀉we􀀉can􀀉do􀀉with􀀉this.”􀀉

Camilla􀀉turned􀀉the􀀉tag􀀉over􀀉in􀀉her􀀉fingers.􀀉“It􀀉tells􀀉us􀀉how􀀉they􀀉track􀀉
everyone.􀀉Numbers􀀉don’t􀀉belong􀀉to􀀉people,􀀉Gage􀀉—􀀉they􀀉belong􀀉to􀀉
positions.􀀉Father􀀉had􀀉this􀀉number;􀀉now􀀉you􀀉have􀀉it.􀀉That􀀉means􀀉the􀀉system􀀉
knows􀀉where􀀉every􀀉position􀀉is.􀀉Every􀀉shaft,􀀉every􀀉shift,􀀉every􀀉body.􀀉If􀀉we􀀉
can􀀉read􀀉what􀀉the􀀉numbers􀀉mean,􀀉we􀀉can􀀉read􀀉the􀀉whole􀀉labor􀀉force.”􀀉She􀀉
paused.􀀉“The􀀉tag􀀉the􀀉system􀀉gave􀀉you􀀉is􀀉a􀀉key􀀉to􀀉its􀀉own􀀉map.”􀀉

Gage􀀉felt􀀉the􀀉Pappy􀀉Debt􀀉settle􀀉into􀀉him􀀉with􀀉new􀀉weight.􀀉He􀀉had􀀉carried􀀉
it􀀉as􀀉a􀀉private􀀉grief;􀀉now􀀉he􀀉saw􀀉it􀀉had􀀉been􀀉a􀀉curriculum.􀀉Pappy􀀉had􀀉taught􀀉
Camilla􀀉to􀀉read􀀉the􀀉system;􀀉he􀀉had􀀉built􀀉Gage􀀉to􀀉break􀀉it.􀀉The􀀉two􀀉of􀀉them,􀀉
working􀀉together,􀀉were􀀉what􀀉Pappy􀀉had􀀉been􀀉preparing􀀉all􀀉along.􀀉

Gage􀀉looked􀀉at􀀉the􀀉map􀀉on􀀉the􀀉wall.􀀉It􀀉was􀀉incomplete􀀉—􀀉a􀀉skeleton􀀉
without􀀉names,􀀉a􀀉machine􀀉with􀀉gears􀀉he􀀉could􀀉sense􀀉but􀀉hadn’t􀀉yet􀀉
identified.􀀉He􀀉knew􀀉Icepick􀀉sat􀀉at􀀉the􀀉top,􀀉and􀀉he􀀉knew􀀉there􀀉were􀀉others􀀉
beneath􀀉him:􀀉men􀀉who􀀉disposed,􀀉men􀀉who􀀉watched,􀀉men􀀉who􀀉enforced.􀀉


But􀀉he􀀉didn’t􀀉know􀀉their􀀉faces􀀉or􀀉their􀀉patterns􀀉yet.􀀉That􀀉knowledge􀀉would􀀉
cost􀀉time􀀉in􀀉the􀀉shafts􀀉—􀀉time􀀉he􀀉was􀀉about􀀉to􀀉start􀀉paying.􀀉

Gage’s􀀉hand􀀉closed􀀉around􀀉his􀀉father’s􀀉wrench.􀀉


Chapter􀀉7􀀉—􀀉The􀀉Whole􀀉Rebellion􀀉

Gage􀀉had􀀉carried􀀉a􀀉heavy􀀉sense􀀉of􀀉rebellion􀀉for􀀉as􀀉long􀀉as􀀉he􀀉could􀀉
remember􀀉—􀀉a􀀉deep-seated􀀉frequency􀀉that􀀉refused􀀉to􀀉harmonize􀀉with􀀉the􀀉
world􀀉around􀀉him.􀀉It􀀉had􀀉nothing􀀉to􀀉do􀀉with􀀉Rita.􀀉Thorton􀀉had􀀉brought􀀉
her􀀉into􀀉their􀀉shack􀀉when􀀉Gage􀀉was􀀉still􀀉an􀀉infant,􀀉in􀀉the􀀉raw􀀉months􀀉after􀀉
his􀀉wife􀀉died􀀉giving􀀉Gage􀀉life􀀉—􀀉not􀀉because􀀉his􀀉love􀀉for􀀉Gage’s􀀉late􀀉mother􀀉
had􀀉faded,􀀉but􀀉because􀀉he􀀉wanted􀀉his􀀉son􀀉to􀀉have􀀉care􀀉in􀀉a􀀉world􀀉that􀀉
offered􀀉nothing􀀉but􀀉grey􀀉rot.􀀉He’d􀀉looked􀀉at􀀉the􀀉small􀀉boy’s􀀉skeletal􀀉frame,􀀉
all􀀉knees􀀉and􀀉elbows􀀉and􀀉a􀀉hunger􀀉the􀀉standard􀀉ration􀀉couldn’t􀀉fill,􀀉and􀀉
known􀀉that􀀉without􀀉a􀀉mother􀀉to􀀉keep􀀉him􀀉fed􀀉and􀀉watched,􀀉Miner􀀉Town􀀉
would􀀉grind􀀉him􀀉down􀀉before􀀉he􀀉ever􀀉grew􀀉tall􀀉enough􀀉to􀀉fight􀀉back.􀀉

And􀀉yet􀀉some􀀉part􀀉of􀀉Gage􀀉had􀀉never􀀉fully􀀉settled.􀀉He􀀉had􀀉grown􀀉up􀀉loving􀀉
Rita􀀉as􀀉the􀀉only􀀉mother􀀉he’d􀀉known,􀀉and􀀉grieving,􀀉in􀀉the􀀉same􀀉breath,􀀉a􀀉
woman􀀉he’d􀀉never􀀉met􀀉—􀀉a􀀉mother􀀉who􀀉existed􀀉only􀀉as􀀉an􀀉absence􀀉the􀀉
stories􀀉couldn’t􀀉fill.􀀉Grief􀀉wasn’t􀀉a􀀉process􀀉he􀀉could􀀉manage;􀀉it􀀉was􀀉a􀀉
structural􀀉defect,􀀉one􀀉that􀀉ached􀀉quietest􀀉and􀀉deepest􀀉in􀀉the􀀉ordinary􀀉hours􀀉
of􀀉their􀀉shack.􀀉

In􀀉his􀀉silent􀀉rebellion,􀀉he’d􀀉built􀀉a􀀉towering􀀉wall􀀉between􀀉himself􀀉and􀀉
Camilla,􀀉leaving􀀉his􀀉younger􀀉sister􀀉to􀀉grow􀀉up􀀉in􀀉the􀀉shadow􀀉of􀀉his􀀉
indifference.􀀉He􀀉continually􀀉pushed􀀉her􀀉aside,􀀉treating􀀉her􀀉as􀀉a􀀉weakling􀀉to􀀉
be􀀉excluded􀀉from􀀉his􀀉world􀀉of􀀉scavenging.􀀉He􀀉left􀀉her􀀉to􀀉watch􀀉from􀀉the􀀉
sidelines,􀀉unaware􀀉that􀀉his􀀉neglect􀀉was􀀉the􀀉very􀀉thing􀀉sharpening􀀉her􀀉
resolve.􀀉

Camilla􀀉spent􀀉her􀀉soot-stained􀀉afternoons􀀉as􀀉a􀀉witness􀀉at􀀉the􀀉edges􀀉of􀀉the􀀉
mine􀀉fields.􀀉She􀀉watched􀀉the􀀉older􀀉boys􀀉engage􀀉in􀀉desperate􀀉games,􀀉her􀀉tiny􀀉
frame􀀉a􀀉silhouette􀀉against􀀉the􀀉backdrop􀀉of􀀉miners􀀉in􀀉training.􀀉She􀀉carried􀀉
an􀀉iron-wood􀀉handle􀀉she’d􀀉salvaged􀀉from􀀉a􀀉broken􀀉coal-shifter􀀉—􀀉it􀀉felt􀀉
more􀀉honest􀀉than􀀉any􀀉doll,􀀉a􀀉weight􀀉she􀀉carried􀀉every􀀉day􀀉to􀀉prove􀀉she􀀉


could􀀉endure􀀉the􀀉same􀀉pressure􀀉as􀀉the􀀉men.􀀉She􀀉tagged􀀉along􀀉after􀀉Gage,􀀉
tracing􀀉his􀀉footsteps􀀉through􀀉oily􀀉silt,􀀉learning􀀉the􀀉terrain􀀉of􀀉his􀀉world􀀉even􀀉
as􀀉he􀀉barred􀀉her􀀉from􀀉it.􀀉

By􀀉twelve,􀀉Camilla’s􀀉schoolhouse􀀉was􀀉the􀀉junk-store.􀀉Pappy􀀉gave􀀉her􀀉chalk􀀉
and􀀉a􀀉single􀀉instruction:􀀉perform􀀉a􀀉Structural􀀉Inspection􀀉on􀀉the􀀉skeletal􀀉
remains􀀉of􀀉the􀀉old􀀉world.􀀉

“Write􀀉down􀀉what’s􀀉broken,􀀉Camilla,”􀀉Pappy􀀉would􀀉say.􀀉

She􀀉crawled􀀉through􀀉scrap􀀉heaps,􀀉breathing􀀉the􀀉acid􀀉tang􀀉of􀀉leaking􀀉
batteries􀀉and􀀉rusted􀀉iron,􀀉searching􀀉for􀀉seized􀀉pistons􀀉or􀀉snapped􀀉supports.􀀉
She􀀉marked􀀉broken􀀉things􀀉with􀀉a􀀉single􀀉scratched􀀉letter􀀉the􀀉way􀀉the􀀉older􀀉
miners􀀉marked􀀉Danger􀀉Signs􀀉—􀀉D􀀉for􀀉the􀀉deep􀀉that􀀉swallowed􀀉people,􀀉V􀀉
for􀀉the􀀉vein-paths􀀉of􀀉coal􀀉and􀀉blood􀀉beneath􀀉the􀀉surface,􀀉X􀀉for􀀉what􀀉would􀀉
kill􀀉you􀀉if􀀉you􀀉touched􀀉it.􀀉She􀀉learned􀀉the􀀉alphabet􀀉through􀀉these􀀉markings:􀀉
each􀀉letter􀀉a􀀉warning,􀀉each􀀉warning􀀉a􀀉survival􀀉tool.􀀉To􀀉Camilla,􀀉letters􀀉
weren’t􀀉just􀀉ink;􀀉they􀀉were􀀉the􀀉Survival􀀉Script􀀉of􀀉her􀀉life.􀀉

She􀀉grew􀀉into􀀉a􀀉tomboy􀀉with􀀉a􀀉sharp􀀉tongue􀀉and􀀉cleverness􀀉that􀀉could􀀉cut􀀉
through􀀉a􀀉conversation􀀉as􀀉easily􀀉as􀀉Gage􀀉cut􀀉through􀀉a􀀉coal􀀉seam.􀀉

The􀀉last􀀉thing􀀉Pappy􀀉gave􀀉her􀀉was􀀉the􀀉machete.􀀉He’d􀀉kept􀀉it􀀉hidden􀀉
beneath􀀉the􀀉floorboards􀀉alongside􀀉the􀀉Master􀀉Scripts􀀉—􀀉the􀀉salvaged􀀉
medical􀀉journals􀀉and􀀉engineering􀀉texts􀀉he’d􀀉hoarded􀀉against􀀉the􀀉day􀀉
someone􀀉in􀀉the􀀉family􀀉would􀀉be􀀉sharp􀀉enough􀀉to􀀉use􀀉them.􀀉The􀀉blade􀀉was􀀉
old-world􀀉steel,􀀉its􀀉edge􀀉still􀀉bearing􀀉a􀀉faint,􀀉stubborn􀀉smear􀀉of􀀉green􀀉—􀀉
dried􀀉sap􀀉from􀀉a􀀉time􀀉when􀀉Pappy􀀉had􀀉cleared􀀉brush􀀉under􀀉the􀀉canopy􀀉of􀀉
real􀀉forests,􀀉back􀀉before􀀉the􀀉Great􀀉Extraction􀀉turned􀀉every􀀉tree􀀉into􀀉a􀀉
memory.􀀉He􀀉pressed􀀉it􀀉into􀀉her􀀉hands􀀉the􀀉week􀀉before􀀉the􀀉Coalball􀀉game,􀀉
as􀀉if􀀉he􀀉already􀀉knew􀀉his􀀉time􀀉was􀀉running􀀉short.􀀉

After􀀉his􀀉death,􀀉she􀀉spent􀀉her􀀉years􀀉in􀀉constant􀀉preparation􀀉—􀀉practicing􀀉
with􀀉the􀀉machete􀀉in􀀉the􀀉shadows􀀉of􀀉the􀀉junk-store,􀀉running􀀉until􀀉her􀀉lungs􀀉
burned,􀀉timing􀀉her􀀉movements􀀉to􀀉the􀀉mechanical􀀉heartbeat􀀉of􀀉the􀀉


community􀀉overseer.􀀉She􀀉lived􀀉two􀀉lives:􀀉the􀀉caring􀀉daughter􀀉helping􀀉Rita􀀉
with􀀉heavy􀀉basins􀀉until􀀉midnight,􀀉and􀀉the􀀉watchful􀀉sister􀀉who􀀉understood􀀉
the􀀉breaking􀀉points􀀉of􀀉her􀀉world􀀉better􀀉than􀀉anyone.􀀉

The􀀉other􀀉girls􀀉in􀀉Miner􀀉Town􀀉had􀀉long􀀉since􀀉given􀀉up􀀉trying􀀉to􀀉recruit􀀉
Camilla􀀉into􀀉their􀀉world.􀀉They􀀉gathered􀀉each􀀉evening􀀉at􀀉the􀀉communal􀀉
pump􀀉—􀀉Mara,􀀉Jessa,􀀉the􀀉Voss􀀉sisters􀀉—􀀉to􀀉braid􀀉rags􀀉into􀀉headbands􀀉and􀀉
draw􀀉charcoal􀀉jewelry􀀉on􀀉each􀀉other’s􀀉wrists.􀀉It􀀉was􀀉their􀀉defiance:􀀉making􀀉
beauty􀀉from􀀉grit,􀀉insisting􀀉that􀀉color􀀉and􀀉adornment􀀉could􀀉survive􀀉even􀀉
here.􀀉Rita􀀉desperately􀀉wanted􀀉Camilla􀀉to􀀉join􀀉them.􀀉She’d􀀉press􀀉her􀀉
daughter’s􀀉hair􀀉flat􀀉with􀀉a􀀉heated􀀉tin􀀉and􀀉beg􀀉her􀀉to􀀉wear􀀉something􀀉other􀀉
than􀀉Thorton’s􀀉oversized􀀉work􀀉shirt.􀀉

“You’re􀀉going􀀉to􀀉get􀀉noticed,􀀉Camilla,”􀀉Rita􀀉would􀀉hiss,􀀉meaning􀀉the􀀉
wrong􀀉kind􀀉of􀀉noticed􀀉—􀀉the􀀉kind􀀉that􀀉drew􀀉the􀀉Enforcers’􀀉attention.􀀉
Trinity􀀉sent􀀉every􀀉kid􀀉into􀀉the􀀉chamber􀀉at􀀉seventeen,􀀉but􀀉the􀀉kids􀀉who􀀉
entered􀀉already􀀉flagged􀀉as􀀉troublemakers􀀉came􀀉out􀀉worse,􀀉or􀀉didn’t􀀉come􀀉
out􀀉at􀀉all.􀀉In􀀉Rita’s􀀉calculus,􀀉invisibility􀀉was􀀉the􀀉only􀀉armor􀀉a􀀉girl􀀉could􀀉
wear.􀀉Be􀀉small.􀀉Be􀀉quiet.􀀉Be􀀉like􀀉the􀀉others.􀀉

But􀀉Camilla􀀉had􀀉watched􀀉those􀀉girls􀀉her􀀉whole􀀉life􀀉and􀀉couldn’t􀀉make􀀉her􀀉
mind􀀉do􀀉what􀀉theirs􀀉did.􀀉It􀀉wasn’t􀀉that􀀉she􀀉didn’t􀀉see􀀉the􀀉beauty.􀀉She􀀉saw􀀉it.􀀉
She􀀉just􀀉couldn’t􀀉afford􀀉it.􀀉Her􀀉hands􀀉had􀀉been􀀉busy􀀉since􀀉she􀀉was􀀉small􀀉—􀀉
pulling􀀉salvage􀀉from􀀉the􀀉heaps,􀀉scratching􀀉letters􀀉into􀀉floorboards,􀀉learning􀀉
the􀀉weight􀀉of􀀉a􀀉machete.􀀉There􀀉were􀀉things􀀉she􀀉had􀀉to􀀉do􀀉that􀀉the􀀉other􀀉
girls􀀉didn’t􀀉yet􀀉know􀀉they􀀉would􀀉have􀀉to􀀉do.􀀉She􀀉loved􀀉them,􀀉in􀀉her􀀉quiet􀀉
way.􀀉She􀀉just􀀉couldn’t􀀉sit􀀉at􀀉the􀀉pump􀀉and􀀉braid􀀉rags􀀉while􀀉the􀀉algorithm􀀉
counted􀀉down􀀉to􀀉her􀀉seventeenth􀀉birthday.􀀉

Camilla􀀉didn’t􀀉resent􀀉them􀀉for􀀉it.􀀉She􀀉understood􀀉the􀀉comfort􀀉of􀀉ritual.􀀉
But􀀉she􀀉couldn’t􀀉make􀀉herself􀀉participate􀀉in􀀉something􀀉that􀀉felt,􀀉to􀀉her􀀉
precise􀀉and􀀉literal􀀉mind,􀀉like􀀉decorating􀀉a􀀉prison􀀉cell.􀀉If􀀉she􀀉was􀀉going􀀉to􀀉
spend􀀉her􀀉energy􀀉on􀀉anything,􀀉it􀀉would􀀉be􀀉on􀀉understanding􀀉the􀀉lock􀀉—􀀉
and􀀉breaking􀀉it.􀀉


She􀀉had􀀉one􀀉friend􀀉among􀀉them􀀉—􀀉Jessa,􀀉whose􀀉father􀀉had􀀉gone􀀉Hollow􀀉
three􀀉months􀀉prior.􀀉Jessa􀀉would􀀉sometimes􀀉bring􀀉Camilla􀀉a􀀉strip􀀉of􀀉cured􀀉
leather􀀉or􀀉a􀀉handful􀀉of􀀉salvaged􀀉screws,􀀉small􀀉offerings􀀉left􀀉on􀀉the􀀉doorstep􀀉
of􀀉the􀀉shack􀀉like􀀉gifts􀀉for􀀉a􀀉strange􀀉god.􀀉In􀀉return,􀀉Camilla􀀉once􀀉fixed􀀉Jessa’s􀀉
family’s􀀉water􀀉filter􀀉—􀀉a􀀉three-hour􀀉job􀀉that􀀉involved􀀉stripping􀀉the􀀉mesh,􀀉
scouring􀀉it􀀉with􀀉sand,􀀉and􀀉re-seating􀀉it􀀉with􀀉scavenged􀀉wire.􀀉She􀀉did􀀉it􀀉
without􀀉being􀀉asked􀀉and􀀉without􀀉mentioning􀀉it􀀉afterward.􀀉That􀀉was􀀉the􀀉
closest􀀉Camilla􀀉came􀀉to􀀉the􀀉language􀀉of􀀉the􀀉pump-girls:􀀉service􀀉without􀀉
words,􀀉care􀀉expressed􀀉through􀀉the􀀉hands.􀀉

The􀀉orange􀀉haze􀀉thickened􀀉over􀀉the􀀉slag􀀉fields,􀀉turning􀀉the􀀉horizon􀀉into􀀉a􀀉
distorted􀀉line􀀉that􀀉wavered􀀉with􀀉the􀀉day’s􀀉last􀀉heat.􀀉Gage􀀉and􀀉Camilla􀀉sat􀀉
together􀀉on􀀉a􀀉ridge􀀉of􀀉compressed􀀉silt􀀉at􀀉the􀀉edge􀀉of􀀉the􀀉salvage􀀉road.􀀉The􀀉
day􀀉before􀀉his􀀉seventeenth􀀉birthday􀀉—􀀉the􀀉day􀀉before􀀉the􀀉gates􀀉would􀀉open􀀉
and􀀉take􀀉him􀀉—􀀉and􀀉the􀀉two􀀉of􀀉them􀀉were􀀉doing􀀉what􀀉they􀀉had􀀉been􀀉doing􀀉
for􀀉years:􀀉sitting􀀉at􀀉the􀀉edge􀀉of􀀉things,􀀉watching􀀉the􀀉world􀀉move􀀉past􀀉them.􀀉

“Look􀀉at􀀉the􀀉silver􀀉needles,􀀉Gage,”􀀉she􀀉whispered,􀀉pointing􀀉toward􀀉the􀀉
Trinity􀀉Cities.􀀉The􀀉silver􀀉caught􀀉the􀀉dying􀀉light,􀀉shimmering􀀉with􀀉cold,􀀉
purple-lit􀀉malice.􀀉“Most􀀉people􀀉see􀀉symbols􀀉of􀀉luxury􀀉when􀀉they􀀉look􀀉at􀀉
them.􀀉I􀀉see􀀉a􀀉machine.”􀀉

“Pappy􀀉didn’t􀀉give􀀉me􀀉poetry.􀀉He􀀉gave􀀉me􀀉chalk􀀉and􀀉a􀀉pile􀀉of􀀉ruins.􀀉He􀀉
taught􀀉me􀀉that􀀉if􀀉you􀀉can’t􀀉read􀀉the􀀉signs,􀀉you􀀉don’t􀀉know􀀉where􀀉the􀀉fire􀀉is􀀉
coming􀀉from.”􀀉She􀀉traced􀀉the􀀉outline􀀉of􀀉the􀀉Mid-Spires.􀀉“I􀀉look􀀉at􀀉those􀀉
lavender-lit􀀉towers􀀉and􀀉I􀀉don’t􀀉see􀀉a􀀉home.􀀉I􀀉see􀀉a􀀉mechanism.􀀉And􀀉every􀀉
machine􀀉has􀀉a􀀉flaw.􀀉I’ve􀀉been􀀉looking􀀉for􀀉the􀀉breaking􀀉point􀀉my􀀉entire􀀉life.”􀀉

Gage􀀉tightened􀀉his􀀉scarred􀀉fingers􀀉around􀀉the􀀉wrench,􀀉the􀀉identification􀀉tag􀀉
fluttering􀀉like􀀉a􀀉tattered􀀉flag.􀀉Beside􀀉him,􀀉Camilla’s􀀉fingers􀀉traced􀀉the􀀉pitted􀀉
hilt􀀉of􀀉Pappy’s􀀉machete􀀉through􀀉the􀀉fabric􀀉of􀀉her􀀉bag.􀀉She􀀉didn’t􀀉pull􀀉it􀀉out􀀉
yet,􀀉but􀀉the􀀉mere􀀉touch􀀉of􀀉the􀀉cold􀀉steel􀀉grounded􀀉her􀀉—􀀉the􀀉green-stained􀀉
edge􀀉a􀀉reminder􀀉that􀀉the􀀉earth􀀉had􀀉once􀀉bled􀀉something􀀉other􀀉than􀀉oil.􀀉


In􀀉that􀀉shared􀀉silence,􀀉the􀀉bond􀀉finally􀀉set.􀀉They􀀉were􀀉no􀀉longer􀀉a􀀉boy􀀉and􀀉a􀀉
little􀀉bird;􀀉they􀀉were􀀉the􀀉Brawn􀀉and􀀉the􀀉Sight,􀀉two􀀉parts􀀉of􀀉a􀀉single􀀉
structure􀀉finally􀀉idling􀀉in􀀉sync.􀀉

From􀀉below,􀀉a􀀉voice􀀉carried􀀉up􀀉from􀀉the􀀉pumps,􀀉sharp􀀉and􀀉hopeful.􀀉
“Camilla!􀀉The􀀉pump’s􀀉behaving􀀉today!􀀉Bring􀀉your􀀉mother’s􀀉basins􀀉before􀀉
the􀀉Enforcers􀀉shut􀀉the􀀉valve!”􀀉It􀀉was􀀉Jessa.􀀉

Gage􀀉turned􀀉his􀀉head,􀀉eyes􀀉tracking􀀉the􀀉human􀀉chain􀀉of􀀉girls􀀉passing􀀉
buckets􀀉below.􀀉“Are􀀉you􀀉going􀀉down?”􀀉he􀀉asked.􀀉

Camilla􀀉touched􀀉the􀀉hilt􀀉of􀀉the􀀉machete􀀉hidden􀀉in􀀉her􀀉bag.􀀉The􀀉decision􀀉
was􀀉instantaneous.􀀉“Ma!”􀀉she􀀉called􀀉down􀀉toward􀀉the􀀉shack.􀀉“Jessa􀀉says􀀉the􀀉
pump’s􀀉running.􀀉Take􀀉the􀀉basins􀀉down􀀉before􀀉the􀀉valve􀀉shuts.”􀀉Then􀀉to􀀉
Gage,􀀉her􀀉voice􀀉hardening:􀀉“I’m􀀉staying􀀉here.”􀀉

An􀀉overseer􀀉drone􀀉crested􀀉the􀀉ridge,􀀉sensors􀀉pulsing􀀉with􀀉purple􀀉light.􀀉

“Unauthorized􀀉gathering􀀉detected.”􀀉A􀀉red􀀉beam􀀉swept􀀉across􀀉their􀀉faces.􀀉
“Two􀀉pre-Initiation􀀉minors􀀉registered􀀉to􀀉Miner􀀉Town.􀀉Subject􀀉one:􀀉
scheduled􀀉intake􀀉oh-six-hundred􀀉tomorrow.􀀉Subject􀀉two:􀀉scheduled􀀉intake􀀉
in􀀉two􀀉annual􀀉cycles.􀀉Remain􀀉still􀀉for􀀉inspection.”􀀉

The􀀉drone􀀉had􀀉called􀀉for􀀉inspection.􀀉If􀀉the􀀉Urban􀀉Servants􀀉arrived􀀉before􀀉
they􀀉could􀀉move,􀀉the􀀉machete􀀉in􀀉Camilla’s􀀉bag􀀉would􀀉be􀀉found.􀀉She’d􀀉be􀀉
flagged􀀉for􀀉disposal.􀀉

Gage􀀉stood.􀀉“Stay􀀉behind􀀉me,􀀉Camilla.”􀀉

“No,”􀀉Camilla􀀉countered,􀀉drawing􀀉the􀀉machete.􀀉“It’s􀀉an􀀉older􀀉model.􀀉Its􀀉
eye􀀉lags􀀉to􀀉the􀀉left􀀉every􀀉time􀀉it􀀉pulses.􀀉When􀀉the􀀉light􀀉turns􀀉red,􀀉the􀀉main􀀉
joint􀀉is􀀉exposed.”􀀉

The􀀉drone’s􀀉light􀀉flared􀀉from􀀉purple􀀉to􀀉violent􀀉red.􀀉

“Now!”􀀉


Gage􀀉lunged,􀀉striking􀀉the􀀉drone’s􀀉lower􀀉chassis􀀉with􀀉the􀀉wrench.􀀉The􀀉
impact􀀉forced􀀉the􀀉eye􀀉to􀀉lag.􀀉Before􀀉it􀀉could􀀉recalibrate,􀀉Camilla􀀉was􀀉already􀀉
moving􀀉—􀀉her􀀉small􀀉frame􀀉a􀀉blur􀀉of􀀉precision.􀀉She􀀉used􀀉the􀀉machete􀀉edge􀀉
to􀀉sever􀀉the􀀉main􀀉lines􀀉she’d􀀉identified􀀉through􀀉inspection.􀀉The􀀉blade,􀀉
bearing􀀉the􀀉stubborn􀀉green􀀉smear􀀉of􀀉a􀀉dead􀀉world,􀀉sliced􀀉through􀀉synthetic􀀉
guts.􀀉

The􀀉drone’s􀀉hum􀀉died􀀉to􀀉a􀀉pathetic􀀉rattle.􀀉It􀀉sparked􀀉and􀀉tumbled􀀉down􀀉
the􀀉coal􀀉mound􀀉into􀀉the􀀉dark.􀀉

Camilla􀀉watched􀀉the􀀉silver􀀉shell􀀉go􀀉still.􀀉“I􀀉cut􀀉the􀀉transmission􀀉line􀀉first,”􀀉
she􀀉said.􀀉“Pappy􀀉taught􀀉me􀀉how􀀉these􀀉older􀀉models􀀉work.􀀉The􀀉facial-rec􀀉
runs􀀉onboard􀀉—􀀉the􀀉drone􀀉recognizes􀀉you􀀉locally􀀉and􀀉stores􀀉the􀀉package,􀀉
but􀀉the􀀉upload􀀉to􀀉Jung􀀉only􀀉happens􀀉when􀀉the􀀉inspection􀀉completes.􀀉The􀀉
call􀀉didn’t􀀉finish.􀀉Trinity􀀉has􀀉nothing􀀉but􀀉a􀀉dropped􀀉signal.”􀀉

Camilla􀀉was􀀉already􀀉sliding􀀉down􀀉the􀀉coal􀀉mound􀀉after􀀉it.􀀉Gage􀀉hissed􀀉her􀀉
name,􀀉but􀀉she􀀉was􀀉gone􀀉—􀀉disappearing􀀉into􀀉the􀀉dark􀀉after􀀉the􀀉fallen􀀉
machine􀀉like􀀉a􀀉scavenger􀀉after􀀉a􀀉carcass.􀀉She􀀉came􀀉back􀀉two􀀉minutes􀀉later􀀉
with􀀉her􀀉pockets􀀉bulging:􀀉the􀀉drone’s􀀉sensor􀀉lens,􀀉a􀀉spool􀀉of􀀉copper􀀉wiring,􀀉
and􀀉a􀀉gyroscopic􀀉motor􀀉no􀀉bigger􀀉than􀀉her􀀉fist.􀀉“The􀀉rest􀀉is􀀉junk,”􀀉she􀀉said,􀀉
breathing􀀉hard.􀀉“I􀀉pushed􀀉slag􀀉over􀀉the􀀉shell.􀀉In􀀉this􀀉interference􀀉zone,􀀉
Jung’s􀀉logs􀀉will􀀉show􀀉a􀀉malfunction􀀉—􀀉drones􀀉fail􀀉near􀀉the􀀉ore􀀉deposits􀀉all􀀉
the􀀉time.􀀉But􀀉we􀀉need􀀉to􀀉move.􀀉If􀀉a􀀉retrieval􀀉team􀀉comes􀀉looking,􀀉I’d􀀉rather􀀉
not􀀉be􀀉standing􀀉where􀀉the􀀉signal􀀉dropped.”􀀉

Gage􀀉looked􀀉at􀀉the􀀉fallen􀀉machine,􀀉then􀀉back􀀉at􀀉Camilla.􀀉The􀀉wall􀀉between􀀉
them􀀉hadn’t􀀉just􀀉cracked;􀀉it􀀉had􀀉been􀀉dismantled.􀀉

“You􀀉timed􀀉the􀀉pulse,”􀀉Gage􀀉said.􀀉

“Letters􀀉are􀀉just􀀉ink,􀀉Gage,”􀀉Camilla􀀉replied,􀀉wiping􀀉grease􀀉from􀀉her􀀉blade.􀀉
“But􀀉the􀀉signs􀀉tell􀀉you􀀉where􀀉the􀀉system􀀉breaks.􀀉And􀀉tonight?􀀉We􀀉just􀀉
made􀀉it􀀉break.”􀀉


Chapter􀀉8􀀉—􀀉The􀀉Hacksaw􀀉

It􀀉had􀀉been􀀉more􀀉than􀀉a􀀉month􀀉since􀀉Gage,􀀉Ben,􀀉and􀀉Otto􀀉started􀀉in􀀉
Sector-1.􀀉Every􀀉day􀀉was􀀉a􀀉battle􀀉against􀀉deafening􀀉drills􀀉and􀀉suffocating􀀉coal􀀉
dust.􀀉At􀀉times,􀀉they􀀉wished􀀉they’d􀀉succumbed􀀉during􀀉the􀀉Initiation􀀉rather􀀉
than􀀉facing􀀉this􀀉slow􀀉erosion.􀀉

The􀀉rhythm􀀉of􀀉a􀀉day􀀉in􀀉the􀀉mine􀀉was􀀉this:􀀉wake􀀉in􀀉darkness,􀀉eat􀀉the􀀉Ration􀀉
Brick􀀉in􀀉darkness,􀀉walk􀀉to􀀉the􀀉gate􀀉while􀀉the􀀉dawn􀀉was􀀉already􀀉burning,􀀉
descend􀀉into􀀉a􀀉different􀀉kind􀀉of􀀉darkness.􀀉The􀀉shafts􀀉were􀀉lit􀀉by􀀉caged􀀉bulbs􀀉
strung􀀉along􀀉fraying􀀉wire􀀉—􀀉each􀀉one􀀉casting􀀉a􀀉yellow􀀉pool􀀉that􀀉reached􀀉
perhaps􀀉ten􀀉feet􀀉before􀀉dissolving􀀉into􀀉black.􀀉Between􀀉pools,􀀉you􀀉worked􀀉
by􀀉touch􀀉and􀀉sound.􀀉You􀀉learned􀀉the􀀉difference􀀉between􀀉the􀀉crunch􀀉of􀀉
stable􀀉rock􀀉and􀀉the􀀉whisper􀀉of􀀉stone􀀉about􀀉to􀀉give.􀀉You􀀉learned􀀉that􀀉a􀀉
change􀀉in􀀉air􀀉pressure􀀉—􀀉a􀀉sudden􀀉pop􀀉in􀀉your􀀉ears􀀉—􀀉meant􀀉a􀀉collapse􀀉was􀀉
building􀀉somewhere􀀉in􀀉the􀀉honeycomb,􀀉and􀀉you􀀉had􀀉maybe􀀉ninety􀀉seconds􀀉
to􀀉figure􀀉out􀀉which􀀉direction􀀉to􀀉run.􀀉

The􀀉noise􀀉was􀀉the􀀉worst􀀉of􀀉it.􀀉Not􀀉a􀀉single􀀉noise􀀉but􀀉a􀀉layered􀀉assault:􀀉the􀀉
shriek􀀉of􀀉drill􀀉bits􀀉biting􀀉anthracite,􀀉the􀀉pneumatic􀀉stutter􀀉of􀀉the􀀉ore-lifts,􀀉
the􀀉percussive􀀉boom􀀉of􀀉blasting􀀉charges􀀉in􀀉distant􀀉shafts,􀀉and􀀉beneath􀀉it􀀉all,􀀉
always,􀀉the􀀉deep􀀉geological􀀉hum􀀉of􀀉the􀀉Stubborn􀀉Carbon􀀉—􀀉that􀀉
piezoelectric􀀉vibration􀀉that􀀉lived􀀉in􀀉your􀀉marrow􀀉and􀀉your􀀉back􀀉teeth􀀉and􀀉
the􀀉bridge􀀉of􀀉your􀀉nose.􀀉After􀀉a􀀉twelve-hour􀀉shift,􀀉the􀀉silence􀀉of􀀉the􀀉surface􀀉
felt􀀉almost􀀉painful,􀀉like􀀉the􀀉sudden􀀉absence􀀉of􀀉a􀀉hand􀀉that􀀉had􀀉been􀀉
pressing􀀉on􀀉your􀀉chest.􀀉

Gage􀀉would􀀉stand􀀉in􀀉a􀀉corner􀀉during􀀉the􀀉brief􀀉meal􀀉breaks,􀀉watching􀀉other􀀉
miners􀀉toil.􀀉Their􀀉eyes􀀉held􀀉no􀀉hope;􀀉they􀀉were􀀉simply􀀉content􀀉being􀀉
allowed􀀉to􀀉breathe.􀀉He􀀉tried􀀉to􀀉see􀀉them􀀉the􀀉way􀀉Camilla􀀉would􀀉—􀀉not􀀉as􀀉
broken􀀉men􀀉but􀀉as􀀉evidence􀀉of􀀉how􀀉the􀀉machine􀀉operated􀀉—􀀉each􀀉one􀀉
reverse-engineered.􀀉Where􀀉had􀀉each􀀉one􀀉cracked?􀀉What􀀉had􀀉been􀀉the􀀉load􀀉


that􀀉tipped􀀉them􀀉from􀀉person􀀉to􀀉Hollow?􀀉Was􀀉it􀀉the􀀉hunger,􀀉the􀀉hum,􀀉the􀀉
lash,􀀉or􀀉something􀀉quieter􀀉—􀀉a􀀉slow􀀉leaking􀀉of􀀉self􀀉that􀀉happened􀀉so􀀉
gradually􀀉they􀀉didn’t􀀉notice􀀉until􀀉the􀀉container􀀉was􀀉empty?􀀉

That’s􀀈going􀀈to􀀈be􀀈me,􀀉he􀀉thought,􀀉watching􀀉a􀀉man􀀉named􀀉Dorsey􀀉eat􀀉his􀀉
starch􀀉cube􀀉with􀀉the􀀉mechanical􀀉precision􀀉of􀀉a􀀉machine􀀉being􀀉refueled.􀀉
Dorsey􀀉had􀀉been􀀉a􀀉talker􀀉once􀀉—􀀉a􀀉man􀀉who􀀉told􀀉jokes􀀉in􀀉the􀀉queue􀀉and􀀉
whistled􀀉between􀀉hammer-strikes.􀀉Now􀀉he􀀉was􀀉silent,􀀉his􀀉eyes􀀉fixed􀀉on􀀉a􀀉
point􀀉approximately􀀉six􀀉inches􀀉in􀀉front􀀉of􀀉his􀀉face,􀀉seeing􀀉nothing.􀀉That’s􀀈
where􀀈this􀀈ends,􀀈if􀀈I􀀈let􀀈it.􀀈Not􀀈with􀀈a􀀈bang.􀀈With􀀈a􀀈slow􀀈fade􀀈to􀀈grey.􀀈

One􀀉morning,􀀉a􀀉miner􀀉named􀀉Griggs􀀉collapsed􀀉at􀀉his􀀉station􀀉—􀀉just􀀉folded􀀉
forward􀀉into􀀉the􀀉sludge􀀉without􀀉a􀀉sound,􀀉his􀀉shovel􀀉still􀀉in􀀉his􀀉hand.􀀉Gage􀀉
started􀀉toward􀀉him,􀀉but􀀉a􀀉hand􀀉clamped􀀉his􀀉shoulder.􀀉“Don’t,”􀀉the􀀉miner􀀉
behind􀀉him􀀉whispered.􀀉“Watch.”􀀉

Within􀀉minutes,􀀉a􀀉thin􀀉man􀀉in􀀉a􀀉grey􀀉smock􀀉appeared􀀉from􀀉a􀀉side􀀉tunnel,􀀉
clipboard􀀉in􀀉hand.􀀉He􀀉didn’t􀀉check􀀉Griggs􀀉for􀀉a􀀉pulse.􀀉He􀀉checked􀀉his􀀉
badge􀀉number,􀀉scribbled􀀉something􀀉on􀀉the􀀉clipboard,􀀉and􀀉made􀀉a􀀉short,􀀉
sharp􀀉whistle.􀀉Two􀀉men􀀉with􀀉a􀀉stretcher􀀉emerged􀀉from􀀉the􀀉same􀀉tunnel,􀀉
loaded􀀉Griggs􀀉like􀀉a􀀉sack􀀉of􀀉spent􀀉coal,􀀉and􀀉disappeared􀀉back􀀉into􀀉the􀀉dark.􀀉
The􀀉whole􀀉process􀀉took􀀉less􀀉than􀀉ninety􀀉seconds.􀀉No􀀉one􀀉spoke.􀀉No􀀉one􀀉
stopped􀀉working.􀀉

“That’s􀀉Lou,”􀀉the􀀉miner􀀉whispered.􀀉“The􀀉Undertaker.􀀉He􀀉manages􀀉the􀀉
waste􀀉—􀀉coal􀀉and􀀉human.􀀉To􀀉him,􀀉Griggs􀀉isn’t􀀉a􀀉man􀀉who􀀉just􀀉died.􀀉He’s􀀉a􀀉
line􀀉of􀀉inventory􀀉that􀀉needs􀀉clearing􀀉before􀀉the􀀉next􀀉shift.”􀀉

Gage􀀉watched􀀉the􀀉tunnel􀀉mouth􀀉where􀀉Griggs􀀉had􀀉vanished.􀀉No􀀉ceremony.􀀉
No􀀉pause.􀀉Just􀀉a􀀉clipboard􀀉and􀀉a􀀉whistle􀀉and􀀉ninety􀀉seconds􀀉of􀀉efficiency.􀀉
He􀀉thought􀀉of􀀉Thorton􀀉being􀀉hauled􀀉away􀀉like􀀉scrap􀀉metal􀀉—􀀉and􀀉realized􀀉
this􀀉was􀀉the􀀉exact􀀉same􀀉process.􀀉The􀀉system􀀉hadn’t􀀉needed􀀉to􀀉change􀀉the􀀉
procedure.􀀉It􀀉already􀀉worked􀀉perfectly.􀀉


As􀀉he􀀉stared􀀉at􀀉the􀀉dark􀀉walls,􀀉he􀀉remembered􀀉Pappy’s􀀉stories.􀀉Long􀀉ago,􀀉
these􀀉weren’t􀀉pits;􀀉they􀀉were􀀉neighborhoods.􀀉Entire􀀉cities􀀉had􀀉been􀀉razed􀀉to􀀉
