bedrock􀀉—􀀉every􀀉playground,􀀉every􀀉home,􀀉leveled􀀉and􀀉converted􀀉into􀀉coal􀀉
mines.􀀉Gage􀀉wasn’t􀀉just􀀉mining􀀉fuel;􀀉he􀀉was􀀉strip-mining􀀉the􀀉fossilized􀀉
remains􀀉of􀀉a􀀉civilization􀀉that􀀉had􀀉forgotten􀀉how􀀉to􀀉breathe.􀀉

Sometimes,􀀉in􀀉the􀀉deepest􀀉shafts,􀀉the􀀉coal􀀉would􀀉break􀀉open􀀉and􀀉release􀀉a􀀉
smell􀀉that􀀉didn’t􀀉belong􀀉underground􀀉—􀀉a􀀉sharp,􀀉synthetic􀀉tang,􀀉like􀀉
heated􀀉plastic.􀀉Ben􀀉said􀀉it􀀉was􀀉the􀀉old-world􀀉polymers,􀀉still􀀉off-gassing􀀉after􀀉
a􀀉century􀀉of􀀉compression.􀀉To􀀉Gage,􀀉it􀀉smelled􀀉like􀀉a􀀉warning.􀀉Like􀀉the􀀉
ghosts􀀉of􀀉the􀀉old􀀉world􀀉trying􀀉to􀀉tell􀀉him􀀉something.􀀉We􀀈made􀀈this.􀀈We􀀈
made􀀈the􀀈thing􀀈that􀀈ate􀀈us.􀀈And􀀈you’re􀀈standing􀀈inside􀀈its􀀈stomach.􀀈

While􀀉workers􀀉lived􀀉like􀀉zombies,􀀉Icepick’s􀀉goons􀀉enjoyed􀀉a􀀉rewarding􀀉life􀀉
within􀀉the􀀉black􀀉walls􀀉—􀀉all􀀉the􀀉food􀀉and􀀉booze􀀉they􀀉wanted􀀉in􀀉exchange􀀉
for􀀉keeping􀀉slaves􀀉in􀀉line􀀉with􀀉leather􀀉lashes.􀀉At􀀉the􀀉center􀀉of􀀉this􀀉cruelty􀀉
was􀀉Andre􀀉Lockhart,􀀉known􀀉as􀀉the􀀉Hacksaw.􀀉

The􀀉sound􀀉arrived􀀉before􀀉the􀀉man:􀀉a􀀉heavy,􀀉mechanical􀀉hitch􀀉echoing􀀉
against􀀉dust-coated􀀉stone.􀀉Clack-thud.􀀉Clack-thud.􀀉The􀀉rhythm􀀉of􀀉Andre’s􀀉
steel-braced􀀉knee􀀉—􀀉a􀀉crude􀀉surgical􀀉pin􀀉and􀀉bolted􀀉housing􀀉the􀀉Trinity􀀉
surgeons􀀉had􀀉given􀀉him􀀉after􀀉a􀀉bullet􀀉shattered􀀉the􀀉joint.􀀉How􀀉that􀀉bullet􀀉
had􀀉found􀀉him􀀉was􀀉a􀀉story􀀉for􀀉later.􀀉They’d􀀉kept􀀉him􀀉walking􀀉but􀀉not􀀉
walking􀀉right;􀀉every􀀉step􀀉drove􀀉metal􀀉against􀀉bone,􀀉producing􀀉a􀀉sound􀀉that􀀉
was􀀉neither􀀉fully􀀉human􀀉nor􀀉fully􀀉machine.􀀉The􀀉pain􀀉was􀀉constant.􀀉Andre􀀉
had􀀉outlived􀀉it.􀀉That􀀉was􀀉what􀀉made􀀉him􀀉terrifying􀀉—􀀉not􀀉the􀀉pin,􀀉not􀀉the􀀉
rhythm,􀀉but􀀉the􀀉fact􀀉that􀀉nothing􀀉the􀀉body􀀉could􀀉feel􀀉had􀀉been􀀉able􀀉to􀀉slow􀀉
him􀀉down.􀀉Every􀀉miner􀀉in􀀉the􀀉sector􀀉knew􀀉that􀀉rhythm.􀀉When􀀉the􀀉clack-
thud􀀉entered􀀉a􀀉tunnel,􀀉pick-rates􀀉doubled􀀉—􀀉not􀀉because􀀉of􀀉any􀀉order,􀀉but􀀉
because􀀉every􀀉body􀀉in􀀉earshot􀀉remembered􀀉what􀀉happened􀀉to􀀉the􀀉ones􀀉
who􀀉slowed􀀉down.􀀉Trinity􀀉didn’t􀀉have􀀉to􀀉order􀀉miners􀀉to􀀉speed􀀉up.􀀉Andre’s􀀉
presence􀀉did􀀉the􀀉ordering􀀉for􀀉them.􀀉The􀀉cameras􀀉simply􀀉tracked􀀉the􀀉
productivity􀀉spike􀀉that􀀉followed􀀉his􀀉patrol,􀀉and􀀉when􀀉the􀀉spike􀀉didn’t􀀉


come,􀀉they􀀉flagged􀀉the􀀉section􀀉for􀀉review.􀀉Hacksaw􀀉was􀀉the􀀉physical􀀉
heartbeat􀀉of􀀉the􀀉mine􀀉—􀀉not􀀉by􀀉design,􀀉but􀀉by􀀉terror.􀀉

He􀀉did􀀉not􀀉look􀀉like􀀉a􀀉monster􀀉—􀀉an􀀉average-looking􀀉man􀀉—􀀉but􀀉his􀀉
presence􀀉made􀀉the􀀉strongest􀀉miners􀀉tremble.􀀉He’d􀀉once􀀉been􀀉elite:􀀉a􀀉top􀀉
graduate􀀉of􀀉Trinity-1’s􀀉enforcement􀀉academy􀀉—􀀉first􀀉in􀀉the􀀉physical􀀉trials􀀉
and􀀉fast-tracked􀀉to􀀉officer.􀀉His􀀉father,􀀉Rodney,􀀉had􀀉been􀀉his􀀉partner􀀉on􀀉
patrol,􀀉a􀀉man􀀉Andre􀀉looked􀀉up􀀉to􀀉with􀀉immense􀀉pride.􀀉

Andre’s􀀉descent􀀉into􀀉cruelty􀀉began􀀉on􀀉his􀀉birthday.􀀉Rodney􀀉had􀀉taken􀀉
Andre’s􀀉night􀀉patrol􀀉so􀀉his􀀉son􀀉could􀀉celebrate.􀀉During􀀉the􀀉shift,􀀉a􀀉massive􀀉
thermal􀀉turbine􀀉began􀀉to􀀉red-line.􀀉The􀀉remote􀀉cooling􀀉system􀀉had􀀉seized􀀉
because􀀉a􀀉safety􀀉pin􀀉jammed􀀉in􀀉the􀀉high-pressure􀀉housing.􀀉

The􀀉turbine􀀉was􀀉seconds􀀉from􀀉a􀀉meltdown􀀉that􀀉would􀀉have􀀉claimed􀀉
thousands􀀉of􀀉lives.􀀉Rodney􀀉was􀀉on􀀉patrol􀀉nearby􀀉when􀀉the􀀉alarm􀀉hit􀀉the􀀉
facility􀀉frequencies.􀀉He􀀉floored􀀉the􀀉patrol􀀉car􀀉and􀀉was􀀉the􀀉first􀀉unit􀀉on􀀉
scene.􀀉He􀀉was􀀉an􀀉enforcement􀀉officer,􀀉not􀀉an􀀉engineer􀀉—􀀉pressure􀀉systems􀀉
and􀀉safety􀀉pins􀀉were􀀉not􀀉his􀀉specialization.􀀉But􀀉he􀀉could􀀉read􀀉the􀀉screaming􀀉
pitch􀀉of􀀉a􀀉turbine􀀉in􀀉failure,􀀉and􀀉he􀀉could􀀉read􀀉the􀀉corridor􀀉distances.􀀉The􀀉
technicians􀀉who􀀉could􀀉have􀀉fixed􀀉it􀀉were􀀉too􀀉far􀀉away􀀉to􀀉reach􀀉the􀀉assembly􀀉
in􀀉time.􀀉

Rodney􀀉didn’t􀀉wait􀀉for􀀉orders.􀀉He􀀉didn’t􀀉wait􀀉for􀀉the􀀉technicians.􀀉He􀀉
pulled􀀉off􀀉his􀀉patrol􀀉vest,􀀉dropped􀀉to􀀉his􀀉knees,􀀉and􀀉crawled􀀉into􀀉the􀀉narrow􀀉
gear􀀉assembly􀀉to􀀉manually􀀉wrench􀀉the􀀉pin􀀉free.􀀉

The􀀉technicians􀀉watched􀀉their􀀉monitors􀀉with􀀉white􀀉knuckles.􀀉The􀀉
moment􀀉Rodney􀀉freed􀀉the􀀉pin,􀀉a􀀉green􀀉light􀀉flickered:􀀉SYSTEM􀀉READY.􀀉
Standard􀀉protocol􀀉required􀀉a􀀉Lock-Out􀀉until􀀉the􀀉technician􀀉signaled􀀉clear.􀀉
But􀀉the􀀉sector􀀉commanders􀀉saw􀀉the􀀉Ready􀀉light􀀉and􀀉remotely􀀉engaged􀀉the􀀉
engine.􀀉They􀀉chose􀀉the􀀉grid.􀀉They􀀉didn’t􀀉know􀀉his􀀉name.􀀉They􀀉didn’t􀀉need􀀉
to.􀀉They􀀉knew􀀉the􀀉pin􀀉had􀀉been􀀉freed􀀉by􀀉hand􀀉and􀀉that􀀉someone􀀉was􀀉
almost􀀉certainly􀀉still􀀉in􀀉the􀀉housing􀀉—􀀉and􀀉they􀀉prioritized􀀉the􀀉power􀀉


supply􀀉to􀀉the􀀉elite􀀉districts􀀉over􀀉the􀀉thirty􀀉seconds􀀉it􀀉would􀀉have􀀉taken􀀉to􀀉
confirm.􀀉

The􀀉gears􀀉surged􀀉to􀀉life.􀀉Andre’s􀀉father􀀉was􀀉consumed􀀉by􀀉the􀀉machine􀀉he’d􀀉
just􀀉saved􀀉—􀀉sacrificed􀀉to􀀉ensure􀀉the􀀉Trinity􀀉elite􀀉didn’t􀀉endure􀀉a􀀉single􀀉
minute􀀉of􀀉darkness.􀀉

Andre􀀉tried􀀉to􀀉call􀀉his􀀉father’s􀀉patrol􀀉car􀀉twice􀀉that􀀉night.􀀉The􀀉first􀀉call􀀉went􀀉
unanswered.􀀉The􀀉second􀀉went􀀉unanswered.􀀉By􀀉the􀀉third,􀀉the􀀉dispatch􀀉
operator’s􀀉voice􀀉came􀀉on􀀉instead,􀀉and􀀉Andre􀀉knew􀀉before􀀉she􀀉said􀀉
anything.􀀉

The􀀉official􀀉report􀀉buried􀀉the􀀉rest􀀉—􀀉officer􀀉killed􀀉in􀀉turbine􀀉accident,􀀉valor􀀉
commendation􀀉pending.􀀉But􀀉Andre,􀀉mad􀀉with􀀉grief,􀀉found􀀉a􀀉technician􀀉
whose􀀉conscience􀀉was􀀉bigger􀀉than􀀉his􀀉orders.􀀉He􀀉learned􀀉about􀀉the􀀉Ready-
light,􀀉the􀀉thirty􀀉seconds,􀀉the􀀉commanders􀀉who􀀉didn’t􀀉wait.􀀉From􀀉there,􀀉he􀀉
traced􀀉the􀀉chain􀀉of􀀉command􀀉himself.􀀉It􀀉took􀀉him􀀉a􀀉week􀀉—􀀉not􀀉because􀀉
the􀀉information􀀉was􀀉hard􀀉to􀀉find,􀀉but􀀉because􀀉Andre􀀉was􀀉thorough.􀀉He􀀉
learned􀀉every􀀉name􀀉that􀀉had􀀉been􀀉in􀀉the􀀉control􀀉room􀀉—􀀉and􀀉one􀀉name􀀉
above􀀉all:􀀉the􀀉sector􀀉commander􀀉who􀀉had􀀉given􀀉the􀀉order􀀉to􀀉engage􀀉the􀀉
engine,􀀉over􀀉the􀀉objections􀀉of􀀉his􀀉own􀀉subordinates􀀉begging􀀉him􀀉to􀀉wait.􀀉
He􀀉learned􀀉the􀀉building’s􀀉layout.􀀉And􀀉on􀀉the􀀉seventh􀀉day,􀀉he􀀉walked􀀉into􀀉
Trinity􀀉headquarters􀀉carrying􀀉a􀀉maintenance􀀉hacksaw􀀉he’d􀀉taken􀀉from􀀉the􀀉
turbine􀀉floor􀀉where􀀉his􀀉father􀀉died.􀀉He􀀉found􀀉the􀀉commander􀀉in􀀉his􀀉office.􀀉
He􀀉used􀀉the􀀉hacksaw􀀉to􀀉disassemble􀀉the􀀉man􀀉who’d􀀉treated􀀉Rodney􀀉like􀀉
scrap􀀉metal.􀀉He􀀉didn’t􀀉just􀀉kill􀀉him;􀀉he􀀉removed􀀉the􀀉friction􀀉the􀀉man􀀉
represented.􀀉During􀀉the􀀉struggle􀀉with􀀉the􀀉guards􀀉who􀀉came􀀉running,􀀉a􀀉
bullet􀀉shattered􀀉his􀀉knee.􀀉The􀀉Trinity􀀉surgeons􀀉pinned􀀉what􀀉was􀀉left􀀉of􀀉the􀀉
joint􀀉together􀀉with􀀉steel􀀉and􀀉bolts􀀉—􀀉enough􀀉to􀀉keep􀀉him􀀉walking,􀀉but􀀉
never􀀉again􀀉in􀀉silence.􀀉

The􀀉media􀀉labeled􀀉him􀀉the􀀉Hacksaw􀀉Killer.􀀉A􀀉judge􀀉sentenced􀀉him􀀉to􀀉
twenty􀀉years.􀀉When􀀉he􀀉emerged,􀀉he􀀉was􀀉a􀀉shell􀀉filled􀀉with􀀉cold􀀉resentment.􀀉


Icepick􀀉hadn’t􀀉hired􀀉Andre􀀉despite􀀉his􀀉reputation;􀀉he’d􀀉hired􀀉him􀀉because􀀉
of􀀉it.􀀉In􀀉Icepick’s􀀉kingdom,􀀉a􀀉good􀀉man􀀉was􀀉a􀀉liability.􀀉He􀀉wanted􀀉broken􀀉
tools􀀉still􀀉sharp􀀉enough􀀉to􀀉cut􀀉others.􀀉Andre􀀉wasn’t􀀉an􀀉employee;􀀉he􀀉was􀀉a􀀉
repurposed􀀉asset.􀀉Icepick􀀉saw􀀉the􀀉killer’s􀀉resentment􀀉as􀀉a􀀉high-tensile􀀉
spring,􀀉wound􀀉tight􀀉and􀀉ready􀀉to􀀉snap􀀉at􀀉any􀀉miner􀀉who􀀉slowed􀀉the􀀉energy􀀉
flow.􀀉

Andre􀀉moved􀀉to􀀉Miner􀀉Town􀀉not􀀉to􀀉protect􀀉people,􀀉but􀀉to􀀉ensure􀀉the􀀉
Machine􀀉never􀀉jammed􀀉again.􀀉

After􀀉weeks􀀉in􀀉the􀀉shafts,􀀉Gage􀀉had􀀉finally􀀉mapped􀀉the􀀉gears􀀉that􀀉made􀀉
Icepick’s􀀉kingdom􀀉tick.􀀉He’d􀀉watched􀀉them􀀉work,􀀉learned􀀉their􀀉rhythms,􀀉
and􀀉scribbled􀀉their􀀉names􀀉onto􀀉the􀀉blank􀀉spaces􀀉of􀀉the􀀉wall􀀉map􀀉back􀀉in􀀉the􀀉
shack.􀀉There􀀉were􀀉three:􀀉Lou,􀀉the􀀉Undertaker,􀀉who􀀉managed􀀉disposal􀀉—􀀉
both􀀉coal􀀉and􀀉human􀀉—􀀉treating􀀉every􀀉life􀀉as􀀉a􀀉line􀀉of􀀉inventory,􀀉ensuring􀀉
the􀀉broken􀀉parts􀀉were􀀉swept􀀉away􀀉before􀀉the􀀉next􀀉shift􀀉to􀀉keep􀀉the􀀉machine􀀉
clean.􀀉Jung,􀀉the􀀉Shadow,􀀉the􀀉surveillance􀀉architect􀀉who􀀉tracked􀀉every􀀉
camera,􀀉microphone,􀀉and􀀉thermal􀀉sensor􀀉in􀀉the􀀉mountain􀀉—􀀉reading􀀉
behavioral􀀉patterns,􀀉predicting􀀉who􀀉would􀀉break􀀉next,􀀉ensuring􀀉the􀀉mind􀀉
cracked􀀉exactly􀀉when􀀉the􀀉machine􀀉needed􀀉it􀀉to.􀀉And􀀉Andre,􀀉the􀀉Hacksaw􀀉

—􀀉the􀀉brutal􀀉muscle􀀉who􀀉didn’t􀀉fix􀀉things􀀉but􀀉cut􀀉the􀀉friction􀀉out,􀀉
whether􀀉it􀀉was􀀉a􀀉seized􀀉gear􀀉or􀀉a􀀉biological􀀉obstruction,􀀉so􀀉that􀀉the􀀉energy􀀉
flow􀀉never􀀉stopped.􀀉

Chapter􀀉9􀀉—􀀉Badge􀀉732􀀉

Gage􀀉woke􀀉with􀀉a􀀉cry,􀀉a􀀉stabbing􀀉pain􀀉radiating􀀉through􀀉his􀀉eyes.􀀉He􀀉must􀀉
have􀀉shifted􀀉in􀀉his􀀉sleep􀀉—􀀉the􀀉ragged􀀉blinds􀀉covered􀀉most􀀉of􀀉the􀀉window,􀀉
but􀀉a􀀉finger􀀉of􀀉pale􀀉violet􀀉light􀀉had􀀉found􀀉the􀀉gap􀀉and􀀉laid􀀉itself􀀉directly􀀉
across􀀉his􀀉face.􀀉He􀀉scrambled􀀉to􀀉pull􀀉the􀀉blinds􀀉the􀀉rest􀀉of􀀉the􀀉way􀀉shut,􀀉
desperate􀀉for􀀉a􀀉few􀀉more􀀉minutes􀀉of􀀉darkness.􀀉The􀀉mine􀀉rotated􀀉crews􀀉
every􀀉seven􀀉hours,􀀉but􀀉Gage’s􀀉High-Yield􀀉status􀀉meant􀀉double􀀉rotations􀀉—􀀉
fourteen􀀉hours􀀉underground􀀉before􀀉the􀀉system􀀉released􀀉him.􀀉It􀀉was􀀉taking􀀉
its􀀉toll.􀀉Ben􀀉and􀀉Otto􀀉came􀀉up􀀉squinting􀀉after􀀉their􀀉shifts;􀀉Gage􀀉came􀀉up􀀉
unable􀀉to􀀉look􀀉at􀀉anything􀀉for􀀉the􀀉first􀀉ten􀀉minutes.􀀉The􀀉High-Yield􀀉
rotation􀀉wasn’t􀀉just􀀉longer􀀉—􀀉it􀀉was􀀉lonelier.􀀉The􀀉other􀀉crews􀀉returned􀀉to􀀉a􀀉
world􀀉he􀀉barely􀀉saw􀀉anymore.􀀉

He􀀉shielded􀀉his􀀉eyes􀀉as􀀉the􀀉sun􀀉crested􀀉the􀀉coal􀀉mounds,􀀉turning􀀉the􀀉sky􀀉
into􀀉a􀀉blistering􀀉pale􀀉violet􀀉—􀀉the􀀉color􀀉of􀀉a􀀉bruise􀀉that􀀉never􀀉healed.􀀉The􀀉
high-frequency􀀉radiation􀀉didn’t􀀉just􀀉burn;􀀉it􀀉felt􀀉like􀀉it􀀉was􀀉vibrating􀀉the􀀉
molecules􀀉of􀀉his􀀉bones.􀀉

In􀀉the􀀉distance,􀀉the􀀉Trinity􀀉domes􀀉sat􀀉like􀀉silent􀀉silver􀀉tombs.􀀉The􀀉Night􀀉
Creatures􀀉inside􀀉were􀀉just􀀉beginning􀀉their􀀉slumber􀀉in􀀉climate-controlled􀀉
luxury,􀀉breathing􀀉recycled,􀀉lavender-scented􀀉air.􀀉They􀀉hadn’t􀀉created􀀉this􀀉
scorched􀀉earth􀀉—􀀉the􀀉old􀀉world􀀉had􀀉done􀀉that.􀀉But􀀉they’d􀀉sealed􀀉themselves􀀉
above􀀉the􀀉ruins􀀉and􀀉put􀀉everyone􀀉else􀀉to􀀉work􀀉in􀀉the􀀉ash,􀀉profiting􀀉from􀀉a􀀉
catastrophe􀀉they􀀉never􀀉had􀀉to􀀉feel.􀀉For􀀉them,􀀉the􀀉day􀀉was􀀉a􀀉blackout.􀀉For􀀉
the􀀉unfortunate􀀉souls􀀉of􀀉Miner􀀉Town,􀀉the􀀉day􀀉was􀀉a􀀉slow-motion􀀉
execution.􀀉

Gage􀀉looked􀀉at􀀉the􀀉peeling􀀉skin􀀉on􀀉his􀀉forearms􀀉—􀀉angry􀀉red􀀉patches􀀉that􀀉
refused􀀉to􀀉heal.􀀉He􀀉was􀀉fighting􀀉Atmospheric􀀉Erosion.􀀉Every􀀉hour􀀉under􀀉
that􀀉violet􀀉sky􀀉was􀀉a􀀉gamble􀀉against􀀉a􀀉cellular􀀉clock􀀉ticking􀀉faster􀀉than􀀉his􀀉
rebellion􀀉could􀀉move.􀀉


Being􀀉six-foot-five􀀉was􀀉a􀀉curse􀀉for􀀉his􀀉joints􀀉but􀀉the􀀉reason􀀉he􀀉was􀀉still􀀉
standing.􀀉Because􀀉he􀀉moved􀀉twice􀀉the􀀉tonnage􀀉of􀀉a􀀉standard􀀉miner,􀀉
Icepick’s􀀉intake􀀉system􀀉had􀀉flagged􀀉him􀀉as􀀉a􀀉High-Yield􀀉Asset.􀀉While􀀉Ben􀀉
and􀀉Otto􀀉survived􀀉on􀀉a􀀉single􀀉ration,􀀉Gage􀀉received􀀉a􀀉Heavy􀀉Laborer􀀉
supplement:􀀉an􀀉extra􀀉starch􀀉cube􀀉and􀀉double-portion􀀉glucose􀀉syrup.􀀉It􀀉
wasn’t􀀉mercy;􀀉it􀀉was􀀉Hardware􀀉Calibration􀀉—􀀉just􀀉enough􀀉industrial􀀉
calories􀀉to􀀉prevent􀀉his􀀉massive􀀉frame􀀉from􀀉burning􀀉its􀀉own􀀉muscle.􀀉To􀀉the􀀉
Trinity,􀀉Gage􀀉wasn’t􀀉a􀀉man􀀉to􀀉be􀀉fed;􀀉he􀀉was􀀉a􀀉high-capacity􀀉tool􀀉requiring􀀉
higher􀀉voltage􀀉to􀀉stay􀀉online.􀀉

They’d􀀉been􀀉hammering􀀉anthracite􀀉for􀀉hours􀀉when􀀉the􀀉lunch􀀉bell􀀉sounded.􀀉
The􀀉trio􀀉sat􀀉on􀀉a􀀉broken􀀉bench􀀉surrounded􀀉by􀀉hundreds􀀉of􀀉dust-caked􀀉
workers.􀀉Ben􀀉tried􀀉breaking􀀉the􀀉silence.􀀉“You􀀉guys􀀉know􀀉if􀀉the􀀉cities􀀉were􀀉
buried􀀉where􀀉we􀀉stand,􀀉why􀀉isn’t􀀉there􀀉any􀀉oil?”􀀉

Gage􀀉jumped􀀉at􀀉the􀀉question.􀀉“Pappy􀀉told􀀉me􀀉there􀀉was􀀉oil􀀉and􀀉gas,􀀉but􀀉
they􀀉were􀀉sucked􀀉dry.”􀀉

“Not􀀉at􀀉our􀀉location,”􀀉Ben􀀉said.􀀉“Oil􀀉and􀀉gas􀀉are􀀉migratory􀀉—􀀉they􀀉move􀀉
through􀀉porous􀀉rock􀀉until􀀉they􀀉hit􀀉a􀀉trap.􀀉The􀀉cities􀀉were􀀉built􀀉on􀀉solid􀀉
foundations,􀀉the􀀉worst􀀉place􀀉for􀀉oil􀀉but􀀉perfect􀀉for􀀉a􀀉Solid􀀉Skeleton􀀉like􀀉
coal.􀀉The􀀉elites’􀀉refineries􀀉used􀀉fracking􀀉algorithms􀀉to􀀉bleed􀀉the􀀉deeper􀀉
layers􀀉dry􀀉decades􀀉ago,􀀉leaving􀀉only􀀉the􀀉Hardware􀀉bones􀀉for􀀉us.”􀀉

Otto􀀉shrugged.􀀉“Maybe.􀀉Maybe􀀉none􀀉of􀀉it􀀉matters􀀉anymore.􀀉The􀀉oil’s􀀉
gone,􀀉the􀀉cities􀀉are􀀉above􀀉us,􀀉we’re􀀉hammering􀀉rock􀀉all􀀉day.􀀉Knowing􀀉why􀀉
doesn’t􀀉change􀀉tomorrow.”􀀉

The􀀉food􀀉cart􀀉came􀀉by.􀀉Otto􀀉took􀀉his􀀉ration􀀉—􀀉the􀀉same􀀉ration􀀉he’d􀀉taken􀀉
yesterday􀀉—􀀉and􀀉his􀀉hands􀀉shook􀀉so􀀉badly􀀉he􀀉almost􀀉dropped􀀉it.􀀉

He􀀉stared􀀉at􀀉the􀀉grey􀀉slab􀀉of􀀉Glucose􀀉Brick􀀉and􀀉the􀀉foil-wrapped􀀉starch􀀉
cube􀀉that􀀉served􀀉as􀀉shift-fuel.􀀉“Oh,􀀉would􀀉you􀀉look􀀉at􀀉this?􀀉A􀀉five-star􀀉
banquet.􀀉Compliments􀀉to􀀉Icepick􀀉himself.􀀉I􀀉don’t􀀉know􀀉whether􀀉to􀀉eat􀀉
this􀀉or􀀉use􀀉it􀀉to􀀉patch􀀉a􀀉hole􀀉in􀀉the􀀉Sector-1􀀉turbine!”􀀉


He􀀉tossed􀀉the􀀉cube􀀉into􀀉the􀀉dust,􀀉where􀀉it􀀉landed􀀉with􀀉a􀀉heavy,􀀉inorganic􀀉
thud.􀀉“I’ve􀀉found􀀉more􀀉moisture􀀉in􀀉spent􀀉coal-ash.􀀉They􀀉don’t􀀉even􀀉give􀀉us􀀉
real􀀉food􀀉—􀀉just􀀉enough􀀉organic􀀉lubricant􀀉so􀀉we􀀉don’t􀀉drop􀀉dead􀀉before􀀉
the􀀉next􀀉shift􀀉change.”􀀉

In􀀉Sector-1,􀀉any􀀉display􀀉of􀀉defiance􀀉was􀀉an􀀉invitation􀀉for􀀉violence􀀉—􀀉and􀀉
throwing􀀉Trinity􀀉rations􀀉into􀀉the􀀉dirt􀀉was􀀉as􀀉loud􀀉as􀀉a􀀉scream.􀀉Within􀀉
minutes􀀉a􀀉dozen􀀉electric􀀉wands􀀉hummed􀀉to􀀉life,􀀉blue􀀉sparks􀀉dancing􀀉like􀀉
angry􀀉insects.􀀉The􀀉guards􀀉moved􀀉in􀀉—􀀉a􀀉wall􀀉of􀀉brute􀀉force.􀀉

Otto􀀉could􀀉have􀀉curled􀀉up.􀀉Most􀀉miners􀀉would􀀉have.􀀉Take􀀉the􀀉wand,􀀉lose􀀉a􀀉
tooth,􀀉eat􀀉the􀀉next􀀉shift’s􀀉ration,􀀉live􀀉another􀀉week.􀀉That􀀉was􀀉how􀀉you􀀉
survived􀀉in􀀉Sector-1.􀀉

Otto􀀉had􀀉been􀀉surviving􀀉for􀀉months.􀀉He􀀉was􀀉done􀀉surviving.􀀉The􀀉food􀀉cart􀀉
had􀀉taken􀀉something􀀉from􀀉him􀀉today􀀉that􀀉he􀀉wasn’t􀀉going􀀉to􀀉get􀀉back.􀀉The􀀉
guards􀀉walking􀀉toward􀀉him􀀉with􀀉their􀀉humming􀀉wands􀀉no􀀉longer􀀉looked􀀉
like􀀉authority.􀀉They􀀉looked􀀉like􀀉bodies􀀉fate􀀉had􀀉placed􀀉in􀀉his􀀉way.􀀉

To􀀉him,􀀉guards􀀉weren’t􀀉an􀀉overwhelming􀀉force;􀀉they􀀉were􀀉poorly􀀉balanced􀀉
weights.􀀉As􀀉the􀀉first􀀉guard􀀉lunged,􀀉Otto􀀉stepped􀀉into􀀉the􀀉strike,􀀉caught􀀉the􀀉
guard’s􀀉wrist􀀉with􀀉a􀀉slight􀀉angle􀀉adjustment,􀀉and􀀉sent􀀉him􀀉stumbling􀀉into􀀉
his􀀉own􀀉teammate,􀀉the􀀉wand􀀉discharging􀀉uselessly􀀉into􀀉a􀀉metal􀀉pillar.􀀉

He􀀉moved􀀉between􀀉them􀀉with􀀉the􀀉efficiency􀀉of􀀉a􀀉well-oiled􀀉gear,􀀉using􀀉each􀀉
man’s􀀉forward􀀉energy􀀉as􀀉a􀀉fulcrum.􀀉To􀀉the􀀉miners􀀉watching,􀀉he􀀉was􀀉a􀀉blur.􀀉
To􀀉Gage,􀀉he􀀉was􀀉a􀀉master􀀉of􀀉Combat􀀉Geometry.􀀉

He􀀉was􀀉winning􀀉until􀀉a􀀉coal􀀉block􀀉flew􀀉from􀀉the􀀉shadows,􀀉striking􀀉his􀀉head􀀉
and􀀉knocking􀀉him􀀉off􀀉balance.􀀉The􀀉guards􀀉tased􀀉Otto􀀉until􀀉he􀀉fell􀀉
unconscious.􀀉

As􀀉they􀀉dragged􀀉Otto􀀉away􀀉to􀀉the􀀉hole􀀉for􀀉a􀀉week-long􀀉penalty,􀀉Gage􀀉
caught􀀉a􀀉glimpse􀀉of􀀉the􀀉thrower.􀀉In􀀉the􀀉gloom:􀀉a􀀉dark􀀉shadow􀀉with􀀉a􀀉badge􀀉


cleaner􀀉than􀀉anything􀀉else􀀉in􀀉the􀀉shaft􀀉—􀀉732.􀀉Most􀀉miners􀀉let􀀉the􀀉grit􀀉cake􀀉
their􀀉badges􀀉into􀀉illegibility.􀀉This􀀉one􀀉didn’t.􀀉

The􀀉next􀀉day,􀀉Gage􀀉arrived􀀉early.􀀉Otto􀀉would􀀉be􀀉in􀀉the􀀉hole􀀉for􀀉a􀀉week,􀀉and􀀉
the􀀉man􀀉who’d􀀉thrown􀀉the􀀉coal􀀉block􀀉was􀀉somewhere􀀉in􀀉this􀀉shaft,􀀉working􀀉
a􀀉normal􀀉shift.􀀉Gage􀀉scanned􀀉the􀀉crews􀀉as􀀉he􀀉walked􀀉the􀀉platform.􀀉Most􀀉of􀀉
the􀀉badges􀀉were􀀉dim􀀉—􀀉coated􀀉in􀀉months􀀉of􀀉grit,􀀉the􀀉numbers􀀉barely􀀉
legible.􀀉He􀀉looked􀀉for􀀉the􀀉one􀀉that􀀉wasn’t.􀀉

It􀀉took􀀉him􀀉an􀀉hour.􀀉Badge􀀉732􀀉was􀀉working􀀉the􀀉eastern􀀉face,􀀉alone,􀀉at􀀉the􀀉
far􀀉end􀀉of􀀉a􀀉tunnel􀀉that􀀉branched􀀉away􀀉from􀀉the􀀉main􀀉shaft.􀀉Gage􀀉waited􀀉
until􀀉732􀀉walked􀀉deeper􀀉into􀀉the􀀉branch􀀉—􀀉out􀀉of􀀉the􀀉cameras’􀀉line􀀉of􀀉
sight,􀀉out􀀉of􀀉the􀀉other􀀉miners’􀀉earshot.􀀉Then􀀉he􀀉followed.􀀉He􀀉tracked􀀉the􀀉
number􀀉deep􀀉into􀀉the􀀉isolated􀀉shaft􀀉and􀀉lunged,􀀉swinging􀀉his􀀉heavy􀀉iron􀀉
shovel􀀉with􀀉all􀀉the􀀉force􀀉of􀀉his􀀉220-pound􀀉frame.􀀉

The􀀉man􀀉didn’t􀀉brace.􀀉He􀀉didn’t􀀉raise􀀉his􀀉hands.􀀉As􀀉the􀀉shovel􀀉hissed􀀉
through􀀉the􀀉air,􀀉he􀀉stepped􀀉to􀀉the􀀉side,􀀉pivoting􀀉on􀀉the􀀉ball􀀉of􀀉his􀀉foot.􀀉He􀀉
caught􀀉Gage’s􀀉shoulder􀀉with􀀉a􀀉palm􀀉—􀀉not􀀉to􀀉stop􀀉him,􀀉but􀀉to􀀉accelerate􀀉
him.􀀉With􀀉a􀀉precise􀀉punch􀀉to􀀉Gage’s􀀉diaphragm􀀉at􀀉the􀀉peak􀀉of􀀉his􀀉
momentum,􀀉Badge􀀉732􀀉sent􀀉the􀀉giant􀀉sprawling􀀉into􀀉coal􀀉dust.􀀉

Gage􀀉hit􀀉the􀀉ground􀀉with􀀉the􀀉full􀀉weight􀀉of􀀉his􀀉frame.􀀉His􀀉diaphragm􀀉
seized.􀀉The􀀉world􀀉went􀀉white􀀉at􀀉the􀀉edges.􀀉He􀀉tried􀀉to􀀉breathe􀀉and􀀉
couldn’t,􀀉tried􀀉again􀀉and􀀉got􀀉nothing.􀀉

“Gravity􀀉is􀀉a􀀉harsh􀀉mistress,􀀉isn’t􀀉it?”􀀉The􀀉voice􀀉was􀀉a􀀉low􀀉rumble􀀉above􀀉
him.􀀉He􀀉offered􀀉a􀀉hand.􀀉“You’re􀀉a􀀉big􀀉boy,􀀉Gage.􀀉The􀀉faster􀀉a􀀉structure􀀉
moves,􀀉the􀀉easier􀀉it􀀉is􀀉to􀀉tip.”􀀉

Gage􀀉finally􀀉got􀀉air.􀀉The􀀉wheeze􀀉that􀀉came􀀉out􀀉of􀀉him􀀉was􀀉barely􀀉a􀀉voice.􀀉
“You􀀉hit􀀉Otto.􀀉You􀀉took􀀉him􀀉out.”􀀉

“A􀀉week􀀉in􀀉the􀀉hole􀀉was􀀉better􀀉than􀀉a􀀉body􀀉bag,”􀀉the􀀉man􀀉countered,􀀉
scanning􀀉the􀀉corridor.􀀉“The􀀉surveillance􀀉system􀀉had􀀉flagged􀀉the􀀉noise.􀀉If􀀉


Otto􀀉had􀀉dropped􀀉one􀀉more􀀉guard,􀀉the􀀉Hacksaw􀀉would􀀉have􀀉performed􀀉a􀀉
Clean􀀉Cut.􀀉I􀀉didn’t􀀉knock􀀉Otto􀀉out􀀉because􀀉I􀀉wanted􀀉him􀀉to􀀉lose;􀀉I􀀉did􀀉it􀀉
so􀀉he’d􀀉survive􀀉to􀀉come􀀉back.”􀀉

In􀀉the􀀉total􀀉gloom,􀀉Gage􀀉noticed􀀉the􀀉fluorescent􀀉badge􀀉pulsing􀀉with􀀉a􀀉faint,􀀉
ghostly􀀉violet􀀉light,􀀉visible􀀉only􀀉in􀀉the􀀉absolute􀀉absence􀀉of􀀉sun.􀀉So􀀉that􀀉was􀀉
why􀀉the􀀉badge􀀉had􀀉stood􀀉out􀀉yesterday.􀀉The􀀉badges􀀉were􀀉made􀀉to􀀉do􀀉this􀀉

—􀀉absorb􀀉lamplight􀀉during􀀉the􀀉shift,􀀉glow􀀉in􀀉the􀀉dark􀀉—􀀉but􀀉most􀀉miners’􀀉
badges􀀉had􀀉stopped􀀉working􀀉years􀀉ago.􀀉The􀀉grit􀀉blocked􀀉the􀀉absorption.􀀉
Only􀀉the􀀉clean􀀉ones􀀉still􀀉glowed.􀀉
Gage􀀉stared􀀉up􀀉at􀀉him,􀀉still􀀉on􀀉the􀀉ground.􀀉“Who􀀉are􀀉you?􀀉Why􀀉did􀀉you􀀉
save􀀉him?”􀀉

“We􀀉don’t􀀉use􀀉names􀀉here,”􀀉the􀀉man􀀉whispered,􀀉his􀀉badge􀀉glowing􀀉like􀀉a􀀉
beacon􀀉for􀀉those􀀉who􀀉knew􀀉where􀀉to􀀉look.􀀉“Names􀀉are􀀉for􀀉the􀀉surface.􀀉
Down􀀉here,􀀉the􀀉only􀀉number􀀉that􀀉matters􀀉is􀀉the􀀉one􀀉on􀀉your􀀉badge.”􀀉He􀀉
paused,􀀉watching􀀉Gage’s􀀉face􀀉for􀀉something.􀀉“And􀀉some􀀉of􀀉us􀀉pay􀀉attention􀀉
to􀀉other􀀉numbers.”􀀉His􀀉eyes􀀉flicked􀀉upward,􀀉past􀀉the􀀉rock,􀀉toward􀀉the􀀉
surface􀀉none􀀉of􀀉them􀀉could􀀉survive.􀀉“And􀀉don’t􀀉go􀀉dreaming􀀉about􀀉
running.􀀉The􀀉drones􀀉aren’t􀀉the􀀉cage.􀀉The􀀉sky􀀉is.􀀉The􀀉drones􀀉are􀀉just􀀉there􀀉
to􀀉make􀀉sure􀀉nobody􀀉figures􀀉that􀀉out.􀀉Trinity􀀉doesn’t􀀉have􀀉to􀀉chase􀀉a􀀉man.􀀉
It􀀉only􀀉has􀀉to􀀉wait.”􀀉

Gage􀀉didn’t􀀉know􀀉what􀀉any􀀉of􀀉it􀀉meant.􀀉But􀀉he􀀉held􀀉the􀀉man’s􀀉gaze,􀀉and􀀉
whatever􀀉the􀀉man􀀉saw􀀉in􀀉his􀀉face􀀉made􀀉him􀀉answer􀀉the􀀉original􀀉question.􀀉

“So􀀉that􀀉you􀀉can􀀉help􀀉us􀀉someday.”􀀉He􀀉paused􀀉at􀀉the􀀉edge􀀉of􀀉the􀀉darkness.􀀉
“The􀀉name’s􀀉Gerald.”􀀉Then􀀉he􀀉vanished􀀉into􀀉the􀀉corridor.􀀉

A􀀉week􀀉later􀀉the􀀉heavy􀀉iron􀀉door􀀉of􀀉the􀀉penalty􀀉block􀀉groaned􀀉open,􀀉
spitting􀀉Otto􀀉back􀀉into􀀉the􀀉soot.􀀉He􀀉was􀀉hollowed􀀉out,􀀉skin􀀉the􀀉color􀀉of􀀉
wet􀀉ash.􀀉Most􀀉notably,􀀉his􀀉eyes􀀉wouldn’t􀀉settle􀀉—􀀉darting􀀉around􀀉like􀀉
malfunctioning􀀉sensors􀀉trying􀀉to􀀉recalibrate􀀉after􀀉total􀀉darkness.􀀉


At􀀉the􀀉shack,􀀉Ben􀀉pushed􀀉a􀀉mug􀀉of􀀉warm􀀉sump􀀉water􀀉toward􀀉him􀀉—􀀉
triple-filtered􀀉through􀀉the􀀉charcoal􀀉rag,􀀉still􀀉faintly􀀉orange,􀀉but􀀉the􀀉best􀀉they􀀉
had.􀀉“Otto,􀀉what􀀉was􀀉it􀀉like?􀀉Did􀀉they􀀉at􀀉least􀀉feed􀀉you?”􀀉

“I􀀉don’t􀀉want􀀉your􀀉pity,􀀉Ben,”􀀉Otto􀀉rasped,􀀉voice􀀉like􀀉two􀀉stones􀀉grinding.􀀉
He􀀉didn’t􀀉touch􀀉the􀀉drink.􀀉He􀀉stared􀀉at􀀉Gage,􀀉his􀀉jaw􀀉tight􀀉enough􀀉to􀀉snap.􀀉
“I􀀉want􀀉a􀀉name.􀀉Whoever􀀉hit􀀉me􀀉from􀀉the􀀉shadows􀀉—􀀉you􀀉were􀀉there,􀀉
Gage.􀀉You􀀉saw􀀉something.”􀀉

“Otto,􀀉you’re􀀉not􀀉in􀀉any􀀉state􀀉for􀀉a􀀉manhunt,”􀀉Gage􀀉said.􀀉

Otto􀀉surged􀀉forward,􀀉slamming􀀉a􀀉fist􀀉onto􀀉the􀀉table,􀀉sending􀀉foil􀀉scraps􀀉
flying.􀀉“Shut􀀉up􀀉and􀀉answer􀀉my􀀉question!􀀉He􀀉cheated.􀀉I’m􀀉going􀀉to􀀉find􀀉
him􀀉and􀀉reward􀀉him􀀉with􀀉a􀀉fate􀀉so􀀉horrifying􀀉those􀀉starch􀀉cubes􀀉will􀀉be􀀉his􀀉
happiest􀀉memory.”􀀉

Gage􀀉didn’t􀀉flinch.􀀉He􀀉shouted􀀉back:􀀉“I􀀉found􀀉him.􀀉And􀀉he􀀉told􀀉me􀀉why􀀉
he􀀉did􀀉it.􀀉He􀀉didn’t􀀉hit􀀉you􀀉to􀀉break􀀉you,􀀉Otto􀀉—􀀉he􀀉hit􀀉you􀀉to􀀉save􀀉you􀀉
from􀀉the􀀉Hacksaw.􀀉If􀀉you’d􀀉dropped􀀉one􀀉more􀀉guard,􀀉Andre􀀉would􀀉have􀀉
performed􀀉a􀀉Clean􀀉Cut.􀀉You􀀉wouldn’t􀀉be􀀉sitting􀀉here􀀉wanting􀀉revenge.􀀉
You’d􀀉be􀀉in􀀉the􀀉disposal􀀉tunnels.”􀀉

Otto􀀉stared􀀉at􀀉him.􀀉The􀀉starch􀀉cube􀀉on􀀉his􀀉plate􀀉was􀀉still􀀉untouched.􀀉

Gage􀀉held􀀉his􀀉gaze􀀉the􀀉way􀀉Gerald􀀉had􀀉held􀀉his.􀀉“His􀀉name􀀉is􀀉Gerald.􀀉He’s􀀉
not􀀉the􀀉enemy.”􀀉


Chapter􀀉10􀀉—􀀉The􀀉Fever􀀉Seed􀀉

While􀀉Gage,􀀉Ben,􀀉and􀀉Otto􀀉toiled􀀉in􀀉Sector-1,􀀉Camilla􀀉found􀀉herself􀀉a􀀉
ghost􀀉in􀀉her􀀉own􀀉neighborhood.􀀉She􀀉tried􀀉to􀀉stay􀀉invisible,􀀉but􀀉everything􀀉
about􀀉her􀀉refused􀀉to􀀉cooperate.􀀉Her􀀉broad􀀉frame􀀉drew􀀉eyes.􀀉Her􀀉hands􀀉
were􀀉always􀀉pulling􀀉something􀀉apart􀀉or􀀉putting􀀉something􀀉together.􀀉She􀀉
asked􀀉questions􀀉that􀀉made􀀉adults􀀉go􀀉quiet.􀀉In􀀉a􀀉world􀀉that􀀉rewarded􀀉
stillness􀀉and􀀉punished􀀉curiosity,􀀉Camilla􀀉was􀀉a􀀉walking􀀉alarm􀀉bell.􀀉

Rita􀀉watched􀀉with􀀉growing􀀉dread.􀀉It􀀉wasn’t􀀉her􀀉daughter’s􀀉strength􀀉that􀀉
frightened􀀉her􀀉—􀀉plenty􀀉of􀀉kids􀀉in􀀉Miner􀀉Town􀀉were􀀉built􀀉for􀀉labor.􀀉It􀀉was􀀉
Camilla’s􀀉visibility.􀀉She􀀉couldn’t􀀉stop􀀉herself􀀉from􀀉being􀀉noticed,􀀉and􀀉in􀀉
Trinity’s􀀉system,􀀉being􀀉noticed􀀉before􀀉your􀀉time􀀉meant􀀉being􀀉marked.􀀉By􀀉
the􀀉time􀀉her􀀉ration􀀉card􀀉came􀀉up􀀉at􀀉seventeen,􀀉she’d􀀉be􀀉flagged􀀉as􀀉a􀀉risk􀀉—􀀉
and􀀉the􀀉kids􀀉who􀀉walked􀀉into􀀉the􀀉chamber􀀉already􀀉flagged􀀉didn’t􀀉always􀀉
walk􀀉back􀀉out.􀀉The􀀉worse􀀉fear,􀀉the􀀉one􀀉Rita􀀉carried􀀉at􀀉night,􀀉was􀀉that􀀉
Camilla􀀉wouldn’t􀀉make􀀉it􀀉that􀀉far􀀉at􀀉all.􀀉Some􀀉kids􀀉never􀀉reached􀀉their􀀉
seventeenth􀀉birthday.􀀉Trinity􀀉made􀀉them􀀉disappear􀀉quietly,􀀉and􀀉the􀀉families􀀉
learned􀀉to􀀉stop􀀉asking.􀀉To􀀉save􀀉her,􀀉Rita􀀉decided􀀉to􀀉hide􀀉her􀀉where􀀉her􀀉
brightness􀀉wouldn’t􀀉show􀀉—􀀉by􀀉sending􀀉her􀀉directly􀀉into􀀉the􀀉heart􀀉of􀀉the􀀉
machine.􀀉

The􀀉journey􀀉from􀀉Miner􀀉Town􀀉to􀀉Sector-1􀀉was􀀉less􀀉than􀀉an􀀉hour􀀉on􀀉foot,􀀉
but􀀉the􀀉landscape􀀉changed􀀉with􀀉every􀀉step.􀀉Miner􀀉Town􀀉sat􀀉on􀀉the􀀉upper􀀉
ridge􀀉—􀀉open􀀉sky,􀀉shack-rows􀀉baking􀀉under􀀉the􀀉violet􀀉haze,􀀉the􀀉communal􀀉
pumps􀀉and􀀉scrap􀀉markets􀀉at􀀉its􀀉center.􀀉The􀀉ridge􀀉path􀀉dropped􀀉steeply􀀉
toward􀀉the􀀉mine􀀉complex,􀀉the􀀉air􀀉thickening􀀉with􀀉coal􀀉dust􀀉and􀀉the􀀉
chemical􀀉bite􀀉of􀀉anthracite.􀀉By􀀉the􀀉time􀀉you􀀉reached􀀉the􀀉perimeter􀀉fence􀀉—􀀉
a􀀉wall􀀉of􀀉corrugated􀀉iron􀀉topped􀀉with􀀉sensor􀀉wire􀀉—􀀉the􀀉sky􀀉had􀀉narrowed􀀉
to􀀉a􀀉strip􀀉between􀀉slag􀀉heaps,􀀉and􀀉the􀀉temperature􀀉had􀀉dropped􀀉ten􀀉degrees􀀉
from􀀉the􀀉mountain’s􀀉shadow.􀀉Sector-1􀀉wasn’t􀀉a􀀉place􀀉you􀀉entered.􀀉It􀀉was􀀉a􀀉


place􀀉that􀀉swallowed􀀉you.􀀉Beyond􀀉the􀀉checkpoint􀀉gates,􀀉the􀀉shack-rows􀀉
resumed􀀉—􀀉smaller,􀀉grimier,􀀉pressed􀀉against􀀉the􀀉mine􀀉entrance􀀉like􀀉rust􀀉
crowding􀀉a􀀉hinge􀀉—􀀉but􀀉these􀀉were􀀉workers’􀀉quarters,􀀉not􀀉family􀀉dwellings.􀀉
The􀀉air􀀉tasted􀀉different􀀉here.􀀉Everything􀀉tasted􀀉of􀀉the􀀉mountain.􀀉Most􀀉who􀀉
walked􀀉through􀀉those􀀉gates􀀉didn’t􀀉walk􀀉back􀀉out􀀉as􀀉the􀀉same􀀉people.􀀉

It􀀉seemed􀀉paradoxical.􀀉Hiding􀀉Camilla􀀉in􀀉the􀀉miner􀀉shacks􀀉at􀀉the􀀉edge􀀉of􀀉
Sector-1􀀉was􀀉like􀀉hiding􀀉a􀀉spark􀀉in􀀉a􀀉furnace.􀀉But􀀉Pappy􀀉had􀀉told􀀉her􀀉
parents􀀉about􀀉the􀀉Signal􀀉Shadows.􀀉The􀀉very􀀉iron-ore􀀉deposits􀀉Icepick􀀉
mined􀀉acted􀀉as􀀉a􀀉natural􀀉Faraday􀀉cage􀀉—􀀉a􀀉shell􀀉of􀀉metal􀀉so􀀉dense􀀉that􀀉radio􀀉
signals􀀉couldn’t􀀉pass􀀉through􀀉it,􀀉the􀀉same􀀉way􀀉a􀀉thick􀀉stone􀀉wall􀀉blocks􀀉
sound.􀀉The􀀉magnetic􀀉interference􀀉from􀀉heavy􀀉drills􀀉was􀀉so􀀉intense􀀉that􀀉
Trinity’s􀀉high-altitude􀀉surveillance􀀉couldn’t􀀉reach􀀉the􀀉shacks.􀀉Near􀀉the􀀉pits,􀀉
the􀀉Shadow􀀉was􀀉blind.􀀉

She􀀉timed􀀉her􀀉approach􀀉to􀀉the􀀉evening􀀉shift􀀉change,􀀉when􀀉hundreds􀀉of􀀉
miners􀀉funneled􀀉through􀀉the􀀉checkpoint􀀉gates􀀉and􀀉the􀀉system􀀉prioritized􀀉
throughput􀀉over􀀉scrutiny.􀀉Armed􀀉with􀀉Pappy’s􀀉old􀀉foreman􀀉clearance,􀀉
Camilla􀀉knew􀀉she􀀉was􀀉carrying􀀉a􀀉ticking􀀉time􀀉bomb.􀀉In􀀉Jung’s􀀉world,􀀉a􀀉
decades-old􀀉ID􀀉should􀀉have􀀉triggered􀀉a􀀉legacy􀀉alert.􀀉But􀀉Camilla􀀉was􀀉a􀀉
scavenger􀀉who􀀉found􀀉the􀀉hardware􀀉loopholes.􀀉Before􀀉leaving,􀀉she’d􀀉used􀀉
salvaged􀀉magnetite􀀉—􀀉a􀀉black􀀉iron-rich􀀉rock􀀉that􀀉holds􀀉a􀀉strong􀀉magnetic􀀉
charge􀀉—􀀉to􀀉magnetically􀀉scar􀀉the􀀉ID􀀉chip,􀀉frying􀀉the􀀉encryption􀀉layer􀀉
without􀀉destroying􀀉it.􀀉When􀀉she􀀉pressed􀀉the􀀉badge􀀉against􀀉checkpoints,􀀉
scanners􀀉didn’t􀀉see􀀉a􀀉stolen􀀉credential;􀀉they􀀉hit􀀉a􀀉read􀀉error.􀀉The􀀉gates,􀀉
programmed􀀉with􀀉a􀀉legacy􀀉override􀀉for􀀉damaged􀀉chips,􀀉switched􀀉to􀀉offline􀀉
mode􀀉and􀀉let􀀉her􀀉through􀀉to􀀉keep􀀉the􀀉intake􀀉moving.􀀉To􀀉the􀀉software,􀀉she􀀉
was􀀉a􀀉data-error;􀀉to􀀉the􀀉hardware,􀀉she􀀉was􀀉just􀀉another􀀉part􀀉of􀀉the􀀉flow.􀀉

Gage􀀉arrived􀀉at􀀉his􀀉ragged􀀉shelter􀀉that􀀉evening􀀉the􀀉way􀀉he􀀉arrived􀀉every􀀉
evening􀀉—􀀉more􀀉dead􀀉than􀀉alive.􀀉He􀀉froze􀀉in􀀉the􀀉doorway.􀀉The􀀉air􀀉inside􀀉
was􀀉wrong􀀉—􀀉a􀀉smell􀀉he􀀉had􀀉no􀀉word􀀉for,􀀉dark􀀉and􀀉rich􀀉and􀀉entirely􀀉out􀀉of􀀉
place.􀀉Nothing􀀉in􀀉Miner􀀉Town􀀉smelled􀀉like􀀉that.􀀉Nothing􀀉was􀀉supposed􀀉to.􀀉


Shovel􀀉gripped􀀉tight,􀀉he􀀉moved􀀉with􀀉soft􀀉steps,􀀉ready􀀉to􀀉strike􀀉—􀀉only􀀉to􀀉
find􀀉himself􀀉flat􀀉on􀀉the􀀉floor􀀉a􀀉second􀀉later.􀀉

“I􀀉knew􀀉it.􀀉You’ve􀀉gone􀀉rusted,”􀀉Camilla􀀉chuckled,􀀉offering􀀉a􀀉hand.􀀉Gage􀀉
took􀀉it􀀉and􀀉let􀀉her􀀉pull􀀉him􀀉up􀀉—􀀉face􀀉flushed,􀀉half􀀉from􀀉anger􀀉and􀀉half􀀉
from􀀉the􀀉dawning􀀉embarrassment􀀉of􀀉being􀀉put􀀉on􀀉the􀀉floor􀀉by􀀉his􀀉little􀀉
sister.􀀉Her􀀉hand􀀉registered􀀉the􀀉heat􀀉of􀀉him􀀉before􀀉her􀀉eyes􀀉did.􀀉He􀀉was􀀉
running􀀉a􀀉fever􀀉—􀀉a􀀉bad􀀉one.􀀉

For􀀉a􀀉moment,􀀉Gage􀀉just􀀉stared􀀉at􀀉her.􀀉She􀀉was􀀉thinner􀀉than􀀉he􀀉
remembered,􀀉her􀀉cheekbones􀀉sharper􀀉under􀀉the􀀉soot,􀀉but􀀉her􀀉eyes􀀉were􀀉the􀀉
same􀀉—􀀉bright,􀀉quick,􀀉already􀀉cataloguing􀀉the􀀉state􀀉of􀀉him􀀉the􀀉way􀀉she’d􀀉
catalogue􀀉a􀀉broken􀀉machine.􀀉He􀀉opened􀀉his􀀉mouth􀀉to􀀉ask􀀉how􀀉she’d􀀉gotten􀀉
here,􀀉why􀀉she’d􀀉come,􀀉what􀀉kind􀀉of􀀉insane􀀉risk􀀉she’d􀀉taken􀀉—􀀉but􀀉the􀀉
words􀀉jammed􀀉in􀀉his􀀉throat.􀀉

She􀀉stepped􀀉close􀀉and􀀉pressed􀀉the􀀉back􀀉of􀀉her􀀉hand􀀉to􀀉his􀀉forehead􀀉—􀀉the􀀉
same􀀉diagnostic􀀉motion􀀉she’d􀀉seen􀀉Rita􀀉do􀀉a􀀉hundred􀀉times.􀀉Confirmation.􀀉
Up􀀉close,􀀉she􀀉could􀀉hear􀀉the􀀉cough􀀉sitting􀀉in􀀉his􀀉chest,􀀉see􀀉the􀀉small􀀉
constant􀀉tremor􀀉of􀀉a􀀉body􀀉that􀀉had􀀉been􀀉pushed􀀉too􀀉hard􀀉for􀀉too􀀉many􀀉
days.􀀉The􀀉shack􀀉creaked􀀉around􀀉them,􀀉metal􀀉contracting􀀉in􀀉the􀀉cooling􀀉
night􀀉air,􀀉and􀀉somewhere􀀉outside􀀉a􀀉shift-change􀀉siren􀀉groaned􀀉its􀀉low,􀀉
mournful􀀉note.􀀉

“You’re􀀉burning􀀉up,”􀀉she􀀉said􀀉quietly.􀀉

“Fever􀀉comes􀀉with􀀉the􀀉soot,􀀉same􀀉as􀀉the􀀉cough,”􀀉Gage􀀉muttered.􀀉“I’ve􀀉
worked􀀉through􀀉worse.”􀀉

“What􀀉is􀀉that􀀉smell?”􀀉Gage􀀉said,􀀉glancing􀀉toward􀀉the􀀉pot.􀀉“Whatever􀀉it􀀉is,􀀉it􀀉
doesn’t􀀉belong􀀉here.􀀉Which􀀉means􀀉you’re􀀉either􀀉a􀀉hallucination􀀉or􀀉you’ve􀀉
done􀀉something􀀉incredibly􀀉stupid.”􀀉

“Both,􀀉probably.”􀀉She􀀉stepped􀀉back􀀉and􀀉took􀀉fuller􀀉stock􀀉—􀀉the􀀉peeling􀀉
skin􀀉on􀀉his􀀉forearms,􀀉the􀀉grey􀀉pallor􀀉beneath􀀉the􀀉soot,􀀉the􀀉way􀀉he􀀉held􀀉


himself􀀉crooked,􀀉left􀀉arm􀀉bracing􀀉his􀀉ribs.􀀉The􀀉worry􀀉that􀀉had􀀉started􀀉in􀀉her􀀉
hand􀀉deepened.􀀉“Gage.􀀉You􀀉look􀀉like􀀉the􀀉mine􀀉is􀀉eating􀀉you􀀉from􀀉the􀀉
inside.”􀀉

“It􀀉is,”􀀉he􀀉said􀀉simply.􀀉“But􀀉I’m􀀉still􀀉here.”􀀉

She􀀉held􀀉his􀀉gaze􀀉for􀀉a􀀉long􀀉beat􀀉—􀀉the􀀉kind􀀉of􀀉look􀀉that􀀉in􀀉their􀀉family􀀉
passed􀀉for􀀉a􀀉full􀀉conversation.􀀉Then􀀉she􀀉turned􀀉and􀀉gestured􀀉toward􀀉a􀀉
battered􀀉tin􀀉pot􀀉on􀀉a􀀉small􀀉heating􀀉element.􀀉

“Sit􀀉down􀀉before􀀉you􀀉fall􀀉down.􀀉I􀀉made􀀉you􀀉something.”􀀉

“Whatever􀀉that􀀉is…”􀀉Gage􀀉wheezed,􀀉staring􀀉at􀀉the􀀉pot.􀀉“It􀀉smells􀀉
impossible.”􀀉

“Almost,”􀀉Camilla􀀉said,􀀉pouring􀀉a􀀉cup.􀀉“Hellhole􀀉Chicory.􀀉Pappy􀀉used􀀉to􀀉
describe􀀉real􀀉coffee􀀉to􀀉me􀀉—􀀉dark􀀈as􀀈soot,􀀈bitter􀀈as􀀈the􀀈mountain,􀀈and􀀈worth􀀈
more􀀈than􀀈salt.􀀈I􀀉caught􀀉a􀀉whiff􀀉of􀀉the􀀉real􀀉thing􀀉once,􀀉drifting􀀉from􀀉a􀀉
guard’s􀀉flask􀀉near􀀉the􀀉mess,􀀉and􀀉I’ve􀀉been􀀉chasing􀀉it􀀉ever􀀉since.􀀉Pappy􀀉gave􀀉
me􀀉the􀀉recipe􀀉—􀀉dandelion􀀉roots,􀀉roasted􀀉acorns,􀀉burnt􀀉starch􀀉—􀀉what􀀉
people􀀉brewed􀀉in􀀉the􀀉old􀀉world􀀉when􀀉they􀀉couldn’t􀀉get􀀉the􀀉real􀀉thing.􀀉I􀀉
added􀀉a􀀉pinch􀀉of􀀉stolen􀀉salt􀀉—􀀉Pappy􀀉said􀀉it􀀉cuts􀀉the􀀉bitterness.􀀉This􀀉is􀀉the􀀉
closest􀀉I’ve􀀉come.”􀀉

She􀀉handed􀀉him􀀉the􀀉mug.􀀉Bitter,􀀉earthy,􀀉lacking􀀉caffeine􀀉—􀀉but􀀉to􀀉Gage’s􀀉
parched􀀉throat,􀀉it􀀉was􀀉the􀀉first􀀉solace􀀉for􀀉his􀀉soul􀀉in􀀉months.􀀉It􀀉wasn’t􀀉just􀀉a􀀉
drink;􀀉it􀀉was􀀉patience,􀀉Pappy’s􀀉knowledge,􀀉and􀀉the􀀉kind􀀉of􀀉skill􀀉that􀀉pulls􀀉
warmth􀀉out􀀉of􀀉dirt􀀉and􀀉roots.􀀉

They􀀉carried􀀉the􀀉mugs􀀉outside􀀉and􀀉sat􀀉on􀀉a􀀉flat􀀉stone􀀉by􀀉the􀀉makeshift􀀉
door,􀀉letting􀀉the􀀉warmth􀀉of􀀉the􀀉chicory􀀉do􀀉its􀀉work􀀉before􀀉the􀀉harder􀀉
conversation􀀉began.􀀉After􀀉a􀀉while,􀀉Gage􀀉looked􀀉toward􀀉the􀀉horizon.􀀉It􀀉
shimmered􀀉with􀀉deceptive􀀉violet.􀀉To􀀉an􀀉outsider,􀀉peaceful􀀉twilight;􀀉to􀀉a􀀉
miner,􀀉the􀀉Killing􀀉Field.􀀉

“How􀀉are􀀉you􀀉here,􀀉Cami?􀀉How􀀉did􀀉you􀀉get􀀉past􀀉the􀀉checkpoints?”􀀉


“Pappy’s􀀉old􀀉foreman􀀉clearance,”􀀉she􀀉said.􀀉“I􀀉scarred􀀉the􀀉chip􀀉with􀀉
magnetite􀀉—􀀉fried􀀉the􀀉encryption􀀉but􀀉left􀀉the􀀉serial􀀉number􀀉intact.􀀉The􀀉
scanners􀀉hit􀀉a􀀉Read􀀉Error􀀉and􀀉waved􀀉me􀀉through.”􀀉

Gage􀀉shook􀀉his􀀉head.􀀉“And􀀉the􀀉drones?􀀉You􀀉crossed􀀉open􀀉ground􀀉to􀀉get􀀉
here.”􀀉

“That􀀉was􀀉the􀀉gamble,”􀀉Camilla􀀉said,􀀉her􀀉voice􀀉losing􀀉its􀀉playfulness.􀀉“I􀀉
didn’t􀀉take􀀉the􀀉direct􀀉path􀀉—􀀉the􀀉direct􀀉path􀀉is􀀉open􀀉ground.􀀉I􀀉moved􀀉at􀀉
night􀀉through􀀉the􀀉iron-ore􀀉corridors.􀀉The􀀉rock􀀉breaks􀀉up􀀉both􀀉the􀀉
radiation􀀉and􀀉the􀀉scanner􀀉signals.􀀉For􀀉the􀀉open􀀉stretches􀀉I􀀉traveled􀀉wet,􀀉
soaked􀀉rags􀀉layered􀀉under􀀉my􀀉outer􀀉clothes,􀀉pulling􀀉the􀀉heat􀀉off􀀉my􀀉skin􀀉
faster􀀉than􀀉the􀀉scanners􀀉could􀀉lock􀀉on.􀀉I􀀉timed􀀉each􀀉leg􀀉to􀀉the􀀉blind􀀉
windows􀀉Pappy􀀉had􀀉mapped􀀉before􀀉he􀀉died.􀀉The􀀉whole􀀉route􀀉stayed􀀉in􀀉the􀀉
deep􀀉hours􀀉—􀀉full􀀉dark,􀀉no􀀉sun-bleed􀀉at􀀉either􀀉end.􀀉The􀀉patrols􀀉missed􀀉
me.”􀀉She􀀉paused.􀀉“Staying􀀉here􀀉is􀀉easier.􀀉The􀀉iron􀀉protects􀀉me􀀉—􀀉that’s􀀉
what􀀉Pappy􀀉taught􀀉us,􀀉and􀀉so􀀉far􀀉it’s􀀉holding.􀀉The􀀉shadow􀀉stays􀀉while􀀉the􀀉
heavy􀀉Sector-1􀀉drills􀀉are􀀉spinning,􀀉and􀀉the􀀉magnetic􀀉scream􀀉creates􀀉the􀀉
interference􀀉that􀀉blinds􀀉the􀀉drones.􀀉The􀀉trick􀀉is􀀉just􀀉learning􀀉to􀀉live􀀉inside􀀉
its􀀉limits.”􀀉

“And􀀉when􀀉the􀀉drills􀀉stop?”􀀉

“Every􀀉six􀀉hours,􀀉for􀀉about􀀉twenty􀀉minutes􀀉during􀀉the􀀉shift􀀉change,􀀉the􀀉
mountain􀀉goes􀀉quiet􀀉and􀀉the􀀉interference􀀉drops.􀀉Jung’s􀀉thermal􀀉scanners􀀉
can􀀉reach􀀉through􀀉these􀀉walls􀀉during􀀉those􀀉windows.”􀀉She􀀉gestured􀀉at􀀉the􀀉
shack’s􀀉iron􀀉cladding.􀀉“So􀀉we􀀉go􀀉cold.􀀉No􀀉heating􀀉element,􀀉no􀀉movement,􀀉
no􀀉body􀀉heat􀀉near􀀉the􀀉outer􀀉walls.􀀉I􀀉lie􀀉flat􀀉on􀀉the􀀉floor􀀉in􀀉the􀀉center􀀉of􀀉the􀀉
room􀀉with􀀉a􀀉salvaged􀀉roof􀀉panel􀀉over􀀉me􀀉—􀀉the􀀉cold􀀉metal􀀉blurs􀀉my􀀉heat􀀉
signature􀀉just􀀉enough􀀉to􀀉read􀀉as􀀉background􀀉noise.􀀉It’s􀀉not􀀉invisible.􀀉Most􀀉
days,􀀉the􀀉system􀀉reads􀀉it􀀉as􀀉dead􀀉air􀀉and􀀉moves􀀉on.􀀉But􀀉it’s􀀉still􀀉a􀀉bet􀀉—􀀉and􀀉
I’m􀀉making􀀉it􀀉four􀀉times􀀉a􀀉day.”􀀉


She􀀉shrugged.􀀉“Twenty􀀉minutes􀀉of􀀉playing􀀉dead,􀀉four􀀉times􀀉a􀀉day.􀀉It’s􀀉not􀀉
comfortable,􀀉but􀀉it’s􀀉survivable.􀀉The􀀉real􀀉danger􀀉isn’t􀀉the􀀉quiet􀀉periods􀀉—􀀉
it’s􀀉getting􀀉sloppy.􀀉Two􀀉ration􀀉wrappers􀀉in􀀉the􀀉trash􀀉where􀀉there􀀉should􀀉
only􀀉be􀀉one.􀀉A􀀉water􀀉level􀀉that􀀉dropped􀀉twice􀀉as􀀉fast􀀉as􀀉the􀀉meter􀀉expects.􀀉
Jung’s􀀉algorithm􀀉doesn’t􀀉watch􀀉the􀀉body􀀉—􀀉it􀀉watches􀀉the􀀉consumption􀀉
pattern.􀀉One􀀉miner􀀉uses􀀉one􀀉miner’s􀀉worth􀀉of􀀉everything.􀀉Two􀀉miners􀀉use􀀉
too􀀉much.”􀀉

Gage􀀉shook􀀉his􀀉head􀀉slowly.􀀉His􀀉sister􀀉had􀀉turned􀀉survival􀀉into􀀉an􀀉
engineering􀀉discipline.􀀉

Camilla􀀉looked􀀉up􀀉at􀀉her􀀉brother.􀀉“I􀀉know􀀉the􀀉sky’s􀀉bad,􀀉Gage􀀉—􀀉I􀀉built􀀉
my􀀉whole􀀉travel􀀉plan􀀉around􀀉hiding􀀉from􀀉it.􀀉But􀀉I􀀉don’t􀀉know􀀉how􀀉bad.􀀉
How􀀉long􀀉can􀀉a􀀉body􀀉actually􀀉take􀀉it?􀀉An􀀉hour?􀀉Three􀀉hours?􀀉Because􀀉if􀀉
it’s􀀉three,􀀉then􀀉someone􀀉with􀀉the􀀉right􀀉preparation􀀉could􀀉maybe􀀉make􀀉it􀀉
past􀀉the􀀉patrols􀀉and􀀉into􀀉the􀀉wastelands.􀀉If􀀉it’s􀀉one,􀀉the􀀉sky􀀉alone􀀉makes􀀉the􀀉
perimeter􀀉unnecessary.􀀉Trinity􀀉wouldn’t􀀉even􀀉need􀀉the􀀉drones.”􀀉

He􀀉looked􀀉up􀀉at􀀉the􀀉blistering􀀉sky.􀀉It􀀉was􀀉the􀀉great􀀉paradox􀀉of􀀉their􀀉lives.􀀉
The􀀉drones,􀀉the􀀉scanners,􀀉the􀀉checkpoints􀀉at􀀉the􀀉gate􀀉—􀀉all􀀉of􀀉it􀀉was􀀉
theater.􀀉Pappy􀀉had􀀉told􀀉them,􀀉when􀀉they􀀉were􀀉small,􀀉about􀀉the􀀉Great􀀉
Extraction􀀉—􀀉how􀀉the􀀉old􀀉world􀀉had􀀉burned􀀉through􀀉its􀀉own􀀉sky􀀉for􀀉
power.􀀉The􀀉miners􀀉had􀀉filled􀀉in􀀉the􀀉rest,􀀉in􀀉the􀀉kind􀀉of􀀉stories􀀉you􀀉heard􀀉
during􀀉the􀀉long􀀉descents:􀀉six􀀉hours􀀉under􀀉open􀀉sun􀀉and􀀉a􀀉body􀀉comes􀀉apart􀀉
at􀀉a􀀉level􀀉too􀀉small􀀉to􀀉see.􀀉The􀀉radiation􀀉didn’t􀀉just􀀉burn;􀀉it􀀉broke􀀉the􀀉bonds􀀉
the􀀉body􀀉was􀀉made􀀉of􀀉—􀀉chemistry􀀉taking􀀉apart􀀉what􀀉chemistry􀀉had􀀉built.􀀉
And􀀉Gerald􀀉—􀀉in􀀉the􀀉brief􀀉minutes􀀉after􀀉the􀀉fight􀀉—􀀉had􀀉said􀀉something􀀉
Gage􀀉hadn’t􀀉fully􀀉understood􀀉until􀀉now.􀀉The􀀉drones􀀉aren’t􀀉the􀀉cage.􀀉The􀀉
sky􀀉is.􀀉The􀀉drones􀀉are􀀉just􀀉there􀀉to􀀉make􀀉sure􀀉no􀀉one􀀉figures􀀉it􀀉out.􀀉

“The􀀉sky􀀉is􀀉the􀀉cage,”􀀉Gage􀀉muttered.􀀉The􀀉drones􀀉were􀀉only􀀉half􀀉the􀀉
prison.􀀉Even􀀉if􀀉you􀀉blinded􀀉them,􀀉even􀀉if􀀉you􀀉slipped􀀉through􀀉every􀀉gap􀀉in􀀉
Jung’s􀀉surveillance􀀉—􀀉where􀀉would􀀉you􀀉go?􀀉Trinity􀀉didn’t􀀉need􀀉to􀀉chase􀀉a􀀉


rebel.􀀉They􀀉just􀀉had􀀉to􀀉wait􀀉for􀀉the􀀉violet􀀉weight􀀉of􀀉the􀀉sun􀀉to􀀉melt􀀉the􀀉
fabric􀀉of􀀉a􀀉runner’s􀀉body􀀉before􀀉they􀀉reached􀀉the􀀉horizon.􀀉

And􀀉running􀀉to􀀉Miner􀀉Town􀀉was􀀉no􀀉answer􀀉either.􀀉Every􀀉shack􀀉there􀀉was􀀉
registered,􀀉every􀀉ration􀀉card􀀉metered,􀀉every􀀉consumption􀀉pattern􀀉logged.􀀉A􀀉
second􀀉body􀀉in􀀉someone􀀉else’s􀀉shack􀀉would􀀉show􀀉up􀀉in􀀉Jung’s􀀉data􀀉within􀀉
a􀀉day,􀀉and􀀉the􀀉punishment􀀉for􀀉sheltering􀀉ran􀀉through􀀉the􀀉whole􀀉household􀀉

—􀀉no􀀉family􀀉would􀀉risk􀀉it.􀀉The􀀉only􀀉real􀀉escape􀀉was􀀉the􀀉wastelands.􀀉Days􀀉of􀀉
travel.􀀉Sky􀀉no􀀉corridor􀀉could􀀉shield􀀉you􀀉from.􀀉And􀀉no􀀉one􀀉knew􀀉what􀀉was􀀉
actually􀀉out􀀉there.􀀉Maybe􀀉nothing.􀀉Maybe􀀉more􀀉Trinity.􀀉Maybe􀀉something􀀉
worse.􀀉The􀀉geography􀀉of􀀉running􀀉was􀀉a􀀉sequence􀀉of􀀉impossibilities􀀉stacked􀀉
on􀀉top􀀉of􀀉each􀀉other.􀀉
Around􀀉them,􀀉the􀀉metal􀀉walls􀀉of􀀉the􀀉shack-rows􀀉ticked􀀉as􀀉they􀀉cooled.􀀉The􀀉
sounds􀀉of􀀉Sector-1’s􀀉evening􀀉routine􀀉carried􀀉through􀀉the􀀉cooling􀀉air:􀀉the􀀉
distant􀀉clang􀀉of􀀉the􀀉intake􀀉bell,􀀉the􀀉shuffle􀀉of􀀉boots􀀉on􀀉hardpan,􀀉the􀀉sharp􀀉
bark􀀉of􀀉a􀀉guard􀀉counting􀀉heads􀀉at􀀉the􀀉gate.􀀉Closer,􀀉the􀀉domestic􀀉sounds􀀉—􀀉
tin􀀉pots􀀉clanking,􀀉a􀀉woman􀀉singing􀀉a􀀉lullaby􀀉in􀀉a􀀉language􀀉Gage􀀉didn’t􀀉
recognize,􀀉the􀀉steady􀀉scrape􀀉of􀀉someone􀀉sharpening􀀉a􀀉tool􀀉on􀀉stone.􀀉

“How􀀉do􀀉people􀀉live􀀉here?”􀀉Camilla􀀉asked,􀀉her􀀉voice􀀉quiet.􀀉

“They􀀉don’t􀀉live,”􀀉Gage􀀉said.􀀉“They􀀉operate.􀀉Water􀀉comes􀀉through􀀉the􀀉
main􀀉pipe􀀉twice􀀉a􀀉day􀀉—􀀉twenty􀀉minutes􀀉at􀀉dawn,􀀉twenty􀀉at􀀉dusk.􀀉Miss􀀉it􀀉
and􀀉you’re􀀉dry􀀉until􀀉the􀀉next􀀉cycle.􀀉Food􀀉is􀀉whatever􀀉the􀀉ration􀀉cart􀀉drops:􀀉
starch􀀉cubes,􀀉glucose􀀉syrup,􀀉sometimes􀀉a􀀉protein􀀉slab􀀉that􀀉tastes􀀉like􀀉wet􀀉
cardboard.􀀉Once􀀉a􀀉month,􀀉if􀀉you’ve􀀉hit􀀉your􀀉tonnage􀀉quota,􀀉they􀀉throw􀀉in􀀉
a􀀉salt􀀉packet.􀀉Salt’s􀀉the􀀉real􀀉currency􀀉—􀀉you􀀉can􀀉trade􀀉it􀀉for􀀉anything.”􀀉

“What􀀉about􀀉when􀀉someone􀀉gets􀀉sick?”􀀉

Gage􀀉laughed􀀉—􀀉a􀀉short,􀀉dry􀀉sound.􀀉“There’s􀀉a􀀉med-station􀀉near􀀉the􀀉main􀀉
gate.􀀉One􀀉room,􀀉one􀀉cot,􀀉one􀀉bottle􀀉of􀀉antiseptic􀀉that’s􀀉been􀀉refilled􀀉with􀀉
sump􀀉water􀀉so􀀉many􀀉times􀀉it’s􀀉just􀀉rust􀀉in􀀉a􀀉jar.􀀉If􀀉you’re􀀉hurt􀀉bad􀀉enough􀀉


to􀀉stop􀀉working,􀀉you􀀉go􀀉on􀀉the􀀉Outflow􀀉list.􀀉Lou’s􀀉people􀀉come􀀉for􀀉you􀀉at􀀉
night.”􀀉

Camilla􀀉absorbed􀀉this􀀉in􀀉silence,􀀉filing􀀉each􀀉detail􀀉into􀀉a􀀉structural􀀉map􀀉of􀀉
the􀀉sector’s􀀉dependencies.􀀉Every􀀉system􀀉had􀀉pressure􀀉points.􀀉

“What􀀉about􀀉families?􀀉Anyone􀀉here􀀉who􀀉isn’t􀀉solo?”􀀉

“Most􀀉miners􀀉here􀀉are􀀉solo􀀉—􀀉separated􀀉during􀀉intake􀀉sorting,􀀉or􀀉whose􀀉
families􀀉didn’t􀀉make􀀉it􀀉here􀀉alive.􀀉Men,􀀉women,􀀉doesn’t􀀉matter􀀉to􀀉the􀀉
system.􀀉The􀀉miners􀀉with􀀉families􀀉fight􀀉to􀀉be􀀉assigned􀀉the􀀉back-row􀀉shacks,􀀉
where􀀉the􀀉iron-ore􀀉mass􀀉is􀀉thickest.􀀉Two􀀉reasons.􀀉The􀀉first􀀉was􀀉the􀀉sky􀀉—􀀉
the􀀉rock􀀉blocked􀀉more􀀉of􀀉the􀀉radiation,􀀉and􀀉a􀀉kid􀀉born􀀉back-row􀀉might􀀉
make􀀉it􀀉a􀀉year􀀉or􀀉two􀀉longer􀀉before􀀉the􀀉bleed􀀉started􀀉taking􀀉pieces.􀀉The􀀉
second􀀉was􀀉the􀀉Shadow.􀀉Trinity􀀉already􀀉knew􀀉the􀀉families􀀉were􀀉there􀀉—􀀉
registered,􀀉ration-carded,􀀉accounted􀀉for􀀉—􀀉but􀀉the􀀉Shadow􀀉let􀀉them􀀉do􀀉the􀀉
small􀀉things􀀉that􀀉kept􀀉a􀀉family􀀉alive:􀀉share􀀉a􀀉sip􀀉beyond􀀉the􀀉meter,􀀉hide􀀉a􀀉bit􀀉
of􀀉scrap􀀉a􀀉kid􀀉picked􀀉up,􀀉eat􀀉together􀀉longer􀀉than􀀉the􀀉schedule􀀉allowed,􀀉
without􀀉Jung’s􀀉algorithm􀀉flagging􀀉the􀀉pattern.􀀉Most􀀉of􀀉those􀀉families􀀉came􀀉
up􀀉through􀀉Initiation􀀉generations􀀉back􀀉—􀀉Trinity􀀉built􀀉this􀀉colony􀀉with􀀉
their􀀉grandparents.􀀉The􀀉pods􀀉are􀀉how􀀉they􀀉top􀀉up􀀉the􀀉labor􀀉when􀀉the􀀉
Initiation􀀉doesn’t􀀉kill􀀉enough.􀀉Different􀀉systems,􀀉same􀀉machine.”􀀉

Camilla􀀉was􀀉quiet􀀉for􀀉a􀀉moment.􀀉“Then􀀉how􀀉was􀀉Pappy􀀉ever􀀉home?􀀉When􀀉
we􀀉were􀀉kids􀀉—􀀉he􀀉was􀀉already􀀉too􀀉broken􀀉for􀀉the􀀉deep􀀉shafts.􀀉He􀀉should􀀉
have􀀉been􀀉on􀀉the􀀉Outflow􀀉list􀀉years􀀉before􀀉the􀀉Coalball􀀉day.”􀀉

“Surface􀀉labor,”􀀉Gage􀀉said.􀀉“When􀀉a􀀉deep-shaft􀀉miner􀀉can’t􀀉hit􀀉tonnage􀀉
anymore􀀉—􀀉too􀀉old,􀀉too􀀉sick,􀀉too􀀉broken􀀉—􀀉the􀀉ones􀀉who􀀉still􀀉have􀀉
something􀀉left􀀉don’t􀀉go􀀉on􀀉the􀀉Outflow􀀉list􀀉right􀀉away.􀀉They􀀉get􀀉reassigned􀀉
up􀀉the􀀉ridge.􀀉Light􀀉work.􀀉Patching􀀉shacks,􀀉boiling􀀉water,􀀉watching􀀉the􀀉
children􀀉of􀀉the􀀉families􀀉who􀀉are􀀉still􀀉mining.􀀉Trinity􀀉keeps􀀉them􀀉around􀀉
because􀀉dead􀀉labor􀀉doesn’t􀀉raise􀀉the􀀉next􀀉generation.􀀉Surface􀀉work􀀉is􀀉the􀀉


bridge􀀉between􀀉the􀀉deep􀀉shafts􀀉and􀀉the􀀉disposal􀀉tunnels.􀀉Pappy􀀉was􀀉on􀀉it􀀉
for􀀉years.􀀉The􀀉Coalball􀀉day􀀉just􀀉moved􀀉him􀀉to􀀉the􀀉next􀀉stage􀀉early.”􀀉

Gage􀀉paused.􀀉“Same􀀉tier􀀉as􀀉your􀀉mother.􀀉I􀀉never􀀉knew􀀉exactly􀀉what􀀉the􀀉
mines􀀉or􀀉the􀀉Initiation􀀉did􀀉to􀀉her􀀉—􀀉nobody􀀉ever􀀉told􀀉us,􀀉and􀀉Mom􀀉didn’t􀀉
talk􀀉about􀀉it.􀀉What􀀉I􀀉knew􀀉was􀀉that􀀉she􀀉couldn’t􀀉take􀀉five􀀉steps􀀉without􀀉
breaking􀀉into􀀉a􀀉sweat.􀀉So􀀉she􀀉was􀀉always􀀉home,􀀉taking􀀉care􀀉of􀀉us.􀀉Trinity􀀉
classed􀀉her􀀉as􀀉surface-only􀀉when􀀉she􀀉was􀀉sixteen.􀀉She’s􀀉been􀀉on􀀉the􀀉rag􀀉
circuit􀀉and􀀉the􀀉back-row􀀉food􀀉rounds􀀉ever􀀉since.􀀉Thorton􀀉worked􀀉the􀀉deep;􀀉
she􀀉worked􀀉the􀀉ridge.􀀉That’s􀀉how􀀉the􀀉family􀀉kept􀀉eating.”􀀉

Camilla􀀉was􀀉quiet􀀉for􀀉a􀀉long􀀉moment.􀀉She􀀉had􀀉never􀀉thought􀀉of􀀉Pappy􀀉that􀀉
way􀀉—􀀉as􀀉someone􀀉Trinity􀀉had􀀉calculated􀀉was􀀉worth􀀉more􀀉alive􀀉than􀀉dead.􀀉
Or􀀉her􀀉mother,􀀉for􀀉that􀀉matter.􀀉The􀀉stories􀀉by􀀉the􀀉firelight,􀀉the􀀉secret􀀉
reading􀀉lessons,􀀉the􀀉half-portions􀀉of􀀉starch􀀉slid􀀉onto􀀉Gage’s􀀉plate,􀀉the􀀉
steady􀀉silent􀀉presence􀀉of􀀉Rita􀀉in􀀉every􀀉shack􀀉of􀀉her􀀉childhood􀀉—􀀉all􀀉of􀀉it􀀉
had􀀉felt􀀉like􀀉family,􀀉pure􀀉and􀀉uncalculated.􀀉But􀀉Trinity􀀉had􀀉measured􀀉the􀀉
value􀀉of􀀉letting􀀉them􀀉live􀀉too.􀀉Even􀀉love􀀉had􀀉been􀀉priced.􀀉

He􀀉pointed􀀉toward􀀉a􀀉cluster􀀉of􀀉shacks􀀉at􀀉the􀀉base􀀉of􀀉the􀀉coal􀀉mound.􀀉
“That’s􀀉the􀀉Nursery􀀉Row.􀀉About􀀉fifteen􀀉families.􀀉Whoever􀀉isn’t􀀉on􀀉shift􀀉
watches􀀉the􀀉kids􀀉so􀀉the􀀉rest􀀉can􀀉work􀀉the􀀉scrap􀀉piles.􀀉Old􀀉Marta􀀉runs􀀉what􀀉
passes􀀉for􀀉a􀀉school􀀉—􀀉surface-only􀀉since􀀉her􀀉hands􀀉lost􀀉the􀀉strength􀀉to􀀉
mine,􀀉but􀀉Trinity􀀉still􀀉found􀀉a􀀉use􀀉for􀀉her.􀀉She􀀉teaches􀀉the􀀉little􀀉ones􀀉their􀀉
letters􀀉using􀀉danger􀀉signs􀀉she􀀉pulled􀀉off􀀉decommissioned􀀉equipment.􀀉The􀀉
kids􀀉think􀀉T􀀉stands􀀉for􀀉‘Toxic’􀀉and􀀉U􀀉stands􀀉for􀀉‘Urgent.’􀀉They’re􀀉not􀀉
wrong.”􀀉

Camilla􀀉stared􀀉at􀀉the􀀉Nursery􀀉Row􀀉for􀀉a􀀉long􀀉time.􀀉“They’re􀀉building􀀉
something.􀀉Even􀀉here.􀀉Even􀀉with􀀉nothing.”􀀉

“That’s􀀉what􀀉people􀀉do,”􀀉Gage􀀉said.􀀉And􀀉for􀀉the􀀉first􀀉time􀀉in􀀉months,􀀉he􀀉
heard􀀉something􀀉in􀀉his􀀉own􀀉voice􀀉that􀀉surprised􀀉him􀀉—􀀉not􀀉anger,􀀉but􀀉


something􀀉almost􀀉like􀀉wonder.􀀉These􀀉people,􀀉ground􀀉down􀀉to􀀉their􀀉last􀀉
gram􀀉of􀀉strength,􀀉still􀀉had􀀉enough􀀉left􀀉to􀀉teach􀀉a􀀉child􀀉the􀀉alphabet.􀀉

As􀀉Gage􀀉sipped,􀀉a􀀉wet,􀀉racking􀀉cough􀀉tore􀀉through􀀉his􀀉chest.􀀉He􀀉doubled􀀉
over,􀀉clutching􀀉his􀀉ribs􀀉as􀀉he􀀉spat􀀉a􀀉glob􀀉of􀀉grey,􀀉coal-laced􀀉phlegm􀀉onto􀀉the􀀉
floor.􀀉Camilla􀀉said,􀀉“The􀀉mine􀀉isn’t􀀉just􀀉taking􀀉your􀀉time,􀀉Gage.􀀉It’s􀀉filling􀀉
your􀀉lungs􀀉with􀀉itself.􀀉You’re􀀉breathing􀀉the􀀉graveyard,􀀉and􀀉it’s􀀉building􀀉a􀀉
tomb􀀉inside􀀉your􀀉chest.”􀀉

Gage􀀉didn’t􀀉answer.􀀉He􀀉turned􀀉to􀀉sit􀀉with􀀉his􀀉face􀀉to􀀉the􀀉wall,􀀉and􀀉when􀀉
Camilla􀀉peered􀀉over􀀉his􀀉shoulder,􀀉her􀀉eyes􀀉widened.􀀉He􀀉was􀀉building􀀉a􀀉
meticulous􀀉wireframe􀀉model􀀉of􀀉the􀀉mine􀀉using􀀉nothing􀀉but􀀉splinters􀀉
shaved􀀉from􀀉pit-prop􀀉scraps􀀉—􀀉sitting􀀉over􀀉the􀀉fragile􀀉structure􀀉with􀀉the􀀉
intensity􀀉of􀀉a􀀉general,􀀉waiting􀀉for􀀉the􀀉map􀀉to􀀉take􀀉shape.􀀉

In􀀉the􀀉weeks􀀉that􀀉followed,􀀉the􀀉small􀀉operations􀀉of􀀉Camilla’s􀀉care􀀉settled􀀉
into􀀉a􀀉rhythm.􀀉Sealed􀀉gaps.􀀉Brewed􀀉chicory.􀀉Wet􀀉rags􀀉during􀀉sweep􀀉
windows.􀀉Gage􀀉was􀀉still􀀉being􀀉eroded􀀉by􀀉the􀀉mine􀀉—􀀉but􀀉slower􀀉now,􀀉
slower􀀉than􀀉the􀀉system􀀉expected.􀀉

It􀀉was􀀉Ben􀀉who􀀉first􀀉noticed􀀉that􀀉Gage’s􀀉cough􀀉was􀀉getting􀀉better.􀀉

Curious,􀀉Ben􀀉walked􀀉home􀀉with􀀉Gage􀀉one􀀉evening.􀀉He􀀉smelled􀀉it􀀉before􀀉
they􀀉reached􀀉the􀀉door􀀉—􀀉dark,􀀉earthy,􀀉impossible􀀉in􀀉Sector-1.􀀉A􀀉smell􀀉with􀀉
no􀀉place􀀉in􀀉their􀀉world.􀀉

When􀀉Gage􀀉pushed􀀉the􀀉door􀀉open,􀀉the􀀉smell􀀉hit􀀉stronger􀀉—􀀉and􀀉
underneath􀀉it,􀀉something􀀉else.􀀉The􀀉air􀀉inside􀀉the􀀉shack􀀉was􀀉clean.􀀉

He􀀉noticed􀀉it􀀉as􀀉soon􀀉as􀀉he􀀉crossed􀀉the􀀉threshold.􀀉The􀀉gaps􀀉sealed􀀉tight,􀀉the􀀉
dust􀀉cut􀀉down􀀉to􀀉almost􀀉nothing.􀀉A􀀉mug􀀉sat􀀉warming􀀉on􀀉the􀀉heating􀀉
element,􀀉already􀀉poured,􀀉waiting.􀀉

He􀀉stopped􀀉in􀀉the􀀉doorway.􀀉


Camilla􀀉was􀀉on􀀉the􀀉floor,􀀉re-wrapping􀀉the􀀉splinter􀀉model􀀉in􀀉cloth.􀀉She􀀉
looked􀀉up.􀀉It􀀉had􀀉been􀀉months􀀉since􀀉they’d􀀉been􀀉in􀀉the􀀉same􀀉room􀀉—􀀉not􀀉
since􀀉before􀀉Gage’s􀀉Initiation.􀀉

“Hey,􀀉Ben.”􀀉
“Hey,􀀉kid.”􀀉His􀀉voice􀀉caught􀀉on􀀉the􀀉word􀀉—􀀉somewhere􀀉between􀀉glad􀀉and􀀉

stricken.􀀉He􀀉took􀀉in􀀉the􀀉sealed􀀉gaps,􀀉the􀀉warm􀀉mug,􀀉the􀀉impossibility􀀉of􀀉
her􀀉being􀀉here.􀀉“Your􀀉brother􀀉is􀀉going􀀉to􀀉get􀀉us􀀉all􀀉killed.”􀀉
“Probably,”􀀉Camilla􀀉said.􀀉
“How?”􀀉
“Pappy’s􀀉old􀀉clearance.􀀉Magnetite􀀉on􀀉the􀀉chip.􀀉Scanner􀀉hit􀀉a􀀉Read􀀉Error􀀉

and􀀉the􀀉gate􀀉let􀀉me􀀉through.”􀀉

Ben􀀉exhaled􀀉slowly,􀀉a􀀉sound􀀉halfway􀀉between􀀉admiration􀀉and􀀉grief.􀀉He􀀉
didn’t􀀉say􀀉what􀀉he􀀉was􀀉thinking.􀀉He􀀉just􀀉said,􀀉“Good􀀉to􀀉see􀀉you􀀉anyway.”􀀉
“You􀀉too,􀀉Ben.”􀀉
Gage􀀉leaned􀀉against􀀉the􀀉wall􀀉and􀀉let􀀉them􀀉have􀀉the􀀉moment.􀀉It􀀉had􀀉been􀀉a􀀉

long􀀉time􀀉since􀀉he’d􀀉seen􀀉Ben􀀉look􀀉glad􀀉about􀀉anything.􀀉
“There’s􀀉chicory,”􀀉Camilla􀀉said,􀀉after􀀉the􀀉silence􀀉had􀀉settled.􀀉“If􀀉you􀀉want.”􀀉
Ben’s􀀉nostrils􀀉flared.􀀉“That’s􀀉what􀀉I’ve􀀉been􀀉smelling.􀀉What􀀉is􀀉it?”􀀉
“Hellhole􀀉Chicory.􀀉My􀀉own􀀉recipe.”􀀉
“Is􀀉it…􀀉is􀀉that􀀉what􀀉Pappy􀀉used􀀉to􀀉talk􀀉about?”􀀉
“The􀀉closest􀀉I’ve􀀉come.”􀀉
They􀀉sat.􀀉She􀀉poured.􀀉Ben􀀉drank􀀉in􀀉silence,􀀉and􀀉Camilla􀀉watched􀀉him􀀉—􀀉

watched􀀉him􀀉inhale􀀉the􀀉steam􀀉and􀀉close􀀉his􀀉eyes􀀉for􀀉a􀀉moment,􀀉watched􀀉her􀀉
brother􀀉not􀀉quite􀀉meeting􀀉anyone’s􀀉gaze􀀉because􀀉he􀀉was􀀉on􀀉the􀀉edge􀀉of􀀉


something􀀉he􀀉couldn’t􀀉hold.􀀉The􀀉shack,􀀉which􀀉had􀀉been􀀉a􀀉holding􀀉cell􀀉for􀀉
weeks,􀀉now􀀉had􀀉three􀀉people􀀉in􀀉it.􀀉It􀀉felt􀀉smaller.􀀉It􀀉felt,􀀉for􀀉the􀀉first􀀉time􀀉

Ben􀀉didn’t􀀉go􀀉back􀀉to􀀉his􀀉own􀀉shack􀀉that􀀉night.􀀉

He􀀉didn’t􀀉quite􀀉explain􀀉why.􀀉He􀀉didn’t􀀉have􀀉to.􀀉He􀀉dragged􀀉his􀀉pallet􀀉across􀀉

needed􀀉to􀀉have.􀀉

Otto􀀉followed􀀉two􀀉days􀀉after􀀉that.􀀉

He’d􀀉been􀀉watching􀀉Ben􀀉and􀀉Gage􀀉for􀀉the􀀉last􀀉two􀀉days􀀉—􀀉both􀀉of􀀉them􀀉
too􀀉steady,􀀉too􀀉quiet,􀀉like􀀉something􀀉neither􀀉would􀀉name.􀀉So􀀉on􀀉day􀀉three􀀉
evening􀀉he􀀉went􀀉to􀀉Ben’s􀀉shack􀀉to􀀉ask.􀀉Ben􀀉wasn’t􀀉there.􀀉Otto􀀉walked􀀉the􀀉
rest􀀉of􀀉the􀀉way􀀉to􀀉Gage’s􀀉row,􀀉pushed􀀉open􀀉the􀀉door,􀀉and􀀉saw􀀉Ben’s􀀉pallet􀀉
already􀀉against􀀉the􀀉wall,􀀉Camilla􀀉at􀀉the􀀉heating􀀉element􀀉stirring􀀉something􀀉
in􀀉a􀀉pot.􀀉He􀀉froze􀀉for􀀉half􀀉a􀀉second􀀉—􀀉the􀀉kind􀀉of􀀉pause􀀉a􀀉martial􀀉artist􀀉
takes􀀉when􀀉a􀀉situation􀀉isn’t􀀉what􀀉they􀀉expected􀀉and􀀉the􀀉body􀀉has􀀉to􀀉
recalibrate􀀉before􀀉the􀀉mind􀀉does.􀀉

“Cami.”􀀉

“Otto.”􀀉

He􀀉crossed􀀉the􀀉shack􀀉in􀀉three􀀉strides􀀉and􀀉pulled􀀉her􀀉off􀀉the􀀉floor􀀉into􀀉a􀀉hug􀀉
that􀀉lifted􀀉her􀀉feet􀀉off􀀉the􀀉ground.􀀉

“Put􀀉me􀀉down,􀀉you􀀉oaf.”􀀉

“Nope.”􀀉

“Otto.􀀉You’re􀀉going􀀉to􀀉break􀀉something.”􀀉

“It’d􀀉be􀀉worth􀀉it.”􀀉


He􀀉set􀀉her􀀉down.􀀉His􀀉eyes􀀉were􀀉wet,􀀉though􀀉he􀀉was􀀉pretending􀀉they􀀉
weren’t,􀀉and􀀉Camilla􀀉—􀀉who􀀉had􀀉already􀀉cried􀀉once􀀉that􀀉week􀀉for􀀉reasons􀀉
she􀀉wasn’t􀀉going􀀉to􀀉explain􀀉to􀀉any􀀉of􀀉them􀀉—􀀉pretended􀀉with􀀉him.􀀉She􀀉

Ben,􀀉sitting􀀉on􀀉the􀀉floor􀀉near􀀉the􀀉radio,􀀉didn’t􀀉look􀀉up􀀉from􀀉the􀀉coil􀀉he􀀉was􀀉
repairing.􀀉“Took􀀉you􀀉long􀀉enough.”􀀉

“Took􀀉me􀀉long􀀉enough􀀉for􀀉what?”􀀉

“To􀀉put􀀉it􀀉together.”􀀉

“Put􀀉what􀀉together?”􀀉

“The􀀉cleaner􀀉air.􀀉The􀀉chicory.􀀉The􀀉way􀀉Gage’s􀀉been􀀉sleeping􀀉through􀀉the􀀉

“Some􀀉of􀀉us􀀉have􀀉responsibilities,􀀉Ben.”􀀉

Camilla􀀉laughed􀀉—􀀉the􀀉first􀀉real􀀉laugh􀀉she’d􀀉let􀀉herself􀀉have􀀉in􀀉weeks.􀀉The􀀉
sound􀀉of􀀉it􀀉startled􀀉her,􀀉the􀀉way􀀉you􀀉get􀀉startled􀀉by􀀉something􀀉you’d􀀉
forgotten􀀉you􀀉knew􀀉how􀀉to􀀉do.􀀉

Otto􀀉brought􀀉his􀀉pallet􀀉over􀀉the􀀉next􀀉morning,􀀉along􀀉with􀀉a􀀉salvaged􀀉tarp􀀉
that􀀉Camilla􀀉stitched􀀉into􀀉a􀀉curtain􀀉so􀀉she􀀉could􀀉have􀀉a􀀉meter􀀉of􀀉privacy􀀉in􀀉
the􀀉corner.􀀉Otto􀀉slept􀀉nearest􀀉the􀀉door.􀀉Ben􀀉slept􀀉nearest􀀉the􀀉radio.􀀉Gage􀀉
worked􀀉on􀀉his􀀉splinter􀀉model􀀉well􀀉past􀀉lights-out􀀉and􀀉slept􀀉on􀀉the􀀉floor􀀉
beside􀀉it.􀀉

It􀀉wasn’t􀀉a􀀉home.􀀉But􀀉it􀀉was􀀉the􀀉closest􀀉any􀀉of􀀉them􀀉had􀀉come􀀉to􀀉one􀀉since􀀉
Pappy􀀉died.􀀉

Over􀀉the􀀉next􀀉few􀀉weeks,􀀉the􀀉cough􀀉turned.􀀉Camilla􀀉had􀀉been􀀉winning􀀉—􀀉
slowly,􀀉but􀀉winning􀀉—􀀉until􀀉Gage􀀉stopped􀀉sleeping.􀀉He􀀉was􀀉obsessed􀀉with􀀉
the􀀉splinter􀀉model,􀀉and􀀉his􀀉body􀀉had􀀉no􀀉spare􀀉hours􀀉to􀀉give.􀀉Each􀀉morning􀀉
he􀀉dragged􀀉himself􀀉to􀀉the􀀉mine􀀉on􀀉three􀀉or􀀉four􀀉hours􀀉of􀀉broken􀀉sleep;􀀉each􀀉


evening􀀉he􀀉came􀀉back􀀉a􀀉little􀀉more􀀉hollowed􀀉out􀀉—􀀉the􀀉grey􀀉phlegm􀀉
thicker,􀀉the􀀉fever􀀉a􀀉permanent􀀉low􀀉burn􀀉behind􀀉his􀀉eyes.􀀉Camilla􀀉couldn’t􀀉
stop􀀉it.􀀉There􀀉was􀀉no􀀉medicine􀀉in􀀉Miner􀀉Town,􀀉no􀀉antibiotics􀀉hidden􀀉in􀀉
the􀀉scrap􀀉piles,􀀉nothing􀀉in􀀉the􀀉rust-water􀀉med-station􀀉worth􀀉swallowing.􀀉So􀀉
she􀀉did􀀉what􀀉she􀀉could􀀉with􀀉what􀀉she􀀉had.􀀉Before􀀉dawn􀀉—􀀉after􀀉the􀀉
midnight􀀉scanner􀀉sweep􀀉had􀀉passed􀀉and􀀉before􀀉the􀀉shift-change􀀉window􀀉—􀀉
she􀀉boiled􀀉sump􀀉water􀀉and􀀉made􀀉him􀀉breathe􀀉the􀀉steam􀀉through􀀉a􀀉rag,􀀉a􀀉
crude􀀉attempt􀀉to􀀉loosen􀀉the􀀉crust􀀉building􀀉up􀀉in􀀉his􀀉chest.􀀉She􀀉stuffed􀀉fresh􀀉
rags􀀉into􀀉the􀀉gaps􀀉nearest􀀉his􀀉sleeping􀀉pallet􀀉to􀀉cut􀀉the􀀉dust􀀉he􀀉inhaled􀀉
during􀀉the􀀉few􀀉hours􀀉he􀀉wasn’t􀀉underground.􀀉She􀀉rationed􀀉the􀀉Hellhole􀀉
Chicory􀀉so􀀉there􀀉was􀀉always􀀉a􀀉warm􀀉mug􀀉waiting􀀉when􀀉he􀀉stumbled􀀉back􀀉
from􀀉the􀀉shaft􀀉—􀀉brewed􀀉during􀀉the􀀉safe􀀉windows,􀀉kept􀀉warm􀀉by􀀉body-
heat􀀉proximity􀀉rather􀀉than􀀉flame.􀀉She􀀉was􀀉just􀀉slowing􀀉down􀀉the􀀉rate􀀉at􀀉
which􀀉the􀀉mine􀀉was􀀉eating􀀉him􀀉alive.􀀉She􀀉couldn’t􀀉heal􀀉him,􀀉and􀀉she􀀉
couldn’t􀀉heal􀀉his􀀉obsession􀀉with􀀉the􀀉splinter􀀉model􀀉either􀀉—􀀉though,􀀉
secretly,􀀉she􀀉was􀀉glad􀀉it􀀉existed.􀀉The􀀉model􀀉had􀀉given􀀉him􀀉something􀀉the􀀉
mine􀀉had􀀉been􀀉trying􀀉to􀀉take􀀉from􀀉every􀀉other􀀉miner􀀉in􀀉Sector-1:􀀉a􀀉
purpose.􀀉The􀀉worse􀀉cough􀀉was􀀉the􀀉cost􀀉Gage􀀉was􀀉paying􀀉not􀀉to􀀉become􀀉a􀀉
hollowed-out􀀉stranger.􀀉Camilla􀀉did􀀉her􀀉best􀀉to􀀉keep􀀉him􀀉going.􀀉It􀀉wasn’t􀀉
enough􀀉—􀀉but􀀉it􀀉kept􀀉his􀀉legs􀀉under􀀉him,􀀉and􀀉in􀀉Miner􀀉Town,􀀉that􀀉was􀀉the􀀉
entire􀀉difference􀀉between􀀉living􀀉and􀀉being􀀉added􀀉to􀀉the􀀉Outflow􀀉list.􀀉

While􀀉the􀀉boys􀀉were􀀉at􀀉the􀀉mine,􀀉Camilla􀀉worked.􀀉She􀀉sat􀀉with􀀉the􀀉re-
engineered􀀉radio􀀉she􀀉had􀀉built􀀉from􀀉junk􀀉electronics􀀉—􀀉the􀀉same􀀉one􀀉she􀀉
had􀀉once􀀉gifted􀀉to􀀉Pappy.􀀉It􀀉ran􀀉on􀀉almost􀀉nothing:􀀉a􀀉sliver􀀉of􀀉salvaged􀀉
piezoelectric􀀉crystal􀀉wedged􀀉between􀀉two􀀉copper􀀉plates,􀀉harvesting􀀉the􀀉
ambient􀀉vibration􀀉of􀀉the􀀉Stubborn􀀉Carbon􀀉itself.􀀉Every􀀉tremor􀀉the􀀉
mountain􀀉produced􀀉—􀀉every􀀉groan,􀀉every􀀉hum􀀉—􀀉fed􀀉a􀀉trickle􀀉of􀀉current􀀉
into􀀉the􀀉device.􀀉The􀀉mine􀀉was􀀉powering􀀉the􀀉very􀀉tool􀀉that􀀉would􀀉eventually􀀉
be􀀉used􀀉to􀀉map􀀉its􀀉weaknesses.􀀉

Usually,􀀉the􀀉device􀀉emitted􀀉nothing􀀉but􀀉the􀀉crackle􀀉of􀀉dead􀀉air,􀀉a􀀉reminder􀀉
of􀀉the􀀉technological􀀉Stone􀀉Age􀀉they􀀉lived􀀉in.􀀉But􀀉here,􀀉on􀀉the􀀉jagged􀀉edge􀀉of􀀉


Sector-1,􀀉the􀀉static􀀉had􀀉a􀀉grit􀀉to􀀉it.􀀉It􀀉wasn’t􀀉just􀀉noise;􀀉it􀀉was􀀉a􀀉rhythmic,􀀉
digital􀀉thrum􀀉that􀀉vibrated􀀉through􀀉the􀀉speakers􀀉—􀀉a􀀉sound􀀉too􀀉clean􀀉and􀀉
too􀀉precise􀀉to􀀉be􀀉a􀀉ghost􀀉of􀀉the􀀉old􀀉world.􀀉Camilla􀀉adjusted􀀉the􀀉copper􀀉
coils,􀀉her􀀉brow􀀉furrowed􀀉as􀀉she􀀉looked􀀉at􀀉the􀀉walls􀀉of􀀉the􀀉shack,􀀉coated􀀉in􀀉a􀀉
fine􀀉layer􀀉of􀀉black􀀉dust.􀀉She􀀉realized􀀉then􀀉that􀀉the􀀉signal􀀉wasn’t􀀉just􀀉coming􀀉
through􀀉the􀀉air;􀀉it􀀉was􀀉hitching􀀉a􀀉ride􀀉on􀀉the􀀉soot.􀀉

She􀀉didn’t􀀉know􀀉the􀀉fancy􀀉words􀀉for􀀉it,􀀉but􀀉she􀀉knew􀀉how􀀉things􀀉worked.􀀉
The􀀉coal􀀉in􀀉Sector-1􀀉was􀀉pure􀀉carbon,􀀉and􀀉carbon􀀉loved􀀉to􀀉carry􀀉a􀀉current.􀀉
Because􀀉the􀀉mine􀀉was􀀉a􀀉graveyard􀀉of􀀉a􀀉razed􀀉civilization,􀀉the􀀉coal􀀉seams􀀉were􀀉
laced􀀉with􀀉the􀀉twisted􀀉copper􀀉guts􀀉and􀀉iron􀀉skeletons􀀉of􀀉the􀀉cities􀀉that􀀉came􀀉
before.􀀉To􀀉Camilla,􀀉the􀀉entire􀀉mountain􀀉wasn’t􀀉just􀀉rock;􀀉it􀀉was􀀉a􀀉giant􀀉
antenna􀀉that􀀉had􀀉been􀀉buried􀀉alive.􀀉Every􀀉shift,􀀉the􀀉machine􀀉drove􀀉a􀀉
thousand􀀉worn-out􀀉hands􀀉to􀀉scratch􀀉deeper􀀉into􀀉the􀀉exposed􀀉coal􀀉—􀀉and􀀉
every􀀉shift,􀀉the􀀉Pulse􀀉beneath􀀉grew􀀉louder.􀀉It􀀉was􀀉like􀀉a􀀉toothache􀀉that􀀉got􀀉
sharper􀀉the􀀉closer􀀉you􀀉got􀀉to􀀉the􀀉nerve.􀀉She􀀉didn’t􀀉tell􀀉Gage􀀉yet􀀉—􀀉he􀀉was􀀉
already􀀉obsessed􀀉with􀀉the􀀉physical􀀉splinters􀀉of􀀉his􀀉map􀀉—􀀉but􀀉she􀀉knew􀀉the􀀉
truth:􀀉the􀀉Skeleton􀀉was􀀉being􀀉uncovered,􀀉and􀀉the􀀉Pulse􀀉was􀀉already􀀉
screaming􀀉at􀀉them􀀉through􀀉the􀀉dust.􀀉

Four􀀉times􀀉that􀀉evening􀀉Camilla􀀉reminded􀀉him􀀉to􀀉drink􀀉his􀀉Hellhole􀀉
Chicory􀀉while􀀉it􀀉was􀀉still􀀉warm.􀀉He􀀉didn’t􀀉look􀀉up􀀉from􀀉the􀀉model.􀀉He􀀉was􀀉
focused􀀉on􀀉the􀀉structural􀀉joints,􀀉but􀀉his􀀉massive􀀉frame􀀉was􀀉doing􀀉
something􀀉he􀀉didn’t􀀉realize:􀀉he􀀉was􀀉shadowing􀀉the􀀉room.􀀉Standing􀀉between􀀉
the􀀉radio􀀉and􀀉the􀀉distant􀀉interference􀀉of􀀉the􀀉Trinity􀀉cities,􀀉his􀀉body􀀉acted􀀉as􀀉
a􀀉fleshy􀀉shield.􀀉

Then􀀉she􀀉saw􀀉it.􀀉Every􀀉time􀀉Gage􀀉leaned􀀉over􀀉a􀀉specific􀀉quadrant􀀉of􀀉the􀀉
splinter􀀉map,􀀉the􀀉garbage􀀉static􀀉from􀀉the􀀉surface􀀉died􀀉down.􀀉“Stay􀀉right􀀉
there,”􀀉she􀀉whispered,􀀉her􀀉hands􀀉flying􀀉over􀀉the􀀉copper􀀉coils.􀀉“You’re􀀉
blocking􀀉the􀀉surface􀀉noise.􀀉You’re􀀉the􀀉only􀀉reason􀀉I􀀉can􀀉hear􀀉the􀀉heartbeat􀀉
at􀀉all.”􀀉


Gage􀀉moved􀀉his􀀉hand􀀉toward􀀉the􀀉South􀀉Shaft􀀉splinter.􀀉It􀀉was􀀉caked􀀉in􀀉the􀀉
same􀀉fine􀀉carbon􀀉dust􀀉that􀀉stained􀀉his􀀉skin.􀀉As􀀉his􀀉fingers􀀉hovered􀀉a􀀉fraction􀀉
of􀀉an􀀉inch􀀉from􀀉the􀀉wood,􀀉the􀀉radio􀀉didn’t􀀉just􀀉crackle􀀉—􀀉it􀀉stabilized.􀀉The􀀉
Pulse􀀉became􀀉a􀀉sharp,􀀉rhythmic􀀉thud.􀀉“The􀀉soot,”􀀉Gage􀀉muttered,􀀉
watching􀀉the􀀉way􀀉the􀀉black􀀉dust􀀉on􀀉the􀀉wood􀀉seemed􀀉to􀀉respond􀀉—􀀉a􀀉faint􀀉
shiver,􀀉or􀀉maybe􀀉just􀀉his􀀉eyes􀀉making􀀉sense􀀉of􀀉what􀀉his􀀉ears􀀉were􀀉hearing.􀀉
“Something’s􀀉happening􀀉to􀀉it.􀀉Why?”􀀉

Camilla’s􀀉eyes􀀉went􀀉wide.􀀉“Because􀀉it’s􀀉not􀀉just􀀉dirt.􀀉It􀀉carries􀀉the􀀉current.􀀉
Pappy􀀉had􀀉a􀀉word􀀉for􀀉it􀀉—􀀉conductor.”􀀉

She􀀉whispered,􀀉her􀀉eyes􀀉on􀀉the􀀉vibrating􀀉radio.􀀉“More􀀉than􀀉a􀀉conductor.􀀉
This􀀉coal􀀉came􀀉from􀀉shredded􀀉cities,􀀉Gage.􀀉It’s􀀉laced􀀉with􀀉whatever􀀉was􀀉in􀀉
those􀀉cities􀀉—􀀉the􀀉wires,􀀉the􀀉circuits,􀀉the􀀉bones􀀉of􀀉the􀀉machines.􀀉All􀀉of􀀉it􀀉
ground􀀉down􀀉and􀀉pressed􀀉into􀀉the􀀉rock.􀀉That’s􀀉why􀀉the􀀉mountain􀀉is􀀉an􀀉
antenna.􀀉We􀀉aren’t􀀉just􀀉mining􀀉fuel;􀀉we’re􀀉mining􀀉the􀀉conductive􀀉remains􀀉
of􀀉the􀀉old􀀉world􀀉—􀀉metals􀀉that􀀉carry􀀉electricity􀀉the􀀉way􀀉veins􀀉carry􀀉blood.”􀀉

He􀀉realized􀀉the􀀉soot􀀉on􀀉the􀀉model􀀉was􀀉doing􀀉something􀀉unexpected.􀀉
Because􀀉his􀀉skin􀀉was􀀉damp􀀉with􀀉sweat􀀉and􀀉coal-grit,􀀉touching􀀉the􀀉dust-
caked􀀉wood􀀉created􀀉a􀀉faint􀀉connection􀀉—􀀉not􀀉a􀀉proper􀀉circuit,􀀉but􀀉enough􀀉
for􀀉the􀀉conductive􀀉dust􀀉to􀀉interact􀀉with􀀉the􀀉signal􀀉bleeding􀀉through􀀉the􀀉
walls.􀀉When􀀉he􀀉moved􀀉his􀀉hand􀀉along􀀉the􀀉soot-covered􀀉splinters,􀀉the􀀉radio’s􀀉
response􀀉shifted􀀉—􀀉not􀀉precisely,􀀉but􀀉noticeably,􀀉the􀀉way􀀉a􀀉compass􀀉needle􀀉
twitches􀀉near􀀉iron.􀀉

“It’s􀀉a􀀉map􀀉of􀀉the􀀉nerves,”􀀉Camilla􀀉breathed,􀀉watching􀀉the􀀉signal􀀉shift.􀀉
“You’ve􀀉got􀀉the􀀉Skeleton􀀉in􀀉wood,􀀉Gage,􀀉but􀀉the􀀉soot􀀉is􀀉giving􀀉us􀀉the􀀉Pulse.􀀉
When􀀉you􀀉touch􀀉different􀀉parts􀀉of􀀉the􀀉model,􀀉the􀀉signal􀀉gets􀀉louder􀀉or􀀉
quieter􀀉—􀀉I􀀉can’t􀀉tell􀀉you􀀉exact􀀉numbers,􀀉but􀀉I􀀉can􀀉hear􀀉where􀀉the􀀉energy􀀉is􀀉
concentrated􀀉and􀀉where􀀉it􀀉thins􀀉out.􀀉It’s􀀉rough,􀀉like􀀉reading􀀉a􀀉map􀀉by􀀉feel􀀉
in􀀉the􀀉dark.􀀉But􀀉it’s􀀉enough.”􀀉

“We􀀉know􀀉where􀀉it’s􀀉most􀀉vulnerable.”􀀉


Chapter􀀉11􀀉—􀀉The􀀉Stubborn􀀉Engine􀀉

Gage􀀉had􀀉been􀀉secretive􀀉about􀀉his􀀉plans,􀀉keeping􀀉family􀀉and􀀉friends􀀉in􀀉the􀀉
dark.􀀉But􀀉few􀀉things􀀉remain􀀉secret􀀉in􀀉the􀀉company􀀉of􀀉two􀀉best􀀉buddies􀀉and􀀉
a􀀉meddling􀀉sister.􀀉He􀀉woke􀀉that􀀉morning􀀉feeling􀀉more􀀉deadbeat􀀉than􀀉usual.􀀉
His􀀉lung􀀉infection􀀉was􀀉worsening,􀀉and􀀉he􀀉was􀀉running􀀉a􀀉fever􀀉—􀀉though􀀉
that􀀉wasn’t􀀉new.􀀉He􀀉had􀀉pushed􀀉through􀀉many􀀉days􀀉at􀀉the􀀉mine􀀉while􀀉
burning􀀉up.􀀉

This􀀉morning􀀉was􀀉different.􀀉Camilla􀀉was􀀉at􀀉the􀀉pot􀀉in􀀉the􀀉narrow􀀉window􀀉
between􀀉the􀀉midnight􀀉scanner􀀉sweep􀀉and􀀉the􀀉dawn􀀉shift-change􀀉—􀀉the􀀉
same􀀉window􀀉she􀀉used􀀉every􀀉day􀀉to􀀉boil􀀉water􀀉to􀀉help􀀉Gage􀀉inhale􀀉steam􀀉
and􀀉ease􀀉his􀀉breathing.􀀉She􀀉heard􀀉Gage􀀉cough􀀉from􀀉across􀀉the􀀉shack􀀉—􀀉the􀀉
same􀀉wet,􀀉racking􀀉sound􀀉she’d􀀉grown􀀉used􀀉to􀀉over􀀉the􀀉past􀀉weeks.􀀉But􀀉
when􀀉she􀀉glanced􀀉at􀀉the􀀉floor􀀉where􀀉he’d􀀉spat,􀀉the􀀉phlegm􀀉wasn’t􀀉grey.􀀉It􀀉
was􀀉streaked􀀉with􀀉red.􀀉Bright,􀀉unmistakable􀀉red􀀉threading􀀉through􀀉the􀀉
metallic􀀉sludge􀀉like􀀉copper􀀉wire􀀉through􀀉coal.􀀉

She􀀉knew􀀉what􀀉that􀀉meant.􀀉The􀀉crust􀀉inside􀀉his􀀉lungs􀀉had􀀉started􀀉tearing􀀉
the􀀉tissue􀀉underneath.􀀉Another􀀉twelve􀀉hours􀀉of􀀉breathing􀀉Urban􀀉
Anthracite􀀉on􀀉top􀀉of􀀉bleeding􀀉airways􀀉would􀀉turn􀀉an􀀉infection􀀉into􀀉
something􀀉his􀀉body􀀉couldn’t􀀉fight􀀉back􀀉from.􀀉She’d􀀉watched􀀉Soot-Wraiths􀀉
go􀀉from􀀉blood-cough􀀉to􀀉Outflow􀀉list􀀉in􀀉a􀀉matter􀀉of􀀉days.􀀉

Knowing􀀉Gage􀀉would􀀉never􀀉listen􀀉to􀀉her􀀉—􀀉he’d􀀉shove􀀉past􀀉her􀀉and􀀉pick􀀉up􀀉
the􀀉shovel􀀉the􀀉way􀀉he􀀉always􀀉did􀀉—􀀉she􀀉shook􀀉Ben􀀉and􀀉Otto􀀉awake􀀉on􀀉their􀀉
pallets.􀀉It􀀉didn’t􀀉take􀀉much􀀉explaining.􀀉She􀀉pointed􀀉at􀀉the􀀉red-streaked􀀉
phlegm􀀉on􀀉the􀀉floor,􀀉and􀀉Ben’s􀀉face􀀉went􀀉white.􀀉There􀀉was􀀉lots􀀉of􀀉shouting􀀉
after􀀉that,􀀉a􀀉little􀀉fist􀀉fighting,􀀉and􀀉some􀀉name-calling.􀀉Gage􀀉made􀀉it􀀉as􀀉far􀀉as􀀉
the􀀉door􀀉before􀀉the􀀉three􀀉of􀀉them􀀉dragged􀀉him􀀉back􀀉to􀀉his􀀉cot.􀀉Eventually,􀀉
all􀀉four􀀉decided􀀉to􀀉spend􀀉the􀀉day􀀉together􀀉—􀀉though􀀉“decided”􀀉was􀀉
generous.􀀉Gage􀀉had􀀉simply􀀉run􀀉out􀀉of􀀉breath􀀉to􀀉argue.􀀉


By􀀉mid-morning,􀀉they􀀉should􀀉have􀀉been􀀉dragged􀀉into􀀉the􀀉pits.􀀉In􀀉Miner􀀉
Town,􀀉being􀀉late􀀉was􀀉a􀀉defiance􀀉that􀀉usually􀀉brought􀀉the􀀉guards􀀉—􀀉or􀀉
worse,􀀉Hacksaw􀀉—􀀉to􀀉your􀀉door.􀀉

“Don’t􀀉worry􀀉about􀀉me,”􀀉Gage􀀉rasped.􀀉“Gerald…􀀉Badge􀀉732.􀀉Standing􀀉deal􀀉

—􀀉the􀀉day􀀉my􀀉body􀀉finally􀀉breaks,􀀉his􀀉runners􀀉ghost􀀉my􀀉number􀀉at􀀉intake.􀀉
Today’s􀀉that􀀉day.”􀀉
“And􀀉us?”􀀉Ben􀀉said.􀀉

“The􀀉deal􀀉was􀀉just􀀉for􀀉me.”􀀉

Ben􀀉and􀀉Otto􀀉exchanged􀀉a􀀉look.􀀉Neither􀀉of􀀉them􀀉had􀀉expected􀀉coverage􀀉for􀀉
themselves􀀉—􀀉Gerald􀀉had􀀉never􀀉met􀀉either􀀉of􀀉them.􀀉

“We’ll􀀉risk􀀉it,”􀀉Otto􀀉said.􀀉“If􀀉Hacksaw􀀉comes􀀉looking,􀀉we􀀉meet􀀉him􀀉at􀀉the􀀉
door.”􀀉

Ben􀀉nodded􀀉once.􀀉“Together.”􀀉

It􀀉was􀀉a􀀉high-risk􀀉gamble.􀀉The􀀉Shadow􀀉Economy􀀉had􀀉produced􀀉duplicate􀀉
chips􀀉—􀀉crude􀀉clones􀀉of􀀉the􀀉original􀀉badge􀀉serials,􀀉slipped􀀉to􀀉accomplices􀀉
who􀀉would􀀉scan􀀉them􀀉at􀀉intake􀀉right􀀉after􀀉their􀀉own.􀀉The􀀉gate􀀉system,􀀉built􀀉
to􀀉read􀀉serial􀀉numbers􀀉rather􀀉than􀀉faces,􀀉logged􀀉two􀀉miners􀀉arriving􀀉from􀀉
one􀀉body.􀀉The􀀉accomplices􀀉then􀀉had􀀉to􀀉make􀀉up􀀉Gage’s􀀉tonnage􀀉—􀀉
working􀀉through􀀉breaks,􀀉splitting􀀉his􀀉expected􀀉output􀀉between􀀉them,􀀉
paying􀀉for􀀉his􀀉absence􀀉in􀀉their􀀉own􀀉bodies.􀀉If􀀉a􀀉guard􀀉performed􀀉a􀀉Physical􀀉
Audit,􀀉the􀀉digital􀀉logs􀀉wouldn’t􀀉save􀀉them.􀀉The􀀉miners􀀉double-scanning􀀉
badges􀀉were􀀉risking􀀉a􀀉Clean􀀉Cut􀀉to􀀉buy􀀉Gage􀀉ten􀀉hours􀀉of􀀉invisibility.􀀉In􀀉
the􀀉ledger􀀉of􀀉the􀀉mine,􀀉Gage􀀉was􀀉at􀀉his􀀉station;􀀉in􀀉reality,􀀉he􀀉was􀀉a􀀉ghost􀀉
hidden􀀉by􀀉the􀀉mountain’s􀀉iron.􀀉

That􀀉afternoon,􀀉Gage􀀉felt􀀉slightly􀀉better􀀉after􀀉napping,􀀉though􀀉his􀀉internal􀀉
temperature􀀉still􀀉felt􀀉like􀀉an􀀉engine􀀉red-lining.􀀉To􀀉get􀀉some􀀉real􀀉food􀀉into􀀉
him,􀀉Camilla􀀉lifted􀀉the􀀉simmering􀀉pot􀀉off􀀉the􀀉heating􀀉element􀀉—􀀉porridge􀀉


cooked􀀉from􀀉grain􀀉she’d􀀉kept􀀉hidden􀀉behind􀀉her􀀉pallet􀀉since􀀉the􀀉day􀀉she􀀉
arrived.􀀉

“Ma􀀉traded􀀉for􀀉it􀀉before􀀉I􀀉left,”􀀉Camilla􀀉said,􀀉ladling􀀉thick􀀉wheat􀀉porridge􀀉
into􀀉the􀀉wooden􀀉bowls􀀉she’d􀀉salvaged􀀉from􀀉Pappy’s􀀉old􀀉kit.􀀉She’d􀀉cooked􀀉it􀀉
while􀀉Gage􀀉napped,􀀉Otto􀀉and􀀉Ben􀀉taking􀀉turns􀀉at􀀉the􀀉door􀀉watching􀀉for􀀉
Andre’s􀀉men􀀉—􀀉cracked􀀉grain,􀀉sump􀀉water,􀀉a􀀉pinch􀀉of􀀉stolen􀀉salt,􀀉
simmered􀀉low􀀉on􀀉the􀀉heating􀀉element.􀀉“She􀀉finally􀀉let􀀉go􀀉of􀀉that􀀉copper-
core􀀉radiator􀀉from􀀉the􀀉old􀀉world􀀉that􀀉Thorton􀀉had􀀉been􀀉hoarding􀀉for􀀉years.􀀉
Traded􀀉it􀀉to􀀉a􀀉scavenger􀀉from􀀉the􀀉North􀀉Ridge􀀉for􀀉two􀀉pounds􀀉of􀀉Surface􀀉
Wheat􀀉—􀀉grain􀀉grown􀀉in􀀉the􀀉high-altitude􀀉dirt􀀉near􀀉the􀀉Trinity􀀉border.􀀉I’ve􀀉
been􀀉saving􀀉it􀀉for􀀉when􀀉it􀀉mattered.”􀀉

She􀀉didn’t􀀉say􀀉for􀀉when􀀉I􀀉thought􀀉you􀀉might􀀉not􀀉make􀀉it.􀀉She􀀉didn’t􀀉need􀀉
to.􀀉

Gage􀀉pushed􀀉his􀀉bowl􀀉toward􀀉Otto,􀀉then􀀉Ben.􀀉Otto􀀉pretended􀀉he’d􀀉already􀀉
eaten􀀉his􀀉ration.􀀉Ben􀀉said􀀉Gage􀀉needed􀀉every􀀉bite􀀉to􀀉keep􀀉his􀀉strength􀀉up􀀉
and􀀉refused.􀀉Gage􀀉was􀀉too􀀉tired􀀉to􀀉push.􀀉

They􀀉sat􀀉for􀀉the􀀉meal􀀉—􀀉Gage􀀉eating􀀉the􀀉porridge,􀀉Camilla,􀀉Otto􀀉and􀀉Ben􀀉
breaking􀀉out􀀉their􀀉standard􀀉starch􀀉cubes􀀉so􀀉no􀀉one􀀉would􀀉go􀀉hungry.􀀉No􀀉
one􀀉ate􀀉casually.􀀉They􀀉watched􀀉Gage􀀉chew􀀉slowly,􀀉aware􀀉that􀀉he􀀉was􀀉eating􀀉
a􀀉piece􀀉of􀀉their􀀉father’s􀀉legacy􀀉—􀀉a􀀉radiator􀀉Thorton􀀉had􀀉scavenged􀀉and􀀉
guarded􀀉for􀀉years,􀀉now􀀉converted􀀉into􀀉grain􀀉and􀀉carried􀀉across􀀉checkpoints􀀉
by􀀉his􀀉daughter.􀀉Every􀀉spoonful􀀉was􀀉something􀀉his􀀉body􀀉hadn’t􀀉had􀀉in􀀉
months􀀉—􀀉complex􀀉grains,􀀉minerals,􀀉the􀀉quiet􀀉nutrients􀀉a􀀉starving􀀉immune􀀉
system􀀉needed􀀉to􀀉keep􀀉fighting.􀀉Real􀀉food.􀀉The􀀉kind􀀉working-class􀀉bodies􀀉
hadn’t􀀉seen􀀉since􀀉long􀀉before􀀉the􀀉chamber,􀀉the􀀉kind􀀉Trinity􀀉had􀀉stopped􀀉
distributing􀀉to􀀉Sector-1􀀉generations􀀉ago.􀀉It􀀉wouldn’t􀀉heal􀀉him.􀀉But􀀉it􀀉might􀀉
keep􀀉his􀀉lungs􀀉working􀀉long􀀉enough􀀉to􀀉matter.􀀉

“So,”􀀉Ben􀀉asked,􀀉“Camilla,􀀉you􀀉been􀀉missing􀀉stuff?􀀉Because􀀉every􀀉time􀀉I􀀉set􀀉
scrap􀀉wood􀀉aside􀀉by􀀉my􀀉pallet,􀀉it􀀉disappears􀀉by􀀉morning.”􀀉


Otto􀀉snorted.􀀉“I􀀉thought􀀉I􀀉had􀀉a􀀉rat􀀉problem.􀀉Figured􀀉one􀀉was􀀉nesting􀀉
under􀀉my􀀉pallet.”􀀉

Camilla􀀉laughed.􀀉“Gentlemen,􀀉meet􀀉your􀀉rat.”􀀉She􀀉tossed􀀉one􀀉of􀀉his􀀉pit-
prop􀀉splinters􀀉onto􀀉the􀀉floor􀀉at􀀉his􀀉feet.􀀉

Gage􀀉snapped,􀀉snatching􀀉it􀀉up􀀉and􀀉throwing􀀉it􀀉back􀀉at􀀉her.􀀉“I’m􀀉not􀀉
stealing􀀉splinters!”􀀉

“Don’t􀀉fool􀀉me,􀀉Gage.􀀉I’ve􀀉been􀀉watching􀀉you􀀉shave􀀉pit-prop􀀉scraps􀀉down􀀉
to􀀉matchstick􀀉size􀀉with􀀉your􀀉fingernails.􀀉Your􀀉obsession􀀉with􀀉building􀀉that􀀉
model􀀉is􀀉going􀀉over􀀉the􀀉limit.”􀀉

Camilla􀀉reached􀀉out􀀉to􀀉smack􀀉Gage’s􀀉head,􀀉then􀀉stopped.􀀉The􀀉fever􀀉was􀀉
still􀀉in􀀉him􀀉—􀀉dry􀀉and􀀉papery􀀉skin,􀀉heat􀀉coming􀀉off􀀉him􀀉in􀀉waves􀀉she􀀉could􀀉
feel􀀉from􀀉inches􀀉away.􀀉The􀀉nap􀀉had􀀉given􀀉him􀀉a􀀉little;􀀉the􀀉porridge􀀉would􀀉
give􀀉him􀀉a􀀉little􀀉more;􀀉neither􀀉was􀀉enough􀀉to􀀉undo􀀉the􀀉months􀀉of􀀉erosion􀀉
underneath.􀀉She􀀉looked􀀉at􀀉Ben.􀀉Ben􀀉looked􀀉at􀀉the􀀉floor􀀉where􀀉the􀀉
morning’s􀀉blood-streaked􀀉phlegm􀀉had􀀉dried􀀉to􀀉a􀀉dark􀀉stain.􀀉Nobody􀀉said􀀉
anything.􀀉They􀀉didn’t􀀉need􀀉to.􀀉

Before􀀉the􀀉silence􀀉could􀀉settle,􀀉a􀀉rhythmic􀀉thrum􀀉from􀀉Camilla’s􀀉junk-radio􀀉
attracted􀀉their􀀉attention.􀀉It􀀉wasn’t􀀉static;􀀉it􀀉was􀀉a􀀉deep􀀉vibration􀀉that􀀉made􀀉
the􀀉coal􀀉dust􀀉on􀀉the􀀉floor􀀉dance􀀉in􀀉tiny􀀉geometric􀀉patterns.􀀉

Gage􀀉doubled􀀉over􀀉as􀀉another􀀉cough􀀉wracked􀀉his􀀉lungs.􀀉He􀀉clutched􀀉his􀀉
ribs,􀀉gasping􀀉for􀀉air􀀉that􀀉felt􀀉like􀀉it􀀉was􀀉being􀀉pulled􀀉through􀀉a􀀉thick􀀉wool􀀉
blanket.􀀉When􀀉he􀀉spat􀀉onto􀀉the􀀉dirt􀀉floor,􀀉the􀀉phlegm􀀉was􀀉worse􀀉than􀀉the􀀉
morning􀀉—􀀉the􀀉red􀀉streaks􀀉thicker􀀉now,􀀉laced􀀉through􀀉the􀀉iridescent􀀉
metallic􀀉sheen􀀉of􀀉silver􀀉and􀀉copper􀀉dust􀀉that􀀉caught􀀉the􀀉dim􀀉light􀀉of􀀉the􀀉
shack.􀀉

He􀀉knew􀀉what􀀉was􀀉coating􀀉his􀀉lungs.􀀉Every􀀉miner􀀉did.􀀉Urban􀀉Anthracite􀀉

—􀀉the􀀉ground-up􀀉bones􀀉of􀀉dead􀀉cities,􀀉copper􀀉wiring􀀉and􀀉silicon􀀉chips􀀉
compressed􀀉back􀀉into􀀉coal.􀀉The􀀉dust􀀉was􀀉too􀀉fine􀀉for􀀉his􀀉airways􀀉to􀀉flush􀀉

and􀀉too􀀉toxic􀀉for􀀉his􀀉tissue􀀉to􀀉absorb,􀀉so􀀉his􀀉body􀀉did􀀉the􀀉only􀀉thing􀀉it􀀉knew􀀉
how􀀉to􀀉do.􀀉It􀀉burned.􀀉The􀀉fever􀀉wasn’t􀀉a􀀉malfunction.􀀉It􀀉was􀀉an􀀉immune􀀉
system􀀉running􀀉at􀀉full􀀉blast􀀉against􀀉an􀀉enemy􀀉that􀀉walked􀀉back􀀉into􀀉the􀀉
mine􀀉with􀀉him􀀉every􀀉morning.􀀉

Ben􀀉and􀀉Otto􀀉breathed􀀉the􀀉same􀀉air,􀀉but􀀉they􀀉weren’t􀀉drowning􀀉the􀀉way􀀉he􀀉
was.􀀉Gage􀀉was􀀉the􀀉biggest􀀉man􀀉in􀀉Sector-1,􀀉so􀀉the􀀉foremen􀀉sent􀀉him􀀉to􀀉the􀀉
deepest􀀉shafts􀀉—􀀉the􀀉tight,􀀉airless􀀉corridors􀀉where􀀉only􀀉a􀀉body􀀉like􀀉his􀀉could􀀉
hit􀀉tonnage􀀉in􀀉air􀀉that􀀉was􀀉more􀀉particle􀀉than􀀉oxygen.􀀉But􀀉size􀀉cut􀀉both􀀉
ways.􀀉His􀀉six-foot-five􀀉frame􀀉dragged􀀉each􀀉breath􀀉deeper,􀀉packed􀀉the􀀉dust􀀉
tighter􀀉into􀀉places􀀉his􀀉lungs􀀉would􀀉never􀀉reach􀀉again.􀀉The􀀉very􀀉strength􀀉
that􀀉kept􀀉him􀀉alive􀀉in􀀉the􀀉pits􀀉was􀀉the􀀉thing􀀉burning􀀉him􀀉out􀀉from􀀉the􀀉
inside.􀀉

A􀀉static􀀉hiss􀀉snagged􀀉at􀀉the􀀉edge􀀉of􀀉his􀀉hearing.􀀉The􀀉radio.􀀉He’d􀀉left􀀉it􀀉on􀀉
the􀀉crate􀀉by􀀉the􀀉wall,􀀉and􀀉now􀀉something􀀉was􀀉crawling􀀉through􀀉the􀀉noise.􀀉

Gage􀀉reached􀀉toward􀀉the􀀉sound,􀀉his􀀉trembling􀀉hand􀀉nearing􀀉the􀀉copper􀀉
coils.􀀉His􀀉salt-heavy􀀉skin􀀉completed􀀉a􀀉circuit.􀀉He􀀉wasn’t􀀉just􀀉a􀀉man􀀉
anymore;􀀉he􀀉was􀀉a􀀉220-pound􀀉antenna.􀀉The􀀉radio􀀉didn’t􀀉just􀀉hiss;􀀉it􀀉let􀀉out􀀉
a􀀉sharp,􀀉digital􀀉scream.􀀉

“It’s􀀉still􀀉screaming,”􀀉Camilla􀀉whispered,􀀉her􀀉eyes􀀉on􀀉the􀀉coils.􀀉“Gage,􀀉your􀀉
skin􀀉is􀀉completing􀀉the􀀉circuit.􀀉You’re􀀉the􀀉antenna.􀀉The􀀉mountain􀀉is􀀉the􀀉
broadcast.”􀀉

The􀀉coal􀀉dust􀀉on􀀉the􀀉table􀀉began􀀉moving􀀉in􀀉perfect,􀀉shivering􀀉rings.􀀉Gage􀀉
looked􀀉at􀀉the􀀉dust.􀀉Then􀀉at􀀉the􀀉splinter􀀉model􀀉under􀀉his􀀉cot.􀀉If􀀉the􀀉real􀀉
mountain􀀉was􀀉broadcasting􀀉through􀀉him,􀀉maybe􀀉the􀀉model􀀉would􀀉too.􀀉He􀀉
reached􀀉down􀀉and􀀉brushed􀀉the􀀉piece􀀉he􀀉had􀀉labeled􀀉South􀀉Shaft.􀀉Instantly,􀀉
the􀀉dust􀀉patterns􀀉shattered􀀉into􀀉chaos.􀀉

Otto􀀉leaned􀀉forward,􀀉tracking􀀉the􀀉splinter􀀉lines.􀀉He􀀉didn’t􀀉see􀀉wood􀀉and􀀉
glue;􀀉he􀀉saw􀀉pressure􀀉points.􀀉“Do􀀉it􀀉again,􀀉Gage.􀀉Touch􀀉a􀀉different􀀉piece.”􀀉


Gage􀀉did.􀀉The􀀉dust􀀉held􀀉its􀀉rings􀀉where􀀉his􀀉hand􀀉rested􀀉on􀀉the􀀉main􀀉
support􀀉pillar.􀀉Then􀀉he􀀉moved􀀉to􀀉a􀀉joint􀀉near􀀉the􀀉Lake􀀉Wall.􀀉The􀀉patterns􀀉
shattered􀀉again.􀀉

“The􀀉dust􀀉only􀀉breaks􀀉at􀀉certain􀀉pieces,”􀀉Camilla􀀉said,􀀉working􀀉it􀀉out􀀉as􀀉she􀀉
spoke.􀀉“Those􀀉pieces􀀉match􀀉where􀀉the􀀉mountain’s􀀉vibration􀀉concentrates.􀀉
A􀀉structure􀀉under􀀉load,􀀉hit􀀉at􀀉its􀀉natural􀀉frequency􀀉—􀀉it􀀉doesn’t􀀉just􀀉shake.􀀉
It􀀉fails.”􀀉

Otto’s􀀉eyes􀀉moved􀀉from􀀉the􀀉dust􀀉to􀀉Gage’s􀀉splinter􀀉model.􀀉“Then􀀉it’s􀀉not􀀉
just􀀉a􀀉map􀀉of􀀉the􀀉mine.􀀉It’s􀀉a􀀉map􀀉of􀀉where􀀉to􀀉strike.”􀀉

Gage􀀉felt􀀉a􀀉chill.􀀉He’d􀀉spent􀀉weeks􀀉building􀀉the􀀉model􀀉to􀀉find􀀉a􀀉weakness.􀀉
Otto􀀉had􀀉just􀀉shown􀀉him􀀉it􀀉was􀀉a􀀉weapon.􀀉

“The􀀉Skeleton􀀉and􀀉the􀀉Pulse,”􀀉Gage􀀉whispered.􀀉“We’re􀀉the􀀉audit􀀉Trinity􀀉
never􀀉saw􀀉coming.”􀀉

“I􀀉can􀀉map􀀉the􀀉shift-change􀀉windows,”􀀉Camilla􀀉said,􀀉already􀀉reaching􀀉for􀀉a􀀉
scrap􀀉of􀀉paper.􀀉“If􀀉I􀀉track􀀉Lou’s􀀉rotations􀀉for􀀉a􀀉week—”􀀉

“No.”􀀉

The􀀉word􀀉came􀀉out􀀉harder􀀉than􀀉he􀀉meant􀀉it.􀀉Camilla􀀉looked􀀉up.􀀉

“Not􀀉you,”􀀉Gage􀀉said.􀀉“Not􀀉any􀀉of􀀉you.”􀀉

Otto􀀉opened􀀉his􀀉mouth.􀀉Gage􀀉held􀀉up􀀉a􀀉hand􀀉and􀀉closed􀀉his􀀉eyes􀀉for􀀉a􀀉beat.􀀉
He􀀉could􀀉see􀀉Camilla􀀉unconscious􀀉in􀀉the􀀉coal􀀉dust􀀉at􀀉the􀀉Coalball􀀉game,􀀉
the􀀉whip􀀉still􀀉wound􀀉around􀀉her􀀉leg.􀀉He􀀉could􀀉see􀀉Pappy􀀉running.􀀉The􀀉
Pappy􀀉Debt􀀉had􀀉one􀀉signature􀀉on􀀉it,􀀉and􀀉his􀀉sister􀀉had􀀉already􀀉paid􀀉more􀀉
on􀀉it􀀉than􀀉she􀀉should􀀉have.􀀉

“Two􀀉things,”􀀉he􀀉said.􀀉“You􀀉listen􀀉without􀀉interrupting􀀉while􀀉I􀀉lay􀀉this􀀉out.􀀉
And􀀉you􀀉don’t􀀉move􀀉on􀀉any􀀉piece􀀉of􀀉it􀀉unless􀀉I􀀉say.􀀉Not􀀉a􀀉meeting,􀀉not􀀉a􀀉
scrap,􀀉not􀀉a􀀉footstep.􀀉You􀀉wait􀀉for􀀉me.”􀀉


Camilla􀀉looked􀀉at􀀉the􀀉paper􀀉in􀀉her􀀉hand.􀀉Then􀀉she􀀉nodded􀀉—􀀉once,􀀉slow.􀀉
Otto􀀉followed.􀀉Ben,􀀉from􀀉the􀀉floor:􀀉“Your􀀉call.”􀀉

It􀀉wasn’t􀀉agreement.􀀉They􀀉were􀀉giving􀀉him􀀉the􀀉room􀀉because􀀉he􀀉needed􀀉it,􀀉
not􀀉because􀀉they􀀉thought􀀉he􀀉was􀀉right.􀀉Gage􀀉saw􀀉the􀀉difference􀀉and􀀉kept􀀉
going􀀉anyway.􀀉

Gage􀀉took􀀉a􀀉deep􀀉breath,􀀉his􀀉face􀀉going􀀉grim􀀉and􀀉his􀀉eyes􀀉intense􀀉as􀀉the􀀉plan􀀉
finally􀀉began􀀉to􀀉load.􀀉He􀀉leaned􀀉over􀀉the􀀉splinter􀀉model,􀀉one􀀉finger􀀉tracing􀀉
the􀀉line􀀉from􀀉the􀀉lake􀀉boundary􀀉to􀀉the􀀉Lower􀀉Hub.􀀉“The􀀉water􀀉is􀀉the􀀉
weapon,”􀀉he􀀉started.􀀉“We􀀉don’t􀀉need􀀉explosives.􀀉We􀀉need—”􀀉

The􀀉cough􀀉hit􀀉him􀀉like􀀉a􀀉fist􀀉from􀀉the􀀉inside.􀀉Not􀀉the􀀉wet􀀉rattle􀀉they’d􀀉been􀀉
hearing􀀉all􀀉day􀀉—􀀉this􀀉was􀀉deeper,􀀉violent,􀀉a􀀉sound􀀉like􀀉something􀀉
structural􀀉giving􀀉way.􀀉He􀀉doubled􀀉over,􀀉hands􀀉slamming􀀉flat􀀉against􀀉the􀀉
dirt􀀉floor.􀀉The􀀉splinter􀀉model􀀉shuddered.􀀉When􀀉he􀀉tried􀀉to􀀉draw􀀉breath,􀀉
nothing􀀉came􀀉—􀀉just􀀉a􀀉thin,􀀉whistling􀀉wheeze,􀀉as􀀉if􀀉the􀀉airways􀀉had􀀉
narrowed􀀉to􀀉the􀀉width􀀉of􀀉a􀀉wire.􀀉

“Gage—”􀀉Camilla􀀉was􀀉on􀀉her􀀉knees􀀉beside􀀉him.􀀉

He􀀉spat.􀀉The􀀉phlegm􀀉that􀀉hit􀀉the􀀉floor􀀉was􀀉no􀀉longer􀀉streaked􀀉with􀀉red.􀀉It􀀉
was􀀉red􀀉—􀀉bright,􀀉vivid􀀉red􀀉with􀀉threads􀀉of􀀉grey􀀉running􀀉through􀀉it.􀀉The􀀉
ratio􀀉had􀀉flipped.􀀉

His􀀉eyes􀀉found􀀉Camilla’s.􀀉He􀀉tried􀀉to􀀉speak􀀉—􀀉the􀀉plan􀀉was􀀉right􀀉there,􀀉
loaded􀀉and􀀉ready,􀀉every􀀉detail􀀉mapped􀀉—􀀉but􀀉his􀀉mouth􀀉just􀀉opened􀀉and􀀉
closed,􀀉fishlike,􀀉soundless.􀀉The􀀉Architect􀀉had􀀉the􀀉blueprint􀀉loaded,􀀉but􀀉the􀀉
engine􀀉beneath􀀉him􀀉had􀀉just􀀉seized.􀀉

Ben􀀉caught􀀉him􀀉before􀀉his􀀉head􀀉hit􀀉the􀀉dirt.􀀉Gage’s􀀉body􀀉went􀀉limp􀀉in􀀉his􀀉
arms􀀉—􀀉220􀀉pounds􀀉of􀀉fever􀀉and􀀉bone􀀉dropping􀀉like􀀉a􀀉dead􀀉turbine.􀀉His􀀉
eyes􀀉rolled􀀉back,􀀉and􀀉the􀀉shack􀀉went􀀉quiet􀀉except􀀉for􀀉the􀀉thin,􀀉broken􀀉
whistle􀀉of􀀉air􀀉fighting􀀉its􀀉way􀀉through􀀉lungs􀀉that􀀉had􀀉finally􀀉buckled.􀀉


Camilla􀀉pressed􀀉her􀀉ear􀀉to􀀉his􀀉chest.􀀉She􀀉could􀀉hear􀀉it􀀉—􀀉the􀀉ragged,􀀉
shallow􀀉pull􀀉of􀀉breath􀀉still􀀉cycling,􀀉still􀀉working,􀀉but􀀉barely.􀀉Still􀀉there.􀀉Just􀀉
barely􀀉there.􀀉

“He’s􀀉breathing,”􀀉she􀀉said.􀀉“He’s􀀉breathing.”􀀉She􀀉said􀀉it􀀉twice􀀉because􀀉the􀀉
first􀀉time􀀉didn’t􀀉feel􀀉real.􀀉


Chapter􀀉12􀀉—􀀉The􀀉Splinter􀀉Architect􀀉

The􀀉two􀀉weeks􀀉following􀀉Gage’s􀀉collapse􀀉were􀀉a􀀉blur􀀉of􀀉shallow􀀉breaths􀀉and􀀉
bitter􀀉remedies.􀀉He􀀉hadn’t􀀉simply􀀉“gotten􀀉better”;􀀉he􀀉had􀀉been􀀉
painstakingly􀀉repaired.􀀉

Gerald􀀉made􀀉the􀀉first􀀉move.􀀉The􀀉morning􀀉after􀀉Gage􀀉went􀀉down,􀀉he􀀉pulled􀀉
strings􀀉through􀀉the􀀉Shadow􀀉Economy􀀉to􀀉get􀀉Gage􀀉reassigned􀀉from􀀉the􀀉deep􀀉
shafts􀀉to􀀉the􀀉upper􀀉tunnels􀀉—􀀉surface􀀉sorting􀀉and􀀉scrap􀀉hauling,􀀉the􀀉kind􀀉
of􀀉work􀀉they􀀉gave􀀉miners􀀉who􀀉were􀀉one􀀉bad􀀉shift􀀉from􀀉the􀀉Outflow􀀉list.􀀉It􀀉
kept􀀉Gage’s􀀉name􀀉in􀀉the􀀉intake􀀉logs,􀀉kept􀀉him􀀉visible􀀉enough􀀉to􀀉avoid􀀉a􀀉
Physical􀀉Audit,􀀉but􀀉cut􀀉his􀀉dust􀀉exposure􀀉to􀀉a􀀉fraction􀀉of􀀉what􀀉the􀀉deep􀀉
corridors􀀉poured􀀉into􀀉him.􀀉The􀀉cost􀀉wasn’t􀀉small.􀀉Gerald􀀉burned􀀉through􀀉
favors􀀉he’d􀀉spent􀀉years􀀉accumulating,􀀉and􀀉three􀀉miners􀀉took􀀉on􀀉extra􀀉
tonnage􀀉to􀀉cover􀀉the􀀉gap.􀀉Gage􀀉owed􀀉a􀀉debt􀀉he􀀉didn’t􀀉yet􀀉know􀀉the􀀉full􀀉
shape􀀉of.􀀉

Meanwhile,􀀉Camilla􀀉worked􀀉the􀀉days􀀉the􀀉boys􀀉were􀀉underground.􀀉She’d􀀉
spent􀀉weeks􀀉running􀀉the􀀉trade􀀉—􀀉a􀀉month’s􀀉worth􀀉of􀀉salt􀀉and􀀉a􀀉rusted􀀉
copper􀀉coil􀀉through􀀉Shadow􀀉Economy􀀉runners,􀀉in􀀉exchange􀀉for􀀉a􀀉cracked􀀉
medical􀀉filter.􀀉Trinity􀀉tech􀀉discarded􀀉because􀀉its􀀉casing􀀉was􀀉smashed,􀀉but􀀉its􀀉
inner􀀉mesh􀀉still􀀉held􀀉a􀀉charge.􀀉

She􀀉forced􀀉Gage􀀉to􀀉breathe􀀉through􀀉the􀀉device􀀉every􀀉evening􀀉when􀀉he􀀉
stumbled􀀉back􀀉from􀀉the􀀉upper􀀉tunnels􀀉and􀀉through􀀉the􀀉night􀀉while􀀉he􀀉
slept􀀉—􀀉the􀀉filter􀀉humming􀀉as􀀉it􀀉stripped􀀉the􀀉residual􀀉dust􀀉from􀀉every􀀉
breath􀀉before􀀉it􀀉reached􀀉his􀀉airways.􀀉For􀀉the􀀉first􀀉time􀀉in􀀉months,􀀉his􀀉lungs􀀉
were􀀉taking􀀉in􀀉clean􀀉air.􀀉Without􀀉fresh􀀉poison􀀉pouring􀀉in􀀉on􀀉top􀀉of􀀉the􀀉old,􀀉
his􀀉body􀀉could􀀉finally􀀉do􀀉what􀀉it􀀉had􀀉been􀀉trying􀀉to􀀉do􀀉all􀀉along:􀀉flush􀀉the􀀉
damage􀀉out.􀀉He􀀉spent􀀉mornings􀀉coughing􀀉grey,􀀉iridescent􀀉sludge􀀉into􀀉a􀀉tin􀀉
bucket􀀉—􀀉the􀀉toxic􀀉remnants􀀉of􀀉Urban􀀉Anthracite􀀉his􀀉lungs􀀉were􀀉finally􀀉
strong􀀉enough􀀉to􀀉expel.􀀉The􀀉combination􀀉of􀀉cleaner􀀉air􀀉during􀀉the􀀉day􀀉and􀀉


filtered􀀉breathing􀀉at􀀉night􀀉gave􀀉his􀀉immune􀀉system􀀉what􀀉it􀀉had􀀉never􀀉had􀀉
before:􀀉a􀀉chance􀀉to􀀉gain􀀉ground􀀉instead􀀉of􀀉just􀀉holding􀀉the􀀉line.􀀉By􀀉the􀀉
fourteenth􀀉day,􀀉the􀀉fever􀀉had􀀉retreated􀀉from􀀉red-line􀀉to􀀉a􀀉dull,􀀉manageable􀀉
ache.􀀉He􀀉wasn’t􀀉healed;􀀉his􀀉lungs􀀉still􀀉felt􀀉lined􀀉with􀀉sandpaper,􀀉and􀀉his􀀉
breath􀀉hitched􀀉whenever􀀉he􀀉moved􀀉too􀀉fast.􀀉But􀀉the􀀉patch􀀉held.􀀉The􀀉
immune􀀉war􀀉inside􀀉his􀀉chest􀀉had􀀉finally􀀉downgraded􀀉from􀀉full􀀉assault􀀉to􀀉a􀀉
bitter,􀀉grinding􀀉truce.􀀉He􀀉was􀀉just􀀉a􀀉man􀀉with􀀉a􀀉map,􀀉a􀀉shovel,􀀉and􀀉limited􀀉
time􀀉before􀀉the􀀉dust􀀉built􀀉up􀀉again.􀀉

As􀀉he􀀉pushed􀀉his􀀉cot􀀉aside􀀉to􀀉reveal􀀉the􀀉splinter􀀉model,􀀉his􀀉hands􀀉were􀀉
steady􀀉for􀀉the􀀉first􀀉time􀀉in􀀉a􀀉fortnight.􀀉The􀀉Architect􀀉was􀀉back􀀉online,􀀉even􀀉
if􀀉the􀀉engine􀀉beneath􀀉his􀀉ribs􀀉was􀀉still􀀉coughing􀀉smoke.􀀉

Ben􀀉took􀀉one􀀉look􀀉and􀀉snorted􀀉—􀀉half􀀉a􀀉laugh,􀀉half􀀉whatever􀀉his􀀉lungs􀀉
would􀀉still􀀉allow.􀀉Camilla􀀉didn’t􀀉bother􀀉trying􀀉to􀀉hide􀀉her􀀉grin.􀀉

“In􀀉spite􀀉of􀀉everything,􀀉I􀀉have􀀉to􀀉say􀀉—􀀉Gage?􀀉Seriously?􀀉Splinters?”􀀉Ben􀀉
wheezed.􀀉“Anyone􀀉else􀀉would􀀉have􀀉used􀀉paper,􀀉but􀀉not􀀉our􀀉Architect!”􀀉

Gage’s􀀉face􀀉flushed􀀉angry􀀉red.􀀉He􀀉shouted􀀉that􀀉they􀀉knew􀀉his􀀉hands􀀉had􀀉
been􀀉built􀀉for􀀉this.􀀉Pappy􀀉and􀀉Thorton􀀉had􀀉over-built􀀉every􀀉part􀀉of􀀉him􀀉for􀀉
the􀀉physical􀀉world,􀀉and􀀉that􀀉included􀀉how􀀉he􀀉thought.􀀉A􀀉drawing􀀉was􀀉
someone􀀉else’s􀀉idea􀀉of􀀉the􀀉mine.􀀉A􀀉model􀀉was􀀉the􀀉mine.􀀉

As􀀉the􀀉laughter􀀉died,􀀉he􀀉began􀀉tracing􀀉the􀀉splinter􀀉lines.􀀉Sector-1􀀉was􀀉
almost􀀉out􀀉of􀀉coal􀀉—􀀉decades􀀉of􀀉work􀀉down􀀉to􀀉scraps.􀀉For􀀉months􀀉now􀀉the􀀉
foremen􀀉had􀀉been􀀉pushing􀀉crews􀀉into􀀉the􀀉unstable􀀉sections􀀉near􀀉the􀀉lake􀀉
boundary,􀀉hunting􀀉silver.􀀉That􀀉was􀀉the􀀉seam􀀉they􀀉were􀀉going􀀉to􀀉ride􀀉to􀀉the􀀉
bottom.􀀉When􀀉it􀀉was􀀉dry,􀀉they’d􀀉let􀀉the􀀉rest􀀉of􀀉the􀀉sector􀀉rot􀀉—􀀉and􀀉
everyone􀀉in􀀉it.􀀉

Gage􀀉looked􀀉up􀀉from􀀉the􀀉model.􀀉His􀀉voice􀀉was􀀉flat,􀀉the􀀉way􀀉it􀀉got􀀉when􀀉
he’d􀀉stopped􀀉feeling􀀉and􀀉started􀀉calculating.􀀉“They’re􀀉going􀀉to􀀉work􀀉us􀀉
until􀀉the􀀉last􀀉seam􀀉is􀀉dry,􀀉and􀀉then􀀉they’ll􀀉send􀀉us􀀉to􀀉the􀀉Outflow.􀀉There’s􀀉
no􀀉version􀀉of􀀉this􀀉where􀀉they􀀉set􀀉us􀀉free􀀉because􀀉we􀀉were􀀉good.􀀉The􀀉only􀀉


language􀀉the􀀉Trinity􀀉understands􀀉is􀀉cost.􀀉So􀀉we􀀉make􀀉this􀀉mine􀀉cost􀀉more􀀉
to􀀉run􀀉than􀀉it’s􀀉worth.􀀉We􀀉don’t􀀉escape.􀀉We􀀉don’t􀀉negotiate.􀀉We􀀉hit􀀉them􀀉
where􀀉it􀀉bleeds􀀉—􀀉their􀀉most􀀉profitable􀀉vein􀀉—􀀉and􀀉we􀀉make􀀉sure􀀉it􀀉never􀀉
produces􀀉another􀀉gram.”􀀉

The􀀉shack􀀉was􀀉quiet.􀀉Ben􀀉stared􀀉at􀀉the􀀉model.􀀉Otto’s􀀉jaw􀀉tightened.􀀉

“They’re􀀉digging􀀉for􀀉gold􀀉and􀀉silver,􀀉but􀀉they’re􀀉going􀀉to􀀉find􀀉a􀀉grave,”􀀉
Otto􀀉muttered.􀀉

“Gage.”􀀉Ben’s􀀉voice􀀉was􀀉low.􀀉“Before􀀉we􀀉tear􀀉into􀀉this􀀉place􀀉—􀀉there’s􀀉
something􀀉you􀀉should􀀉hear.􀀉The􀀉old-timers􀀉say􀀉something􀀉in􀀉the􀀉pits.􀀉They􀀉
talk􀀉about􀀉the􀀉Clean􀀉World􀀉—􀀉a􀀉ghost􀀉city􀀉buried􀀉beneath􀀉the􀀉Junkyard.􀀉
Cold􀀉air􀀉that􀀉never􀀉stops􀀉and􀀉lights􀀉that􀀉don’t􀀉need􀀉a􀀉match.”􀀉

Gage􀀉looked􀀉up􀀉from􀀉the􀀉model.􀀉He􀀉thought􀀉of􀀉the􀀉rhythmic􀀉thrum􀀉from􀀉
Camilla’s􀀉radio􀀉—􀀉the􀀉sound􀀉that􀀉made􀀉the􀀉dust􀀉dance.􀀉It􀀉didn’t􀀉sound􀀉like􀀉
a􀀉ghost􀀉story;􀀉it􀀉sounded􀀉like􀀉a􀀉machine.􀀉

“Those􀀉are􀀉just􀀉stories,􀀉Ben.􀀉Myths􀀉for􀀉men􀀉too􀀉tired􀀉to􀀉keep􀀉digging.”􀀉

“Maybe.􀀉But􀀉they􀀉say􀀉the􀀉people􀀉down􀀉there􀀉aren’t􀀉miners.􀀉They’re􀀉
something􀀉else.􀀉And􀀉they􀀉don’t􀀉like􀀉visitors.”􀀉

Gage􀀉filed􀀉it􀀉away.􀀉If􀀉something􀀉was􀀉down􀀉there,􀀉he􀀉needed􀀉to􀀉make􀀉sure􀀉
his􀀉plan􀀉didn’t􀀉destroy􀀉it􀀉by􀀉accident.􀀉He􀀉held􀀉Ben’s􀀉eyes􀀉for􀀉a􀀉beat􀀉—􀀉long􀀉
enough􀀉that􀀉Ben􀀉would􀀉know􀀉he’d􀀉been􀀉heard,􀀉even􀀉if􀀉the􀀉public􀀉answer􀀉
hadn’t􀀉said􀀉so􀀉—􀀉then􀀉turned􀀉back􀀉to􀀉the􀀉model.􀀉

“The􀀉lake􀀉is􀀉here,􀀉in􀀉the􀀉Basin􀀉Sector,”􀀉Gage􀀉muttered,􀀉tracing􀀉the􀀉lowest􀀉
line.􀀉“The􀀉Sector-1􀀉silver􀀉veins􀀉and􀀉Trinity􀀉transport􀀉hubs􀀉sit􀀉right􀀉in􀀉the􀀉
drainage􀀉path.􀀉It’s􀀉a􀀉natural􀀉gravity-well.”􀀉

Otto􀀉leaned􀀉in,􀀉eyes􀀉narrowing.􀀉He􀀉pointed􀀉where􀀉the􀀉splinters􀀉were􀀉
reinforced􀀉with􀀉extra􀀉glue.􀀉“The􀀉Junkyard􀀉sits􀀉on􀀉the􀀉North-East􀀉Shelf􀀉—􀀉a􀀉
massive􀀉granite􀀉slab􀀉a􀀉hundred􀀉feet􀀉higher􀀉than􀀉the􀀉silver􀀉pits.”􀀉


“And􀀉the􀀉Junkyard?”􀀉Ben􀀉asked.􀀉

“Gravity􀀉is􀀉a􀀉hard􀀉rule,”􀀉Gage􀀉said.􀀉“We’re􀀉sinking􀀉the􀀉enemy’s􀀉ship,􀀉but􀀉
leaving􀀉the􀀉island􀀉untouched.”􀀉

Gage’s􀀉finger􀀉hovered􀀉over􀀉the􀀉splinter􀀉joint􀀉representing􀀉the􀀉Lake􀀉Wall􀀉—􀀉
the􀀉thin􀀉barrier􀀉holding􀀉back􀀉millions􀀉of􀀉tons􀀉of􀀉water.􀀉“It’s􀀉a􀀉hardware􀀉
solution,”􀀉he􀀉whispered.􀀉“If􀀉we􀀉snap􀀉this􀀉support,􀀉we􀀉don’t􀀉just􀀉stop􀀉
production􀀉—􀀉we􀀉drown􀀉the􀀉machine.􀀉We􀀉flood􀀉the􀀉Lower􀀉Hub,􀀉short􀀉out􀀉
the􀀉turbine􀀉floor,􀀉and􀀉destroy􀀉the􀀉Trinity􀀉transport􀀉pods.􀀉Their􀀉silver􀀉
pipeline􀀉dies.􀀉Permanently.”􀀉

“And􀀉the􀀉men?”􀀉Otto􀀉asked􀀉quietly.􀀉“If􀀉you􀀉crack􀀉that􀀉wall,􀀉the􀀉water􀀉
won’t􀀉differentiate􀀉between􀀉a􀀉guard’s􀀉boots􀀉and􀀉a􀀉miner’s􀀉skin.”􀀉

Gage􀀉didn’t􀀉flinch.􀀉“I’m􀀉not􀀉a􀀉murderer.􀀉I’ve􀀉been􀀉mapping􀀉the􀀉rotations􀀉
for􀀉weeks.􀀉There’s􀀉a􀀉ten-minute􀀉window􀀉during􀀉the􀀉Sector-Shift􀀉Change.􀀉
The􀀉Lower􀀉Hub􀀉is􀀉cleared􀀉of􀀉miners􀀉while􀀉the􀀉new􀀉shift􀀉is􀀉being􀀉processed􀀉
at􀀉the􀀉top􀀉of􀀉the􀀉lift.􀀉We􀀉hit􀀉it􀀉then.􀀉We􀀉blow􀀉the􀀉seam,􀀉the􀀉water􀀉claims􀀉the􀀉
sector,􀀉and􀀉the􀀉Trinity􀀉loses􀀉its􀀉most􀀉profitable􀀉silver􀀉vein.􀀉We􀀉snap􀀉their􀀉
straw􀀉—􀀉but􀀉we􀀉do􀀉it􀀉when􀀉the􀀉tunnels􀀉are􀀉empty􀀉of􀀉our􀀉own.”􀀉

It􀀉was􀀉a􀀉suicidal􀀉mission,􀀉and􀀉they􀀉all􀀉knew􀀉it.􀀉To􀀉plant􀀉the􀀉charge,􀀉someone􀀉
would􀀉have􀀉to􀀉be􀀉in􀀉the􀀉very􀀉tunnel􀀉the􀀉water􀀉would􀀉claim􀀉first.􀀉They􀀉
weren’t􀀉just􀀉planning􀀉a􀀉disaster.􀀉They􀀉were􀀉planning􀀉a􀀉race􀀉against􀀉a􀀉
drowning􀀉tide􀀉—􀀉and􀀉one􀀉of􀀉them􀀉was􀀉going􀀉to􀀉have􀀉to􀀉run􀀉it.􀀉

Nobody􀀉asked􀀉who􀀉someone 
was.􀀉They􀀉didn’t􀀉need􀀉to.􀀉

“I’ll􀀉go,”􀀉Otto􀀉said.􀀉“I’m􀀉faster􀀉than􀀉you.”􀀉

“You􀀉haven’t􀀉worked􀀉the􀀉deep.”􀀉Gage􀀉didn’t􀀉look􀀉up􀀉from􀀉the􀀉model.􀀉“I􀀉
have􀀉—􀀉two􀀉months􀀉of􀀉it.􀀉The􀀉water’s􀀉going􀀉to􀀉come􀀉fast􀀉and􀀉the􀀉lamps􀀉
will􀀉go.􀀉Whoever􀀉plants􀀉this􀀉has􀀉to􀀉know􀀉the􀀉corridors􀀉by􀀉hand.􀀉You􀀉don’t.􀀉
I􀀉do.”􀀉


“There􀀉has􀀉to􀀉be􀀉another􀀉way,”􀀉Ben􀀉said.􀀉

“There􀀉isn’t.”􀀉

Camilla􀀉said􀀉nothing.􀀉She􀀉just􀀉held􀀉Gage’s􀀉gaze,􀀉and􀀉Gage􀀉knew,􀀉looking􀀉at􀀉
her,􀀉that􀀉the􀀉promise􀀉she’d􀀉made􀀉him􀀉an􀀉hour􀀉ago􀀉had􀀉just􀀉gotten􀀉heavier􀀉
than􀀉either􀀉of􀀉them􀀉had􀀉thought􀀉it􀀉would.􀀉

He􀀉turned􀀉back􀀉to􀀉the􀀉model.􀀉The􀀉Lake􀀉Wall􀀉was􀀉the􀀉target.􀀉The􀀉water􀀉was􀀉
the􀀉weapon.􀀉What􀀉he􀀉didn’t􀀉have􀀉yet􀀉was􀀉the􀀉trigger􀀉—􀀉Trinity􀀉had􀀉built􀀉
that􀀉wall􀀉to􀀉hold,􀀉and􀀉whatever􀀉opened􀀉it􀀉on􀀉cue􀀉would􀀉have􀀉to􀀉know􀀉more􀀉
than􀀉Gage􀀉did􀀉about􀀉how􀀉their􀀉safety􀀉systems􀀉thought.􀀉There􀀉was􀀉one􀀉man􀀉
in􀀉Sector-1􀀉who􀀉might.􀀉

Gage􀀉spent􀀉the􀀉next􀀉two􀀉weeks􀀉hunting􀀉a􀀉ghost.􀀉He􀀉swapped􀀉shifts􀀉just􀀉to􀀉
get􀀉into􀀉the􀀉same􀀉ventilation􀀉loop􀀉as􀀉Badge􀀉732.􀀉He􀀉finally􀀉cornered􀀉Gerald􀀉
in􀀉a􀀉dead􀀉air􀀉pocket􀀉—􀀉a􀀉forgotten􀀉service􀀉tunnel􀀉where􀀉the􀀉rock􀀉was􀀉old􀀉
and􀀉the􀀉dust􀀉was􀀉thick.􀀉

Gerald􀀉didn’t􀀉even􀀉turn􀀉around.􀀉“You’ve􀀉got􀀉a􀀉heavy􀀉step􀀉for􀀉a􀀉stalker,􀀉kid.”􀀉

Gage􀀉froze􀀉as􀀉a􀀉small􀀉flame􀀉cut􀀉through􀀉the􀀉gloom.􀀉Gerald􀀉was􀀉lighting􀀉a􀀉
hand-rolled􀀉joint,􀀉the􀀉amber􀀉glow􀀉dancing􀀉off􀀉the􀀉deep,􀀉structural􀀉scars􀀉on􀀉
his􀀉face.􀀉

“Are􀀉you􀀉out􀀉of􀀉your􀀉mind?”􀀉Gage􀀉hissed.􀀉“A􀀉flame􀀉in􀀉a􀀉dead􀀉pocket?􀀉This􀀉
hole􀀉has􀀉got􀀉no􀀉air􀀉and􀀉bad􀀉gas,􀀉Gerald.􀀉You’re􀀉going􀀉to􀀉blow􀀉us􀀉both􀀉to􀀉
pieces.”􀀉

Gerald􀀉didn’t􀀉flinch.􀀉He􀀉took􀀉a􀀉drag􀀉and􀀉blew􀀉smoke􀀉toward􀀉a􀀉jagged􀀉split􀀉
in􀀉the􀀉rock.􀀉The􀀉smoke􀀉didn’t􀀉linger;􀀉it􀀉was􀀉pulled􀀉into􀀉the􀀉crack􀀉like􀀉a􀀉
ghost􀀉through􀀉a􀀉keyhole.􀀉

“The􀀉machines􀀉think􀀉this􀀉tunnel􀀉is􀀉a􀀉tomb.􀀉But􀀉if􀀉you􀀉live􀀉in􀀉the􀀉dark􀀉long􀀉
enough,􀀉you􀀉find􀀉where􀀉the􀀉mountain􀀉breathes.􀀉There’s􀀉a􀀉tiny􀀉leak􀀉here􀀉—􀀉
not􀀉enough􀀉for􀀉a􀀉breeze,􀀉but􀀉enough􀀉to􀀉pull􀀉the􀀉rot􀀉out.”􀀉


Gage􀀉let􀀉out􀀉a􀀉breath,􀀉his􀀉tall􀀉frame􀀉trembling􀀉slightly􀀉from􀀉the􀀉lingering􀀉
fatigue􀀉in􀀉his􀀉lungs.􀀉

“You􀀉saved􀀉Otto,”􀀉Gage􀀉said,􀀉dropping􀀉the􀀉defensive􀀉edge.􀀉“You􀀉didn’t􀀉have􀀉
to.􀀉You􀀉put􀀉your􀀉own􀀉badge􀀉on􀀉the􀀉line.􀀉And􀀉the􀀉upper􀀉tunnels􀀉—􀀉the􀀉
reassignment.􀀉I􀀉know􀀉what􀀉that􀀉cost􀀉you.”􀀉

Gerald􀀉shrugged.􀀉“I􀀉don’t􀀉like􀀉seeing􀀉good􀀉men􀀉go􀀉to􀀉waste.􀀉Otto’s􀀉a􀀉
fighter.􀀉You’re􀀉a􀀉planner.􀀉The􀀉mine􀀉eats􀀉both.”􀀉

“My􀀉sister􀀉is􀀉making􀀉a􀀉meal􀀉two􀀉shifts􀀉from􀀉now,”􀀉Gage􀀉said,􀀉the􀀉offer􀀉
hanging􀀉heavy􀀉in􀀉the􀀉air.􀀉He􀀉felt􀀉a􀀉sharp􀀉pang􀀉of􀀉guilt.􀀉Camilla􀀉had􀀉rationed􀀉
the􀀉last􀀉of􀀉the􀀉Surface􀀉Wheat􀀉into􀀉careful􀀉portions􀀉—􀀉just􀀉enough􀀉to􀀉
stretch􀀉through􀀉his􀀉recovery.􀀉Offering􀀉a􀀉share􀀉to􀀉a􀀉man􀀉who’d􀀉already􀀉given􀀉
them􀀉more􀀉than􀀉they􀀉could􀀉repay􀀉meant􀀉the􀀉rest􀀉of􀀉them􀀉would􀀉go􀀉without􀀉
for􀀉days.􀀉But􀀉some􀀉debts􀀉couldn’t􀀉wait.􀀉

“Real􀀉flatbread.􀀉Surface􀀉Wheat􀀉and􀀉salt.􀀉No􀀉guard-chow.􀀉Come􀀉to􀀉the􀀉
shack.􀀉Let􀀉us􀀉pay􀀉what􀀉we􀀉can.”􀀉

Gerald􀀉really􀀉looked􀀉at􀀉him􀀉for􀀉the􀀉first􀀉time􀀉—􀀉past􀀉the􀀉soot,􀀉at􀀉the􀀉man􀀉
underneath.􀀉He􀀉saw􀀉desperation,􀀉but􀀉also􀀉spine.􀀉

“I􀀉haven’t􀀉eaten􀀉anything􀀉resembling􀀉a􀀉meal􀀉in􀀉three􀀉years.􀀉I’ll􀀉be􀀉there.”􀀉


Chapter􀀉13􀀉—􀀉We􀀉Have􀀉a􀀉Guest􀀉

Gage􀀉entered􀀉the􀀉shack􀀉with􀀉a􀀉look􀀉that􀀉almost􀀉set􀀉off􀀉an􀀉alarm􀀉—􀀉he􀀉was􀀉
smiling.􀀉In􀀉a􀀉place􀀉where􀀉breath􀀉was􀀉a􀀉struggle􀀉and􀀉every􀀉day􀀉was􀀉a􀀉
calculation􀀉for􀀉survival,􀀉a􀀉genuine􀀉smile􀀉was􀀉contraband.􀀉Camilla􀀉didn’t􀀉
smile􀀉back.􀀉She􀀉exchanged􀀉a􀀉sharp􀀉look􀀉with􀀉Otto􀀉and􀀉Ben,􀀉and􀀉before􀀉
Gage􀀉could􀀉drop􀀉his􀀉shovel,􀀉they’d􀀉pulled􀀉him􀀉to􀀉the􀀉back􀀉of􀀉the􀀉shack􀀉and􀀉
sat􀀉him􀀉down􀀉hard􀀉on􀀉a􀀉crate.􀀉

“Sit,”􀀉Otto􀀉commanded.􀀉

“You’re􀀉beaming,􀀉Gage,”􀀉Camilla􀀉whispered.􀀉“And􀀉in􀀉this􀀉shack,􀀉a􀀉smile􀀉is􀀉a􀀉
leak.􀀉What􀀉have􀀉you􀀉done?”􀀉

Gage􀀉told􀀉them.􀀉He’d􀀉found􀀉Gerald􀀉—􀀉Badge􀀉732􀀉—􀀉cornered􀀉him􀀉in􀀉a􀀉
dead􀀉air􀀉pocket,􀀉and􀀉invited􀀉him􀀉to􀀉dinner.􀀉Two􀀉shifts􀀉from􀀉now.􀀉Here.􀀉

The􀀉silence􀀉that􀀉followed􀀉was􀀉the􀀉kind􀀉that􀀉precedes􀀉either􀀉an􀀉explosion􀀉or􀀉
a􀀉burial.􀀉

“Badge􀀉732?”􀀉Camilla’s􀀉voice􀀉went􀀉flat.􀀉“A􀀉man􀀉who􀀉hit􀀉Otto􀀉in􀀉the􀀉dark?􀀉
You􀀉invited􀀉him􀀉to􀀉our􀀉shack?”􀀉

“He􀀉saved􀀉us,”􀀉Gage􀀉rasped.􀀉“He􀀉got􀀉me􀀉the􀀉upper􀀉tunnels􀀉for􀀉two􀀉weeks.􀀉
Burned􀀉through􀀉years􀀉of􀀉favors.􀀉I’d􀀉be􀀉on􀀉the􀀉Outflow􀀉list􀀉right􀀉now􀀉if􀀉it􀀉
wasn’t􀀉for􀀉him.”􀀉

A􀀉wet􀀉breath.􀀉“And􀀉he’s􀀉more􀀉than􀀉a􀀉debt.􀀉I􀀉want􀀉him􀀉in.􀀉He􀀉sees􀀉things􀀉in􀀉
the􀀉rock􀀉the􀀉rest􀀉of􀀉us􀀉don’t.”􀀉

“Or􀀉he’s􀀉bait,”􀀉Ben􀀉said􀀉quietly.􀀉“What􀀉if􀀉he’s􀀉working􀀉for􀀉Jung?􀀉Feeding􀀉us􀀉
trust􀀉so􀀉we􀀉open􀀉the􀀉door?􀀉One􀀉wrong􀀉move􀀉and􀀉Lou’s􀀉people􀀉come􀀉for􀀉us􀀉
in􀀉the􀀉night􀀉—􀀉all􀀉of􀀉us,􀀉Gage.􀀉Not􀀉just􀀉you.”􀀉


Heavy􀀉silence,􀀉broken􀀉only􀀉by􀀉the􀀉thrum􀀉of􀀉drills􀀉above.􀀉

“He􀀉knows􀀉things,”􀀉Gage􀀉said.􀀉“He􀀉knows􀀉where􀀉the􀀉mountain􀀉breathes.􀀉I􀀉
need􀀉him􀀉for􀀉the􀀉Lake􀀉Wall.􀀉And􀀉I􀀉need􀀉you􀀉three􀀉to􀀉help􀀉me􀀉make􀀉this􀀉
happen.􀀉We􀀉can’t􀀉pay􀀉what􀀉we􀀉actually􀀉owe􀀉him􀀉—􀀉not􀀉with􀀉a􀀉dinner.􀀉But􀀉
we􀀉can􀀉show􀀉him􀀉we’re􀀉the􀀉kind􀀉of􀀉people􀀉worth􀀉investing􀀉in.”􀀉

Camilla􀀉watched􀀉him􀀉for􀀉a􀀉long􀀉beat.􀀉Finally,􀀉a􀀉short􀀉nod.􀀉“We􀀉do􀀉it􀀉during􀀉
the􀀉Signal􀀉Shadow.􀀉But􀀉if􀀉his􀀉eyes􀀉drift􀀉toward􀀉anything􀀉he􀀉shouldn’t􀀉see,􀀉
I’m􀀉putting􀀉a􀀉spike􀀉through􀀉the􀀉back􀀉of􀀉his􀀉neck.”􀀉

The􀀉days􀀉that􀀉followed􀀉were􀀉a􀀉scavenged􀀉sacrifice.􀀉Each􀀉of􀀉them􀀉paid􀀉a􀀉price􀀉
to􀀉put􀀉food􀀉on􀀉the􀀉table,􀀉and􀀉none􀀉of􀀉it􀀉came􀀉cheap.􀀉

Gage􀀉had􀀉emptied􀀉his􀀉salt􀀉cache􀀉on􀀉a􀀉tough􀀉mountain􀀉marmot􀀉—􀀉mostly􀀉
gristle􀀉but􀀉smelling􀀉like􀀉real􀀉meat.􀀉Otto􀀉traded􀀉his􀀉Recovery􀀉Rotation,􀀉
working􀀉a􀀉Double􀀉Intake􀀉—􀀉twenty-eight􀀉hours􀀉straight􀀉in􀀉the􀀉pits􀀉—􀀉to􀀉
secure􀀉the􀀉scavenger􀀉who􀀉trapped􀀉it.􀀉Ben􀀉traded􀀉his􀀉Water􀀉Quota􀀉for􀀉the􀀉
next􀀉three􀀉shifts,􀀉handing􀀉over􀀉his􀀉hydration􀀉tabs􀀉to􀀉a􀀉guard’s􀀉cook􀀉in􀀉
exchange􀀉for􀀉the􀀉fermented􀀉berry􀀉skins􀀉that􀀉made􀀉the􀀉Refined􀀉Mash.􀀉In􀀉a􀀉
world􀀉where􀀉the􀀉sun􀀉sucked􀀉the􀀉moisture􀀉from􀀉your􀀉pores􀀉in􀀉minutes,􀀉Ben􀀉
was􀀉risking􀀉dehydration􀀉so􀀉Gerald􀀉could􀀉have􀀉a􀀉drink􀀉that􀀉didn’t􀀉taste􀀉like􀀉
silt.􀀉Camilla􀀉surrendered􀀉the􀀉last􀀉portion􀀉of􀀉the􀀉Surface􀀉Wheat􀀉—􀀉the􀀉final􀀉
remnant􀀉of􀀉what􀀉she’d􀀉carried􀀉across􀀉the􀀉checkpoints,􀀉rationed􀀉carefully􀀉
through􀀉Gage’s􀀉recovery􀀉and􀀉now􀀉given􀀉up􀀉for􀀉this􀀉one􀀉gamble.􀀉

The􀀉meal􀀉cost􀀉more􀀉than􀀉marmot􀀉and􀀉mash.􀀉He’d􀀉better􀀉be􀀉worth􀀉it.􀀉

By􀀉evening,􀀉the􀀉meal􀀉was􀀉ready.􀀉They􀀉had􀀉only􀀉just􀀉sat􀀉down􀀉when􀀉the􀀉
knock􀀉came.􀀉

Gage􀀉opened􀀉the􀀉door.􀀉Gerald􀀉stood􀀉there,􀀉the􀀉long􀀉coat􀀉of􀀉a􀀉Patroller􀀉
tattered􀀉and􀀉oil-stained,􀀉but􀀉his􀀉posture􀀉precise􀀉as􀀉a􀀉structural􀀉pillar.􀀉His􀀉
piercing􀀉blue􀀉eyes􀀉scanned􀀉the􀀉room􀀉—􀀉and􀀉stopped.􀀉The􀀉splinter􀀉model􀀉


was􀀉half-visible􀀉beneath􀀉the􀀉cot,􀀉a􀀉corner􀀉of􀀉its􀀉fragile􀀉framework􀀉poking􀀉
out􀀉past􀀉the􀀉blanket􀀉Camilla􀀉had􀀉draped􀀉over􀀉it.􀀉

Gerald􀀉looked􀀉at􀀉it.􀀉Looked􀀉at􀀉Gage.􀀉Said􀀉nothing.􀀉

Camilla’s􀀉hand􀀉moved􀀉toward􀀉the􀀉iron􀀉spike􀀉she􀀉kept􀀉behind􀀉the􀀉door􀀉
frame.􀀉Gage􀀉stepped􀀉between􀀉them.􀀉“It’s􀀉not􀀉what􀀉you􀀉think,”􀀉he􀀉said,􀀉
though􀀉they􀀉both􀀉knew􀀉it􀀉was􀀉exactly􀀉what􀀉Gerald􀀉thought.􀀉

Gerald􀀉looked􀀉past􀀉Gage􀀉to􀀉the􀀉model.􀀉Then􀀉back.􀀉“If􀀉I􀀉was􀀉going􀀉to􀀉call􀀉
this􀀉in,􀀉son,􀀉you􀀉wouldn’t􀀉have􀀉made􀀉it􀀉to􀀉dinner.”􀀉He􀀉stepped􀀉inside.􀀉“We􀀉
eat􀀉first.􀀉Talk􀀉after.”􀀉

The􀀉dinner􀀉was􀀉quiet􀀉at􀀉first,􀀉thick􀀉with􀀉the􀀉smell􀀉of􀀉roasted􀀉marmot􀀉and􀀉
the􀀉char􀀉of􀀉flatbread􀀉Camilla􀀉had􀀉pulled􀀉off􀀉the􀀉stove-stone.􀀉They􀀉ate􀀉with􀀉
their􀀉hands􀀉—􀀉there􀀉were􀀉no􀀉utensils􀀉in􀀉the􀀉shack􀀉that􀀉weren’t􀀉also􀀉tools􀀉—􀀉
tearing􀀉strips􀀉of􀀉gristly􀀉meat􀀉from􀀉the􀀉bone􀀉and􀀉chewing􀀉slowly.􀀉In􀀉Miner􀀉
Town,􀀉you􀀉didn’t􀀉bolt􀀉your􀀉food.􀀉You􀀉made􀀉it􀀉last.􀀉You􀀉made􀀉every􀀉calorie􀀉
count,􀀉because􀀉the􀀉next􀀉one􀀉wasn’t􀀉guaranteed.􀀉

Gerald􀀉ate􀀉with􀀉a􀀉discipline􀀉that􀀉surprised􀀉them.􀀉He􀀉didn’t􀀉grab􀀉or􀀉rush.􀀉
He􀀉took􀀉measured􀀉portions,􀀉chewed􀀉each􀀉bite􀀉to􀀉nothing,􀀉and􀀉paused􀀉
between􀀉mouthfuls􀀉as􀀉if􀀉giving􀀉his􀀉body􀀉time􀀉to􀀉register􀀉that􀀉real􀀉food􀀉was􀀉
actually􀀉arriving.􀀉It􀀉was􀀉the􀀉eating􀀉style􀀉of􀀉a􀀉man􀀉who􀀉had􀀉learned􀀉hunger􀀉
not􀀉as􀀉a􀀉temporary􀀉discomfort􀀉but􀀉as􀀉a􀀉permanent􀀉condition􀀉to􀀉be􀀉
managed.􀀉

“This􀀉marmot,”􀀉Gerald􀀉said􀀉after􀀉a􀀉long􀀉silence.􀀉“You􀀉trapped􀀉it􀀉yourself?”􀀉

Camilla􀀉shook􀀉her􀀉head,􀀉watching􀀉Gerald􀀉carefully.􀀉“Gage􀀉emptied􀀉his􀀉salt􀀉
cache􀀉for􀀉the􀀉meat.􀀉Otto􀀉traded􀀉a􀀉Double􀀉Intake􀀉to􀀉pay􀀉the􀀉scavenger􀀉who􀀉
trapped􀀉it.􀀉Twenty-eight􀀉hours􀀉in􀀉the􀀉pits.”􀀉

Gerald’s􀀉hand􀀉stopped􀀉halfway􀀉to􀀉the􀀉meat.􀀉He􀀉looked􀀉at􀀉Otto􀀉—􀀉really􀀉
looked􀀉at􀀉him􀀉—􀀉and􀀉something􀀉shifted􀀉behind􀀉his􀀉eyes.􀀉The􀀉calculation􀀉of􀀉
a􀀉man􀀉reassessing􀀉the􀀉value􀀉of􀀉the􀀉people􀀉around􀀉him.􀀉


“And􀀉the􀀉flatbread?”􀀉

“My􀀉mother’s􀀉last􀀉copper-core􀀉radiator,”􀀉Gage􀀉said.􀀉“My􀀉father’s􀀉legacy,􀀉
traded􀀉for􀀉two􀀉pounds􀀉of􀀉Surface􀀉Wheat.􀀉This􀀉is􀀉what’s􀀉left􀀉of􀀉it.”􀀉

Gerald􀀉set􀀉down􀀉his􀀉food.􀀉The􀀉shack􀀉was􀀉silent􀀉except􀀉for􀀉the􀀉distant􀀉hum􀀉
of􀀉the􀀉drills􀀉and􀀉the􀀉faint􀀉crackle􀀉of􀀉the􀀉heating􀀉element.􀀉When􀀉he􀀉spoke,􀀉
his􀀉voice􀀉was􀀉different􀀉—􀀉stripped􀀉of􀀉the􀀉dry􀀉sarcasm,􀀉exposing􀀉something􀀉
raw􀀉beneath.􀀉

“In􀀉Trinity-2,􀀉I􀀉used􀀉to􀀉throw􀀉away􀀉more􀀉food􀀉in􀀉a􀀉week􀀉than􀀉you’ve􀀉put􀀉
on􀀉this􀀉table.􀀉I􀀉had􀀉a􀀉refrigeration􀀉unit􀀉the􀀉size􀀉of􀀉this􀀉room,􀀉stocked􀀉with􀀉
fruits􀀉I􀀉couldn’t􀀉even􀀉name.􀀉I􀀉ate􀀉because􀀉I􀀉was􀀉bored,􀀉not􀀉because􀀉I􀀉was􀀉
hungry.”􀀉He􀀉picked􀀉up􀀉the􀀉flatbread􀀉again,􀀉turned􀀉it􀀉over􀀉in􀀉his􀀉scarred􀀉
fingers.􀀉“This􀀉is􀀉the􀀉most􀀉expensive􀀉meal􀀉I’ve􀀉ever􀀉eaten.”􀀉

He􀀉meant􀀉the􀀉cost􀀉in􀀉human􀀉hours,􀀉in􀀉pain,􀀉in􀀉the􀀉slow􀀉erosion􀀉of􀀉bodies􀀉
already􀀉running􀀉on􀀉empty.􀀉Gage􀀉understood.􀀉He􀀉nodded􀀉once.􀀉

After􀀉they􀀉ate,􀀉they􀀉sat􀀉by􀀉the􀀉small􀀉heating􀀉element.􀀉The􀀉Refined􀀉Mash􀀉
went􀀉around􀀉—􀀉a􀀉bottle􀀉of􀀉something􀀉that􀀉burned􀀉going􀀉down􀀉and􀀉spread􀀉
warmth􀀉through􀀉the􀀉gut􀀉like􀀉a􀀉slow-motion􀀉coal􀀉fire.􀀉Gerald􀀉winced􀀉at􀀉the􀀉
taste􀀉but􀀉took􀀉a􀀉second􀀉pull.􀀉

“I􀀉know􀀉you’re􀀉planning􀀉something,”􀀉Gerald􀀉said.􀀉“And􀀉you􀀉want􀀉my􀀉help.􀀉
But􀀉my􀀉wild􀀉days􀀉are􀀉past.􀀉All􀀉I􀀉want􀀉is􀀉to􀀉live􀀉the􀀉rest􀀉of􀀉my􀀉life􀀉peacefully.”􀀉

The􀀉light􀀉in􀀉Gage’s􀀉eyes􀀉went􀀉out.􀀉“I’m􀀉not􀀉sure􀀉what􀀉gave􀀉you􀀉that􀀉idea.􀀉
This􀀉was􀀉a􀀉gesture􀀉of􀀉appreciation.􀀉It􀀉was􀀉nice􀀉having􀀉you.”􀀉

Gerald􀀉smiled,􀀉leaning􀀉on􀀉a􀀉heavy,􀀉hollow-pipe􀀉cane􀀉—􀀉the􀀉same􀀉length􀀉of􀀉
mine􀀉pipe􀀉he’d􀀉carried􀀉for􀀉three􀀉years,􀀉worn􀀉smooth􀀉by􀀉his􀀉grip.􀀉“Right􀀉
back􀀉at􀀉ya.”􀀉

“Gerald,”􀀉Camilla􀀉interrupted.􀀉“You􀀉don’t􀀉look􀀉weak􀀉enough􀀉for􀀉a􀀉cane.􀀉
What􀀉happened?”􀀉


Gerald􀀉stopped.􀀉He􀀉looked􀀉at􀀉the􀀉group􀀉—􀀉the􀀉Engine,􀀉the􀀉Eyes,􀀉the􀀉
Circuitry,􀀉and􀀉the􀀉Fighter.􀀉Slowly,􀀉he􀀉pulled􀀉up􀀉his􀀉oil-stained􀀉pant􀀉leg.􀀉

It􀀉wasn’t􀀉a􀀉shining􀀉rod.􀀉It􀀉was􀀉a􀀉masterpiece􀀉of􀀉illegal􀀉salvage.􀀉Piston-driven􀀉
struts􀀉from􀀉a􀀉discarded􀀉drone,􀀉held􀀉together􀀉by􀀉high-tensile􀀉mining􀀉wire􀀉
and􀀉rusted􀀉springs.􀀉It􀀉looked􀀉like􀀉a􀀉piece􀀉of􀀉the􀀉mine􀀉grafted􀀉onto􀀉his􀀉bone.􀀉

“Andre􀀉Lockhart􀀉hacksawed􀀉the􀀉first􀀉one􀀉off􀀉on􀀉Highway􀀉2B􀀉three􀀉years􀀉
ago,”􀀉Gerald􀀉whispered.􀀉“Trinity􀀉gave􀀉me􀀉a􀀉Grade􀀉A􀀉prosthetic􀀉leg,􀀉then􀀉
fired􀀉me􀀉and􀀉stopped􀀉paying􀀉for􀀉it.􀀉A􀀉few􀀉weeks􀀉later􀀉it􀀉seized􀀉up􀀉under􀀉me􀀉
at􀀉a􀀉coffee􀀉shop􀀉—􀀉left􀀉me􀀉a􀀉paperweight􀀉bolted􀀉to􀀉bone.􀀉They􀀉didn’t􀀉know􀀉
I􀀉knew􀀉how􀀉to􀀉rebuild􀀉the􀀉machine.”􀀉

“Gerald…”􀀉Gage􀀉breathed.􀀉He􀀉had􀀉no􀀉words.􀀉

“Another􀀉story􀀉for􀀉another􀀉day,”􀀉Gerald􀀉said,􀀉his􀀉cane􀀉clicking􀀉as􀀉he􀀉
stepped􀀉out􀀉into􀀉the􀀉night.􀀉

They􀀉stood􀀉in􀀉silence.􀀉Somewhere􀀉in􀀉the􀀉dark,􀀉a􀀉man􀀉with􀀉a􀀉leg􀀉of􀀉drone􀀉
struts􀀉was􀀉walking􀀉home􀀉—􀀉and􀀉their􀀉secret􀀉was􀀉walking􀀉with􀀉him.􀀉


Chapter􀀉14􀀉—􀀉Phantom􀀉Leg􀀉

Life􀀉was􀀉good.􀀉

A􀀉stable􀀉job,􀀉a􀀉lovely􀀉girlfriend,􀀉and􀀉a􀀉high-rise􀀉apartment􀀉where􀀉the􀀉air􀀉was􀀉
always􀀉cool.􀀉Trinity-2􀀉was􀀉an􀀉island􀀉of􀀉luxury,􀀉oblivious􀀉to􀀉the􀀉fact􀀉that􀀉
their􀀉climate􀀉arrays􀀉and􀀉mag-lev􀀉lifts􀀉were􀀉powered􀀉by􀀉the􀀉bone-grinding􀀉
labor􀀉of􀀉the􀀉colonies.􀀉To􀀉the􀀉elites,􀀉the􀀉crisis􀀉was􀀉a􀀉headline.􀀉To􀀉Gerald,􀀉life􀀉
was􀀉a􀀉high-performance􀀉engine.􀀉

Gerald􀀉was􀀉the􀀉poster􀀉boy􀀉of􀀉young􀀉achievers􀀉—􀀉a􀀉Grid􀀉Architect􀀉who􀀉
rerouted􀀉gigawatts􀀉with􀀉a􀀉flick􀀉of􀀉his􀀉wrist.􀀉While􀀉miners􀀉broke􀀉their􀀉backs􀀉
pulling􀀉coal,􀀉Gerald􀀉sat􀀉in􀀉a􀀉glass-walled􀀉office,􀀉digitally􀀉harvesting􀀉their􀀉
power􀀉and􀀉trading􀀉it􀀉across􀀉the􀀉Trinity􀀉grid􀀉like􀀉high-stakes􀀉poker.􀀉He􀀉
didn’t􀀉drive􀀉the􀀉silent􀀉electric􀀉pods􀀉of􀀉his􀀉colleagues.􀀉He􀀉drove􀀉a􀀉Legacy􀀉V8Interceptor
􀀉—􀀉a􀀉fossil-fuel􀀉relic􀀉from􀀉the􀀉ruins􀀉of􀀉the􀀉21st􀀉century,􀀉its􀀉high-
compression􀀉engine􀀉a􀀉prehistoric􀀉roar􀀉that􀀉terrified􀀉the􀀉silent􀀉pods􀀉of􀀉his􀀉
peers.􀀉To􀀉the􀀉elite,􀀉the􀀉smell􀀉of􀀉burning􀀉gasoline􀀉wasn’t􀀉a􀀉pollutant;􀀉it􀀉was􀀉
the􀀉ultimate􀀉status􀀉symbol􀀉—􀀉the􀀉scent􀀉of􀀉a􀀉world􀀉they􀀉had􀀉consumed.􀀉

Every􀀉weekend,􀀉he􀀉pushed􀀉the􀀉Interceptor􀀉onto􀀉Highway􀀉2B,􀀉the􀀉Outflow􀀉
route􀀉connecting􀀉Trinity-2􀀉to􀀉its􀀉colonies.􀀉No􀀉cameras,􀀉no􀀉surveillance.􀀉He􀀉
could􀀉rev􀀉the􀀉beast􀀉until􀀉the􀀉exhaust􀀉glowed􀀉cherry􀀉red.􀀉

One􀀉such􀀉day,􀀉pushing􀀉the􀀉envelope􀀉with􀀉his􀀉girlfriend􀀉beside􀀉him,􀀉he􀀉saw􀀉a􀀉
broken-down􀀉truck.􀀉A􀀉massive􀀉man􀀉stood􀀉by􀀉the􀀉stalled􀀉engine,􀀉silhouetted􀀉
against􀀉orange􀀉dust.􀀉Gerald􀀉saw􀀉the􀀉rags,􀀉the􀀉grime􀀉of􀀉the􀀉colonies,􀀉and􀀉
simply􀀉smirked􀀉as􀀉he􀀉roared􀀉past.􀀉

But􀀉the􀀉elite􀀉had􀀉underestimated􀀉the􀀉grime􀀉of􀀉the􀀉road.􀀉

A􀀉sharp􀀉crack.􀀉His􀀉pressurized􀀉rear􀀉windshield􀀉shattered􀀉as􀀉a􀀉coal􀀉block􀀉
slammed􀀉into􀀉the􀀉glass􀀉with􀀉catapult􀀉precision.􀀉Gerald􀀉slammed􀀉the􀀉brakes.􀀉


Rage􀀉eclipsed􀀉his􀀉judgment.􀀉He􀀉reached􀀉into􀀉the􀀉dash􀀉for􀀉his􀀉High-
Frequency􀀉Security􀀉Baton􀀉—􀀉a􀀉Trinity-spec􀀉tool􀀉designed􀀉to􀀉scramble􀀉the􀀉
nervous􀀉system􀀉of􀀉anyone􀀉who􀀉touched􀀉his􀀉property.􀀉

The􀀉massive􀀉man􀀉was􀀉waiting.􀀉Bulging􀀉muscles,􀀉eyes􀀉cold􀀉as􀀉ice.􀀉Andre􀀉
Lockhart􀀉—􀀉the􀀉Hacksaw􀀉—􀀉didn’t􀀉look􀀉at􀀉Gerald’s􀀉face.􀀉His􀀉gaze􀀉was􀀉fixed􀀉
on􀀉the􀀉chrome􀀉intake􀀉of􀀉the􀀉Interceptor.􀀉To􀀉Andre,􀀉Gerald􀀉wasn’t􀀉a􀀉man;􀀉
he􀀉was􀀉the􀀉body􀀉currently􀀉occupying􀀉a􀀉mountain􀀉of􀀉Grade-A􀀉Salvage.􀀉

“You􀀉threw􀀉that􀀉block.”􀀉Gerald’s􀀉voice􀀉came􀀉out􀀉high,􀀉almost􀀉shaking.􀀉
“You􀀉son􀀉of􀀉a—”􀀉

Andre’s􀀉eyes􀀉finally􀀉moved􀀉—􀀉not􀀉to􀀉Gerald’s􀀉face,􀀉but􀀉to􀀉the􀀉baton􀀉on􀀉his􀀉
hip.􀀉“You􀀉stopped􀀉the􀀉car,”􀀉he􀀉said.􀀉His􀀉voice􀀉was􀀉flat.􀀉“That􀀉was􀀉your􀀉
mistake.”􀀉

Gerald􀀉thrust􀀉the􀀉sparking􀀉baton􀀉into􀀉Andre’s􀀉midsection.􀀉The􀀉blue􀀉
current􀀉turned􀀉Andre’s􀀉muscles􀀉into􀀉seizing􀀉fibers.􀀉He􀀉was􀀉in􀀉agony,􀀉jaw􀀉
clamped􀀉so􀀉tight􀀉his􀀉teeth􀀉threatened􀀉to􀀉shatter􀀉—􀀉but􀀉he􀀉did􀀉not􀀉let􀀉out􀀉
