the􀀉tiniest􀀉shriek.􀀉His􀀉eyes􀀉never􀀉left􀀉Gerald’s􀀉face.􀀉Gerald’s􀀉hand􀀉began􀀉to􀀉
tremble.􀀉He􀀉pressed􀀉the􀀉baton􀀉harder.􀀉Andre’s􀀉body􀀉shuddered.􀀉The􀀉eyes􀀉
did􀀉not.􀀉

Before􀀉Gerald􀀉could􀀉understand􀀉what􀀉was􀀉happening,􀀉four􀀉men􀀉appeared􀀉
from􀀉the􀀉dust􀀉and􀀉had􀀉him􀀉on􀀉the􀀉ground.􀀉Andre􀀉leaned􀀉over􀀉him,􀀉voice􀀉
flat􀀉and􀀉unhurried.􀀉“You􀀉smirk􀀉at􀀉me.􀀉On􀀉my􀀉road.􀀉While􀀉I􀀉keep􀀉your􀀉lights􀀉
on􀀉and􀀉your􀀉air􀀉cold.”􀀉He􀀉nodded􀀉to􀀉his􀀉men.􀀉“Hold􀀉him.”􀀉

Gerald􀀉struggled.􀀉Got􀀉a􀀉hand􀀉free.􀀉He􀀉yanked􀀉the􀀉pistol􀀉from􀀉the􀀉belt􀀉of􀀉
the􀀉man􀀉pinning􀀉him􀀉and􀀉fired􀀉wildly􀀉—􀀉one􀀉shot􀀉catching􀀉a􀀉goon􀀉in􀀉the􀀉
foot,􀀉another􀀉punching􀀉through􀀉the􀀉gas􀀉tank􀀉of􀀉the􀀉stranded􀀉truck.􀀉The􀀉
explosion􀀉ripped􀀉through􀀉the􀀉air,􀀉scattering􀀉Andre’s􀀉men􀀉and􀀉buying􀀉
Gerald􀀉three􀀉seconds􀀉of􀀉chaos.􀀉


He􀀉ran.􀀉Lungs􀀉burning􀀉with􀀉the􀀉diesel􀀉smoke,􀀉legs􀀉pumping􀀉on􀀉pure􀀉
adrenaline.􀀉He’d􀀉made􀀉it􀀉maybe􀀉forty􀀉feet􀀉when􀀉his􀀉ankle􀀉rolled􀀉on􀀉a􀀉chunk􀀉
of􀀉truck􀀉debris􀀉—􀀉and􀀉the􀀉asphalt􀀉came􀀉up􀀉fast.􀀉He􀀉hit􀀉hard.􀀉The􀀉pistol􀀉
spun􀀉out􀀉of􀀉his􀀉hand.􀀉

He􀀉tried􀀉to􀀉push􀀉himself􀀉up.􀀉The􀀉ankle􀀉wouldn’t􀀉take􀀉his􀀉weight.􀀉He􀀉tried􀀉
again,􀀉dragging􀀉the􀀉bad􀀉leg􀀉behind􀀉him,􀀉and􀀉gasped􀀉at􀀉the􀀉pain􀀉of􀀉it.􀀉

Then􀀉he􀀉heard􀀉them.􀀉Clack-thud.􀀉Clack-thud.􀀉Heavy􀀉boots􀀉on􀀉asphalt,􀀉
unhurried,􀀉precise􀀉—􀀉a􀀉rhythm􀀉so􀀉devoid􀀉of􀀉human􀀉hesitation􀀉it􀀉sounded􀀉
like􀀉a􀀉machine􀀉resetting􀀉its􀀉gears.􀀉

Andre’s􀀉remaining􀀉men􀀉reached􀀉him􀀉without􀀉hurrying.􀀉They􀀉pinned􀀉his􀀉
arms􀀉with􀀉the􀀉casual􀀉precision􀀉of􀀉men􀀉who􀀉had􀀉done􀀉this􀀉before.􀀉Gerald􀀉
thrashed,􀀉but􀀉they􀀉held􀀉him.􀀉

Andre’s􀀉shadow􀀉fell􀀉over􀀉him.􀀉Gerald􀀉twisted􀀉his􀀉neck􀀉far􀀉enough􀀉to􀀉see􀀉
what􀀉would􀀉follow􀀉him􀀉for􀀉the􀀉rest􀀉of􀀉his􀀉life:􀀉an􀀉industrial􀀉hacksaw􀀉in􀀉
Andre’s􀀉hand,􀀉teeth􀀉still􀀉flecked􀀉with􀀉rust􀀉and􀀉coal􀀉dust.􀀉The􀀉blue􀀉light􀀉of􀀉
the􀀉burning􀀉truck􀀉danced􀀉off􀀉the􀀉steel.􀀉

“You􀀉shot􀀉my􀀉man,”􀀉Andre􀀉said.􀀉“So􀀉I’m􀀉going􀀉to􀀉take􀀉something􀀉of􀀉
yours.”􀀉

Gerald􀀉screamed􀀉before􀀉the􀀉blade􀀉touched􀀉him.􀀉He􀀉screamed􀀉through􀀉the􀀉
cut.􀀉And􀀉then􀀉the􀀉pain􀀉became􀀉so􀀉absolute􀀉that􀀉his􀀉body􀀉shut􀀉down􀀉and􀀉
the􀀉world􀀉went􀀉black.􀀉

He􀀉woke􀀉in􀀉an􀀉ambulance.􀀉His􀀉girlfriend􀀉had􀀉fled􀀉with􀀉his􀀉car.􀀉When􀀉he􀀉
looked􀀉down,􀀉his􀀉left􀀉leg􀀉was􀀉gone􀀉from􀀉the􀀉knee.􀀉

The􀀉doctors􀀉told􀀉him􀀉the􀀉wound􀀉was􀀉clean􀀉and􀀉healing􀀉well.􀀉No􀀉infection.􀀉
But􀀉the􀀉pain􀀉didn’t􀀉stop.􀀉It􀀉got􀀉worse􀀉—􀀉a􀀉burning,􀀉twisting􀀉ache􀀉in􀀉
muscles􀀉that􀀉no􀀉longer􀀉existed.􀀉Phantom􀀉limb,􀀉they􀀉called􀀉it.􀀉His􀀉brain􀀉
hadn’t􀀉accepted􀀉the􀀉loss.􀀉It􀀉kept􀀉sending􀀉signals􀀉to􀀉a􀀉foot􀀉that􀀉wasn’t􀀉there,􀀉
and􀀉when􀀉nothing􀀉answered,􀀉it􀀉screamed.􀀉Gerald􀀉couldn’t􀀉sleep􀀉—􀀉either􀀉


the􀀉phantom􀀉pain􀀉kept􀀉him􀀉staring􀀉at􀀉the􀀉ceiling,􀀉or􀀉he’d􀀉close􀀉his􀀉eyes􀀉and􀀉
see􀀉Andre’s􀀉silhouette,􀀉hear􀀉the􀀉clack-thud,􀀉and􀀉feel􀀉the􀀉asphalt􀀉under􀀉his􀀉
back.􀀉The􀀉wound􀀉in􀀉his􀀉body􀀉was􀀉healing.􀀉The􀀉wound􀀉in􀀉his􀀉head􀀉was􀀉just􀀉
getting􀀉started.􀀉

Trinity􀀉issued􀀉him􀀉a􀀉custom􀀉prosthetic􀀉—􀀉titanium􀀉rods􀀉and􀀉proprietary􀀉
movement-code􀀉that􀀉made􀀉him􀀉feel􀀉whole􀀉again.􀀉Sleek,􀀉aerodynamic,􀀉
responsive.􀀉But􀀉the􀀉Software􀀉was􀀉Trinity’s.􀀉

He􀀉went􀀉back􀀉to􀀉work.􀀉He􀀉had􀀉to.􀀉

But􀀉he􀀉was􀀉different.􀀉His􀀉glass-walled􀀉office􀀉held􀀉its􀀉perfect􀀉cool,􀀉and􀀉still􀀉
the􀀉sweat􀀉came􀀉—􀀉running􀀉down􀀉his􀀉temples􀀉while􀀉he􀀉stood􀀉at􀀉the􀀉wall􀀉of􀀉
grid􀀉monitors,􀀉watching􀀉gigawatt􀀉flows􀀉light􀀉up􀀉Trinity􀀉in􀀉green􀀉and􀀉gold.􀀉
The􀀉phantom􀀉foot􀀉was􀀉always􀀉screaming.􀀉His􀀉hand􀀉would􀀉drift􀀉to􀀉the􀀉seam􀀉
of􀀉his􀀉thigh,􀀉find􀀉the􀀉prosthetic,􀀉grip􀀉it􀀉until􀀉his􀀉knuckles􀀉went􀀉white.􀀉The􀀉
sensor􀀉would􀀉register􀀉the􀀉pressure􀀉and􀀉pretend.􀀉The􀀉pain􀀉didn’t􀀉change.􀀉

The􀀉first􀀉incident􀀉was􀀉an􀀉intern.􀀉A􀀉kid􀀉in􀀉a􀀉clean􀀉shirt,􀀉two􀀉good􀀉legs,􀀉
asking􀀉a􀀉routine􀀉question􀀉about􀀉a􀀉load-balance􀀉schedule.􀀉Gerald􀀉turned􀀉on􀀉
him􀀉and􀀉told􀀉him􀀉to􀀉get􀀉the􀀉hell􀀉out􀀉of􀀉his􀀉office􀀉and􀀉not􀀉come􀀉back􀀉until􀀉
he􀀉understood􀀉how􀀉the􀀉grid􀀉actually􀀉worked.􀀉The􀀉kid􀀉left.􀀉Someone􀀉
reported􀀉it.􀀉HR􀀉scheduled􀀉a􀀉chat.􀀉Gerald􀀉apologized.􀀉

Two􀀉weeks􀀉later􀀉he􀀉yelled􀀉at􀀉his􀀉deputy􀀉in􀀉front􀀉of􀀉three􀀉engineers.􀀉A􀀉week􀀉
after􀀉that􀀉he􀀉threw􀀉a􀀉stylus􀀉across􀀉the􀀉room􀀉when􀀉a􀀉status􀀉query􀀉came􀀉back􀀉
wrong.􀀉The􀀉next􀀉week􀀉he􀀉kicked􀀉over􀀉a􀀉chair􀀉and􀀉broke􀀉a􀀉monitor.􀀉Each􀀉
incident􀀉was􀀉logged.􀀉Each􀀉apology􀀉was􀀉thinner􀀉than􀀉the􀀉last.􀀉He􀀉couldn’t􀀉
say􀀉what􀀉he􀀉wanted􀀉to􀀉say,􀀉which􀀉was􀀉why􀀈me,􀀈why􀀈me,􀀈why􀀈does􀀈the􀀈kid􀀈still􀀈
have􀀈his􀀈leg.􀀉

The􀀉operations􀀉review􀀉was􀀉at􀀉noon􀀉on􀀉a􀀉Wednesday.􀀉Gerald􀀉had􀀉been􀀉at􀀉the􀀉
monitors􀀉since􀀉six,􀀉the􀀉phantom􀀉pain􀀉wound􀀉up􀀉tight,􀀉sweat􀀉already􀀉on􀀉his􀀉
collar.􀀉The􀀉room􀀉was􀀉full􀀉—􀀉three􀀉department􀀉heads,􀀉two􀀉compliance􀀉
officers,􀀉the􀀉deputy􀀉who’d􀀉been􀀉logging􀀉incidents􀀉for􀀉HR.􀀉Someone􀀉


projected􀀉last􀀉week’s􀀉load-balance􀀉figures􀀉and􀀉asked􀀉him􀀉to􀀉walk􀀉through􀀉
the􀀉Sector-12􀀉reroute.􀀉

He􀀉started􀀉fine.􀀉Got􀀉through􀀉the􀀉first􀀉slide.􀀉On􀀉the􀀉second,􀀉the􀀉deputy􀀉
interrupted􀀉to􀀉clarify􀀉a􀀉number.􀀉Gerald􀀉told􀀉him􀀉the􀀉number􀀉was􀀉right.􀀉
The􀀉deputy􀀉said􀀉it􀀉wasn’t.􀀉Someone􀀉pulled􀀉up􀀉the􀀉system􀀉log.􀀉The􀀉number􀀉
was􀀉wrong􀀉by􀀉a􀀉factor􀀉of􀀉three.􀀉

A􀀉small􀀉mistake.􀀉The􀀉kind􀀉the􀀉old􀀉Gerald􀀉would􀀉have􀀉caught􀀉and􀀉laughed􀀉
about.􀀉The􀀉kind􀀉the􀀉new􀀉Gerald􀀉couldn’t􀀉separate􀀉from􀀉a􀀉personal􀀉attack.􀀉

He􀀉told􀀉the􀀉deputy􀀉to􀀉read􀀉the􀀉screen􀀉again.􀀉The􀀉deputy􀀉read􀀉it.􀀉Gerald􀀉
told􀀉him􀀉he􀀉was􀀉reading􀀉it􀀉wrong.􀀉The􀀉room􀀉went􀀉quiet.􀀉

The􀀉compliance􀀉officer,􀀉a􀀉woman􀀉with􀀉patient􀀉eyes,􀀉said􀀉gently:􀀉“Gerald,􀀉
can􀀉we􀀉take􀀉five?”􀀉

Gerald􀀉threw􀀉the􀀉mug􀀉at􀀉the􀀉projector.􀀉

The􀀉coffee􀀉was􀀉hot.􀀉The􀀉mug􀀉shattered.􀀉A􀀉piece􀀉of􀀉ceramic􀀉caught􀀉one􀀉of􀀉
the􀀉department􀀉heads􀀉under􀀉the􀀉eye􀀉and􀀉drew􀀉blood.􀀉The􀀉projector􀀉kept􀀉
running.􀀉The􀀉Sector-12􀀉numbers􀀉were􀀉still􀀉wrong􀀉on􀀉the􀀉wall.􀀉

There􀀉was􀀉no􀀉apology􀀉this􀀉time.􀀉By􀀉the􀀉end􀀉of􀀉the􀀉week,􀀉he􀀉was􀀉discharged.􀀉

Trinity􀀉didn’t􀀉come􀀉for􀀉the􀀉leg.􀀉The􀀉titanium􀀉was􀀉surgically􀀉fused􀀉to􀀉what􀀉
remained􀀉of􀀉his􀀉femur􀀉—􀀉threaded􀀉into􀀉the􀀉bone􀀉with􀀉anchor􀀉pins􀀉that􀀉
would􀀉require􀀉an􀀉operating􀀉room􀀉and􀀉a􀀉surgical􀀉team􀀉to􀀉extract.􀀉That􀀉cost􀀉
money,􀀉and􀀉Gerald􀀉no􀀉longer􀀉generated􀀉revenue.􀀉To􀀉Trinity,􀀉he􀀉was􀀉a􀀉closed􀀉
account.􀀉Reclaiming􀀉the􀀉metal􀀉would􀀉cost􀀉them􀀉thousands.􀀉

They􀀉didn’t􀀉kill􀀉the􀀉leg,􀀉either.􀀉They􀀉just􀀉stopped􀀉paying􀀉for􀀉it􀀉to􀀉live.􀀉

It􀀉worked􀀉for􀀉a􀀉while.􀀉He􀀉walked􀀉on􀀉it􀀉through􀀉three􀀉weeks􀀉of􀀉severance,􀀉
two􀀉appointments􀀉with􀀉a􀀉job􀀉coach,􀀉one􀀉humiliating􀀉interview􀀉at􀀉a􀀉


competitor􀀉where􀀉the􀀉recruiter􀀉spent􀀉the􀀉whole􀀉hour􀀉staring􀀉at􀀉the􀀉seam􀀉of􀀉
his􀀉trousers.􀀉

Then,􀀉on􀀉a􀀉Thursday􀀉morning,􀀉in􀀉line􀀉at􀀉a􀀉Trinity-2􀀉coffee􀀉shop,􀀉his􀀉left􀀉leg􀀉
seized􀀉mid-step.􀀉He􀀉went􀀉down􀀉hard.􀀉The􀀉leg􀀉didn’t􀀉unlock.􀀉He􀀉had􀀉to􀀉
drag􀀉himself􀀉to􀀉a􀀉chair􀀉while􀀉the􀀉barista􀀉watched,􀀉frozen􀀉behind􀀉the􀀉
counter.􀀉By􀀉the􀀉time􀀉he􀀉could􀀉think􀀉clearly􀀉enough􀀉to􀀉call􀀉anyone,􀀉the􀀉
prosthetic􀀉had􀀉stopped􀀉responding􀀉to􀀉nerve􀀉signals􀀉entirely.􀀉The􀀉license􀀉
had􀀉quietly􀀉lapsed􀀉sometime􀀉overnight.􀀉No􀀉alert.􀀉No􀀉notification.􀀉The􀀉
system􀀉had􀀉simply􀀉stopped􀀉recognizing􀀉him.􀀉

He􀀉was􀀉a􀀉man􀀉carrying􀀉a􀀉piece􀀉of􀀉corporate􀀉property􀀉welded􀀉to􀀉his􀀉skeleton􀀉
that􀀉he􀀉couldn’t􀀉use􀀉and􀀉they􀀉couldn’t􀀉be􀀉bothered􀀉to􀀉collect.􀀉

He􀀉lost􀀉his􀀉job.􀀉His􀀉apartment􀀉—􀀉corporate,􀀉tied􀀉to􀀉his􀀉employment􀀉
contract􀀉—􀀉had􀀉been􀀉clawed􀀉back􀀉the􀀉same􀀉week.􀀉His􀀉girlfriend􀀉had􀀉already􀀉
fled􀀉with􀀉the􀀉car;􀀉she􀀉didn’t􀀉want􀀉a􀀉roommate􀀉she􀀉had􀀉to􀀉lift􀀉onto􀀉a􀀉toilet.􀀉
In􀀉the􀀉three􀀉weeks􀀉of􀀉severance􀀉that􀀉followed,􀀉he􀀉did􀀉one􀀉smart􀀉thing:􀀉he􀀉
pulled􀀉every􀀉credit􀀉he􀀉could􀀉from􀀉his􀀉Trinity􀀉Pay􀀉account􀀉and􀀉converted􀀉it􀀉
to􀀉physical􀀉cash,􀀉kept􀀉folded􀀉in􀀉his􀀉coat.􀀉He􀀉was􀀉right􀀉to.􀀉By􀀉the􀀉day􀀉his􀀉leg􀀉
seized􀀉at􀀉the􀀉coffee􀀉shop,􀀉the􀀉account􀀉had􀀉gone􀀉dark.􀀉

The􀀉barista􀀉called􀀉him􀀉a􀀉cab.􀀉The􀀉driver􀀉took􀀉one􀀉look􀀉at􀀉the􀀉dead􀀉leg􀀉and􀀉
the􀀉dark􀀉Trinity􀀉Pay􀀉reader,􀀉and􀀉drove􀀉off􀀉without􀀉a􀀉word.􀀉Gerald􀀉sat􀀉on􀀉
the􀀉curb􀀉for􀀉an􀀉hour􀀉before􀀉he􀀉tried􀀉to􀀉stand.􀀉

The􀀉first􀀉hours􀀉were􀀉the􀀉worst.􀀉He􀀉tried􀀉to􀀉stand,􀀉bracing􀀉on􀀉a􀀉lamp􀀉post,􀀉
but􀀉the􀀉locked􀀉leg􀀉threw􀀉his􀀉balance􀀉off􀀉so􀀉completely􀀉he􀀉went􀀉down􀀉twice􀀉
in􀀉the􀀉first􀀉minute.􀀉He􀀉tried􀀉hopping,􀀉but􀀉he􀀉hadn’t􀀉hopped􀀉since􀀉he􀀉was􀀉a􀀉
child,􀀉and􀀉the􀀉phantom􀀉pain􀀉wouldn’t􀀉let􀀉him􀀉plant􀀉the􀀉prosthetic􀀉to􀀉push􀀉
off.􀀉So􀀉he􀀉dragged􀀉himself􀀉—􀀉elbows,􀀉one􀀉knee,􀀉the􀀉dead􀀉limb􀀉swinging􀀉
behind􀀉him􀀉like􀀉a􀀉steel􀀉beam.􀀉He􀀉made􀀉it􀀉to􀀉the􀀉end􀀉of􀀉the􀀉block􀀉on􀀉his􀀉
elbows.􀀉Made􀀉it􀀉around􀀉the􀀉corner􀀉on􀀉his􀀉hands􀀉and􀀉one􀀉good􀀉knee.􀀉People􀀉
stepped􀀉around􀀉him.􀀉A􀀉few􀀉looked.􀀉Nobody􀀉stopped.􀀉In􀀉Trinity-2,􀀉a􀀉man􀀉


on􀀉the􀀉ground􀀉was􀀉a􀀉system􀀉error􀀉—􀀉an􀀉anomaly􀀉best􀀉ignored􀀉until􀀉
sanitation􀀉cleared􀀉it􀀉away.􀀉

He􀀉found􀀉a􀀉length􀀉of􀀉pipe􀀉in􀀉a􀀉skip􀀉behind􀀉a􀀉service􀀉block􀀉and􀀉used􀀉it􀀉as􀀉a􀀉
crutch􀀉—􀀉jamming􀀉it􀀉under􀀉his􀀉arm,􀀉hauling􀀉himself􀀉upright􀀉for􀀉the􀀉first􀀉
time􀀉in􀀉hours.􀀉The􀀉locked􀀉leg􀀉hung􀀉from􀀉his􀀉hip􀀉like􀀉a􀀉pendulum,􀀉each􀀉
swing􀀉sending􀀉a􀀉jolt􀀉of􀀉phantom􀀉fire􀀉through􀀉nerves􀀉that􀀉no􀀉longer􀀉had􀀉
anywhere􀀉to􀀉go.􀀉

He􀀉dragged􀀉himself􀀉toward􀀉the􀀉outer􀀉district.􀀉He􀀉was􀀉a􀀉Grid􀀉Architect􀀉—􀀉
he􀀉knew􀀉what􀀉he􀀉needed.􀀉The􀀉Junkyards􀀉out􀀉there􀀉were􀀉the􀀉only􀀉place􀀉in􀀉
the􀀉city􀀉he􀀉could􀀉find􀀉drone􀀉struts,􀀉scavenged􀀉actuators,􀀉mechanical􀀉hinges􀀉

—􀀉the􀀉kind􀀉of􀀉raw􀀉hardware􀀉that􀀉ran􀀉on􀀉muscle􀀉and􀀉air􀀉pressure,􀀉not􀀉on􀀉
Trinity􀀉firmware.􀀉The􀀉journey􀀉took􀀉a􀀉week.􀀉He􀀉slept􀀉in􀀉doorways,􀀉ate􀀉from􀀉
waste􀀉bins,􀀉the􀀉pipe􀀉wearing􀀉a􀀉raw􀀉groove􀀉into􀀉his􀀉armpit.􀀉But􀀉he􀀉kept􀀉
moving,􀀉because􀀉he􀀉had􀀉a􀀉plan,􀀉and􀀉the􀀉plan􀀉needed􀀉scrap.􀀉
He􀀉didn’t􀀉go􀀉to􀀉the􀀉junk-heaps􀀉first.􀀉Two􀀉streets􀀉over􀀉he􀀉found􀀉a􀀉row􀀉of􀀉
cash-only􀀉walk-ups􀀉above􀀉shuttered􀀉storefronts,􀀉run􀀉by􀀉an􀀉old􀀉woman􀀉who􀀉
didn’t􀀉care􀀉who􀀉he􀀉was􀀉as􀀉long􀀉as􀀉the􀀉cash􀀉was􀀉real.􀀉He􀀉counted􀀉out􀀉a􀀉
month’s􀀉rent􀀉from􀀉the􀀉wad􀀉in􀀉his􀀉coat􀀉and􀀉was􀀉given􀀉a􀀉key􀀉to􀀉a􀀉room􀀉that􀀉
smelled􀀉of􀀉old􀀉grease􀀉and􀀉engine􀀉oil.􀀉It􀀉would􀀉do.􀀉

The􀀉Mechanical􀀉Jailbreak􀀉took􀀉weeks.􀀉He􀀉did􀀉it􀀉in􀀉that􀀉rented􀀉room,􀀉living􀀉
on􀀉noodles􀀉from􀀉a􀀉cart􀀉that􀀉came􀀉around􀀉at􀀉noon􀀉and􀀉tap􀀉water􀀉from􀀉a􀀉sink􀀉
that􀀉smelled􀀉of􀀉rust.􀀉The􀀉titanium􀀉chassis􀀉—􀀉the􀀉part􀀉Trinity􀀉had􀀉threaded􀀉
into􀀉his􀀉femur􀀉—􀀉was􀀉beyond􀀉him.􀀉That􀀉stayed.􀀉What􀀉he􀀉could􀀉do􀀉was􀀉
rebuild􀀉everything􀀉that􀀉hung􀀉from􀀉it.􀀉

Piece􀀉by􀀉piece,􀀉he􀀉stripped􀀉the􀀉dead􀀉components􀀉away.􀀉He􀀉ground􀀉through􀀉
the􀀉actuator􀀉housings􀀉with􀀉a􀀉borrowed􀀉angle􀀉grinder,􀀉the􀀉vibration􀀉shaking􀀉
up􀀉his􀀉femur􀀉into􀀉his􀀉hip􀀉until􀀉his􀀉teeth􀀉chattered.􀀉He􀀉pried􀀉out􀀉servo􀀉
motors,􀀉sensors,􀀉gyros􀀉—􀀉every􀀉part􀀉of􀀉the􀀉leg􀀉that􀀉had􀀉once􀀉needed􀀉
Trinity’s􀀉handshake􀀉to􀀉function.􀀉He􀀉replaced􀀉them􀀉with􀀉drone􀀉struts􀀉and􀀉


high-tensile􀀉wire,􀀉with􀀉mechanical􀀉hinges􀀉from􀀉a􀀉Junkyard,􀀉with􀀉a􀀉foot􀀉
assembly􀀉cannibalized􀀉from􀀉an􀀉industrial􀀉gripper􀀉arm.􀀉He􀀉worked􀀉under􀀉a􀀉
single􀀉bulb.􀀉When􀀉the􀀉phantom􀀉pain􀀉peaked,􀀉he􀀉set􀀉the􀀉tools􀀉down􀀉and􀀉
waited􀀉it􀀉out.􀀉

He’d􀀉traded􀀉the􀀉sleek􀀉Software􀀉leg􀀉for􀀉a􀀉jagged􀀉Hardware􀀉solution􀀉that􀀉
didn’t􀀉need􀀉a􀀉corporate􀀉handshake􀀉to􀀉move,􀀉only􀀉the􀀉raw􀀉pressure􀀉of􀀉his􀀉
own􀀉weight.􀀉

It􀀉took􀀉three􀀉months􀀉for􀀉Trinity􀀉to􀀉find􀀉him.􀀉Not􀀉the􀀉Trinity􀀉that􀀉had􀀉fired􀀉
him􀀉—􀀉that􀀉arm􀀉of􀀉the􀀉bureaucracy􀀉had􀀉closed􀀉his􀀉account􀀉and􀀉moved􀀉on.􀀉
The􀀉Trinity􀀉that􀀉found􀀉him􀀉was􀀉a􀀉different􀀉arm,􀀉the􀀉one􀀉that􀀉ran􀀉sanitation􀀉
sweeps􀀉through􀀉the􀀉outer􀀉districts􀀉every􀀉two􀀉weeks,􀀉collecting􀀉vagrants􀀉the􀀉
way􀀉a􀀉filter􀀉collects􀀉grit.􀀉They􀀉didn’t􀀉read􀀉employee􀀉files.􀀉They􀀉didn’t􀀉check􀀉
names.􀀉They􀀉read􀀉body􀀉weight,􀀉age,􀀉ambulatory􀀉status,􀀉and􀀉a􀀉stamped􀀉
license􀀉to􀀉take􀀉whatever􀀉didn’t􀀉have􀀉papers.􀀉They􀀉processed􀀉humans􀀉the􀀉
way􀀉they􀀉processed􀀉scrap:􀀉by􀀉category,􀀉not􀀉by􀀉history.􀀉

Gerald􀀉was􀀉picked􀀉up􀀉on􀀉a􀀉Tuesday.􀀉His􀀉records􀀉existed.􀀉The􀀉Intake􀀉officers􀀉
didn’t􀀉pull􀀉them.􀀉To􀀉them,􀀉he􀀉was􀀉an􀀉able-bodied􀀉male,􀀉mid-thirties,􀀉
walking􀀉on􀀉what􀀉looked􀀉like􀀉a􀀉prosthetic􀀉—􀀉fit􀀉for􀀉colonial􀀉labor.􀀉

And􀀉there,􀀉waiting􀀉for􀀉him􀀉like􀀉a􀀉punchline􀀉to􀀉the􀀉worst􀀉joke􀀉in􀀉the􀀉world,􀀉
was􀀉the􀀉man􀀉who’d􀀉ruined􀀉his􀀉life.􀀉Andre􀀉Lockhart􀀉—􀀉Judge,􀀉Jury,􀀉and􀀉
Executioner􀀉of􀀉the􀀉sector.􀀉Gerald􀀉recognized􀀉him􀀉on􀀉the􀀉first􀀉day.􀀉The􀀉
massive􀀉frame,􀀉the􀀉cold􀀉eyes,􀀉the􀀉clack-thud􀀉of􀀉boots􀀉that􀀉he􀀉heard􀀉every􀀉
night􀀉in􀀉his􀀉sleep.􀀉Andre􀀉didn’t􀀉recognize􀀉Gerald􀀉—􀀉why􀀉would􀀉he?􀀉Gerald􀀉
was􀀉just􀀉another􀀉piece􀀉of􀀉grit􀀉in􀀉the􀀉intake,􀀉one􀀉of􀀉a􀀉thousand􀀉broken􀀉men􀀉
Andre􀀉had􀀉chewed􀀉through􀀉and􀀉forgotten.􀀉

Gerald􀀉knew􀀉that􀀉in􀀉his􀀉current􀀉state,􀀉any􀀉confrontation􀀉with􀀉the􀀉Hacksaw􀀉
would􀀉be􀀉his􀀉last.􀀉So􀀉he􀀉kept􀀉his􀀉head􀀉down􀀉—􀀉though􀀉in􀀉truth,􀀉there􀀉was􀀉
little􀀉to􀀉hide.􀀉Half􀀉of􀀉Miner􀀉Town􀀉walked􀀉on􀀉something􀀉cobbled􀀉from􀀉
scrap;􀀉the􀀉other􀀉half􀀉were􀀉too􀀉exhausted􀀉to􀀉look􀀉twice.􀀉And􀀉Andre􀀉


Lockhart􀀉hadn’t􀀉cared􀀉who􀀉Gerald􀀉was􀀉on􀀉Highway􀀉2B;􀀉he􀀉didn’t􀀉care􀀉
now.􀀉The􀀉phantom􀀉fire􀀉in􀀉his􀀉stump􀀉never􀀉stopped.􀀉Each􀀉day􀀉was􀀉
endurance.􀀉Each􀀉night􀀉was􀀉a􀀉war􀀉with􀀉a􀀉leg􀀉that􀀉wasn’t􀀉there.􀀉

After􀀉dinner􀀉with􀀉Gage􀀉and􀀉his􀀉crew,􀀉Gerald􀀉lay􀀉on􀀉his􀀉cot􀀉and􀀉stared􀀉at􀀉the􀀉
dark􀀉ceiling.􀀉He􀀉replayed􀀉the􀀉evening􀀉—􀀉the􀀉marmot,􀀉the􀀉flatbread,􀀉the􀀉way􀀉
Gage’s􀀉eyes􀀉had􀀉dimmed􀀉when􀀉Gerald􀀉shut􀀉the􀀉door􀀉on􀀉his􀀉plan.􀀉

He􀀉should􀀉have􀀉listened.􀀉Part􀀉of􀀉him􀀉wanted􀀉to.􀀉But􀀉wanting􀀉and􀀉being􀀉
able􀀉were􀀉two􀀉different􀀉currencies,􀀉and􀀉Gerald􀀉was􀀉running􀀉short􀀉in􀀉both.􀀉
The􀀉phantom􀀉pain􀀉was􀀉climbing􀀉again􀀉—􀀉that􀀉familiar􀀉twist,􀀉as􀀉if􀀉invisible􀀉
hands􀀉were􀀉wringing􀀉out􀀉muscles􀀉that􀀉no􀀉longer􀀉existed.􀀉He􀀉reached􀀉under􀀉
his􀀉cot􀀉for􀀉the􀀉grimy􀀉cloth􀀉pouch􀀉he􀀉kept􀀉hidden􀀉there􀀉—􀀉three􀀉tablets􀀉left,􀀉
scavenged􀀉from􀀉a􀀉guard’s􀀉stolen􀀉med-kit􀀉and􀀉traded􀀉through􀀉the􀀉Shadow􀀉
Economy􀀉at􀀉a􀀉cost􀀉of􀀉two􀀉days’􀀉salt􀀉rations􀀉per􀀉pill.􀀉He􀀉bit􀀉down􀀉on􀀉one􀀉
and􀀉pressed􀀉his􀀉teeth􀀉into􀀉his􀀉lip􀀉until􀀉the􀀉chemical􀀉warmth􀀉crept􀀉through􀀉
his􀀉jaw􀀉and􀀉softened􀀉the􀀉edges.􀀉Two􀀉left.􀀉At􀀉this􀀉rate,􀀉he’d􀀉be􀀉out􀀉by􀀉
Thursday􀀉and􀀉back􀀉to􀀉chewing􀀉on􀀉nothing􀀉but􀀉his􀀉own􀀉tongue􀀉until􀀉he􀀉
could􀀉scrape􀀉together􀀉enough􀀉salt􀀉to􀀉buy􀀉more.􀀉

Even􀀉if􀀉he’d􀀉said􀀉yes,􀀉what􀀉could􀀉he􀀉offer?􀀉A􀀉body􀀉held􀀉together􀀉with􀀉scrap􀀉
wire􀀉and􀀉stubbornness,􀀉a􀀉mind􀀉that􀀉flinched􀀉at􀀉silhouettes,􀀉and􀀉a􀀉leg􀀉that􀀉
punished􀀉him􀀉for􀀉every􀀉step􀀉he’d􀀉ever􀀉taken.􀀉Gage􀀉needed􀀉a􀀉soldier.􀀉Gerald􀀉
was􀀉a􀀉ruin.􀀉

Tomorrow􀀉would􀀉be􀀉another􀀉struggle.􀀉He􀀉wasn’t􀀉going􀀉to􀀉waste􀀉any􀀉more􀀉
time􀀉chasing􀀉dreams.􀀉


Chapter􀀉15􀀉—􀀉No􀀉Pain􀀉

Camilla􀀉was􀀉getting􀀉impatient.􀀉She’d􀀉promised􀀉along􀀉with􀀉Ben􀀉and􀀉Otto􀀉to􀀉
keep􀀉out􀀉of􀀉Gage’s􀀉plans,􀀉but􀀉deep􀀉inside􀀉she􀀉knew􀀉that􀀉promise􀀉wouldn’t􀀉
hold.􀀉She􀀉couldn’t􀀉bear􀀉the􀀉thought􀀉of􀀉her􀀉brother􀀉dying􀀉while􀀉fighting􀀉for􀀉
a􀀉worthy􀀉cause􀀉—􀀉not􀀉because􀀉he􀀉didn’t􀀉know􀀉the􀀉risks,􀀉but􀀉because􀀉the􀀉
odds􀀉were􀀉stacked􀀉against􀀉him􀀉without􀀉support.􀀉

She’d􀀉watched􀀉Gerald􀀉shut􀀉Gage􀀉down􀀉at􀀉dinner􀀉—􀀉watched􀀉the􀀉light􀀉go􀀉
out􀀉of􀀉her􀀉brother’s􀀉eyes􀀉when􀀉the􀀉man􀀉with􀀉the􀀉cane􀀉said􀀉his􀀉wild􀀉days􀀉
were􀀉past.􀀉Gage􀀉had􀀉accepted􀀉it.􀀉Camilla􀀉hadn’t.􀀉Because􀀉she’d􀀉seen􀀉
something􀀉Gage􀀉had􀀉missed:􀀉the􀀉way􀀉Gerald’s􀀉hand􀀉trembled􀀉when􀀉he􀀉set􀀉
down􀀉his􀀉food,􀀉the􀀉micro-flinch􀀉every􀀉time􀀉he􀀉shifted􀀉his􀀉weight,􀀉the􀀉jaw􀀉
clenched􀀉so􀀉tight􀀉the􀀉tendons􀀉stood􀀉out􀀉like􀀉cables.􀀉Gerald􀀉hadn’t􀀉refused􀀉
because􀀉he􀀉didn’t􀀉care.􀀉He’d􀀉refused􀀉because􀀉he􀀉was􀀉drowning􀀉in􀀉
something,􀀉and􀀉whatever􀀉it􀀉was,􀀉it􀀉was􀀉eating􀀉him􀀉from􀀉the􀀉inside.􀀉

Ever􀀉since􀀉that􀀉evening,􀀉Camilla􀀉was􀀉obsessed􀀉with􀀉learning􀀉more􀀉about􀀉
him.􀀉Gage􀀉had􀀉written􀀉the􀀉man􀀉off􀀉after􀀉dinner􀀉—􀀉too􀀉broken,􀀉too􀀉risky,􀀉
leave􀀉it􀀉alone.􀀉So􀀉she􀀉did􀀉what􀀉came􀀉naturally:􀀉sneaked􀀉behind􀀉Gage,􀀉
watching􀀉guard􀀉rotations􀀉and􀀉supply􀀉runs􀀉through􀀉Sector-1􀀉until􀀉she’d􀀉
mapped􀀉where􀀉the􀀉miner􀀉records􀀉were􀀉kept.􀀉

Once􀀉she􀀉identified􀀉the􀀉records􀀉room,􀀉it􀀉was􀀉a􀀉matter􀀉of􀀉playing􀀉naive􀀉in􀀉
front􀀉of􀀉the􀀉security􀀉guard􀀉until􀀉he􀀉let􀀉her􀀉close.􀀉While􀀉he􀀉checked􀀉her􀀉for􀀉
weapons,􀀉Camilla􀀉went􀀉for􀀉the􀀉carotid􀀉sinus􀀉—􀀉a􀀉pressure􀀉point􀀉she’d􀀉read􀀉
about􀀉in􀀉one􀀉of􀀉Pappy’s􀀉salvaged􀀉medical􀀉handbooks.􀀉Two􀀉fingers,􀀉steady􀀉
pressure,􀀉on􀀉the􀀉side􀀉of􀀉his􀀉neck􀀉just􀀉below􀀉the􀀉angle􀀉of􀀉the􀀉jaw.􀀉The􀀉book􀀉
had􀀉warned􀀉it􀀉didn’t􀀉always􀀉work,􀀉and􀀉for􀀉a􀀉sickening􀀉half-second􀀉she􀀉felt􀀉
the􀀉guard􀀉tense􀀉and􀀉thought􀀉she’d􀀉blown􀀉it.􀀉Then􀀉his􀀉eyes􀀉rolled􀀉back.􀀉His􀀉
knees􀀉buckled.􀀉She􀀉caught􀀉him􀀉under􀀉the􀀉arms􀀉and􀀉eased􀀉him􀀉into􀀉his􀀉chair􀀉
before􀀉he􀀉hit􀀉the􀀉floor.􀀉


She􀀉sifted􀀉coal􀀉dust􀀉across􀀉the􀀉keypad.􀀉The􀀉keys􀀉the􀀉guard’s􀀉fingers􀀉had􀀉
touched􀀉came􀀉back􀀉marked;􀀉a􀀉few􀀉permutations􀀉later,􀀉the􀀉door􀀉unlocked.􀀉
The􀀉records􀀉inside􀀉were􀀉bound􀀉in􀀉ledgers􀀉—􀀉no􀀉second􀀉lock.􀀉She􀀉found􀀉
Gerald’s􀀉entry,􀀉tore􀀉out􀀉the􀀉address􀀉page,􀀉and􀀉locked􀀉the􀀉door􀀉behind􀀉her.􀀉
Outside,􀀉she􀀉propped􀀉the􀀉unconscious􀀉guard􀀉back􀀉on􀀉his􀀉chair􀀉—􀀉the􀀉
posture􀀉of􀀉a􀀉man􀀉who’d􀀉dozed􀀉off􀀉on􀀉shift.􀀉

When􀀉the􀀉guard􀀉came􀀉to,􀀉he􀀉was􀀉groggy􀀉and􀀉confused.􀀉Something􀀉at􀀉the􀀉
side􀀉of􀀉his􀀉neck􀀉ached,􀀉in􀀉a􀀉small􀀉precise􀀉spot.􀀉He􀀉remembered􀀉a􀀉girl.􀀉He􀀉
remembered􀀉checking􀀉her􀀉for􀀉weapons.􀀉After􀀉that􀀉—􀀉nothing.􀀉He􀀉looked􀀉
around.􀀉The􀀉records􀀉room􀀉door􀀉was􀀉still􀀉locked.􀀉His􀀉hands􀀉were􀀉on􀀉his􀀉
knees,􀀉the􀀉posture􀀉of􀀉a􀀉man􀀉who’d􀀉nodded􀀉off􀀉on􀀉shift.􀀉

He􀀉thought􀀉about􀀉reporting􀀉it.􀀉Once.􀀉Then􀀉he􀀉thought􀀉about􀀉Hacksaw􀀉—􀀉
about􀀉how􀀉men􀀉who􀀉admitted􀀉weakness􀀉didn’t􀀉get􀀉reassigned,􀀉they􀀉
disappeared.􀀉He􀀉thought􀀉about􀀉explaining􀀉that􀀉a􀀉girl􀀉—􀀉a􀀉girl􀀉—􀀉had􀀉put􀀉
him􀀉out􀀉with􀀉no􀀉blow,􀀉no􀀉marks,􀀉no􀀉weapon.􀀉He􀀉stopped􀀉thinking.􀀉The􀀉
corridor􀀉cameras􀀉only􀀉got􀀉pulled􀀉when􀀉an􀀉incident􀀉was􀀉flagged.􀀉If􀀉he􀀉kept􀀉
quiet,􀀉nobody􀀉would􀀉look.􀀉He􀀉took􀀉his􀀉chances.􀀉Checked􀀉the􀀉locks􀀉twice.􀀉
Decided􀀉to􀀉never􀀉mention􀀉the􀀉girl􀀉to􀀉anyone.􀀉

At􀀉eight􀀉that􀀉evening,􀀉Camilla􀀉knocked􀀉on􀀉Gerald’s􀀉door.􀀉No􀀉response.􀀉She􀀉
went􀀉around􀀉and􀀉peeked􀀉through􀀉a􀀉gap􀀉in􀀉the􀀉patchwork􀀉wall.􀀉Gerald􀀉sat􀀉
on􀀉a􀀉broken􀀉chair􀀉removing􀀉his􀀉prosthetic􀀉leg,􀀉clearly􀀉in􀀉excruciating􀀉pain.􀀉
Sweat􀀉dropped􀀉from􀀉his􀀉forehead􀀉as􀀉he􀀉grunted,􀀉removing􀀉the􀀉leather􀀉
casing􀀉from􀀉his􀀉thigh.􀀉He􀀉almost􀀉collapsed􀀉once􀀉the􀀉prosthetic􀀉was􀀉off.􀀉

Camilla􀀉had􀀉never􀀉seen􀀉someone􀀉in􀀉so􀀉much􀀉pain􀀉and􀀉couldn’t􀀉reconcile􀀉it􀀉
with􀀉the􀀉man􀀉who’d􀀉been􀀉smiling􀀉effortlessly􀀉at􀀉dinner.􀀉She􀀉watched􀀉him􀀉
reach􀀉under􀀉his􀀉cot􀀉for􀀉a􀀉grimy􀀉cloth􀀉pouch􀀉—􀀉the􀀉kind􀀉everyone􀀉with􀀉a􀀉
Shadow􀀉Economy􀀉contact􀀉kept􀀉hidden.􀀉He􀀉shook􀀉out􀀉a􀀉single􀀉tablet,􀀉stared􀀉
at􀀉it􀀉for􀀉a􀀉moment􀀉as􀀉if􀀉calculating􀀉whether􀀉tonight􀀉was􀀉worth􀀉the􀀉cost,􀀉
then􀀉bit􀀉down.􀀉One􀀉pill.􀀉Not􀀉a􀀉handful.􀀉From􀀉the􀀉way􀀉he􀀉held􀀉the􀀉pouch􀀉


—􀀉the􀀉careful􀀉tilt,􀀉the􀀉count􀀉of􀀉what􀀉was􀀉left􀀉—􀀉she􀀉could􀀉tell􀀉he􀀉was􀀉close􀀉
to􀀉empty.􀀉
She􀀉drew􀀉breath􀀉to􀀉call􀀉to􀀉him􀀉through􀀉the􀀉gap.􀀉Then􀀉she􀀉stopped,􀀉mouth􀀉
half-open.􀀉He􀀉didn’t􀀉need􀀉to􀀉be􀀉seen􀀉like􀀉this􀀉—􀀉bare-stump,􀀉drugged,􀀉
alone􀀉with􀀉his􀀉pain.􀀉To􀀉call􀀉him􀀉out􀀉now􀀉would􀀉be􀀉to􀀉humiliate􀀉him.􀀉She􀀉
stepped􀀉back􀀉from􀀉the􀀉wall􀀉and􀀉made􀀉her􀀉way􀀉around􀀉to􀀉the􀀉door􀀉—􀀉
slowly,􀀉to􀀉give􀀉him􀀉time􀀉to􀀉put􀀉the􀀉leg􀀉back􀀉on,􀀉wipe􀀉his􀀉face,􀀉find􀀉his􀀉
composure.􀀉And􀀉knocked􀀉again.􀀉“Who􀀉is􀀉it?”􀀉he􀀉yelled,􀀉voice􀀉equal􀀉parts􀀉
anger􀀉and􀀉anguish.􀀉

“It’s􀀉Camilla.􀀉Gage’s􀀉sister.􀀉We􀀉met􀀉at􀀉dinner􀀉the􀀉other􀀉day.􀀉I􀀉have􀀉
something􀀉important􀀉to􀀉talk􀀉to􀀉you􀀉about.”􀀉

Gerald􀀉didn’t􀀉answer.􀀉What􀀉did􀀉this􀀉girl􀀉want?􀀉Whatever􀀉it􀀉was,􀀉he􀀉didn’t􀀉
want􀀉to􀀉see􀀉anyone.􀀉Not􀀉tonight.􀀉

Another􀀉knock.􀀉“Please.􀀉Let􀀉me􀀉in.􀀉It’s􀀉important.”􀀉

After􀀉two􀀉minutes,􀀉the􀀉door􀀉opened.􀀉Camilla􀀉stepped􀀉into􀀉an􀀉unkempt􀀉
shack􀀉that􀀉smelled􀀉of􀀉stale􀀉sweat􀀉and􀀉the􀀉bitter􀀉chemical􀀉residue􀀉of􀀉
painkillers.􀀉Before􀀉she􀀉could􀀉gather􀀉her􀀉thoughts,􀀉Gerald􀀉snapped:􀀉“Who􀀉
the􀀉heck􀀉gave􀀉you􀀉my􀀉address?􀀉Just􀀉because􀀉I􀀉came􀀉to􀀉dinner􀀉doesn’t􀀉mean􀀉
you􀀉can􀀉stalk􀀉me!”􀀉

Camilla􀀉came􀀉close􀀉and􀀉pulled􀀉a􀀉small􀀉bottle􀀉of􀀉Refined􀀉Mash􀀉from􀀉inside􀀉
her􀀉jacket􀀉—􀀉she’d􀀉fermented􀀉it􀀉herself􀀉—􀀉along􀀉with􀀉two􀀉plastic􀀉glasses.􀀉
She􀀉wasn’t􀀉the􀀉kind􀀉of􀀉girl􀀉who􀀉left􀀉things􀀉to􀀉chance.􀀉Showing􀀉up􀀉empty-
handed􀀉at􀀉a􀀉stranger’s􀀉door􀀉was􀀉a􀀉good􀀉way􀀉to􀀉get􀀉the􀀉door􀀉shut􀀉in􀀉your􀀉
face.􀀉She􀀉poured􀀉Gerald􀀉a􀀉measure􀀉and􀀉took􀀉one􀀉herself.􀀉Gerald􀀉smirked􀀉—􀀉
someone􀀉who􀀉came􀀉prepared.􀀉

After􀀉a􀀉minute􀀉of􀀉silence:􀀉“Tell􀀉me􀀉what􀀉you􀀉want.”􀀉


“I􀀉wanted􀀉to􀀉meet􀀉you,”􀀉Camilla􀀉said,􀀉pacing.􀀉“Gage􀀉would􀀉tell􀀉me􀀉to􀀉leave􀀉
you􀀉alone􀀉—􀀉too􀀉broken,􀀉too􀀉risky.􀀉But􀀉since􀀉dinner,􀀉I’ve􀀉been􀀉constantly􀀉
thinking􀀉about􀀉your􀀉leg.”􀀉

Gerald􀀉wasn’t􀀉in􀀉a􀀉sharing􀀉mood.􀀉“None􀀉of􀀉your􀀉business.”􀀉Suddenly􀀉his􀀉
face􀀉contorted􀀉as􀀉another􀀉wave􀀉of􀀉pain􀀉shook􀀉his􀀉body.􀀉Camilla􀀉was􀀉fixated􀀉
on􀀉his􀀉legs.􀀉“Why􀀉does􀀉your􀀉leg􀀉still􀀉hurt􀀉if􀀉all􀀉the􀀉wounds􀀉have􀀉healed?”􀀉

Gerald􀀉decided􀀉to􀀉tell􀀉her􀀉about􀀉the􀀉phantom􀀉limb,􀀉hoping􀀉she’d􀀉be􀀉
content􀀉and􀀉leave.􀀉

After􀀉he􀀉finished,􀀉Camilla􀀉was􀀉silent􀀉for􀀉two􀀉full􀀉minutes.􀀉Then:􀀉“I’m􀀉sorry􀀉
about􀀉your􀀉leg.􀀉I􀀉can’t􀀉fix􀀉that.􀀉But􀀉the􀀉pain􀀉—􀀉I􀀉think􀀉I􀀉can􀀉help.”􀀉

“What􀀉do􀀉you􀀉mean􀀉you􀀉can􀀉help?􀀉I’m􀀉not􀀉crazy,􀀉alright?􀀉I􀀉know􀀉what􀀉
everyone􀀉thinks.􀀉I􀀉know􀀉what􀀉they􀀉say􀀉when􀀉my􀀉back􀀉is􀀉turned.􀀉The􀀉pain􀀉is􀀉
real.􀀉Not􀀉in􀀉my􀀉head.􀀉And􀀉no􀀉mind􀀉tricks􀀉or􀀉magic􀀉potions􀀉are􀀉going􀀉to􀀉
take􀀉it􀀉away.”􀀉

Camilla􀀉pulled􀀉her􀀉chair􀀉closer.􀀉“At􀀉least􀀉let􀀉me􀀉have􀀉a􀀉look.􀀉I’ve􀀉been􀀉
reading.􀀉Phantom􀀉pain􀀉isn’t􀀉really􀀉a􀀉wound􀀉problem􀀉—􀀉it’s􀀉a􀀉brain􀀉
problem.􀀉Your􀀉brain􀀉still􀀉has􀀉the􀀉leg􀀉mapped,􀀉still􀀉believes􀀉it’s􀀉there􀀉and􀀉
hurting.􀀉The􀀉map􀀉can􀀉be􀀉redrawn.”􀀉

She􀀉thought􀀉back􀀉to􀀉the􀀉Master􀀉Scripts􀀉Pappy􀀉had􀀉hidden􀀉under􀀉the􀀉
floorboards􀀉—􀀉salvaged􀀉medical􀀉journals􀀉and􀀉engineering􀀉texts􀀉from􀀉the􀀉old􀀉
world.􀀉“The􀀉miners􀀉think􀀉the􀀉mountain􀀉is􀀉just􀀉rock,”􀀉Pappy􀀉had􀀉told􀀉her.􀀉
“But􀀉everything􀀉has􀀉a􀀉manual􀀉—􀀉the􀀉mountain,􀀉the􀀉body,􀀉all􀀉of􀀉it.􀀉If􀀉you􀀉
can􀀉read􀀉the􀀉manual,􀀉you􀀉own􀀉the􀀉machine.”􀀉

“And􀀉that􀀉makes􀀉you􀀉a􀀉doctor􀀉now?”􀀉Gerald􀀉almost􀀉yelled.􀀉

Camilla􀀉paid􀀉no􀀉heed􀀉and􀀉started􀀉mumbling􀀉from􀀉memory,􀀉piecing􀀉
together􀀉what􀀉she’d􀀉read.􀀉Suddenly􀀉she􀀉stood.􀀉“I􀀉think􀀉I’ve􀀉got􀀉it.􀀉Can􀀉you􀀉
answer􀀉a􀀉few􀀉questions􀀉—􀀉simple􀀉yes􀀉or􀀉no?􀀉If􀀉I􀀉have􀀉nothing􀀉after􀀉three􀀉
questions,􀀉I’ll􀀉leave􀀉you􀀉alone􀀉with􀀉your􀀉pain.”􀀉


Gerald􀀉was􀀉in􀀉no􀀉mood􀀉for􀀉this,􀀉but􀀉another􀀉wave􀀉of􀀉pain􀀉almost􀀉buckled􀀉
him.􀀉What’s􀀉the􀀉worst􀀉that􀀉could􀀉happen􀀉—􀀉she􀀉asks􀀉stupid􀀉questions􀀉and􀀉
leaves?􀀉Something􀀉about􀀉Camilla􀀉made􀀉him􀀉believe􀀉her􀀉offer.􀀉He􀀉used􀀉his􀀉
stick􀀉to􀀉tap􀀉her􀀉shin.􀀉“You􀀉have􀀉five􀀉minutes.􀀉Shoot.”􀀉

Camilla􀀉almost􀀉jumped􀀉to􀀉her􀀉feet.􀀉“You􀀉won’t􀀉regret􀀉it.􀀉Here􀀉I􀀉go􀀉—􀀉each􀀉
answer􀀉just􀀉yes􀀉or􀀉no.”􀀉

“This􀀉pain􀀉didn’t􀀉exist􀀉all􀀉the􀀉time.􀀉It􀀉must􀀉have􀀉started􀀉suddenly􀀉after􀀉you􀀉
were􀀉discharged􀀉from􀀉the􀀉hospital?”􀀉—􀀉“Yes.􀀉But􀀉that’s􀀉simply􀀉because􀀉—”􀀉
Camilla􀀉interrupted:􀀉“Only􀀉yes􀀉or􀀉no.”􀀉Gerald􀀉gulped􀀉his􀀉sentence.􀀉

“Does􀀉the􀀉pain􀀉remind􀀉you􀀉of􀀉the􀀉time􀀉your􀀉leg􀀉would􀀉stiffen􀀉after􀀉a􀀉long􀀉
run?”􀀉—􀀉“Yes.”􀀉

“Does􀀉the􀀉pain􀀉exist􀀉immediately􀀉after􀀉you􀀉wake􀀉up?”􀀉—􀀉“No.”􀀉

Gerald􀀉was􀀉thrown􀀉off-balance.􀀉Why􀀉did􀀉the􀀉pain􀀉start􀀉only􀀉a􀀉while􀀉after􀀉
waking?􀀉

Camilla􀀉was􀀉smiling,􀀉knowing􀀉Gerald􀀉had􀀉seen􀀉something􀀉interesting􀀉in􀀉his􀀉
own􀀉answers.􀀉She􀀉began􀀉explaining:􀀉“The􀀉reason􀀉for􀀉phantom􀀉pain􀀉is􀀉that􀀉
your􀀉brain􀀉still􀀉has􀀉the􀀉map􀀉of􀀉the􀀉leg,􀀉but􀀉the􀀉leg􀀉itself􀀉isn’t􀀉there􀀉to􀀉
answer.􀀉The􀀉part􀀉of􀀉the􀀉map􀀉above􀀉your􀀉knee􀀉feels􀀉like􀀉the􀀉leg􀀉is􀀉constantly􀀉
stretched􀀉or􀀉twisted􀀉in􀀉a􀀉position􀀉it􀀉can’t􀀉escape.􀀉Your􀀉brain􀀉keeps􀀉sending􀀉
signals􀀉to􀀉relax􀀉the􀀉muscles,􀀉to􀀉straighten􀀉the􀀉foot,􀀉to􀀉flex􀀉the􀀉toes􀀉—􀀉but􀀉
nothing􀀉answers.􀀉No􀀉signal􀀉comes􀀉back.􀀉So􀀉your􀀉brain􀀉sends􀀉the􀀉signals􀀉
harder.􀀉Louder.􀀉Over􀀉and􀀉over.􀀉A􀀉never-ending􀀉loop􀀉—􀀉the􀀉map􀀉trying􀀉to􀀉
operate􀀉a􀀉leg􀀉that􀀉no􀀉longer􀀉exists,􀀉and􀀉your􀀉nervous􀀉system􀀉interpreting􀀉the􀀉
silence􀀉as􀀉pain.”􀀉

Gerald􀀉didn’t􀀉move.􀀉He􀀉didn’t􀀉speak.􀀉Nobody􀀉had􀀉ever􀀉described􀀉it􀀉like􀀉
that.􀀉Not􀀉the􀀉doctors.􀀉Not􀀉the􀀉engineers.􀀉Nobody.􀀉“Are􀀉you􀀉some􀀉doctor􀀉
or􀀉something?􀀉I’ve􀀉never􀀉seen􀀉a􀀉doctor􀀉in􀀉Sector-1􀀉offering􀀉counseling􀀉to􀀉
lowlifes􀀉like􀀉me.”􀀉


Camilla􀀉smiled.􀀉“I􀀉read,􀀉sir.􀀉I􀀉observe.􀀉And􀀉I􀀉remember.”􀀉

Gerald􀀉studied􀀉her􀀉for􀀉a􀀉long􀀉moment.􀀉Then􀀉he􀀉lifted􀀉the􀀉hollow􀀉pipe􀀉he􀀉
leaned􀀉on􀀉and􀀉rapped􀀉it􀀉against􀀉the􀀉floor􀀉—􀀉Tink.􀀉Tink-tink.􀀉—􀀉the􀀉sound􀀉
vanishing􀀉into􀀉the􀀉drone􀀉of􀀉the􀀉cooling􀀉drills.􀀉“You􀀉read􀀉the􀀉body􀀉like􀀉a􀀉
manual.􀀉Signals,􀀉loops,􀀉silence.”􀀉He􀀉nodded􀀉at􀀉the􀀉wall,􀀉the􀀉mountain􀀉
beyond􀀉it.􀀉“Then􀀉tell􀀉me􀀉how􀀉a􀀉man􀀉passes􀀉a􀀉warning􀀉across􀀉half􀀉a􀀉mile􀀉of􀀉
shaft􀀉when􀀉Jung􀀉hears􀀉every􀀉word􀀉—􀀉and􀀉the􀀉wrong􀀉one􀀉earns􀀉a􀀉Clean􀀉
Cut.”􀀉

Camilla􀀉looked􀀉at􀀉the􀀉pipe.􀀉“You􀀉don’t􀀉say􀀉it.􀀉You􀀉tap􀀉it.”􀀉

The􀀉card􀀉he’d􀀉kept􀀉hidden􀀉turned􀀉face-up􀀉behind􀀉his􀀉eyes.􀀉“The􀀉system􀀉
listens􀀉for􀀉voices.􀀉It’s􀀉deaf􀀉to􀀉the􀀉Hardware􀀉—􀀉drills,􀀉rock,􀀉the􀀉mountain􀀉
settling.􀀉So􀀉we􀀉don’t􀀉speak.􀀉We􀀉drum.􀀉This􀀉coal’s􀀉full􀀉of􀀉the􀀉guts􀀉of􀀉dead􀀉
cities;􀀉it􀀉carries􀀉a􀀉tap􀀉the􀀉way􀀉wire􀀉carries􀀉current.”􀀉He􀀉set􀀉the􀀉pipe􀀉down.􀀉
“But􀀉Jung􀀉flags􀀉any􀀉rhythm􀀉that􀀉doesn’t􀀉fit􀀉the􀀉drill􀀉cycles.􀀉So􀀉we􀀉don’t􀀉just􀀉
drum􀀉—􀀉we􀀉Sync.􀀉We􀀉send􀀉only􀀉when􀀉the􀀉engines􀀉turn􀀉over􀀉and􀀉the􀀉whole􀀉
mountain’s􀀉already􀀉screaming.􀀉Our􀀉signal’s􀀉just􀀉a􀀉ghost􀀉riding􀀉the􀀉noise.􀀉He􀀉
can’t􀀉debug􀀉a􀀉sound􀀉he’s􀀉sure􀀉is􀀉a􀀉loose􀀉bolt.”􀀉

A􀀉true􀀉signal,􀀉buried􀀉inside􀀉a􀀉wall􀀉of􀀉noise􀀉—􀀉the􀀉same􀀉trick􀀉his􀀉own􀀉nerves􀀉
were􀀉playing􀀉on􀀉his􀀉missing􀀉leg,􀀉except􀀉the􀀉miners􀀉had􀀉learned􀀉to􀀉run􀀉it􀀉on􀀉
purpose.􀀉Camilla􀀉filed􀀉it􀀉away􀀉and􀀉said􀀉nothing.􀀉

She􀀉knew􀀉that􀀉naming􀀉his􀀉pain􀀉wasn’t􀀉the􀀉same􀀉as􀀉ending􀀉it.􀀉As􀀉long􀀉as􀀉the􀀉
pain􀀉consumed􀀉him,􀀉Gerald􀀉couldn’t􀀉think􀀉about􀀉anything􀀉else􀀉—􀀉not􀀉
what􀀉she􀀉might􀀉ask􀀉of􀀉him,􀀉not􀀉freedom.􀀉But􀀉this􀀉wasn’t􀀉the􀀉right􀀉time􀀉to􀀉
push􀀉further.􀀉

“I􀀉was􀀉just􀀉curious􀀉about􀀉your􀀉leg,”􀀉she􀀉said.􀀉“Besides,􀀉we􀀉couldn’t􀀉wave􀀉a􀀉
proper􀀉goodbye􀀉after􀀉dinner.”􀀉

Gerald􀀉smiled.􀀉“Bye􀀉right􀀉back􀀉at􀀉ya.􀀉Now􀀉don’t􀀉come􀀉here􀀉anymore!”􀀉


Camilla􀀉walked􀀉out􀀉into􀀉the􀀉night,􀀉the􀀉air􀀉sharp􀀉with􀀉coal􀀉dust􀀉and􀀉the􀀉
distant􀀉metallic􀀉groaning􀀉of􀀉the􀀉cooling􀀉drills.􀀉She􀀉moved􀀉quickly􀀉through􀀉
the􀀉shack-rows,􀀉her􀀉feet􀀉finding􀀉the􀀉familiar􀀉path􀀉by􀀉instinct􀀉—􀀉stepping􀀉
over􀀉the􀀉drainage􀀉channel􀀉that􀀉ran􀀉along􀀉the􀀉perimeter􀀉fence,􀀉ducking􀀉the􀀉
low-hanging􀀉tarp􀀉behind􀀉the􀀉shaft-vent,􀀉cutting􀀉through􀀉the􀀉gap􀀉between􀀉
the􀀉old􀀉generator􀀉housing􀀉and􀀉the􀀉slag􀀉wall.􀀉

Her􀀉mind􀀉was􀀉already􀀉building􀀉the􀀉schematic.􀀉Not􀀉the􀀉device􀀉itself􀀉—􀀉that􀀉
would􀀉come􀀉later.􀀉She􀀉was􀀉building􀀉something􀀉more􀀉abstract:􀀉the􀀉complete􀀉
picture􀀉of􀀉Gerald.􀀉His􀀉body􀀉was􀀉damaged􀀉but􀀉functional,􀀉rebuilt􀀉with􀀉
salvaged􀀉parts,􀀉running􀀉on􀀉pain􀀉and􀀉stubbornness.􀀉His􀀉mind􀀉was􀀉sharp,􀀉
experienced,􀀉full􀀉of􀀉knowledge􀀉they􀀉desperately􀀉needed􀀉—􀀉but􀀉locked􀀉
behind􀀉a􀀉wall􀀉of􀀉pain􀀉that􀀉consumed􀀉almost􀀉all􀀉of􀀉his􀀉capacity.􀀉The􀀉
phantom􀀉limb􀀉was􀀉the􀀉lock􀀉she􀀉needed􀀉to􀀉pick􀀉—􀀉a􀀉relentless􀀉loop􀀉of􀀉
suffering􀀉that􀀉stole􀀉his􀀉attention,􀀉his􀀉sleep,􀀉his􀀉ability􀀉to􀀉think􀀉beyond􀀉the􀀉
next􀀉dose􀀉of􀀉painkillers.􀀉

If􀀉they􀀉could􀀉break􀀉that􀀉loop,􀀉they􀀉wouldn’t􀀉just􀀉be􀀉helping􀀉a􀀉man.􀀉They’d􀀉
be􀀉unlocking􀀉everything􀀉Gerald􀀉had􀀉learned􀀉in􀀉his􀀉years􀀉walking􀀉the􀀉deep􀀉
tunnels􀀉—􀀉how􀀉the􀀉mountain􀀉breathed,􀀉how􀀉Trinity’s􀀉safety􀀉systems􀀉held􀀉
the􀀉Lake􀀉Wall􀀉closed,􀀉how􀀉a􀀉structure􀀉built􀀉to􀀉last􀀉could􀀉be􀀉made􀀉to􀀉fail.􀀉
That􀀉knowledge,􀀉freed􀀉from􀀉the􀀉prison􀀉of􀀉chronic􀀉pain􀀉and􀀉put􀀉to􀀉work􀀉
alongside􀀉Gage’s􀀉physical􀀉map􀀉of􀀉the􀀉mine,􀀉was􀀉the􀀉missing􀀉piece􀀉of􀀉the􀀉
plan􀀉—􀀉not􀀉where􀀉to􀀉strike,􀀉but􀀉how.􀀉

And􀀉she􀀉already􀀉had􀀉an􀀉idea􀀉how􀀉to􀀉do􀀉it.􀀉In􀀉one􀀉of􀀉Pappy’s􀀉medical􀀉
journals,􀀉she’d􀀉read􀀉about􀀉a􀀉technique􀀉so􀀉simple􀀉it􀀉seemed􀀉like􀀉a􀀉trick:􀀉a􀀉box􀀉
with􀀉a􀀉mirror􀀉down􀀉the􀀉center.􀀉The􀀉patient􀀉places􀀉their􀀉intact􀀉limb􀀉on􀀉one􀀉
side􀀉and􀀉looks􀀉at􀀉its􀀉reflection􀀉—􀀉and􀀉the􀀉brain,􀀉seeing􀀉the􀀉reflection􀀉where􀀉
the􀀉missing􀀉limb􀀉should􀀉be,􀀉accepts􀀉the􀀉mirror􀀉image􀀉as􀀉the􀀉limb􀀉obeying.􀀉
The􀀉visual􀀉signal􀀉closes􀀉the􀀉loop􀀉the􀀉motor􀀉cortex􀀉had􀀉been􀀉screaming􀀉into.􀀉
The􀀉muscles􀀉that􀀉don’t􀀉exist􀀉finally􀀉“relax.”􀀉The􀀉screaming􀀉stops.􀀉


A􀀉mirror􀀉and􀀉a􀀉box.􀀉That’s􀀉all􀀉it􀀉would􀀉take.􀀉If􀀉she􀀉could􀀉build􀀉one􀀉from􀀉
scrap.􀀉

Pappy􀀉used􀀉to􀀉say􀀉if􀀈you􀀈could􀀈read􀀈the􀀈manual,􀀈you􀀈owned􀀈the􀀈machine,􀀉she􀀉
thought,􀀉pulling􀀉her􀀉collar􀀉against􀀉the􀀉chemical􀀉wind.􀀉She􀀉didn’t􀀉own􀀉
anything􀀉yet.􀀉But􀀉she’d􀀉found􀀉the􀀉right􀀉page.􀀉

She􀀉slipped􀀉into􀀉the􀀉shack􀀉and􀀉found􀀉Gage􀀉asleep􀀉on􀀉the􀀉floor􀀉near􀀉the􀀉
splinter􀀉model,􀀉his􀀉breath􀀉catching􀀉every􀀉few􀀉seconds􀀉with􀀉the􀀉wet􀀉rattle􀀉of􀀉
soot-lined􀀉lungs.􀀉She􀀉covered􀀉him􀀉with􀀉the􀀉cleanest􀀉rag􀀉they􀀉had􀀉and􀀉sat􀀉in􀀉
the􀀉dark,􀀉listening􀀉to􀀉his􀀉breathing􀀉and􀀉to􀀉the􀀉distant􀀉thrum􀀉of􀀉the􀀉
mountain,􀀉already􀀉planning􀀉tomorrow’s􀀉trip􀀉to􀀉the􀀉Junkyard.􀀉

Gerald,􀀉meanwhile,􀀉lay􀀉on􀀉his􀀉cot􀀉staring􀀉at􀀉the􀀉ceiling.􀀉He􀀉kept􀀉thinking􀀉
about􀀉what􀀉Camilla􀀉had􀀉said.􀀉She􀀉was􀀉the􀀉only􀀉person􀀉he’d􀀉met􀀉in􀀉years􀀉
who􀀉actually􀀉understood􀀉his􀀉problem􀀉—􀀉described􀀉it􀀉better􀀉than􀀉he􀀉ever􀀉
could.􀀉For􀀉the􀀉first􀀉time􀀉since􀀉the􀀉accident,􀀉someone􀀉had􀀉looked􀀉at􀀉his􀀉pain􀀉
and􀀉seen􀀉not􀀉a􀀉dead􀀉end􀀉but􀀉a􀀉solvable􀀉equation.􀀉That􀀉night,􀀉Gerald􀀉didn’t􀀉
need􀀉another􀀉pill.􀀉A􀀉glimmer􀀉of􀀉hope􀀉had􀀉taken􀀉its􀀉place,􀀉and􀀉it􀀉rocked􀀉him􀀉
to􀀉sleep.􀀉


Chapter􀀉16􀀉—􀀉The􀀉Water􀀉Hammer􀀉

Gage􀀉was􀀉pacing􀀉his􀀉room,􀀉which􀀉was􀀉riddled􀀉with􀀉small􀀉pieces􀀉of􀀉paper􀀉—􀀉
sketches􀀉of􀀉the􀀉Lake􀀉Wall,􀀉cross-sections􀀉of􀀉pipe􀀉junctions,􀀉drainage􀀉
calculations􀀉scratched􀀉in􀀉coal􀀉pencil.􀀉These􀀉drawings􀀉were􀀉the􀀉foundation􀀉
of􀀉his􀀉plan,􀀉but􀀉he􀀉was􀀉still􀀉missing􀀉a􀀉way􀀉to􀀉make􀀉it􀀉work.􀀉He􀀉stopped􀀉in􀀉
front􀀉of􀀉the􀀉splinter􀀉model􀀉and􀀉studied􀀉every􀀉weak􀀉spot.􀀉

The􀀉plan􀀉he’d􀀉laid􀀉out􀀉before􀀉his􀀉collapse􀀉—􀀉flood􀀉the􀀉mine,􀀉drown􀀉the􀀉
Trinity’s􀀉silver􀀉pipeline􀀉—􀀉had􀀉been􀀉clear􀀉in􀀉his􀀉head.􀀉The􀀉target􀀉was􀀉
mapped,􀀉the􀀉timing􀀉calculated,􀀉the􀀉escape􀀉window􀀉identified.􀀉What􀀉he􀀉
didn’t􀀉have􀀉was􀀉a􀀉weapon.􀀉

“Flood􀀉the􀀉mine,”􀀉he􀀉whispered.􀀉“Sounds􀀉simple,􀀉but􀀉it’s􀀉a􀀉hard􀀉problem.􀀉
How􀀉do􀀉I􀀉breach􀀉the􀀉Lake􀀉Wall?􀀉A􀀉bomb’s􀀉out􀀉of􀀉reach􀀉—􀀉and􀀉even􀀉if􀀉I􀀉
had􀀉one,􀀉the􀀉blast􀀉would􀀉be􀀉too􀀉loud.􀀉The􀀉sensors􀀉would􀀉pick􀀉it􀀉up.􀀉Guards􀀉
would􀀉seal􀀉the􀀉tunnels,􀀉and􀀉we’d􀀉be􀀉trapped􀀉inside􀀉with􀀉the􀀉water.”􀀉

He􀀉paced.􀀉Drilling􀀉through?􀀉The􀀉wall􀀉corridor􀀉was􀀉the􀀉most􀀉surveilled􀀉
choke-point􀀉in􀀉the􀀉mine􀀉—􀀉the􀀉first􀀉scream􀀉of􀀉a􀀉bit􀀉on􀀉stone􀀉would􀀉have􀀉
Patrollers􀀉sealing􀀉the􀀉tunnels􀀉before􀀉he’d􀀉cut􀀉a􀀉centimeter.􀀉A􀀉slow􀀉collapse,􀀉
then?􀀉Cave􀀉the􀀉support􀀉tunnels􀀉above􀀉the􀀉lake􀀉and􀀉let􀀉gravity􀀉do􀀉the􀀉rest?􀀉
He􀀉glanced􀀉at􀀉the􀀉model.􀀉The􀀉supports􀀉were􀀉on􀀉the􀀉wrong􀀉side􀀉of􀀉the􀀉fault􀀉
lines.􀀉Bring􀀉those􀀉down􀀉and􀀉he’d􀀉just􀀉bury􀀉the􀀉lake􀀉deeper,􀀉not􀀉breach􀀉the􀀉
wall.􀀉

Every􀀉weapon􀀉he􀀉could􀀉think􀀉of􀀉either􀀉took􀀉too􀀉long􀀉or􀀉rang􀀉too􀀉loud.􀀉He􀀉
needed􀀉something􀀉already􀀉inside􀀉the􀀉system􀀉—􀀉something􀀉Trinity􀀉wouldn’t􀀉
notice􀀉until􀀉it􀀉was􀀉too􀀉late.􀀉

He􀀉touched􀀉the􀀉spot􀀉on􀀉the􀀉model􀀉where􀀉the􀀉high-pressure􀀉water􀀉pipes􀀉ran􀀉
along􀀉the􀀉main􀀉wall􀀉of􀀉the􀀉lake􀀉—􀀉the􀀉same􀀉pipes􀀉that􀀉fed􀀉the􀀉turbines􀀉
powering􀀉the􀀉Lower􀀉Hub.􀀉


“I􀀉don’t􀀉need􀀉a􀀉bomb,”􀀉he􀀉realized,􀀉his􀀉pacing􀀉slowing.􀀉“I􀀉need􀀉the􀀉right􀀉
vibration.􀀉Camilla􀀉and􀀉I􀀉already􀀉mapped􀀉where􀀉the􀀉cracks􀀉are􀀉—􀀉the􀀉
Skeleton􀀉and􀀉the􀀉Pulse.􀀉But􀀉I􀀉need􀀉a􀀉way􀀉to􀀉shake􀀉them􀀉loose.”􀀉

He􀀉studied􀀉the􀀉water􀀉turbines􀀉on􀀉the􀀉model.􀀉The􀀉pipes􀀉feeding􀀉them􀀉ran􀀉
fast􀀉and􀀉heavy􀀉—􀀉more􀀉force􀀉than􀀉anything􀀉else􀀉moving􀀉in􀀉the􀀉mine.􀀉If􀀉that􀀉
flow􀀉was􀀉suddenly􀀉stopped…􀀉

“A􀀉water􀀉hammer,”􀀉he􀀉breathed.􀀉“Slam􀀉the􀀉pipes􀀉shut􀀉all􀀉at􀀉once􀀉and􀀉the􀀉
water􀀉can’t􀀉stop.􀀉It􀀉kicks􀀉back􀀉—􀀉slams􀀉the􀀉pipe􀀉like􀀉a􀀉fist,􀀉and􀀉the􀀉pipe􀀉
carries􀀉that􀀉blow􀀉straight􀀉into􀀉the􀀉rock.􀀉If􀀉I􀀉hit􀀉the􀀉right􀀉junction􀀉—􀀉the􀀉one􀀉
I’ve􀀉already􀀉mapped,􀀉where􀀉the􀀉cracks􀀉run􀀉through􀀉the􀀉wall􀀉—􀀉the􀀉water􀀉
punches􀀉through􀀉where􀀉it’s􀀉weakest.􀀉I􀀉don’t􀀉need􀀉an􀀉explosion.􀀉I􀀉just􀀉need􀀉
to􀀉turn􀀉the􀀉water􀀉against􀀉the􀀉mountain.”􀀉

He’d􀀉heard􀀉the􀀉old􀀉hands􀀉talk􀀉about􀀉it􀀉—􀀉pipes􀀉that􀀉burst􀀉when􀀉a􀀉valve􀀉
came􀀉down􀀉too􀀉fast,􀀉steel􀀉torn􀀉open􀀉by􀀉water􀀉that􀀉had􀀉nowhere􀀉to􀀉go.􀀉Pipes􀀉
were􀀉one􀀉thing.􀀉Hitting􀀉a􀀉lake􀀉wall􀀉was􀀉another.􀀉The􀀉blow􀀉had􀀉to􀀉land􀀉
where􀀉the􀀉rock􀀉was􀀉already􀀉cracked,􀀉again􀀉and􀀉again􀀉if􀀉it􀀉had􀀉to,􀀉until􀀉the􀀉
mountain’s􀀉own􀀉weight􀀉took􀀉over􀀉and􀀉finished􀀉the􀀉work.􀀉That􀀉meant􀀉the􀀉
splinter􀀉model.􀀉Every􀀉seam,􀀉every􀀉fault,􀀉every􀀉weak􀀉place􀀉he􀀉and􀀉Camilla􀀉
had􀀉mapped.􀀉The􀀉hammer􀀉was􀀉only􀀉as􀀉good􀀉as􀀉where􀀉it􀀉landed.􀀉

He􀀉had􀀉the􀀉target.􀀉He􀀉had􀀉the􀀉weapon.􀀉What􀀉he􀀉didn’t􀀉have􀀉was􀀉a􀀉trigger.􀀉

He􀀉sank􀀉back,􀀉the􀀉heat􀀉of􀀉the􀀉idea􀀉gone􀀉cold.􀀉Those􀀉valves􀀉didn’t􀀉answer􀀉to􀀉
wrenches􀀉anymore􀀉—􀀉they􀀉answered􀀉to􀀉Trinity.􀀉To􀀉cameras􀀉and􀀉codes􀀉and􀀉
locked􀀉rooms􀀉full􀀉of􀀉machines􀀉nobody􀀉in􀀉Sector-1􀀉had􀀉ever􀀉opened.􀀉
Whoever􀀉could􀀉open􀀉that􀀉door􀀉knew􀀉more􀀉than􀀉a􀀉man􀀉who􀀉thought􀀉in􀀉
splinters􀀉and􀀉coal􀀉dust.􀀉He􀀉was􀀉a􀀉miner􀀉with􀀉a􀀉model􀀉and􀀉a􀀉plan,􀀉standing􀀉
in􀀉front􀀉of􀀉a􀀉locked􀀉door􀀉with􀀉no􀀉key.􀀉

He􀀉collapsed􀀉on􀀉the􀀉floor,􀀉lying􀀉amidst􀀉his􀀉blueprints.􀀉While􀀉he􀀉slept,􀀉
Camilla􀀉sat􀀉watching􀀉him.􀀉She􀀉didn’t􀀉see􀀉a􀀉dreamer􀀉anymore;􀀉she􀀉saw􀀉a􀀉
machine􀀉running􀀉on􀀉fumes,􀀉one􀀉bad􀀉night􀀉from􀀉stalling􀀉out􀀉for􀀉good.􀀉She􀀉


was􀀉going􀀉to􀀉lose􀀉her􀀉brother􀀉unless􀀉they􀀉found􀀉a􀀉way􀀉to􀀉bridge􀀉the􀀉gap􀀉
between􀀉Gage’s􀀉physical􀀉map􀀉of􀀉the􀀉mine􀀉and􀀉the􀀉digital􀀉systems􀀉that􀀉
controlled􀀉it.􀀉

She’d􀀉promised􀀉to􀀉stay􀀉out􀀉of􀀉his􀀉plans.􀀉She’d􀀉meant􀀉it􀀉at􀀉the􀀉time.􀀉But􀀉
promises􀀉made􀀉to􀀉protect􀀉a􀀉man’s􀀉pride􀀉were􀀉worth􀀉less􀀉than􀀉the􀀉man􀀉
himself,􀀉and􀀉Gage􀀉was􀀉burning􀀉down􀀉to􀀉the􀀉wire.􀀉She􀀉decided􀀉to􀀉call􀀉a􀀉
meeting􀀉with􀀉Ben􀀉and􀀉Otto􀀉—􀀉three􀀉heads􀀉to􀀉work􀀉the􀀉problem􀀉Gage􀀉
couldn’t􀀉solve􀀉alone.􀀉

“I􀀉told􀀉him􀀉this􀀉is􀀉a􀀉useless􀀉quest,”􀀉Ben􀀉muttered,􀀉leaning􀀉against􀀉the􀀉wall.􀀉
“Miner􀀉Town􀀉has􀀉been􀀉this􀀉way􀀉since􀀉before􀀉we􀀉were􀀉born.􀀉It􀀉isn’t􀀉going􀀉to􀀉
change􀀉because􀀉one􀀉fool􀀉started􀀉a􀀉revolution.”􀀉

Otto􀀉sat􀀉in􀀉the􀀉corner,􀀉eyes􀀉half-closed,􀀉his􀀉breathing􀀉barely􀀉visible.􀀉Above􀀉
his􀀉open􀀉palm,􀀉his􀀉heavy􀀉iron􀀉house􀀉keys􀀉hung􀀉motionless􀀉in􀀉the􀀉air􀀉—􀀉not􀀉
spinning,􀀉not􀀉wobbling,􀀉just􀀉suspended,􀀉as􀀉if􀀉gravity􀀉had􀀉quietly􀀉decided􀀉to􀀉
look􀀉the􀀉other􀀉way.􀀉

Camilla􀀉had􀀉seen􀀉him􀀉do􀀉this􀀉before􀀉—􀀉small􀀉demonstrations􀀉during􀀉his􀀉
meditation􀀉sessions,􀀉moments􀀉where􀀉the􀀉air􀀉around􀀉Otto􀀉seemed􀀉to􀀉
thicken􀀉and􀀉behave􀀉differently.􀀉She’d􀀉never􀀉understood􀀉it,􀀉and􀀉tonight􀀉she􀀉
had􀀉no􀀉patience􀀉for􀀉it.􀀉

“Can􀀉you􀀉stop􀀉being􀀉a􀀉freak􀀉for􀀉just􀀉a􀀉minute􀀉and􀀉think􀀉about􀀉how􀀉we􀀉can􀀉
help􀀉Gage?􀀉I’m􀀉tired􀀉of􀀉your􀀉good-for-nothing􀀉tricks!􀀉Floating􀀉keys􀀉won’t􀀉
stop􀀉Trinity’s􀀉guards.”􀀉

Otto􀀉didn’t􀀉open􀀉his􀀉eyes.􀀉He􀀉flicked􀀉his􀀉wrist,􀀉and􀀉the􀀉air􀀉around􀀉the􀀉keys􀀉
shimmered􀀉and􀀉thickened.􀀉

Camilla􀀉reached􀀉out􀀉to􀀉swat􀀉them􀀉away,􀀉but􀀉her􀀉hand􀀉stopped􀀉short􀀉—􀀉
hitting􀀉a􀀉wall􀀉of􀀉dense,􀀉vibrating􀀉air.􀀉It􀀉felt􀀉like􀀉pushing􀀉through􀀉thick􀀉
rubber.􀀉Not􀀉magnetism.􀀉Not􀀉a􀀉wire􀀉trick.􀀉A􀀉physical􀀉resistance􀀉she􀀉could􀀉
feel􀀉pressing􀀉back􀀉against􀀉her􀀉palm.􀀉


“It’s􀀉not􀀉a􀀉trick,􀀉Camilla,”􀀉Otto􀀉said,􀀉his􀀉voice􀀉a􀀉steady􀀉hum􀀉that􀀉seemed􀀉to􀀉
resonate􀀉from􀀉his􀀉chest􀀉rather􀀉than􀀉his􀀉throat.􀀉“Master􀀉Chan􀀉called􀀉it􀀉
Focused􀀉Intent.􀀉He􀀉taught􀀉me􀀉the􀀉mind􀀉is􀀉a􀀉high-pressure􀀉system.􀀉Most􀀉
people􀀉let􀀉that􀀉pressure􀀉leak􀀉out􀀉in􀀉talk􀀉and􀀉fear.􀀉I􀀉learned􀀉to􀀉hold􀀉it.􀀉To􀀉
compress􀀉it.”􀀉

He􀀉opened􀀉his􀀉eyes,􀀉and􀀉for􀀉a􀀉second􀀉they􀀉looked􀀉like􀀉polished􀀉glass.􀀉“I’m􀀉
not􀀉moving􀀉the􀀉keys.􀀉I’m􀀉making􀀉the􀀉air􀀉around􀀉them􀀉too􀀉dense􀀉for􀀉them􀀉
to􀀉fall.􀀉I􀀉haven’t􀀉unlocked􀀉my􀀉full􀀉potential􀀉—􀀉Chan􀀉said􀀉a􀀉true􀀉master􀀉
could􀀉use􀀉this􀀉to􀀉exert􀀉force􀀉on􀀉an􀀉opponent,􀀉to􀀉create􀀉a􀀉barrier􀀉that􀀉even􀀉a􀀉
coal-slide􀀉couldn’t􀀉penetrate.􀀉I’m􀀉not􀀉there􀀉yet.􀀉But􀀉the􀀉resonance􀀉is􀀉
starting􀀉to􀀉listen.”􀀉

Camilla’s􀀉scorn􀀉died􀀉in􀀉her􀀉throat.􀀉The􀀉keys􀀉fell􀀉to􀀉the􀀉floor􀀉with􀀉a􀀉heavy􀀉
clatter,􀀉Otto’s􀀉invisible􀀉wall􀀉vanishing􀀉as􀀉he􀀉slumped􀀉from􀀉the􀀉effort.􀀉

She􀀉didn’t􀀉understand􀀉the􀀉mechanism.􀀉Otto􀀉did􀀉—􀀉at􀀉least􀀉the􀀉version􀀉
Master􀀉Chan􀀉had􀀉given􀀉him.􀀉But􀀉the􀀉word􀀉he􀀉kept􀀉using􀀉—􀀉resonance􀀉—􀀉
was􀀉one􀀉Camilla􀀉recognized,􀀉from􀀉Pappy’s􀀉engineering􀀉manuals.􀀉The􀀉
Stubborn􀀉Carbon􀀉beneath􀀉their􀀉feet􀀉produced􀀉measurable􀀉vibrations􀀉—􀀉
piezoelectric􀀉energy􀀉that􀀉bled􀀉through􀀉the􀀉rock􀀉and􀀉made􀀉radios􀀉hum􀀉and􀀉
salt􀀉dance.􀀉If􀀉that􀀉energy􀀉could􀀉interact􀀉with􀀉copper􀀉dust􀀉and􀀉splinter􀀉
models,􀀉was􀀉it􀀉so􀀉impossible􀀉that􀀉a􀀉human􀀉body􀀉—􀀉sixty􀀉percent􀀉water,􀀉
laced􀀉with􀀉salt􀀉and􀀉iron􀀉—􀀉could􀀉learn􀀉to􀀉interact􀀉with􀀉it􀀉too?􀀉Maybe􀀉
Master􀀉Chan􀀉and􀀉Pappy􀀉had􀀉been􀀉reading􀀉the􀀉same􀀉manual􀀉in􀀉different􀀉
languages.􀀉She􀀉didn’t􀀉have􀀉an􀀉answer.􀀉But􀀉she􀀉filed􀀉the􀀉question􀀉away.􀀉

Otto􀀉looked􀀉at􀀉Ben,􀀉ignoring􀀉Camilla’s􀀉silence,􀀉and􀀉asked􀀉if􀀉he’d􀀉like􀀉a􀀉
break.􀀉Ben,􀀉exhausted􀀉by􀀉the􀀉circular􀀉logic􀀉of􀀉their􀀉planning,􀀉agreed.􀀉
Camilla􀀉kept􀀉insisting􀀉they􀀉stay􀀉until􀀉they􀀉found􀀉a􀀉concrete􀀉solution􀀉—􀀉one􀀉
that􀀉could􀀉move􀀉the􀀉plan􀀉forward􀀉without􀀉Gage􀀉even􀀉knowing􀀉they􀀉were􀀉
involved.􀀉


But􀀉as􀀉they􀀉prepared􀀉to􀀉say􀀉goodnight,􀀉Otto􀀉stopped􀀉Camilla.􀀉He􀀉had􀀉a􀀉
mysterious,􀀉calculated􀀉smile􀀉painted􀀉across􀀉his􀀉face.􀀉

“Camilla,”􀀉he􀀉said􀀉softly.􀀉“Tell􀀉me􀀉about􀀉your􀀉meeting􀀉with􀀉Gerald􀀉at􀀉his􀀉
house􀀉again.”􀀉


Chapter􀀉17􀀉—􀀉Mirror􀀉Mirror􀀉in􀀉the􀀉Box􀀉

Camilla􀀉told􀀉him􀀉everything.􀀉The􀀉prosthetic􀀉removal,􀀉the􀀉sweat,􀀉every􀀉
single􀀉painkiller􀀉rationed􀀉like􀀉gold.􀀉The􀀉three􀀉questions.􀀉The􀀉loop􀀉of􀀉
suffering􀀉—􀀉the􀀉brain􀀉screaming􀀉signals􀀉into􀀉a􀀉void,􀀉the􀀉silence􀀉interpreted􀀉
as􀀉pain.􀀉Gerald’s􀀉face􀀉when􀀉she􀀉described􀀉it􀀉back􀀉to􀀉him.􀀉The􀀉way􀀉he􀀉looked􀀉
at􀀉her􀀉like􀀉she􀀉was􀀉the􀀉first􀀉person􀀉in􀀉years􀀉who’d􀀉seen􀀉inside􀀉his􀀉skull.􀀉

Otto􀀉listened􀀉without􀀉interrupting.􀀉When􀀉she􀀉finished,􀀉he􀀉sat􀀉still􀀉for􀀉a􀀉
long􀀉time.􀀉Then􀀉he􀀉said:􀀉“Master􀀉Chan􀀉did􀀉something􀀉close􀀉to􀀉this􀀉once.􀀉
His􀀉hand􀀉locked􀀉up􀀉after􀀉he􀀉overworked􀀉it􀀉—􀀉weeks􀀉of􀀉cramping,􀀉fingers􀀉
that􀀉wouldn’t􀀉open􀀉even􀀉when􀀉he􀀉willed􀀉them.􀀉He􀀉didn’t􀀉try􀀉to􀀉force􀀉it.􀀉He􀀉
sat􀀉cross-legged􀀉every􀀉morning􀀉and􀀉opened􀀉the􀀉hand􀀉in􀀉his􀀉mind􀀉—􀀉slowly,􀀉
fully,􀀉every􀀉finger􀀉uncurling,􀀉an􀀉hour􀀉at􀀉a􀀉time.􀀉After􀀉a􀀉month􀀉the􀀉hand􀀉
started􀀉answering􀀉him􀀉again.􀀉I􀀉never􀀉understood􀀉why􀀉it􀀉worked.􀀉But􀀉what􀀉
you􀀉just􀀉described􀀉—􀀉the􀀉brain􀀉needing􀀉feedback􀀉to􀀉break􀀉the􀀉loop􀀉—􀀉that’s􀀉
the􀀉same􀀉principle.􀀉You􀀉give􀀉the􀀉brain􀀉a􀀉mirror;􀀉Chan􀀉gave􀀉it􀀉attention.”􀀉

Camilla􀀉stared􀀉at􀀉him.􀀉The􀀉Mirror􀀉Box.􀀉She’d􀀉read􀀉about􀀉it􀀉in􀀉Pappy’s􀀉
journals􀀉and􀀉had􀀉been􀀉turning􀀉it􀀉over􀀉in􀀉her􀀉head􀀉since􀀉she􀀉left􀀉Gerald’s􀀉
door.􀀉Otto􀀉had􀀉arrived􀀉at􀀉the􀀉same􀀉place􀀉from􀀉a􀀉completely􀀉different􀀉
direction.􀀉

“I􀀉can􀀉build􀀉it,”􀀉she􀀉said.􀀉“But􀀉I􀀉need􀀉help􀀉with􀀉the􀀉physical􀀉construction􀀉

—􀀉cutting􀀉the􀀉box,􀀉seating􀀉the􀀉glass,􀀉making􀀉it􀀉sturdy􀀉enough􀀉that􀀉the􀀉
mirror􀀉doesn’t􀀉shift􀀉during􀀉use.􀀉If􀀉the􀀉reflection􀀉moves􀀉even􀀉slightly,􀀉his􀀉
brain􀀉will􀀉reject􀀉it.”􀀉
“I􀀉can􀀉do􀀉that,”􀀉Otto􀀉said.􀀉“Precision􀀉and􀀉stillness􀀉—􀀉that’s􀀉what􀀉Chan􀀉
trained􀀉me􀀉for.􀀉You􀀉design􀀉it.􀀉I’ll􀀉build􀀉it.”􀀉

Ben􀀉looked􀀉between􀀉them,􀀉not􀀉fully􀀉following􀀉the􀀉science􀀉but􀀉
understanding􀀉the􀀉stakes.􀀉“So􀀉we􀀉fix􀀉Gerald’s􀀉pain.􀀉And􀀉then􀀉what?”􀀉


“Then􀀉we􀀉have􀀉someone􀀉who􀀉knows􀀉how􀀉Trinity’s􀀉systems􀀉work􀀉from􀀉the􀀉
inside,”􀀉Camilla􀀉said.􀀉“And􀀉Gage􀀉has􀀉someone􀀉who􀀉can􀀉help􀀉him􀀉open􀀉the􀀉
door􀀉he’s􀀉been􀀉standing􀀉in􀀉front􀀉of.”􀀉

They􀀉split􀀉for􀀉the􀀉night.􀀉Camilla􀀉didn’t􀀉sleep.􀀉She􀀉sat􀀉with􀀉a􀀉stub􀀉of􀀉coal􀀉
pencil􀀉and􀀉a􀀉scrap􀀉of􀀉flat􀀉wood,􀀉working􀀉out􀀉the􀀉rough􀀉geometry􀀉—􀀉where􀀉
the􀀉mirror􀀉would􀀉sit,􀀉how􀀉far􀀉from􀀉him,􀀉the􀀉angle􀀉that􀀉would􀀉put􀀉the􀀉
reflection􀀉where􀀉his􀀉missing􀀉leg􀀉should􀀉be.􀀉She􀀉didn’t􀀉have􀀉Pappy’s􀀉optics􀀉
notes􀀉in􀀉front􀀉of􀀉her􀀉—􀀉those􀀉were􀀉still􀀉hidden􀀉under􀀉the􀀉floorboards􀀉back􀀉
home􀀉—􀀉but􀀉she’d􀀉read􀀉them􀀉so􀀉many􀀉times􀀉she􀀉could􀀉half-see􀀉the􀀉diagrams􀀉
in􀀉the􀀉dark.􀀉The􀀉principle􀀉was􀀉simple􀀉enough􀀉to􀀉hold􀀉in􀀉her􀀉head:􀀉a􀀉mirror􀀉
only􀀉works􀀉when􀀉the􀀉eye,􀀉the􀀉reflection,􀀉and􀀉the􀀉body􀀉line􀀉up􀀉the􀀉way􀀉the􀀉
brain􀀉expects.􀀉She􀀉pictured􀀉Gerald􀀉on􀀉his􀀉cot􀀉—􀀉the􀀉stump,􀀉the􀀉line􀀉of􀀉his􀀉
good􀀉leg,􀀉his􀀉eye􀀉line􀀉when􀀉seated􀀉—􀀉and􀀉sketched.􀀉By􀀉dawn,􀀉she􀀉had􀀉a􀀉
plan.􀀉Not􀀉exact􀀉—􀀉they’d􀀉refine􀀉it􀀉when􀀉they􀀉built􀀉—􀀉but􀀉a􀀉place􀀉to􀀉start.􀀉

The􀀉next􀀉day􀀉at􀀉the􀀉mine,􀀉Otto􀀉could􀀉barely􀀉focus.􀀉He􀀉sat􀀉in􀀉a􀀉corner􀀉of􀀉the􀀉
shaft,􀀉his􀀉mind􀀉running􀀉through􀀉the􀀉build􀀉—􀀉how􀀉to􀀉cut􀀉the􀀉box,􀀉where􀀉to􀀉
seat􀀉the􀀉mirror,􀀉what􀀉scrap􀀉materials􀀉would􀀉hold􀀉glass􀀉steady􀀉without􀀉
shifting􀀉—􀀉when􀀉he􀀉felt􀀉the􀀉air􀀉density􀀉shift􀀉behind􀀉him􀀉before􀀉the􀀉heavy􀀉
thud􀀉of􀀉boots.􀀉

Splitz􀀉—􀀉a􀀉guard􀀉heavy􀀉enough􀀉to􀀉break􀀉the􀀉rhythm􀀉of􀀉any􀀉crew􀀉he􀀉passed.􀀉
Otto􀀉didn’t􀀉turn􀀉around.􀀉He􀀉focused􀀉on􀀉his􀀉center,􀀉dropping􀀉into􀀉the􀀉
passive􀀉defensive􀀉state􀀉he’d􀀉practiced􀀉a􀀉thousand􀀉times􀀉in􀀉the􀀉dark􀀉—􀀉a􀀉
quiet􀀉compression􀀉of􀀉his􀀉awareness,􀀉his􀀉body􀀉bracing􀀉itself􀀉from􀀉the􀀉inside􀀉
out.􀀉

The􀀉guard’s􀀉boot􀀉met􀀉Otto’s􀀉lower􀀉back􀀉with􀀉sickening􀀉force,􀀉but􀀉Otto􀀉
didn’t􀀉fly􀀉forward.􀀉His􀀉body􀀉absorbed􀀉the􀀉impact􀀉like􀀉a􀀉wall􀀉of􀀉wet􀀉sand􀀉—􀀉
heavy,􀀉dead,􀀉no􀀉satisfying􀀉snap.􀀉To􀀉Splitz,􀀉it􀀉felt􀀉like􀀉kicking􀀉something􀀉that􀀉
simply􀀉refused􀀉to􀀉move􀀉the􀀉way􀀉a􀀉human􀀉body􀀉should.􀀉


Otto􀀉let􀀉out􀀉a􀀉calculated􀀉groan,􀀉sinking􀀉to􀀉one􀀉knee􀀉to􀀉maintain􀀉the􀀉illusion􀀉
of􀀉weakness.􀀉A􀀉miner􀀉who􀀉didn’t􀀉break􀀉was􀀉friction,􀀉and􀀉in􀀉Sector-1,􀀉
friction􀀉was􀀉removed.􀀉He􀀉picked􀀉up􀀉his􀀉shovel􀀉and􀀉got􀀉back􀀉to􀀉work,􀀉
promising􀀉himself􀀉that􀀉soon􀀉this􀀉anguish􀀉would􀀉end.􀀉

That􀀉evening,􀀉Camilla􀀉arrived􀀉at􀀉Otto’s􀀉shack􀀉looking􀀉worn􀀉out􀀉—􀀉no􀀉
sleep,􀀉watching􀀉Gage’s􀀉mental􀀉state􀀉deteriorate􀀉in􀀉real􀀉time,􀀉terrified􀀉of􀀉
where􀀉it􀀉was􀀉heading.􀀉Ben􀀉was􀀉already􀀉there.􀀉

Otto􀀉wasted􀀉no􀀉time.􀀉“Your􀀉diagnosis􀀉was􀀉sharp.􀀉But􀀉diagnosis􀀉won’t􀀉fix􀀉it.􀀉
We􀀉have􀀉to􀀉build.”􀀉

Camilla􀀉nodded􀀉—􀀉she’d􀀉had􀀉the􀀉answer􀀉since􀀉she􀀉walked􀀉out􀀉of􀀉Gerald’s􀀉
door.􀀉“A􀀉mirror􀀉box.􀀉But􀀉I􀀉can’t􀀉build􀀉it􀀉from􀀉scrap􀀉tin􀀉and􀀉hope.􀀉The􀀉
reflection􀀉has􀀉to􀀉be􀀉clean􀀉—􀀉distortion-free.􀀉If􀀉the􀀉image􀀉is􀀉warped,􀀉the􀀉
brain􀀉won’t􀀉buy􀀉it,􀀉and􀀉we’re􀀉back􀀉to􀀉nothing.􀀉I􀀉need􀀉proper􀀉glass􀀉and􀀉
proper􀀉silver-backing,􀀉and􀀉I􀀉need􀀉someone􀀉who􀀉can􀀉calculate􀀉the􀀉angles􀀉so􀀉
the􀀉reflection􀀉sits􀀉exactly􀀉where􀀉the􀀉missing􀀉leg􀀉should􀀉be.”􀀉

Camilla􀀉pulled􀀉the􀀉plan􀀉from􀀉her􀀉jacket􀀉—􀀉the􀀉rough􀀉geometry􀀉she’d􀀉
worked􀀉out􀀉overnight.􀀉She􀀉walked􀀉Otto􀀉and􀀉Ben􀀉through􀀉the􀀉build.􀀉

“If􀀉we􀀉get􀀉this􀀉right,”􀀉Camilla􀀉said,􀀉“Gerald􀀉inserts􀀉his􀀉stump􀀉on􀀉one􀀉side􀀉
and􀀉his􀀉healthy􀀉leg􀀉on􀀉the􀀉other.􀀉He􀀉moves􀀉the􀀉healthy􀀉leg.􀀉The􀀉mirror􀀉
shows􀀉him􀀉the􀀉movement􀀉where􀀉his􀀉missing􀀉leg􀀉should􀀉be.􀀉His􀀉brain􀀉
accepts􀀉it.􀀉The􀀉loop􀀉stops.”􀀉

“And􀀉then􀀉we􀀉have􀀉Gerald,”􀀉Otto􀀉added.􀀉“Everything􀀉he􀀉knows􀀉about􀀉
Trinity’s􀀉grid,􀀉freed􀀉up􀀉and􀀉pointed􀀉at􀀉our􀀉problem.”􀀉

Their􀀉best􀀉bet􀀉for􀀉parts􀀉was􀀉the􀀉Junkyard􀀉—􀀉the􀀉great􀀉graveyard􀀉of􀀉Old􀀉Era􀀉
waste􀀉at􀀉the􀀉edge􀀉of􀀉Miner􀀉Town.􀀉They􀀉needed􀀉optical-grade􀀉glass􀀉and􀀉
silver-backing.􀀉Nothing􀀉less􀀉would􀀉produce􀀉a􀀉reflection􀀉clean􀀉enough􀀉to􀀉
fool􀀉a􀀉human􀀉brain.􀀉


The􀀉next􀀉morning,􀀉Gage􀀉knocked􀀉on􀀉Ben’s􀀉door􀀉furiously.􀀉No􀀉response.􀀉
He􀀉usually􀀉walked􀀉to􀀉the􀀉mine􀀉with􀀉Ben􀀉and􀀉Otto,􀀉but􀀉today􀀉both􀀉doors􀀉
were􀀉locked􀀉and􀀉silent.􀀉The􀀉morning􀀉whistle􀀉blew􀀉—􀀉the􀀉dark􀀉monster􀀉
sending􀀉smoke􀀉hoops􀀉into􀀉the􀀉sky􀀉—􀀉and􀀉Gage􀀉couldn’t􀀉afford􀀉to􀀉wait.􀀉He􀀉
hurried􀀉toward􀀉the􀀉pits,􀀉his􀀉mind􀀉heavy􀀉with􀀉the􀀉weight􀀉of􀀉his􀀉stalling􀀉plan.􀀉

As􀀉he􀀉disappeared􀀉into􀀉the􀀉soot,􀀉the􀀉silence􀀉in􀀉Otto’s􀀉shack􀀉broke􀀉—􀀉not􀀉
with􀀉laughter,􀀉but􀀉with􀀉the􀀉sharp,􀀉held􀀉breathing􀀉of􀀉three􀀉people􀀉who􀀉had􀀉
just􀀉dodged􀀉discovery.􀀉They’d􀀉waited􀀉motionless,􀀉pressed􀀉against􀀉the􀀉walls,􀀉
until􀀉Gage’s􀀉heavy􀀉footsteps􀀉faded􀀉down􀀉the􀀉ridge􀀉path.􀀉

Camilla􀀉had􀀉planned􀀉this􀀉days􀀉ago.􀀉She’d􀀉traded􀀉her􀀉last􀀉salt􀀉ration􀀉to􀀉a􀀉
runner􀀉in􀀉the􀀉Shadow􀀉Economy􀀉to􀀉ghost􀀉Ben􀀉and􀀉Otto’s􀀉badge􀀉numbers􀀉
for􀀉one􀀉shift􀀉—􀀉two􀀉miners􀀉in􀀉the􀀉upper􀀉tunnels􀀉would􀀉double-scan􀀉at􀀉
intake􀀉and􀀉cover􀀉the􀀉gap.􀀉It􀀉was􀀉the􀀉same􀀉trick􀀉Gerald􀀉had􀀉used􀀉for􀀉Gage,􀀉
but􀀉cheaper􀀉with􀀉two􀀉bodies􀀉instead􀀉of􀀉three,􀀉and􀀉Camilla􀀉had􀀉learned􀀉the􀀉
system􀀉well􀀉enough􀀉to􀀉arrange􀀉it􀀉herself.􀀉One􀀉day.􀀉That’s􀀉all􀀉they􀀉had􀀉
before􀀉the􀀉numbers􀀉would􀀉be􀀉missed.􀀉

“He’s􀀉gone,”􀀉Ben􀀉whispered.􀀉“Now􀀉let’s􀀉go􀀉find􀀉that􀀉glass.”􀀉

They􀀉slipped􀀉out􀀉of􀀉Otto’s􀀉shack􀀉and􀀉into􀀉the􀀉dust-yellow􀀉dawn􀀉—􀀉three􀀉
small􀀉figures􀀉moving􀀉fast􀀉against􀀉the􀀉ridge,􀀉the􀀉Junkyard􀀉two􀀉hours􀀉of􀀉
broken􀀉ground􀀉ahead.􀀉


Chapter􀀉18􀀉—􀀉A􀀉Fever􀀉of􀀉the􀀉Eyes􀀉

The􀀉terrain􀀉shifted􀀉on􀀉every􀀉step.􀀉Sector-1’s􀀉compacted􀀉dust􀀉gave􀀉way􀀉to􀀉
slag,􀀉slag􀀉gave􀀉way􀀉to􀀉broken􀀉concrete􀀉and􀀉twisted􀀉rebar􀀉from􀀉the􀀉old􀀉world.􀀉
The􀀉sun􀀉climbed􀀉fast􀀉at􀀉their􀀉backs􀀉—􀀉and􀀉that􀀉was􀀉the􀀉real􀀉enemy,􀀉not􀀉the􀀉
ground.􀀉Two􀀉hours􀀉across􀀉the􀀉open􀀉flats􀀉was􀀉a􀀉dose􀀉every􀀉surface􀀉worker􀀉
knew􀀉how􀀉to􀀉count:􀀉an􀀉hour􀀉of􀀉the􀀉violet􀀉sky􀀉here,􀀉an􀀉hour􀀉there,􀀉
survivable.􀀉A􀀉full􀀉day􀀉under􀀉the􀀉open􀀉sun,􀀉though,􀀉and􀀉a􀀉body􀀉came􀀉apart􀀉
at􀀉a􀀉level􀀉too􀀉small􀀉to􀀉see.􀀉They􀀉had􀀉to􀀉be􀀉inside􀀉the􀀉Junkyard’s􀀉metal􀀉
canyons􀀉before􀀉the􀀉light􀀉turned􀀉lethal􀀉—􀀉the􀀉waste􀀉mountains􀀉were􀀉the􀀉
only􀀉roof􀀉for􀀉miles,􀀉the􀀉only􀀉thing􀀉standing􀀉between􀀉them􀀉and􀀉the􀀉cage.􀀉
One􀀉shift.􀀉That􀀉was􀀉all􀀉Camilla􀀉had􀀉ghosted􀀉Ben􀀉and􀀉Otto’s􀀉numbers􀀉for.􀀉
If􀀉they􀀉weren’t􀀉back􀀉by􀀉the􀀉next􀀉morning’s􀀉intake,􀀉the􀀉cover􀀉would􀀉collapse.􀀉

They􀀉had􀀉never􀀉been􀀉to􀀉the􀀉Junkyard􀀉—􀀉the􀀉Wastelands,􀀉as􀀉miners􀀉called􀀉it.􀀉
They􀀉were􀀉too􀀉excited􀀉for􀀉the􀀉adventure􀀉to􀀉realize􀀉they􀀉were􀀉walking􀀉into􀀉a􀀉
sensory􀀉trap.􀀉

The􀀉Junkyard􀀉had􀀉grown􀀉into􀀉a􀀉monster􀀉after􀀉the􀀉energy􀀉crisis.􀀉When􀀉the􀀉
world􀀉ran􀀉out􀀉of􀀉fuel,􀀉people􀀉simply􀀉walked􀀉away􀀉from􀀉their􀀉machines.􀀉
Streets􀀉lined􀀉with􀀉abandoned􀀉cars,􀀉garbage􀀉bins􀀉overflowing􀀉with􀀉electronic􀀉
corpses.􀀉After􀀉the􀀉Trinity􀀉cities􀀉formed,􀀉dedicated􀀉Grave􀀉Sites􀀉were􀀉created􀀉
for􀀉each􀀉sector􀀉—􀀉a􀀉high-density􀀉archive􀀉of􀀉a􀀉world􀀉that􀀉didn’t􀀉know􀀉how􀀉
to􀀉delete􀀉its􀀉history.􀀉

A􀀉monthly􀀉dumping􀀉truck􀀉added􀀉more􀀉poisonous􀀉jewels􀀉—􀀉not􀀉stones,􀀉
but􀀉the􀀉shattered􀀉remains􀀉of􀀉old-world􀀉luxury:􀀉crushed􀀉OLED􀀉screens􀀉that􀀉
shimmered􀀉like􀀉peacock􀀉feathers􀀉and􀀉leaking􀀉battery􀀉capacitors􀀉that􀀉bled􀀉a􀀉
toxic,􀀉oily􀀉rainbow􀀉onto􀀉the􀀉soil.􀀉The􀀉shimmer􀀉was􀀉mercury􀀉and􀀉lead-salts􀀉
leaching􀀉into􀀉the􀀉air.􀀉A􀀉beautiful,􀀉iridescent􀀉rot􀀉that􀀉promised􀀉value􀀉while􀀉
slowly􀀉poisoning􀀉anyone􀀉who􀀉breathed􀀉it.􀀉


They􀀉reached􀀉the􀀉Junkyard􀀉by􀀉mid-morning,􀀉ducking􀀉into􀀉the􀀉shadow􀀉of􀀉
the􀀉first􀀉waste􀀉mountain􀀉just􀀉as􀀉the􀀉sun􀀉climbed􀀉high􀀉enough􀀉to􀀉bite.􀀉
Inside,􀀉the􀀉metal􀀉canyons􀀉swallowed􀀉the􀀉open􀀉sky􀀉—􀀉close,􀀉shaded,􀀉the􀀉heat􀀉
trapped􀀉but􀀉the􀀉killing􀀉light􀀉held􀀉off.􀀉The􀀉place􀀉was􀀉deserted,􀀉but􀀉the􀀉scale􀀉
was􀀉overwhelming􀀉—􀀉a􀀉mini-town􀀉of􀀉waste􀀉mountains.􀀉They􀀉decided􀀉to􀀉
split􀀉up.􀀉Camilla􀀉carried􀀉an􀀉old􀀉sack􀀉for􀀉parts􀀉while􀀉Ben􀀉and􀀉Otto􀀉believed􀀉
their􀀉bare􀀉hands􀀉would􀀉suffice.􀀉

Ben􀀉moved􀀉toward􀀉rows􀀉of􀀉arcade􀀉cabinets.􀀉The􀀉cracked􀀉glass􀀉did􀀉more􀀉
than􀀉pull􀀉at􀀉his􀀉wonder􀀉for􀀉a􀀉world􀀉he’d􀀉only􀀉heard􀀉described.􀀉The􀀉sun’s􀀉
rays􀀉hit􀀉the􀀉fractures􀀉at􀀉shifting􀀉angles􀀉as􀀉he􀀉moved􀀉between􀀉the􀀉rows,􀀉
creating􀀉a􀀉sharp􀀉strobe􀀉effect.􀀉His􀀉eyes􀀉tracked􀀉the􀀉unnatural􀀉flashes,􀀉
triggering􀀉flicker-vertigo􀀉—􀀉his􀀉brain􀀉unable􀀉to􀀉reconcile􀀉what􀀉his􀀉eyes􀀉were􀀉
reporting􀀉with􀀉what􀀉his􀀉body􀀉felt.􀀉The􀀉ground􀀉seemed􀀉to􀀉tilt.􀀉Walls􀀉shifted.􀀉
He􀀉reached􀀉out􀀉for􀀉stability,􀀉but􀀉every􀀉flash􀀉drew􀀉him􀀉deeper􀀉into􀀉the􀀉maze,􀀉
chasing􀀉solid􀀉footing􀀉that􀀉his􀀉confused􀀉senses􀀉couldn’t􀀉find.􀀉

Otto􀀉started􀀉by􀀉searching􀀉dashboards􀀉—􀀉the􀀉gauge􀀉mechanisms􀀉inside􀀉held􀀉
the􀀉kind􀀉of􀀉precision􀀉springs􀀉Camilla􀀉needed􀀉for􀀉the􀀉mirror􀀉mounts.􀀉Eyes􀀉
closed,􀀉he􀀉used􀀉Focused􀀉Intent􀀉to􀀉sense􀀉their􀀉weight􀀉and􀀉tension.􀀉But􀀉the􀀉
Junkyard􀀉wasn’t􀀉a􀀉silent􀀉grave;􀀉it􀀉was􀀉a􀀉cemetery􀀉of􀀉discarded􀀉energy.􀀉Every􀀉
jagged􀀉dashboard􀀉hummed􀀉with􀀉the􀀉ghost􀀉of􀀉its􀀉former􀀉purpose,􀀉vibrating􀀉
with􀀉a􀀉thousand􀀉discordant􀀉rhythms.􀀉The􀀉more􀀉he􀀉tried􀀉to􀀉calm􀀉the􀀉chaos􀀉
with􀀉his􀀉focus,􀀉the􀀉more􀀉the􀀉Junkyard􀀉pushed􀀉back,􀀉flooding􀀉his􀀉mind􀀉with􀀉
a􀀉sensory􀀉deluge.􀀉His􀀉concentration􀀉snapped,􀀉and􀀉he􀀉wandered􀀉aimlessly􀀉
into􀀉the􀀉rusted􀀉labyrinth.􀀉

Camilla􀀉started􀀉with􀀉the􀀉car􀀉mirrors􀀉—􀀉side,􀀉rearview,􀀉vanity􀀉—􀀉hunting􀀉
any􀀉silvered􀀉glass􀀉with􀀉the􀀉backing􀀉still􀀉smooth.􀀉The􀀉entire􀀉mountain􀀉was􀀉
made􀀉of􀀉needles.􀀉Every􀀉shard􀀉of􀀉silver-backed􀀉glass􀀉and􀀉polished􀀉chrome􀀉
acted􀀉like􀀉a􀀉lure,􀀉catching􀀉the􀀉light.􀀉Every􀀉fragment􀀉was􀀉a􀀉relic􀀉of􀀉a􀀉
forgotten􀀉age.􀀉Her􀀉mind􀀉couldn’t􀀉look􀀉away􀀉—􀀉like􀀉trying􀀉to􀀉count􀀉


raindrops􀀉in􀀉a􀀉storm􀀉until􀀉her􀀉senses􀀉drowned.􀀉She􀀉was􀀉trapped􀀉in􀀉a􀀉
spinning􀀉wheel􀀉of􀀉her􀀉own􀀉making􀀉—􀀉a􀀉fever􀀉of􀀉the􀀉eyes.􀀉

Every􀀉mirror􀀉shard􀀉she􀀉touched􀀉was􀀉clouded􀀉by􀀉acid􀀉rain􀀉or􀀉pitted􀀉by􀀉
leaching􀀉lead.􀀉She􀀉didn’t􀀉just􀀉need􀀉silvered􀀉glass;􀀉she􀀉needed􀀉a􀀉clean􀀉
reflection􀀉—􀀉a􀀉piece􀀉of􀀉the􀀉past􀀉not􀀉yet􀀉eaten􀀉by􀀉the􀀉present.􀀉

Hours􀀉vanished.􀀉The􀀉sun􀀉was􀀉already􀀉dipping􀀉when􀀉Camilla􀀉stopped􀀉and􀀉
looked􀀉around.􀀉“Oh􀀉my􀀉God,”􀀉she􀀉breathed.􀀉She􀀉stood􀀉before􀀉a􀀉staggering􀀉
mountain􀀉of􀀉broken􀀉glass􀀉with􀀉no􀀉idea􀀉how􀀉she’d􀀉lost􀀉the􀀉mission􀀉—􀀉had􀀉
become􀀉a􀀉collector􀀉of􀀉ghosts,􀀉senses􀀉blinded􀀉by􀀉a􀀉world􀀉offering􀀉too􀀉many􀀉
broken􀀉relics.􀀉This􀀉was􀀉what􀀉the􀀉old􀀉hands􀀉warned􀀉about􀀉—􀀉total􀀉paralysis􀀉
of􀀉the􀀉mind,􀀉overwhelmed􀀉by􀀉the􀀉volume􀀉of􀀉broken􀀉things,􀀉locked􀀉into􀀉
hunting􀀉one􀀉more􀀉piece􀀉until􀀉completely􀀉worn􀀉down.􀀉

Her􀀉hand􀀉was􀀉still􀀉clenched􀀉around􀀉a􀀉single􀀉shard􀀉—􀀉small,􀀉sharp,􀀉edges􀀉still􀀉
keen.􀀉The􀀉one􀀉piece􀀉her􀀉fever􀀉hadn’t􀀉let􀀉her􀀉drop.􀀉She􀀉slipped􀀉it􀀉into􀀉her􀀉
thigh􀀉pocket􀀉and􀀉didn’t􀀉think􀀉about􀀉it􀀉again.􀀉

She􀀉called􀀉out􀀉for􀀉Ben􀀉and􀀉Otto.􀀉Her􀀉voice􀀉disappeared􀀉into􀀉the􀀉vast,􀀉rusted􀀉
silence􀀉of􀀉the􀀉Wastelands.􀀉No􀀉answer.􀀉They􀀉were􀀉lost􀀉somewhere􀀉in􀀉the􀀉
labyrinth,􀀉each􀀉trapped􀀉in􀀉their􀀉own􀀉version􀀉of􀀉the􀀉same􀀉fever.􀀉

The􀀉sun􀀉was􀀉gone.􀀉They’d􀀉missed􀀉their􀀉window􀀉to􀀉walk􀀉back􀀉before􀀉dark􀀉

—􀀉the􀀉wasteland’s􀀉terrain􀀉was􀀉too􀀉treacherous􀀉to􀀉navigate􀀉blind,􀀉and􀀉the􀀉
temperature􀀉was􀀉already􀀉dropping.􀀉They’d􀀉have􀀉to􀀉wait􀀉for􀀉dawn􀀉and􀀉hope􀀉
they􀀉could􀀉make􀀉it􀀉back􀀉before􀀉the􀀉badge􀀉cover􀀉collapsed􀀉at􀀉morning􀀉
intake.􀀉
Camilla􀀉found􀀉shelter􀀉against􀀉a􀀉wall􀀉of􀀉compacted􀀉metal,􀀉wary􀀉of􀀉the􀀉
unstable􀀉heaps􀀉above.􀀉She􀀉needed􀀉heat.􀀉She􀀉piled􀀉her􀀉empty􀀉sack􀀉with􀀉dry􀀉
foam􀀉stripped􀀉from􀀉old􀀉car􀀉seats,􀀉then􀀉went􀀉looking􀀉for􀀉a􀀉spark.􀀉A􀀉few􀀉steps􀀉
away􀀉she􀀉found􀀉what􀀉she􀀉needed􀀉—􀀉a􀀉leaking􀀉battery􀀉cell􀀉with􀀉enough􀀉
residual􀀉charge􀀉to􀀉glow􀀉a􀀉wire.􀀉She􀀉touched􀀉a􀀉strip􀀉of􀀉fine􀀉copper􀀉wire􀀉


across􀀉the􀀉terminals.􀀉The􀀉wire􀀉glowed􀀉orange,􀀉then􀀉white.􀀉She􀀉pressed􀀉it􀀉
into􀀉the􀀉foam􀀉and􀀉blew.􀀉The􀀉fire􀀉caught.􀀉

She􀀉knew􀀉what􀀉she􀀉was􀀉breathing.􀀉Polyurethane􀀉foam􀀉released􀀉poisons􀀉
when􀀉it􀀉burned􀀉—􀀉she’d􀀉read􀀉about􀀉it􀀉in􀀉Pappy’s􀀉chemistry􀀉notes.􀀉But􀀉the􀀉
wasteland’s􀀉chill􀀉was􀀉more􀀉immediate􀀉than􀀉the􀀉slow􀀉damage􀀉of􀀉toxic􀀉
smoke,􀀉and􀀉without􀀉heat,􀀉the􀀉night􀀉would􀀉take􀀉more􀀉from􀀉her􀀉than􀀉fumes􀀉
ever􀀉could.􀀉

As􀀉she􀀉sat􀀉by􀀉the􀀉fire,􀀉the􀀉fog􀀉in􀀉her􀀉mind􀀉began􀀉to􀀉lift,􀀉replaced􀀉by􀀉a􀀉swarm􀀉
of􀀉gnawing􀀉questions.􀀉What􀀉if􀀉the􀀉mirror􀀉glass􀀉didn’t􀀉exist􀀉—􀀉what􀀉if􀀉every􀀉
piece􀀉in􀀉this􀀉graveyard􀀉was􀀉too􀀉damaged􀀉to􀀉produce􀀉a􀀉clean􀀉reflection?􀀉
What􀀉if􀀉they􀀉built􀀉the􀀉box􀀉and􀀉it􀀉didn’t􀀉work?􀀉What􀀉if􀀉Gerald􀀉still􀀉refused,􀀉
even􀀉with􀀉the􀀉pain􀀉gone?􀀉What􀀉if􀀉Gage􀀉found􀀉out􀀉she􀀉had􀀉defied􀀉his􀀉orders,􀀉
burned􀀉through􀀉their􀀉last􀀉salt􀀉rations,􀀉and􀀉gambled􀀉everything􀀉on􀀉a􀀉man􀀉
who’d􀀉already􀀉said􀀉no?􀀉

Then􀀉she􀀉heard􀀉it.􀀉Faint,􀀉at􀀉the􀀉edge􀀉of􀀉hearing􀀉—􀀉too􀀉steady􀀉to􀀉be􀀉the􀀉
wind,􀀉too􀀉patterned􀀉to􀀉be􀀉imagination.􀀉The􀀉Wastelands􀀉had􀀉been􀀉silent􀀉
since􀀉sundown.􀀉Whatever􀀉this􀀉was,􀀉it􀀉didn’t􀀉belong􀀉here.􀀉She􀀉held􀀉her􀀉
breath􀀉and􀀉listened.􀀉

Through􀀉the􀀉haze􀀉of􀀉the􀀉toxic􀀉smoke,􀀉a􀀉single􀀉melody􀀉cut􀀉through.􀀉A􀀉flute,􀀉
its􀀉notes􀀉clear􀀉and􀀉steady.􀀉The􀀉music􀀉was􀀉a􀀉rhythmic􀀉anchor,􀀉forcing􀀉her􀀉
frantic􀀉heart􀀉to􀀉slow,􀀉pulling􀀉her􀀉back􀀉from􀀉the􀀉edge􀀉of􀀉delirium.􀀉

She􀀉drifted􀀉in􀀉that􀀉soft􀀉space􀀉between􀀉waking􀀉and􀀉sleeping,􀀉her􀀉mind􀀉finally􀀉
quieted􀀉by􀀉the􀀉flute’s􀀉steady􀀉rhythm.􀀉Footsteps􀀉stopped􀀉beside􀀉her.􀀉She􀀉felt􀀉
no􀀉fear.􀀉A􀀉hand􀀉reached􀀉for􀀉hers􀀉—􀀉not􀀉the􀀉heavy􀀉grip􀀉of􀀉a􀀉miner􀀉or􀀉the􀀉
soot-slicked􀀉skin􀀉of􀀉a􀀉pit-boss.􀀉The􀀉callouses􀀉felt􀀉different:􀀉precise􀀉ridges􀀉of􀀉
someone􀀉who􀀉gripped􀀉thin􀀉tools􀀉or􀀉smoothed􀀉fine􀀉wood.􀀉A􀀉firm,􀀉steadying􀀉
touch􀀉that􀀉pulled􀀉her􀀉spirit􀀉back􀀉from􀀉the􀀉toxic􀀉clouds.􀀉

She􀀉let􀀉herself􀀉be􀀉led􀀉into􀀉the􀀉dark.􀀉The􀀉labyrinth􀀉changed􀀉under􀀉her􀀉feet􀀉—􀀉
broken􀀉concrete,􀀉then􀀉something􀀉harder,􀀉then􀀉something􀀉colder􀀉still.􀀉The􀀉


air􀀉sharpened􀀉to􀀉a􀀉cleanness􀀉she’d􀀉never􀀉smelled.􀀉Somewhere􀀉there􀀉were􀀉
stairs.􀀉Somewhere􀀉the􀀉flute􀀉had􀀉stopped.􀀉The􀀉hand􀀉in􀀉hers􀀉stayed􀀉steady,􀀉
the􀀉only􀀉fixed􀀉point􀀉as􀀉the􀀉rest􀀉of􀀉the􀀉world􀀉thinned􀀉and􀀉went􀀉silent.􀀉


Chapter􀀉19􀀉—􀀉Shadow􀀉Architecture􀀉

Camilla􀀉woke􀀉shivering,􀀉the􀀉mysterious􀀉flute􀀉melody􀀉still􀀉ringing􀀉in􀀉her􀀉
ears.􀀉For􀀉a􀀉moment􀀉she􀀉found􀀉herself􀀉humming􀀉along􀀉before􀀉the􀀉biting􀀉air􀀉
snapped􀀉her􀀉back.􀀉

When􀀉she􀀉opened􀀉her􀀉eyes,􀀉she􀀉wasn’t􀀉under􀀉the􀀉open􀀉sky.􀀉She􀀉was􀀉tied􀀉to􀀉
an􀀉iron􀀉rod􀀉bolted􀀉to􀀉the􀀉ceiling􀀉of􀀉a􀀉massive,􀀉metallic􀀉room􀀉—􀀉wrists􀀉
lashed􀀉above􀀉her􀀉head,􀀉the􀀉rope􀀉cinched􀀉tight􀀉enough􀀉that􀀉pulling􀀉down􀀉
only􀀉burned􀀉the􀀉skin.􀀉

She􀀉had􀀉little􀀉memory􀀉of􀀉the􀀉descent􀀉—􀀉only􀀉the􀀉stranger’s􀀉hand􀀉in􀀉hers,􀀉
the􀀉rest􀀉fading􀀉into􀀉a􀀉fog􀀉she􀀉couldn’t􀀉pierce.􀀉The􀀉cold􀀉air,􀀉the􀀉dead􀀉
stillness,􀀉the􀀉stairs􀀉in􀀉her􀀉memory􀀉that􀀉had􀀉only􀀉gone􀀉down􀀉—􀀉somewhere􀀉
underground,􀀉she􀀉guessed.􀀉She􀀉didn’t􀀉know􀀉how􀀉deep,􀀉or􀀉how􀀉far􀀉from􀀉the􀀉
surface.􀀉The􀀉stranger􀀉had􀀉led􀀉her􀀉into􀀉a􀀉world􀀉she􀀉didn’t􀀉know􀀉existed,􀀉and􀀉
then􀀉someone􀀉had􀀉tied􀀉her􀀉to􀀉the􀀉ceiling􀀉of􀀉it.􀀉

She􀀉remembered􀀉the􀀉steady􀀉hand􀀉of􀀉the􀀉stranger􀀉—􀀉the􀀉flute􀀉player,􀀉she􀀉was􀀉
certain􀀉now􀀉—􀀉the􀀉feeling􀀉of􀀉rescue􀀉that􀀉had􀀉led􀀉her􀀉into􀀉the􀀉dark.􀀉Had􀀉it􀀉
been􀀉a􀀉lure􀀉—􀀉a􀀉melodic􀀉trap􀀉designed􀀉to􀀉lead􀀉the􀀉dazed􀀉into􀀉this􀀉cage?􀀉Or􀀉
was􀀉the􀀉musician􀀉as􀀉much􀀉a􀀉prisoner􀀉of􀀉this􀀉place􀀉as􀀉she􀀉was?􀀉If􀀉her􀀉rescuer􀀉
was􀀉a􀀉jailer,􀀉she􀀉was􀀉truly􀀉alone.􀀉If􀀉they􀀉were􀀉a􀀉fellow􀀉victim,􀀉then􀀉
something􀀉even􀀉more􀀉dangerous􀀉controlled􀀉these􀀉halls.􀀉

The􀀉room􀀉wasn’t􀀉lit􀀉by􀀉fire􀀉or􀀉the􀀉dying􀀉flicker􀀉of􀀉the􀀉pit􀀉lanterns.􀀉The􀀉light􀀉
above􀀉her􀀉was􀀉white􀀉—􀀉not􀀉the􀀉orange-yellow􀀉of􀀉flame􀀉and􀀉smoke-filtered􀀉
sun􀀉she’d􀀉lived􀀉under􀀉her􀀉entire􀀉life,􀀉but􀀉a􀀉clean,􀀉cold􀀉white􀀉that􀀉didn’t􀀉
waver􀀉or􀀉pulse.􀀉It􀀉burned􀀉with􀀉a􀀉steadiness􀀉she’d􀀉never􀀉seen.􀀉No􀀉fuel,􀀉no􀀉
flame,􀀉no􀀉struggle􀀉for􀀉current.􀀉Just􀀉light,􀀉as􀀉if􀀉someone􀀉had􀀉trapped􀀉a􀀉piece􀀉
of􀀉the􀀉sun􀀉and􀀉tamed􀀉it.􀀉


Beyond􀀉her􀀉own􀀉breathing,􀀉she􀀉heard􀀉a􀀉deep,􀀉rhythmic􀀉throb􀀉—􀀉a􀀉machine􀀉
sound,􀀉but􀀉not􀀉like􀀉the􀀉groaning􀀉drills􀀉of􀀉the􀀉mine.􀀉This􀀉was􀀉smooth,􀀉
controlled,􀀉relentless.􀀉And􀀉the􀀉cold􀀉wasn’t􀀉natural.􀀉Outside,􀀉cold􀀉came􀀉with􀀉
wind􀀉and􀀉open􀀉sky.􀀉This􀀉cold􀀉was􀀉sealed􀀉in􀀉—􀀉manufactured,􀀉maintained,􀀉
deliberate.􀀉In􀀉Miner􀀉Town,􀀉the􀀉air􀀉burned􀀉your􀀉lungs􀀉with􀀉heat􀀉and􀀉dust.􀀉
Here,􀀉it􀀉was􀀉clean􀀉and􀀉cool,􀀉the􀀉kind􀀉of􀀉air􀀉she’d􀀉only􀀉ever􀀉read􀀉about.􀀉
Maintaining􀀉this􀀉—􀀉a􀀉comfort􀀉the􀀉colonies􀀉hadn’t􀀉felt􀀉in􀀉decades􀀉—􀀉was􀀉
drawing􀀉power􀀉on􀀉a􀀉scale􀀉Camilla􀀉couldn’t􀀉comprehend.􀀉Back􀀉home,􀀉
Camilla􀀉had􀀉built􀀉a􀀉radio􀀉that􀀉ran􀀉on􀀉a􀀉sliver􀀉of􀀉piezoelectric􀀉crystal,􀀉
harvesting􀀉the􀀉mountain’s􀀉own􀀉vibrations􀀉—􀀉and􀀉even􀀉that􀀉tiny􀀉trickle􀀉of􀀉
energy􀀉had􀀉felt􀀉like􀀉a􀀉miracle.􀀉Here,􀀉power􀀉was􀀉flowing􀀉through􀀉thick􀀉
conduits􀀉on􀀉the􀀉walls􀀉in􀀉quantities􀀉that􀀉could􀀉have􀀉lit􀀉Miner􀀉Town􀀉for􀀉
years,􀀉spent􀀉instead􀀉on􀀉keeping􀀉one􀀉room􀀉comfortable.􀀉

This􀀉wasn’t􀀉ancient􀀉and􀀉it􀀉wasn’t􀀉accidental.􀀉It􀀉was􀀉being􀀉run,􀀉right􀀉now,􀀉
by􀀉people􀀉who􀀉had􀀉built􀀉it􀀉knowing􀀉exactly􀀉what􀀉they􀀉were􀀉keeping􀀉from􀀉
the􀀉colonies􀀉above.􀀉That􀀉was􀀉the􀀉terrible􀀉part􀀉—􀀉not􀀉the􀀉technology,􀀉but􀀉
the􀀉choice􀀉behind􀀉it.􀀉A􀀉splinter􀀉of􀀉humanity􀀉that􀀉had􀀉built􀀉a􀀉parallel􀀉world􀀉
beneath􀀉the􀀉wasteland,􀀉spending􀀉decades􀀉hoarding􀀉the􀀉world’s􀀉remaining􀀉
knowledge,􀀉hiding􀀉behind􀀉the􀀉silence􀀉of􀀉the􀀉Junkyard􀀉while􀀉the􀀉rest􀀉of􀀉
humanity􀀉rotted􀀉in􀀉soot.􀀉They􀀉weren’t􀀉from􀀉another􀀉planet;􀀉they􀀉were􀀉the􀀉
ones􀀉who􀀉had􀀉kept􀀉the􀀉keys􀀉to􀀉the􀀉Old􀀉Era􀀉and􀀉locked􀀉the􀀉door􀀉behind􀀉
them.􀀉Camilla􀀉wasn’t􀀉just􀀉a􀀉prisoner;􀀉she􀀉was􀀉an􀀉unauthorized􀀉witness􀀉to􀀉a􀀉
crime􀀉that􀀉had􀀉lasted􀀉generations.􀀉

A􀀉heat􀀉had􀀉been􀀉building􀀉in􀀉her􀀉left􀀉thigh􀀉—􀀉at􀀉first􀀉lost􀀉beneath􀀉the􀀉larger􀀉
ache􀀉of􀀉being􀀉strung􀀉up,􀀉but􀀉now􀀉sharp􀀉and􀀉specific.􀀉She􀀉looked􀀉down􀀉to􀀉
find􀀉a􀀉dark,􀀉spreading􀀉stain􀀉across􀀉her􀀉denim.􀀉A􀀉four-inch􀀉shard􀀉of􀀉mirror􀀉
glass􀀉—􀀉the􀀉relic􀀉of􀀉yesterday’s􀀉fever,􀀉the􀀉one􀀉she’d􀀉forgotten􀀉was􀀉in􀀉her􀀉
pocket.􀀉In􀀉the􀀉fog􀀉of􀀉being􀀉led􀀉through􀀉the􀀉dark,􀀉she􀀉must􀀉have􀀉fallen,􀀉
driving􀀉it􀀉through􀀉the􀀉fabric􀀉and􀀉into􀀉her􀀉leg,􀀉hidden􀀉under􀀉blood􀀉and􀀉
denim􀀉where􀀉neither􀀉she􀀉nor􀀉her􀀉captors􀀉would􀀉have􀀉spotted􀀉it.􀀉The􀀉very􀀉


madness􀀉that􀀉had􀀉blinded􀀉her􀀉in􀀉the􀀉Junkyard􀀉had􀀉armed􀀉her􀀉with􀀉the􀀉only􀀉
tool􀀉sharp􀀉enough􀀉to􀀉cut􀀉through􀀉her􀀉restraints.􀀉

She􀀉carefully􀀉removed􀀉the􀀉glass,􀀉the􀀉pain􀀉momentarily􀀉blinding􀀉her.􀀉She􀀉
tore􀀉a􀀉strip􀀉from􀀉the􀀉bottom􀀉of􀀉her􀀉shirt,􀀉wadded􀀉it􀀉against􀀉the􀀉wound􀀉and􀀉
pressed􀀉hard,􀀉holding􀀉it􀀉until􀀉the􀀉bleeding􀀉slowed􀀉from􀀉a􀀉flow􀀉to􀀉a􀀉seep.􀀉
Then􀀉she􀀉sawed􀀉through􀀉the􀀉rope􀀉binding􀀉her􀀉wrists.􀀉The􀀉cut􀀉was􀀉deep􀀉—􀀉
she􀀉could􀀉feel􀀉the􀀉muscle􀀉underneath􀀉resisting􀀉when􀀉she􀀉tried􀀉to􀀉bend􀀉her􀀉
knee.􀀉Walking􀀉would􀀉be􀀉hard.􀀉Running􀀉would􀀉be􀀉worse.􀀉

Her􀀉arms􀀉dropped􀀉—􀀉heavy,􀀉foreign,􀀉dead􀀉at􀀉the􀀉shoulder.􀀉She􀀉shook􀀉them􀀉
out,􀀉waited􀀉for􀀉sensation􀀉to􀀉return􀀉through􀀉the􀀉burning􀀉pins􀀉and􀀉needles.􀀉
Her􀀉hands􀀉didn’t􀀉quite􀀉obey􀀉yet;􀀉she􀀉flexed􀀉them􀀉until􀀉they􀀉did.􀀉She􀀉
listened.􀀉No􀀉alarm,􀀉no􀀉footsteps􀀉in􀀉the􀀉corridor.􀀉Either􀀉no􀀉one􀀉was􀀉
watching􀀉the􀀉door􀀉yet,􀀉or􀀉someone􀀉was􀀉and􀀉hadn’t􀀉moved.􀀉Either􀀉way,􀀉she􀀉
had􀀉a􀀉window􀀉—􀀉narrow,􀀉closing􀀉fast.􀀉

As􀀉she􀀉stepped􀀉into􀀉the􀀉tunnel,􀀉the􀀉weakness􀀉was􀀉already􀀉settling􀀉into􀀉her􀀉
bones.􀀉Every􀀉step􀀉sent􀀉a􀀉jolt􀀉of􀀉hot􀀉pain􀀉up􀀉her􀀉thigh,􀀉and􀀉the􀀉leg􀀉buckled􀀉
slightly􀀉on􀀉each􀀉stride􀀉—􀀉the􀀉sliced􀀉muscle􀀉refusing􀀉to􀀉bear􀀉her􀀉full􀀉weight.􀀉
She􀀉didn’t􀀉know􀀉where􀀉she􀀉was,􀀉didn’t􀀉know􀀉the􀀉layout,􀀉didn’t􀀉know􀀉who􀀉
was􀀉watching.􀀉All􀀉she􀀉knew􀀉was􀀉that􀀉her􀀉body􀀉was􀀉failing􀀉and􀀉the􀀉corridor􀀉
stretched􀀉ahead􀀉with􀀉no􀀉end􀀉in􀀉sight.􀀉

She􀀉followed􀀉a􀀉light􀀉source􀀉down􀀉an􀀉intersection􀀉and􀀉stopped.􀀉In􀀉front􀀉of􀀉
her,􀀉machines􀀉were􀀉working.􀀉Not􀀉the􀀉rusted,􀀉hand-cranked􀀉contraptions􀀉of􀀉
the􀀉mine􀀉—􀀉these􀀉were􀀉moving􀀉on􀀉their􀀉own.􀀉Belts􀀉carried􀀉objects􀀉from􀀉
one􀀉end􀀉of􀀉the􀀉room􀀉to􀀉the􀀉other,􀀉sorting􀀉them􀀉into􀀉separate􀀉bins􀀉without􀀉a􀀉
single􀀉person􀀉touching􀀉them.􀀉No􀀉one􀀉was􀀉operating􀀉them.􀀉No􀀉one􀀉was􀀉
shoveling,􀀉pushing,􀀉or􀀉hauling.􀀉The􀀉machines􀀉just􀀉ran,􀀉as􀀉if􀀉work􀀉didn’t􀀉
have􀀉to􀀉break􀀉a􀀉body􀀉to􀀉happen.􀀉Camilla􀀉had􀀉never􀀉seen􀀉anything􀀉move􀀉
with􀀉that􀀉kind􀀉of􀀉purpose􀀉unless􀀉a􀀉person􀀉was􀀉bleeding􀀉for􀀉it.􀀉


She􀀉slipped􀀉past􀀉the􀀉assembly􀀉through􀀉a􀀉swinging􀀉partition􀀉door􀀉—􀀉not􀀉a􀀉
security􀀉barrier,􀀉just􀀉a􀀉divider􀀉between􀀉work􀀉zones􀀉—􀀉and􀀉entered􀀉a􀀉steel-
cased􀀉tunnel􀀉leading􀀉to􀀉a􀀉room􀀉with􀀉cubicles.􀀉Whoever􀀉designed􀀉this􀀉place􀀉
for􀀉secrecy􀀉hadn’t􀀉accounted􀀉for􀀉the􀀉carelessness􀀉of􀀉people􀀉who’d􀀉never􀀉had􀀉
an􀀉intruder.􀀉Through􀀉a􀀉partition􀀉gap,􀀉she􀀉overheard􀀉a􀀉conversation􀀉that􀀉
turned􀀉her􀀉blood􀀉colder􀀉than􀀉the􀀉storage􀀉room.􀀉

“They’re􀀉going􀀉to􀀉wake􀀉up􀀉soon.”􀀉A􀀉man’s􀀉voice,􀀉tense.􀀉“All􀀉three􀀉of􀀉them.􀀉
What􀀉do􀀉we􀀉do?”􀀉

All􀀉three.􀀉If􀀉those􀀉three􀀉were􀀉her􀀉three􀀉—􀀉and􀀉who􀀉else􀀉would􀀉Trinity􀀉have􀀉
caught􀀉in􀀉one􀀉night?􀀉—􀀉Ben􀀉and􀀉Otto􀀉had􀀉been􀀉collected􀀉from􀀉the􀀉
Junkyard􀀉too,􀀉swept􀀉up􀀉from􀀉wherever􀀉the􀀉labyrinth􀀉had􀀉stranded􀀉them,􀀉
brought􀀉down􀀉here􀀉by􀀉the􀀉same􀀉operatives􀀉or􀀉the􀀉same􀀉flute.􀀉

“What􀀉do􀀉you􀀉think􀀉we􀀉do?”􀀉A􀀉woman’s􀀉voice,􀀉sharp􀀉and􀀉clinical.􀀉
“They’ve􀀉seen􀀉the􀀉facility.􀀉They’ve􀀉seen􀀉the􀀉power􀀉grid.􀀉If􀀉even􀀉one􀀉of􀀉them􀀉
walks􀀉back􀀉to􀀉Miner􀀉Town􀀉and􀀉talks,􀀉we􀀉lose􀀉everything􀀉we’ve􀀉built.􀀉
They’ve􀀉become􀀉liabilities.􀀉None􀀉of􀀉them􀀉walks􀀉back.”􀀉

Camilla’s􀀉stomach􀀉dropped.􀀉Ben􀀉and􀀉Otto􀀉were􀀉still􀀉locked􀀉up􀀉somewhere􀀉
in􀀉this􀀉loop,􀀉facing􀀉a􀀉death􀀉sentence􀀉they􀀉didn’t􀀉know􀀉was􀀉coming.􀀉

“Seal􀀉the􀀉perimeter􀀉and􀀉activate􀀉the􀀉Electro-Barrages.”􀀉

Camilla􀀉didn’t􀀉wait.􀀉She􀀉moved􀀉as􀀉fast􀀉as􀀉her􀀉ruined􀀉leg􀀉allowed,􀀉boots􀀉
thudding􀀉unevenly􀀉against􀀉the􀀉steel􀀉corridor􀀉—􀀉one􀀉solid􀀉step,􀀉one􀀉
dragging􀀉half-step.􀀉But􀀉as􀀉she􀀉fled,􀀉she􀀉glanced􀀉upward􀀉and􀀉saw􀀉the􀀉
hallway’s􀀉true􀀉teeth.􀀉Thick􀀉coils􀀉of􀀉copper􀀉were􀀉mounted􀀉along􀀉the􀀉ceiling􀀉

—􀀉she􀀉recognized􀀉the􀀉metal􀀉from􀀉years􀀉of􀀉scavenging,􀀉but􀀉she’d􀀉never􀀉seen􀀉
it􀀉wound􀀉in􀀉shapes􀀉like􀀉these.􀀉Something􀀉was􀀉alive􀀉in􀀉them.􀀉The􀀉low-
frequency􀀉hum􀀉she’d􀀉been􀀉registering􀀉as􀀉her􀀉own􀀉pulse􀀉wasn’t􀀉hers􀀉—􀀉it􀀉
was􀀉vibration􀀉in􀀉the􀀉floor,􀀉traveling􀀉up􀀉through􀀉her􀀉bones,􀀉and􀀉a􀀉sharp,􀀉
metallic􀀉taste􀀉filled􀀉the􀀉back􀀉of􀀉her􀀉throat􀀉—􀀉like􀀉licking􀀉a􀀉coin,􀀉but􀀉
stronger,􀀉coating􀀉her􀀉tongue􀀉and􀀉making􀀉her􀀉eyes􀀉water.􀀉The􀀉air􀀉between􀀉

the􀀉walls􀀉shimmered􀀉with􀀉a􀀉faint􀀉violet􀀉light,􀀉and􀀉the􀀉hair􀀉on􀀉her􀀉arms􀀉
stood􀀉straight􀀉up.􀀉The􀀉hum􀀉had􀀉been􀀉there􀀉since􀀉she’d􀀉stepped􀀉out􀀉of􀀉the􀀉
cell.􀀉Trinity􀀉hadn’t􀀉turned􀀉it􀀉on􀀉for􀀉her􀀉—􀀉they􀀉didn’t􀀉need􀀉to.􀀉Every􀀉
instinct􀀉in􀀉her􀀉body􀀉told􀀉her􀀉the􀀉same􀀉thing:􀀉one􀀉step􀀉further􀀉and􀀉the􀀉air􀀉
would􀀉kill􀀉her.􀀉

One􀀉step􀀉into􀀉that􀀉field􀀉would􀀉seize􀀉her􀀉muscles􀀉and􀀉stop􀀉her􀀉heart.􀀉

Then􀀉she􀀉stopped.􀀉A􀀉dent􀀉in􀀉the􀀉steel􀀉wall􀀉—􀀉the􀀉same􀀉dent,􀀉the􀀉same􀀉
scratch􀀉pattern􀀉she’d􀀉traced􀀉with􀀉her􀀉fingers􀀉when􀀉she􀀉first􀀉stepped􀀉out􀀉of􀀉
the􀀉storage􀀉room.􀀉She􀀉was􀀉back􀀉where􀀉she􀀉started.􀀉

She􀀉hadn’t􀀉been􀀉running􀀉in􀀉a􀀉line.􀀉The􀀉corridors􀀉curved􀀉—􀀉so􀀉gently􀀉she􀀉
hadn’t􀀉noticed,􀀉but􀀉they􀀉curved.􀀉The􀀉facility􀀉was􀀉built􀀉as􀀉a􀀉ring,􀀉and􀀉she’d􀀉
been􀀉running􀀉its􀀉perimeter,􀀉one􀀉curving􀀉hallway􀀉feeding􀀉into􀀉the􀀉next􀀉
stretch􀀉of􀀉the􀀉same􀀉loop.􀀉There􀀉was􀀉no􀀉straight􀀉way􀀉out.􀀉The􀀉ring􀀉had􀀉no􀀉
door.􀀉

With􀀉her􀀉vision􀀉tilting􀀉and􀀉her􀀉leg􀀉going􀀉numb􀀉beneath􀀉her,􀀉Camilla􀀉made􀀉
a􀀉desperate􀀉choice.􀀉She􀀉couldn’t􀀉go􀀉forward􀀉—􀀉the􀀉electrified􀀉corridor􀀉
blocked􀀉every􀀉path.􀀉She􀀉couldn’t􀀉outrun􀀉anyone􀀉on􀀉a􀀉leg􀀉that􀀉was􀀉failing.􀀉
The􀀉only􀀉room􀀉she􀀉knew􀀉was􀀉the􀀉one􀀉she’d􀀉woken􀀉up􀀉in,􀀉and􀀉it􀀉had􀀉a􀀉door􀀉
she􀀉could􀀉lock􀀉from􀀉the􀀉inside.􀀉

She􀀉slipped􀀉back􀀉into􀀉the􀀉cold􀀉storage􀀉and􀀉sealed􀀉the􀀉door􀀉behind􀀉her.􀀉The􀀉
freezing􀀉air􀀉hit􀀉her􀀉like􀀉a􀀉wall,􀀉but􀀉the􀀉lock􀀉was􀀉solid􀀉—􀀉a􀀉heavy􀀉steel􀀉bolt􀀉
she􀀉could􀀉slide􀀉into􀀉place􀀉with􀀉her􀀉own􀀉hands.􀀉Simple.􀀉Mechanical.􀀉The􀀉
kind􀀉of􀀉lock􀀉she􀀉understood.􀀉It􀀉would􀀉hold􀀉long􀀉enough􀀉for􀀉her􀀉to􀀉think.􀀉
She􀀉pressed􀀉her􀀉back􀀉against􀀉the􀀉cold􀀉metal􀀉wall􀀉and􀀉slid􀀉to􀀉the􀀉floor,􀀉
clutching􀀉her􀀉wounded􀀉leg,􀀉her􀀉breath􀀉misting􀀉in􀀉front􀀉of􀀉her􀀉face.􀀉She􀀉
needed􀀉to􀀉find􀀉a􀀉way􀀉out􀀉of􀀉this􀀉ring􀀉—􀀉a􀀉path􀀉the􀀉builders􀀉hadn’t􀀉shown􀀉
her􀀉—􀀉before􀀉the􀀉cold􀀉finished􀀉what􀀉the􀀉glass􀀉shard􀀉had􀀉started.􀀉


Chapter􀀉20􀀉—􀀉Mr.􀀉Flitzberg􀀉

The􀀉cold􀀉was􀀉killing􀀉her.􀀉Minutes,􀀉an􀀉hour􀀉—􀀉she􀀉couldn’t􀀉tell􀀉anymore.􀀉
Her􀀉fingers􀀉had􀀉gone􀀉white􀀉and􀀉stiff.􀀉Her􀀉breaths􀀉were􀀉getting􀀉shallow.􀀉
And􀀉the􀀉shivering􀀉had􀀉stopped,􀀉which􀀉was􀀉the􀀉part􀀉Pappy􀀉had􀀉warned􀀉
about􀀉most.􀀉When􀀉the􀀉body􀀉stopped􀀉trying􀀉to􀀉warm􀀉itself,􀀉he’d􀀉said,􀀉it􀀉was􀀉
already􀀉giving􀀉up.􀀉The􀀉shaking􀀉was􀀉the􀀉fight.􀀉

Forced􀀉out􀀉by􀀉the􀀉last􀀉flicker􀀉of􀀉survival􀀉instinct,􀀉she􀀉unbolted􀀉the􀀉door􀀉and􀀉
stumbled􀀉back􀀉into􀀉the􀀉corridor.􀀉

Her􀀉head􀀉was􀀉spinning,􀀉vision􀀉fraying􀀉as􀀉the􀀉blood􀀉loss􀀉from􀀉her􀀉thigh􀀉
continued􀀉its􀀉silent􀀉drain.􀀉She􀀉wasn’t􀀉exploring􀀉anymore;􀀉she􀀉was􀀉on􀀉a􀀉
countdown.􀀉Her􀀉sluggish􀀉leg􀀉dragged􀀉against􀀉the􀀉steel􀀉floor.􀀉She􀀉leaned􀀉
against􀀉the􀀉smooth􀀉wall􀀉of􀀉the􀀉curved􀀉corridor􀀉just􀀉to􀀉keep􀀉the􀀉world􀀉from􀀉
tilting,􀀉pressing􀀉her􀀉back􀀉against􀀉a􀀉panel􀀉she􀀉thought􀀉was􀀉solid.􀀉

Instead,􀀉the􀀉panel􀀉gave􀀉way􀀉with􀀉a􀀉soft􀀉hiss.􀀉Before􀀉she􀀉could􀀉react,􀀉she􀀉was􀀉
falling􀀉—􀀉then􀀉submerged􀀉in􀀉water􀀉so􀀉cold􀀉it􀀉locked􀀉every􀀉muscle􀀉in􀀉her􀀉
body􀀉at􀀉once.􀀉The􀀉freezing􀀉temperature􀀉numbed􀀉her􀀉thigh􀀉instantly,􀀉but􀀉
her􀀉heart􀀉kicked􀀉into􀀉a􀀉panicked,􀀉erratic􀀉rhythm􀀉that􀀉pounded􀀉behind􀀉her􀀉
eyes.􀀉

The􀀉shock􀀉yanked􀀉her􀀉body􀀉awake􀀉even􀀉when􀀉her􀀉mind􀀉couldn’t􀀉follow.􀀉
She􀀉gasped􀀉before􀀉she􀀉could􀀉think.􀀉Something􀀉—􀀉adrenaline,􀀉panic,􀀉the􀀉
simple􀀉animal􀀉refusal􀀉to􀀉drown􀀉—􀀉flooded􀀉through􀀉her􀀉slowed􀀉blood.􀀉Her􀀉
good􀀉leg􀀉kicked,􀀉dragging􀀉the􀀉wounded􀀉one􀀉with􀀉it.􀀉Survival􀀉had􀀉become􀀉
reflex,􀀉not􀀉choice.􀀉

When􀀉her􀀉head􀀉broke􀀉the􀀉surface,􀀉she􀀉saw􀀉glass􀀉walls.􀀉She􀀉was􀀉in􀀉a􀀉tank􀀉—􀀉
long,􀀉narrow,􀀉lit􀀉from􀀉below.􀀉Dark􀀉shapes􀀉ribboned􀀉through􀀉the􀀉water􀀉
around􀀉her.􀀉Sleek,􀀉sinuous􀀉things􀀉that􀀉moved􀀉without􀀉sound.􀀉Then􀀉she􀀉felt􀀉
it:􀀉a􀀉low,􀀉steady􀀉buzz􀀉traveling􀀉through􀀉her􀀉body,􀀉vibrating􀀉in􀀉her􀀉teeth􀀉and􀀉


the􀀉roots􀀉of􀀉her􀀉hair.􀀉Pappy􀀉had􀀉told􀀉her􀀉stories􀀉about􀀉the􀀉old-world􀀉
creatures􀀉that􀀉carried􀀉their􀀉own􀀉lightning􀀉—􀀉that􀀉could􀀉stun􀀉prey􀀉with􀀉a􀀉
single􀀉touch.􀀉She’d􀀉never􀀉imagined􀀉meeting􀀉one.􀀉

Pappy’s􀀉stories􀀉had􀀉been􀀉right􀀉—􀀉the􀀉buzz􀀉in􀀉her􀀉teeth􀀉said􀀉so􀀉—􀀉and􀀉she􀀉
was􀀉the􀀉worst􀀉possible􀀉target􀀉in􀀉this􀀉water.􀀉Her􀀉skin􀀉was􀀉caked􀀉in􀀉mineral􀀉
dust􀀉from􀀉the􀀉mines􀀉and􀀉crusted􀀉with􀀉dried􀀉sweat.􀀉Salt􀀉and􀀉metal.􀀉Every􀀉
pore􀀉a􀀉wire􀀉for􀀉whatever􀀉was􀀉about􀀉to􀀉come􀀉through􀀉it.􀀉

She􀀉lunged􀀉for􀀉the􀀉edge􀀉of􀀉the􀀉tank.􀀉Something􀀉clamped􀀉onto􀀉her􀀉foot􀀉—􀀉a􀀉
sharp,􀀉muscular􀀉bite􀀉followed􀀉by􀀉a􀀉jolt􀀉that􀀉seized􀀉every􀀉muscle􀀉from􀀉her􀀉
ankle􀀉to􀀉her􀀉jaw.􀀉Her􀀉arms􀀉moved􀀉on􀀉pure􀀉reflex,􀀉hauling􀀉her􀀉over􀀉the􀀉rim.􀀉
She􀀉hit􀀉the􀀉steel􀀉floor􀀉on􀀉the􀀉other􀀉side,􀀉her􀀉back􀀉slamming􀀉flat􀀉against􀀉the􀀉
cold􀀉metal,􀀉the􀀉thigh􀀉wound􀀉torn􀀉open􀀉again􀀉with􀀉a􀀉fresh􀀉hot􀀉surge.􀀉

For􀀉a􀀉long􀀉minute􀀉she􀀉lay􀀉still,􀀉paralyzed,􀀉the􀀉adrenaline􀀉evaporating.􀀉The􀀉
pain􀀉in􀀉her􀀉thigh􀀉came􀀉roaring􀀉back􀀉without􀀉the􀀉cold􀀉water􀀉to􀀉numb􀀉it.􀀉She􀀉
tried􀀉to􀀉push􀀉herself􀀉up,􀀉but􀀉her􀀉arms􀀉felt􀀉like􀀉dead􀀉weight.􀀉Her􀀉vision􀀉tilted􀀉

—􀀉a􀀉sign􀀉that􀀉her􀀉heart􀀉was􀀉struggling􀀉to􀀉push􀀉what􀀉was􀀉left􀀉of􀀉her􀀉blood􀀉
through􀀉a􀀉body􀀉that􀀉was􀀉running􀀉out􀀉of􀀉pressure.􀀉
She􀀉lay􀀉there􀀉and􀀉took􀀉stock􀀉of􀀉what􀀉she􀀉still􀀉had.􀀉The􀀉cold􀀉had􀀉at􀀉least􀀉
slowed􀀉the􀀉bleed􀀉—􀀉her􀀉thigh􀀉was􀀉sticky,􀀉not􀀉soaked.􀀉She􀀉tore􀀉another􀀉strip􀀉
from􀀉her􀀉shirt􀀉with􀀉shaking􀀉hands,􀀉wrapped􀀉it􀀉tight􀀉over􀀉the􀀉first,􀀉and􀀉tied􀀉
it􀀉off􀀉with􀀉her􀀉teeth.􀀉

She􀀉got􀀉herself􀀉to􀀉her􀀉knees,􀀉then􀀉to􀀉her􀀉feet,􀀉the􀀉wall􀀉taking􀀉most􀀉of􀀉her􀀉
weight.􀀉Walking􀀉wasn’t􀀉walking􀀉anymore􀀉—􀀉it􀀉was􀀉lurching􀀉against􀀉
architecture.􀀉And􀀉the􀀉architecture􀀉was􀀉letting􀀉her.􀀉As􀀉she􀀉followed􀀉the􀀉
curve􀀉of􀀉the􀀉corridor,􀀉another􀀉section􀀉of􀀉wall􀀉retracted􀀉silently.􀀉The􀀉path􀀉
was􀀉opening􀀉for􀀉her.􀀉She􀀉couldn’t􀀉tell􀀉yet􀀉whether􀀉that􀀉was􀀉rescue􀀉or􀀉trap.􀀉

What􀀉she􀀉found􀀉made􀀉no􀀉sense.􀀉Row􀀉after􀀉row􀀉of􀀉painted􀀉wooden􀀉figures,􀀉
ranged􀀉in􀀉graduated􀀉sizes.􀀉On􀀉the􀀉nearest􀀉shelf,􀀉one􀀉of􀀉the􀀉larger􀀉figures􀀉had􀀉
been􀀉opened􀀉—􀀉smaller􀀉versions􀀉of􀀉itself􀀉nested􀀉inside,􀀉each􀀉a􀀉tiny􀀉variation􀀉


of􀀉the􀀉same􀀉face.􀀉She’d􀀉never􀀉seen􀀉anything􀀉like􀀉them.􀀉But􀀉the􀀉figures􀀉
weren’t􀀉abstract;􀀉they􀀉were􀀉shaped􀀉like􀀉people.􀀉She􀀉could􀀉make􀀉out􀀉
uniforms,􀀉robes,􀀉tiny􀀉painted􀀉faces􀀉with􀀉expressions􀀉she􀀉didn’t􀀉recognize.􀀉
Some􀀉held􀀉tools􀀉she’d􀀉never􀀉seen.􀀉Some􀀉wore􀀉clothing􀀉that􀀉looked􀀉like􀀉
costumes􀀉from􀀉Pappy’s􀀉stories􀀉about􀀉the􀀉old􀀉world.􀀉There􀀉were􀀉hundreds􀀉
of􀀉them,􀀉arranged􀀉with􀀉obsessive􀀉precision􀀉on􀀉shelves􀀉that􀀉stretched􀀉from􀀉
floor􀀉to􀀉ceiling.􀀉

She􀀉didn’t􀀉know􀀉what􀀉they􀀉were􀀉for.􀀉But􀀉she􀀉understood􀀉what􀀉they􀀉meant:􀀉
someone􀀉had􀀉been􀀉keeping􀀉the􀀉old􀀉world􀀉alive􀀉down􀀉here􀀉—􀀉preserving􀀉its􀀉
shapes􀀉and􀀉roles􀀉and􀀉rituals􀀉like􀀉specimens􀀉in􀀉a􀀉jar􀀉—􀀉while􀀉the􀀉surface􀀉
rotted.􀀉This􀀉wasn’t􀀉a􀀉shelter.􀀉It􀀉was􀀉a􀀉museum􀀉—􀀉and􀀉no􀀉one􀀉above􀀉had􀀉
ever􀀉been􀀉told􀀉it􀀉existed.􀀉

In􀀉the􀀉next􀀉section,􀀉the􀀉air􀀉changed.􀀉Music􀀉—􀀉real􀀉music,􀀉not􀀉the􀀉static-
choked􀀉fragments􀀉from􀀉her􀀉junk􀀉radio􀀉or􀀉the􀀉descriptions􀀉in􀀉Pappy’s􀀉
books.􀀉A􀀉melody􀀉she’d􀀉never􀀉heard,􀀉played􀀉by􀀉instruments􀀉she􀀉couldn’t􀀉
name,􀀉flowing􀀉through􀀉the􀀉space􀀉with􀀉a􀀉richness􀀉that􀀉made􀀉the􀀉air􀀉feel􀀉
thicker.􀀉

In􀀉the􀀉center􀀉of􀀉the􀀉room􀀉sat􀀉a􀀉man,􀀉surrounded􀀉by􀀉sketches􀀉and􀀉blueprints􀀉
spread􀀉across􀀉a􀀉wide􀀉table.􀀉He􀀉was􀀉humming􀀉along􀀉with􀀉the􀀉music,􀀉his􀀉
hands􀀉moving􀀉across􀀉the􀀉paper􀀉with􀀉an􀀉ease􀀉Camilla􀀉had􀀉never􀀉seen􀀉on􀀉
anyone􀀉above.􀀉

Camilla􀀉stood􀀉in􀀉the􀀉doorway􀀉longer􀀉than􀀉she􀀉meant􀀉to,􀀉just􀀉staring.􀀉How􀀉
did􀀉he􀀉have􀀉music?􀀉How􀀉did􀀉he􀀉have􀀉paper􀀉—􀀉clean,􀀉white,􀀉unmarked􀀉
paper,􀀉the􀀉kind􀀉she’d􀀉only􀀉ever􀀉read􀀉about?􀀉What􀀉was􀀉he􀀉drawing?􀀉

“Come􀀉on􀀉in,􀀉Camilla,”􀀉the􀀉man􀀉said􀀉without􀀉turning􀀉around.􀀉“I􀀉see􀀉
you’ve􀀉found􀀉my􀀉doll􀀉collection.􀀉No􀀉need􀀉to􀀉be􀀉scared.”􀀉

Her􀀉stomach􀀉dropped.􀀉He􀀉knew􀀉her􀀉name.􀀉He’d􀀉been􀀉watching􀀉—􀀉the􀀉
same􀀉way􀀉the􀀉voices􀀉in􀀉the􀀉cubicle􀀉room􀀉had􀀉known􀀉there􀀉were􀀉three􀀉of􀀉
them.􀀉This􀀉place􀀉had􀀉eyes􀀉she􀀉couldn’t􀀉see.􀀉


“How􀀉do􀀉you􀀉know􀀉my􀀉name?”􀀉she􀀉managed.􀀉“What􀀉is􀀉this􀀉place?”􀀉

“There􀀉will􀀉be􀀉plenty􀀉of􀀉time􀀉for􀀉questions.􀀉Let􀀉us􀀉make􀀉introductions􀀉first􀀉

—􀀉the􀀉least􀀉courtesy􀀉I􀀉can􀀉offer􀀉a􀀉brave􀀉girl􀀉from􀀉Miner􀀉Town.”􀀉
Camilla􀀉wanted􀀉to􀀉run􀀉but􀀉her􀀉legs􀀉had􀀉stopped􀀉taking􀀉orders.􀀉She􀀉stood􀀉
frozen􀀉as􀀉the􀀉man􀀉rose􀀉from􀀉his􀀉chair,􀀉his􀀉back􀀉still􀀉toward􀀉her.􀀉

“I􀀉am􀀉Mr.􀀉Flitzberg,”􀀉he􀀉said,􀀉“and􀀉you􀀉would􀀉be􀀉my􀀉protégé,􀀉provided􀀉you􀀉
want􀀉to􀀉live.”􀀉

He􀀉turned􀀉around,􀀉and􀀉a􀀉shriek􀀉tore􀀉out􀀉of􀀉her􀀉throat.􀀉

“It􀀉cannot􀀉be􀀉—􀀉you’ve􀀉been􀀉dead􀀉for􀀉a􀀉long􀀉time􀀉now!”􀀉

The􀀉face􀀉staring􀀉back􀀉at􀀉her􀀉was􀀉Pappy’s.􀀉Every􀀉line,􀀉every􀀉crease,􀀉the􀀉deep-
set􀀉eyes􀀉she􀀉remembered􀀉from􀀉the􀀉coal-fire􀀉stories,􀀉the􀀉jaw􀀉that􀀉used􀀉to􀀉
clench􀀉when􀀉he􀀉was􀀉thinking􀀉hard.􀀉For􀀉one􀀉reeling􀀉second,􀀉Camilla’s􀀉mind􀀉
simply􀀉refused􀀉to􀀉process􀀉what􀀉she􀀉was􀀉seeing.􀀉

Then􀀉the􀀉man􀀉laughed,􀀉and􀀉the􀀉spell􀀉cracked.􀀉Something􀀉was􀀉wrong􀀉with􀀉
his􀀉jaw􀀉—􀀉a􀀉flicker,􀀉a􀀉shimmer,􀀉as􀀉if􀀉the􀀉skin􀀉didn’t􀀉quite􀀉sit􀀉on􀀉the􀀉bone􀀉
beneath􀀉it.􀀉It􀀉was􀀉like􀀉looking􀀉at􀀉a􀀉reflection􀀉in􀀉water􀀉that􀀉someone􀀉had􀀉
disturbed.􀀉The􀀉face􀀉was􀀉Pappy’s,􀀉but􀀉the􀀉skull􀀉underneath􀀉was􀀉the􀀉wrong􀀉
shape.􀀉It􀀉was􀀉a􀀉mask􀀉—􀀉not􀀉fabric􀀉or􀀉paint,􀀉but􀀉something􀀉she􀀉had􀀉no􀀉word􀀉
for.􀀉A􀀉picture􀀉of􀀉a􀀉dead􀀉man􀀉stretched􀀉over􀀉the􀀉frame􀀉of􀀉a􀀉living􀀉one.􀀉

“I􀀉knew􀀉this􀀉would􀀉surprise􀀉you,”􀀉Flitzberg􀀉said.􀀉His􀀉voice􀀉was􀀉smooth􀀉in􀀉a􀀉
way􀀉that􀀉felt􀀉manufactured􀀉—􀀉too􀀉even,􀀉too􀀉controlled.􀀉“My􀀉apologies.􀀉I􀀉
borrowed􀀉your􀀉grandfather’s􀀉likeness􀀉from􀀉the􀀉old􀀉Miner􀀉Town􀀉records.􀀉I􀀉
thought􀀉a􀀉familiar􀀉face􀀉might􀀉put􀀉you􀀉at􀀉ease.”􀀉

It􀀉hadn’t.􀀉Despite􀀉the􀀉familiar􀀉face,􀀉Camilla􀀉could􀀉feel􀀉it􀀉—􀀉this􀀉was􀀉a􀀉
different􀀉man􀀉entirely.􀀉Something􀀉about􀀉the􀀉stillness􀀉behind􀀉the􀀉eyes,􀀉the􀀉
way􀀉he􀀉measured􀀉her􀀉reaction􀀉before􀀉choosing􀀉his􀀉next􀀉words.􀀉Pappy􀀉had􀀉


been􀀉warm􀀉and􀀉scattered􀀉and􀀉full􀀉of􀀉stories􀀉that􀀉tumbled􀀉over􀀉each􀀉other.􀀉
This􀀉man􀀉was􀀉precise.􀀉Calculating.􀀉Maybe􀀉dangerous.􀀉

Camilla’s􀀉skin􀀉was􀀉crawling.􀀉Whatever􀀉technology􀀉let􀀉this􀀉man􀀉wear􀀉
Pappy’s􀀉face,􀀉it􀀉was􀀉worse􀀉than􀀉the􀀉lightning􀀉creatures􀀉or􀀉the􀀉electrified􀀉
corridors:􀀉these􀀉people􀀉had􀀉capabilities􀀉Trinity􀀉had􀀉never􀀉shown􀀉on􀀉the􀀉
surface.􀀉And􀀉they’d􀀉been􀀉hiding􀀉down􀀉here􀀉long􀀉enough􀀉to􀀉build􀀉all􀀉of􀀉it.􀀉

Flitzberg’s􀀉expression􀀉shifted􀀉to􀀉something􀀉grave.􀀉“Not􀀉yet.􀀉I􀀉need􀀉you􀀉to􀀉
be􀀉silent􀀉until􀀉we􀀉bring􀀉your􀀉friends􀀉back􀀉to􀀉consciousness.􀀉All􀀉of􀀉us􀀉need􀀉
to􀀉talk.􀀉I􀀉have􀀉been􀀉waiting􀀉over􀀉twenty􀀉years􀀉for􀀉this􀀉day.”􀀉

He􀀉turned􀀉back􀀉to􀀉his􀀉table.􀀉Camilla􀀉took􀀉a􀀉moment􀀉to􀀉steady􀀉herself􀀉—􀀉
but􀀉the􀀉floor􀀉had􀀉begun􀀉to􀀉tilt,􀀉and􀀉her􀀉vision􀀉was􀀉narrowing.􀀉

Without􀀉turning,􀀉Flitzberg􀀉spoke.􀀉“You’re􀀉swaying.􀀉Sit.􀀉Eat.􀀉The􀀉cookies􀀉
are􀀉the􀀉finest􀀉in􀀉the􀀉world,􀀉and􀀉you􀀉won’t􀀉last􀀉long􀀉enough􀀉to􀀉enjoy􀀉them􀀉
otherwise.”􀀉

Before􀀉she􀀉could􀀉respond,􀀉a􀀉section􀀉of􀀉the􀀉floor􀀉slid􀀉open􀀉and􀀉a􀀉chair􀀉rose􀀉
into􀀉view,􀀉alongside􀀉a􀀉small􀀉table􀀉holding􀀉a􀀉plate􀀉of􀀉cookies.􀀉Camilla’s􀀉inner􀀉
reserves􀀉were􀀉gone􀀉—􀀉blood􀀉loss,􀀉cold,􀀉the􀀉eel􀀉shock,􀀉and􀀉the􀀉gut-punch􀀉of􀀉
Pappy’s􀀉stolen􀀉face􀀉had􀀉stripped􀀉her􀀉to􀀉the􀀉wire.􀀉

She􀀉stared􀀉at􀀉the􀀉plate.􀀉She􀀉didn’t􀀉trust􀀉it.􀀉Gage􀀉had􀀉told􀀉her􀀉about􀀉the􀀉
Initiation􀀉—􀀉about􀀉the􀀉feast􀀉that􀀉had􀀉tasted􀀉too􀀉good􀀉to􀀉be􀀉true,􀀉and􀀉the􀀉
sharp,􀀉metallic􀀉bitterness􀀉hiding􀀉underneath.􀀉A􀀉copper-coin􀀉tang,􀀉he’d􀀉
called􀀉it,􀀉pressed􀀉against􀀉the􀀉back􀀉of􀀉the􀀉throat.􀀉The􀀉taste􀀉that􀀉preceded􀀉the􀀉
drugging.􀀉She􀀉lifted􀀉a􀀉cookie􀀉to􀀉her􀀉nose􀀉and􀀉breathed􀀉in􀀉slowly,􀀉searching􀀉
for􀀉any􀀉trace􀀉of􀀉that􀀉warning.􀀉She􀀉found􀀉only􀀉a􀀉clean,􀀉nutty􀀉warmth􀀉—􀀉the􀀉
smell􀀉of􀀉real􀀉grain,􀀉and􀀉something􀀉rich􀀉and􀀉creamy􀀉she􀀉couldn’t􀀉name,􀀉the􀀉
kind􀀉of􀀉food􀀉she’d􀀉only􀀉ever􀀉read􀀉about.􀀉

She􀀉surrendered􀀉to􀀉hunger.􀀉


The􀀉first􀀉cookie􀀉crumbled􀀉in􀀉her􀀉mouth􀀉and􀀉hit􀀉her􀀉bloodstream􀀉like􀀉a􀀉
wave􀀉of􀀉warmth.􀀉The􀀉sugar􀀉steadied􀀉her􀀉pulse.􀀉Her􀀉vision􀀉sharpened.􀀉She􀀉
climbed􀀉onto􀀉the􀀉chair􀀉and􀀉ate􀀉until􀀉the􀀉plate􀀉was􀀉empty,􀀉feeling􀀉her􀀉failing􀀉
body􀀉pull􀀉itself􀀉back􀀉from􀀉the􀀉edge􀀉on􀀉fuel􀀉it􀀉had􀀉never􀀉tasted􀀉before􀀉—􀀉the􀀉
stolen􀀉riches􀀉of􀀉the􀀉world􀀉these􀀉people􀀉had􀀉locked􀀉away.􀀉

Mr.􀀉Flitzberg􀀉watched􀀉her􀀉eat􀀉without􀀉turning􀀉around.􀀉When􀀉the􀀉last􀀉
cookie􀀉was􀀉gone,􀀉he􀀉muttered􀀉to􀀉himself,􀀉barely􀀉audible:􀀉“It􀀉has􀀉begun.”􀀉


Chapter􀀉21􀀉—􀀉The􀀉Ring􀀉

Camilla􀀉was􀀉still􀀉in􀀉the􀀉chair􀀉—􀀉the􀀉one􀀉that􀀉had􀀉risen􀀉from􀀉the􀀉floor􀀉with􀀉
the􀀉cookies.􀀉It􀀉hummed􀀉beneath􀀉her,􀀉something􀀉mechanical􀀉ticking􀀉inside􀀉
its􀀉frame􀀉like􀀉a􀀉slow􀀉heartbeat.􀀉Her􀀉thigh􀀉didn’t􀀉hurt􀀉—􀀉and􀀉that􀀉was􀀉the􀀉
first􀀉wrong􀀉thing.􀀉The􀀉deep􀀉glass􀀉gash􀀉she’d􀀉carried􀀉out􀀉of􀀉the􀀉Junkyard,􀀉the􀀉
one􀀉that􀀉had􀀉soaked􀀉her􀀉bandage􀀉black,􀀉was􀀉sealed􀀉under􀀉a􀀉cool􀀉seam􀀉of􀀉
something􀀉blue􀀉and􀀉synthetic,􀀉smooth􀀉under􀀉her􀀉fingers,􀀉not􀀉quite􀀉skin.􀀉
She􀀉hadn’t􀀉felt􀀉it􀀉happen,􀀉and􀀉that􀀉frightened􀀉her􀀉more􀀉than􀀉the􀀉wound􀀉
ever􀀉had.􀀉The􀀉plate􀀉was􀀉empty.􀀉The􀀉sugar􀀉had􀀉pulled􀀉her􀀉back􀀉from􀀉the􀀉
edge,􀀉but􀀉her􀀉mind􀀉was􀀉still􀀉catching􀀉up􀀉to􀀉what􀀉her􀀉eyes􀀉had􀀉seen.􀀉A􀀉man􀀉
wearing􀀉a􀀉dead􀀉man’s􀀉face.􀀉Technology􀀉she􀀉had􀀉no􀀉name􀀉for.􀀉The􀀉Clean􀀉
World􀀉the􀀉old-timers􀀉had􀀉whispered􀀉about􀀉—􀀉not􀀉a􀀉story􀀉after􀀉all.􀀉

She􀀉didn’t􀀉know􀀉how􀀉long􀀉she’d􀀉been􀀉sitting􀀉there􀀉when􀀉the􀀉door􀀉opened􀀉
with􀀉a􀀉hiss.􀀉Two􀀉guards􀀉entered􀀉—􀀉not􀀉the􀀉scrap-armored􀀉thugs􀀉of􀀉Miner􀀉
Town,􀀉but􀀉men􀀉in􀀉clean,􀀉fitted􀀉uniforms􀀉who􀀉moved􀀉with􀀉the􀀉rehearsed􀀉
silence􀀉of􀀉people􀀉who’d􀀉done􀀉this􀀉a􀀉thousand􀀉times.􀀉Between􀀉them,􀀉they􀀉
held􀀉Ben􀀉and􀀉Otto􀀉—􀀉groggy,􀀉blinking,􀀉their􀀉bodies􀀉sluggish􀀉the􀀉way􀀉
people􀀉look􀀉when􀀉they’ve􀀉been􀀉asleep􀀉too􀀉long􀀉and􀀉woken􀀉up􀀉wrong.􀀉They􀀉
must􀀉have􀀉been􀀉unconscious􀀉somewhere􀀉in􀀉this􀀉facility􀀉since􀀉the􀀉Junkyard.􀀉
Behind􀀉them,􀀉a􀀉third􀀉guard􀀉brought􀀉Gage.􀀉

Camilla’s􀀉breath􀀉caught.􀀉Gage.􀀉He􀀉hadn’t􀀉been􀀉in􀀉the􀀉Junkyard􀀉with􀀉them􀀉

—􀀉he’d􀀉been􀀉at􀀉the􀀉mine.􀀉Her􀀉eyes􀀉went􀀉to􀀉his􀀉boots:􀀉pale,􀀉chalky􀀉dust,􀀉not􀀉
pit-black.􀀉He’d􀀉gone􀀉looking􀀉for􀀉them.􀀉She􀀉didn’t􀀉know􀀉how􀀉he’d􀀉found􀀉
the􀀉trail.􀀉But􀀉here􀀉he􀀉was,􀀉dazed,􀀉his􀀉massive􀀉frame􀀉slumped􀀉between􀀉the􀀉
guards􀀉like􀀉a􀀉machine􀀉that􀀉had􀀉been􀀉powered􀀉down􀀉mid-stride.􀀉
The􀀉guards􀀉moved􀀉them􀀉into􀀉high-backed􀀉seats􀀉and􀀉strapped􀀉them􀀉in.􀀉Cold􀀉
metal􀀉bands􀀉clamped􀀉across􀀉their􀀉chests􀀉and􀀉wrists􀀉—􀀉not􀀉rope,􀀉not􀀉chains,􀀉
but􀀉smooth,􀀉precise􀀉restraints􀀉that􀀉tightened􀀉with􀀉a􀀉hiss􀀉and􀀉locked􀀉with􀀉a􀀉


click.􀀉No􀀉give.􀀉No􀀉flex.􀀉Gage’s􀀉arms􀀉strained􀀉against􀀉the􀀉bands􀀉once,􀀉
instinctively,􀀉and􀀉the􀀉metal􀀉didn’t􀀉budge.􀀉In􀀉Miner􀀉Town,􀀉his􀀉strength􀀉was􀀉
the􀀉thing􀀉that􀀉kept􀀉people􀀉alive.􀀉Against􀀉these􀀉bands,􀀉it􀀉bought􀀉him􀀉
nothing.􀀉

After􀀉several􀀉minutes,􀀉they􀀉began􀀉to􀀉stir.􀀉Ben􀀉muttered􀀉about􀀉dreams􀀉that􀀉
wouldn’t􀀉stop.􀀉Otto’s􀀉eyes􀀉were􀀉unfocused,􀀉scanning􀀉the􀀉white􀀉walls􀀉as􀀉if􀀉
trying􀀉to􀀉locate􀀉a􀀉horizon􀀉that􀀉didn’t􀀉exist.􀀉Gage􀀉was􀀉the􀀉last􀀉to􀀉surface,􀀉his􀀉
breathing􀀉heavy􀀉and􀀉ragged.􀀉Camilla􀀉kicked􀀉the􀀉leg􀀉of􀀉his􀀉chair􀀉hard􀀉
enough􀀉to􀀉jolt􀀉him􀀉awake.􀀉

“Focus,”􀀉she􀀉said.􀀉“This􀀉is􀀉real.”􀀉

The􀀉room􀀉filled􀀉with􀀉frantic􀀉questions􀀉—􀀉where􀀉are􀀉we,􀀉how􀀉did􀀉we􀀉get􀀉
here,􀀉what􀀉is􀀉this􀀉place.􀀉Gage’s􀀉voice􀀉cut􀀉through􀀉the􀀉noise,􀀉hoarse􀀉and􀀉
angry:􀀉“You􀀉didn’t􀀉come􀀉home.􀀉None􀀉of􀀉you.􀀉I􀀉came􀀉back􀀉from􀀉the􀀉mine􀀉
and􀀉the􀀉shack􀀉was􀀉empty.􀀉Found􀀉drawings􀀉scattered􀀉around􀀉—􀀉schematics,􀀉
measurements,􀀉lists􀀉of􀀉parts􀀉I􀀉didn’t􀀉recognize.􀀉Whatever􀀉you􀀉three􀀉were􀀉
building,􀀉you􀀉needed􀀉supplies.􀀉And􀀉there’s􀀉only􀀉one􀀉place􀀉in􀀉Sector-1􀀉to􀀉
scavenge􀀉parts.”􀀉

He􀀉looked􀀉at􀀉Camilla,􀀉equal􀀉parts􀀉fury􀀉and􀀉fear.􀀉“I􀀉got􀀉to􀀉the􀀉Junkyard􀀉after􀀉
dark.􀀉Found􀀉the􀀉ashes􀀉of􀀉a􀀉fire􀀉and􀀉boot􀀉prints􀀉going􀀉in􀀉three􀀉directions.􀀉I􀀉
took􀀉the􀀉smallest􀀉set􀀉—􀀉figured􀀉those􀀉were􀀉yours.􀀉They􀀉led􀀉me􀀉into􀀉the􀀉
scrap􀀉rows.”􀀉His􀀉jaw􀀉tightened.􀀉“The􀀉ground􀀉gave􀀉way.􀀉One􀀉second􀀉I􀀉was􀀉
walking,􀀉the􀀉next􀀉I􀀉was􀀉falling.􀀉I􀀉hit􀀉something􀀉hard,􀀉and􀀉that’s􀀉the􀀉last􀀉
thing􀀉I􀀉remember.”􀀉

“What􀀉the􀀉hell􀀉were􀀉you􀀉cooking?”􀀉he􀀉asked.􀀉“And􀀉why􀀉didn’t􀀉you􀀉tell􀀉
me?”􀀉

Before􀀉she􀀉could􀀉answer,􀀉Mr.􀀉Flitzberg􀀉stood􀀉from􀀉his􀀉table􀀉in􀀉the􀀉corner􀀉
and􀀉turned􀀉to􀀉face􀀉them.􀀉

Gasps.􀀉The􀀉sound􀀉of􀀉restraints􀀉straining􀀉as􀀉bodies􀀉lurched􀀉backward.􀀉


Standing􀀉before􀀉them􀀉was􀀉Gerald􀀉—􀀉Badge􀀉732􀀉—􀀉face􀀉contorted􀀉in􀀉pain,􀀉
favoring􀀉his􀀉left􀀉side.􀀉

“Gerald?”􀀉Otto’s􀀉voice􀀉came􀀉out􀀉thin.􀀉“How􀀉did􀀉you􀀉get􀀉here?􀀉What􀀉is􀀉this􀀉
place?”􀀉

Camilla􀀉was􀀉already􀀉on􀀉her􀀉feet.􀀉She’d􀀉seen􀀉this􀀉trick􀀉before􀀉—􀀉the􀀉same􀀉
technology,􀀉the􀀉same􀀉wrongness􀀉beneath􀀉the􀀉skin.􀀉She􀀉watched􀀉the􀀉figure􀀉
move􀀉and􀀉saw􀀉it􀀉immediately:􀀉the􀀉limp􀀉was􀀉wrong.􀀉

“That’s􀀉not􀀉Gerald,”􀀉she􀀉said,􀀉her􀀉voice􀀉cutting􀀉through􀀉the􀀉room.􀀉“It’s􀀉the􀀉
same􀀉trick􀀉he􀀉played􀀉on􀀉me􀀉with􀀉Pappy’s􀀉face.􀀉The􀀉face􀀉is􀀉right􀀉—􀀉but􀀉they􀀉
didn’t􀀉have􀀉his􀀉walk􀀉in􀀉their􀀉records.􀀉Look􀀉at􀀉how􀀉he’s􀀉moving􀀉—􀀉limping􀀉
like􀀉a􀀉man􀀉with􀀉a􀀉wounded􀀉leg,􀀉wincing􀀉with􀀉every􀀉step.􀀉Gerald􀀉doesn’t􀀉
move􀀉like􀀉that.􀀉Gerald’s􀀉step􀀉is􀀉a􀀉clack-thud.􀀉Dead􀀉metal􀀉on􀀉stone.􀀉His􀀉leg􀀉is􀀉
made􀀉of􀀉drone􀀉struts􀀉and􀀉mining􀀉wire􀀉—􀀉it􀀉doesn’t􀀉feel􀀉pain,􀀉and􀀉it􀀉doesn’t􀀉
bend􀀉like􀀉flesh.􀀉Whoever􀀉is􀀉wearing􀀉Gerald’s􀀉face􀀉doesn’t􀀉know􀀉what􀀉
Gerald’s􀀉body􀀉actually􀀉does.”􀀉

She􀀉stepped􀀉between􀀉Gage􀀉and􀀉the􀀉figure.􀀉“They􀀉pulled􀀉the􀀉face􀀉from􀀉the􀀉
archives􀀉—􀀉the􀀉Gerald􀀉you􀀉remember􀀉from􀀉dinner.􀀉But􀀉the􀀉records􀀉never􀀉
had􀀉him􀀉in􀀉motion.􀀉So􀀉they’re􀀉guessing.􀀉They’re􀀉performing􀀉what􀀉they􀀉
think􀀉a􀀉wounded􀀉man􀀉walks􀀉like.􀀉Not􀀉Gerald.”􀀉

The􀀉figure􀀉stopped􀀉moving.􀀉A􀀉slow,􀀉rhythmic􀀉clapping􀀉filled􀀉the􀀉room,􀀉and􀀉
the􀀉image􀀉of􀀉Gerald􀀉blurred􀀉and􀀉fell􀀉away􀀉like􀀉smoke,􀀉revealing􀀉the􀀉
unfamiliar,􀀉clean-shaven􀀉face􀀉of􀀉Mr.􀀉Flitzberg.􀀉

It􀀉was􀀉a􀀉face􀀉Camilla􀀉had􀀉never􀀉seen􀀉on􀀉a􀀉living􀀉person.􀀉The􀀉skin􀀉was􀀉
smooth􀀉and􀀉pale􀀉—􀀉no􀀉dust-pitting,􀀉no􀀉scars􀀉from􀀉mine􀀉work,􀀉no􀀉deep􀀉
lines􀀉from􀀉squinting􀀉against􀀉the􀀉wind.􀀉The􀀉cheeks􀀉were􀀉full􀀉in􀀉a􀀉way􀀉no􀀉one􀀉
in􀀉Miner􀀉Town􀀉could􀀉afford.􀀉He􀀉looked􀀉like􀀉he􀀉had􀀉never􀀉spent􀀉a􀀉day􀀉
outside.􀀉


“I􀀉expected􀀉you􀀉to􀀉see􀀉through􀀉it,”􀀉Flitzberg􀀉said,􀀉turning􀀉to􀀉Camilla.􀀉
“You’d􀀉caught􀀉me􀀉once􀀉before.􀀉The􀀉harder􀀉question􀀉was􀀉whether􀀉you􀀉could􀀉
make􀀉the􀀉others􀀉see􀀉it.􀀉You􀀉did.”􀀉

He􀀉paced􀀉in􀀉front􀀉of􀀉them,􀀉studying􀀉each􀀉of􀀉them􀀉the􀀉way􀀉Camilla􀀉had􀀉
seen􀀉Pappy􀀉study􀀉a􀀉broken􀀉engine􀀉—􀀉not􀀉with􀀉affection,􀀉but􀀉with􀀉the􀀉
question􀀉of􀀉whether􀀉the􀀉parts􀀉were􀀉worth􀀉salvaging.􀀉

“Under􀀉normal􀀉circumstances,”􀀉Flitzberg􀀉continued,􀀉“you􀀉would􀀉be􀀉
eliminated.􀀉Witnesses􀀉to􀀉this􀀉facility􀀉are􀀉a􀀉risk􀀉we􀀉don’t􀀉tolerate.”􀀉He􀀉
paused,􀀉studying􀀉them􀀉one􀀉by􀀉one.􀀉“But􀀉I’ve􀀉been􀀉watching􀀉Miner􀀉Town􀀉
for􀀉a􀀉very􀀉long􀀉time.􀀉I􀀉know􀀉what􀀉each􀀉of􀀉you􀀉is.􀀉A􀀉man􀀉who􀀉mapped􀀉an􀀉
entire􀀉mine􀀉with􀀉splinters􀀉and􀀉instinct.􀀉A􀀉fighter􀀉who􀀉can􀀉absorb􀀉a􀀉blow􀀉
that􀀉would􀀉break􀀉most􀀉men.􀀉A􀀉boy􀀉who􀀉holds􀀉a􀀉group􀀉together􀀉when􀀉
everything􀀉else􀀉falls􀀉apart.􀀉And􀀉a􀀉girl􀀉who􀀉escaped􀀉a􀀉locked􀀉room,􀀉survived􀀉
an􀀉eel􀀉tank,􀀉outran􀀉an􀀉electrified􀀉corridor,􀀉and􀀉saw􀀉through􀀉my􀀉best􀀉trick.”􀀉

He􀀉let􀀉the􀀉silence􀀉settle.􀀉“You􀀉don’t􀀉have􀀉to􀀉be􀀉eliminated.􀀉I􀀉need􀀉people􀀉
with􀀉exactly􀀉those􀀉qualities.􀀉I’m􀀉offering􀀉you􀀉a􀀉chance􀀉to􀀉be􀀉useful.􀀉The􀀉
alternative􀀉is􀀉one􀀉I’d􀀉rather􀀉not􀀉describe.”􀀉

They􀀉all􀀉understood􀀉—􀀉the􀀉offer􀀉and􀀉the􀀉alternative􀀉both.􀀉It􀀉was􀀉the􀀉tone􀀉
of􀀉a􀀉man􀀉who􀀉held􀀉every􀀉card􀀉and􀀉was􀀉deciding􀀉whether􀀉to􀀉play􀀉them􀀉or􀀉
burn􀀉them.􀀉

The􀀉four􀀉of􀀉them􀀉looked􀀉at􀀉each􀀉other.􀀉They’d􀀉been􀀉making􀀉decisions􀀉like􀀉
this􀀉since􀀉they􀀉were􀀉kids􀀉—􀀉no􀀉words􀀉needed.􀀉One􀀉by􀀉one,􀀉they􀀉nodded.􀀉

“We’re􀀉listening,”􀀉Gage􀀉said.􀀉

Flitzberg􀀉studied􀀉them􀀉for􀀉a􀀉long􀀉moment,􀀉as􀀉if􀀉checking􀀉whether􀀉the􀀉
agreement􀀉was􀀉real􀀉or􀀉performance.􀀉Whatever􀀉he􀀉saw􀀉satisfied􀀉him.􀀉He􀀉
turned􀀉to􀀉the􀀉guards􀀉and􀀉said􀀉something􀀉too􀀉quiet􀀉to􀀉hear.􀀉The􀀉restraints􀀉
released􀀉with􀀉a􀀉click.􀀉


“You’ll􀀉rest􀀉first,”􀀉Flitzberg􀀉said.􀀉“We’ll􀀉talk􀀉tomorrow.􀀉You’re􀀉no􀀉use􀀉to􀀉me􀀉
half-dead.”􀀉

The􀀉guards􀀉led􀀉them􀀉down􀀉the􀀉curving􀀉corridor􀀉to􀀉a􀀉long,􀀉narrow􀀉room􀀉—􀀉
a􀀉section􀀉of􀀉the􀀉facility’s􀀉ring􀀉lined􀀉with􀀉twenty􀀉beds.􀀉Above􀀉each􀀉bed,􀀉
panels􀀉glowed􀀉with􀀉a􀀉steady􀀉white􀀉light􀀉that􀀉felt􀀉different􀀉from􀀉anything􀀉
they’d􀀉experienced.􀀉Every􀀉light􀀉they’d􀀉known􀀉—􀀉pit􀀉lamps􀀉in􀀉the􀀉mine,􀀉
lanterns􀀉in􀀉the􀀉shacks,􀀉fires􀀉they􀀉sat􀀉around􀀉at􀀉night􀀉—􀀉had􀀉been􀀉weak􀀉and􀀉
flickering.􀀉Here,􀀉it􀀉pressed􀀉down􀀉on􀀉their􀀉soot-stained􀀉skin􀀉like􀀉a􀀉physical􀀉
weight,􀀉steady􀀉and􀀉unblinking.􀀉

But􀀉first,􀀉there􀀉was􀀉the􀀉water.􀀉

A􀀉guard􀀉pointed􀀉to􀀉four􀀉stalls􀀉along􀀉the􀀉inner􀀉wall.􀀉Inside􀀉each􀀉one,􀀉water􀀉
ran􀀉from􀀉a􀀉panel􀀉in􀀉the􀀉wall􀀉—􀀉clear,􀀉hot,􀀉in􀀉a􀀉quantity􀀉none􀀉of􀀉them􀀉had􀀉
ever􀀉seen􀀉given􀀉to􀀉a􀀉single􀀉person.􀀉Bathing,􀀉in􀀉Sector-1,􀀉meant􀀉a􀀉few􀀉tin􀀉
cups􀀉of􀀉sump􀀉water􀀉and􀀉a􀀉cloth,􀀉sponged􀀉across􀀉the􀀉skin􀀉in􀀉the􀀉cold􀀉of􀀉
morning􀀉—􀀉the􀀉cloth􀀉always􀀉coming􀀉away􀀉orange􀀉no􀀉matter􀀉how􀀉clean􀀉
you’d􀀉thought􀀉you’d􀀉been.􀀉Here􀀉the􀀉water􀀉kept􀀉coming.􀀉It􀀉ran􀀉down􀀉their􀀉
arms􀀉and􀀉faces􀀉and􀀉stayed􀀉clear,􀀉and􀀉they􀀉stood􀀉under􀀉it􀀉longer􀀉than􀀉they􀀉
should􀀉have,􀀉watching􀀉the􀀉dust􀀉of􀀉half􀀉their􀀉lives􀀉leave􀀉them􀀉and􀀉disappear􀀉
into􀀉the􀀉drain.􀀉Camilla􀀉thought􀀉of􀀉Pappy’s􀀉rivers􀀉—􀀉the􀀉roads􀀉made􀀉of􀀉
water􀀉—􀀉and􀀉understood􀀉that􀀉he􀀉hadn’t􀀉been􀀉telling􀀉a􀀉story.􀀉He’d􀀉been􀀉
describing􀀉what􀀉some􀀉people􀀉still􀀉had.􀀉

When􀀉they􀀉came􀀉out,􀀉food􀀉was􀀉waiting􀀉on􀀉a􀀉low􀀉table.􀀉

It􀀉was􀀉nothing􀀉any􀀉of􀀉them􀀉had􀀉words􀀉for.􀀉Real􀀉grain,􀀉yes􀀉—􀀉Camilla􀀉
recognized􀀉that􀀉—􀀉formed􀀉into􀀉soft􀀉warm􀀉rounds􀀉she􀀉had􀀉no􀀉name􀀉for.􀀉
Something􀀉green􀀉that􀀉might􀀉have􀀉been􀀉an􀀉herb􀀉from􀀉Pappy’s􀀉books.􀀉A􀀉
paste􀀉the􀀉color􀀉of􀀉rust.􀀉Slices􀀉of􀀉something􀀉firm􀀉and􀀉yellow􀀉none􀀉of􀀉them􀀉
could􀀉place.􀀉None􀀉of􀀉it􀀉tasted􀀉like􀀉the􀀉grey􀀉starch􀀉mush􀀉they􀀉had􀀉grown􀀉up􀀉
swallowing,􀀉or􀀉the􀀉salt-cured􀀉strips􀀉that􀀉passed􀀉for􀀉protein􀀉in􀀉Sector-1.􀀉It􀀉


tasted􀀉like􀀉food􀀉that􀀉had􀀉been􀀉made􀀉for􀀉a􀀉person,􀀉not􀀉handed􀀉to􀀉one.􀀉They􀀉
ate􀀉without􀀉speaking,􀀉too􀀉hungry􀀉to􀀉question,􀀉too􀀉tired􀀉to􀀉refuse.􀀉

Gage􀀉lay􀀉on􀀉the􀀉thin􀀉mattress􀀉and􀀉stared􀀉at􀀉the􀀉glowing􀀉panel􀀉above􀀉him.􀀉
Something􀀉was􀀉monitoring􀀉him􀀉—􀀉he􀀉could􀀉feel􀀉a􀀉faint􀀉vibration􀀉in􀀉the􀀉
frame,􀀉a􀀉hum􀀉that􀀉seemed􀀉to􀀉respond􀀉to􀀉his􀀉breathing.􀀉He􀀉didn’t􀀉know􀀉
what􀀉it􀀉was􀀉measuring,􀀉but􀀉he􀀉knew􀀉he􀀉was􀀉being􀀉watched􀀉in􀀉a􀀉way􀀉that􀀉
went􀀉deeper􀀉than􀀉eyes􀀉or􀀉cameras.􀀉In􀀉this􀀉place,􀀉even􀀉his􀀉heartbeat􀀉was􀀉a􀀉
piece􀀉of􀀉information􀀉someone􀀉was􀀉collecting.􀀉

“How􀀉do􀀉we􀀉know􀀉we􀀉aren’t􀀉dreaming?”􀀉Otto􀀉asked,􀀉his􀀉voice􀀉small􀀉in􀀉the􀀉
white􀀉silence.􀀉

Gage􀀉reached􀀉over􀀉and􀀉slapped􀀉him􀀉hard􀀉across􀀉the􀀉cheek.􀀉“That’s􀀉how.”􀀉

Otto􀀉blinked,􀀉the􀀉red􀀉of􀀉the􀀉slap􀀉blooming􀀉on􀀉his􀀉cheek.􀀉For􀀉a􀀉heartbeat􀀉
there􀀉was􀀉silence.􀀉Then􀀉Ben􀀉snorted􀀉—􀀉once,􀀉helpless􀀉—􀀉and􀀉that􀀉was􀀉it.􀀉
He􀀉dropped􀀉backward􀀉onto􀀉his􀀉mattress,􀀉laughing􀀉into􀀉the􀀉white􀀉ceiling.􀀉
Camilla􀀉folded􀀉over􀀉her􀀉knees,􀀉shoulders􀀉shaking.􀀉Otto,􀀉still􀀉blinking,􀀉
started􀀉laughing􀀉too,􀀉one􀀉hand􀀉pressed􀀉to􀀉his􀀉cheek.􀀉Even􀀉Gage􀀉cracked,􀀉his􀀉
shoulders􀀉dropping􀀉for􀀉the􀀉first􀀉time􀀉in􀀉days.􀀉

They􀀉laughed􀀉the􀀉way􀀉they􀀉hadn’t􀀉laughed􀀉since􀀉they􀀉were􀀉children􀀉—􀀉
before􀀉the􀀉mines,􀀉before􀀉the􀀉Outflow,􀀉before􀀉any􀀉of􀀉them􀀉had􀀉learned􀀉to􀀉
count􀀉their􀀉breaths.􀀉It􀀉came􀀉up􀀉from􀀉somewhere􀀉the􀀉dust􀀉hadn’t􀀉reached.􀀉
They􀀉laughed􀀉until􀀉they􀀉couldn’t􀀉breathe.􀀉

Then,􀀉slowly,􀀉the􀀉laughter􀀉wound􀀉down.􀀉They􀀉lay􀀉back􀀉on􀀉their􀀉mattresses,􀀉
breathing􀀉settling,􀀉the􀀉white􀀉panels􀀉steady􀀉above􀀉them.􀀉For􀀉a􀀉few􀀉breaths,􀀉
there􀀉was􀀉nothing􀀉to􀀉plan,􀀉nothing􀀉to􀀉track,􀀉nothing􀀉to􀀉brace􀀉against.􀀉Just􀀉
four􀀉bodies􀀉on􀀉clean􀀉mattresses,􀀉finally􀀉still.􀀉

A􀀉whirring􀀉sound􀀉echoed􀀉through􀀉the􀀉room.􀀉Soft􀀉bands􀀉extended􀀉from􀀉the􀀉
bed􀀉frames􀀉and􀀉wrapped􀀉around􀀉their􀀉wrists􀀉and􀀉ankles􀀉—􀀉firm􀀉enough􀀉to􀀉
