hold􀀉them􀀉still.􀀉From􀀉the􀀉side􀀉of􀀉each􀀉bed,􀀉something􀀉thin􀀉and􀀉cold􀀉pressed􀀉


against􀀉the􀀉inside􀀉of􀀉each􀀉wrist.􀀉A􀀉small􀀉sting.􀀉A􀀉pulse􀀉of􀀉pressure.􀀉Then􀀉a􀀉
withdrawal.􀀉Whatever􀀉it􀀉was,􀀉it􀀉stayed􀀉in􀀉them.􀀉

Gage􀀉felt􀀉his􀀉pulse􀀉quicken,􀀉but􀀉exhaustion􀀉was􀀉already􀀉pulling􀀉him􀀉under.􀀉
One􀀉by􀀉one,􀀉they􀀉stopped􀀉fighting􀀉and􀀉let􀀉sleep􀀉take􀀉them.􀀉

That􀀉night,􀀉Gage’s􀀉mind􀀉didn’t􀀉rest.􀀉He􀀉was􀀉back􀀉in􀀉the􀀉desert,􀀉sand􀀉
clogging􀀉his􀀉respirator.􀀉Scorpions􀀉crawled􀀉over􀀉his􀀉skin,􀀉their􀀉stings􀀉carrying􀀉
a􀀉searing􀀉heat􀀉that􀀉drove􀀉straight􀀉into􀀉his􀀉nerves.􀀉One􀀉burrowed􀀉beneath􀀉his􀀉
skin,􀀉its􀀉movement􀀉a􀀉sharp,􀀉grinding􀀉pain􀀉—􀀉the􀀉same􀀉sensation􀀉as􀀉the􀀉coal􀀉
dust􀀉buried􀀉in􀀉his􀀉chest,􀀉eating􀀉him􀀉from􀀉the􀀉inside.􀀉He􀀉drew􀀉one􀀉last􀀉thick􀀉
breath􀀉and􀀉fell􀀉from􀀉the􀀉bed,􀀉the􀀉restraints􀀉catching􀀉him􀀉halfway,􀀉his􀀉body􀀉
hanging􀀉at􀀉an􀀉angle􀀉as􀀉the􀀉nightmare􀀉released􀀉him.􀀉He􀀉hung􀀉there􀀉until􀀉the􀀉
panic􀀉passed,􀀉then􀀉pulled􀀉himself􀀉back􀀉onto􀀉the􀀉mattress.􀀉The􀀉bands􀀉gave􀀉
slack􀀉as􀀉he􀀉climbed,􀀉then􀀉—􀀉reading􀀉something􀀉he􀀉couldn’t􀀉see􀀉—􀀉
withdrew􀀉into􀀉the􀀉frame.􀀉

He􀀉lay􀀉there􀀉drenched􀀉in􀀉sweat,􀀉his􀀉body􀀉aching􀀉—􀀉not􀀉from􀀉a􀀉day􀀉in􀀉the􀀉
pits,􀀉but􀀉from􀀉somewhere􀀉deeper.􀀉He􀀉thought􀀉of􀀉Thorton􀀉—􀀉how􀀉his􀀉
father􀀉had􀀉gone􀀉to􀀉the􀀉mine􀀉one􀀉morning􀀉the􀀉same􀀉way􀀉he􀀉always􀀉did,􀀉and􀀉
never􀀉come􀀉back.􀀉No􀀉warning.􀀉No􀀉goodbye.􀀉The􀀉mines􀀉didn’t􀀉negotiate.􀀉
They􀀉just􀀉took.􀀉

But􀀉what􀀉he􀀉was􀀉feeling􀀉now􀀉wasn’t􀀉that.􀀉This􀀉pain􀀉was􀀉different.􀀉Slower.􀀉
Almost􀀉methodical.􀀉Not􀀉the􀀉sharp􀀉grinding􀀉of􀀉dust􀀉against􀀉tissue,􀀉but􀀉
something􀀉steadier􀀉—􀀉as􀀉if􀀉his􀀉body,􀀉given􀀉a􀀉clean􀀉bed􀀉and􀀉a􀀉full􀀉belly􀀉for􀀉
the􀀉first􀀉time􀀉in􀀉his􀀉life,􀀉had􀀉decided􀀉to􀀉take􀀉inventory􀀉of􀀉everything􀀉that􀀉
had􀀉gone􀀉untended􀀉for􀀉as􀀉long􀀉as􀀉he􀀉could􀀉remember.􀀉Pappy􀀉had􀀉said􀀉it􀀉
once:􀀉the􀀈body􀀈knows􀀈what􀀈it’s􀀈owed.􀀉Pay􀀉it􀀉too􀀉much􀀉at􀀉once􀀉and􀀉it’ll􀀉spend􀀉
the􀀉surplus􀀉on􀀉what􀀉it’s􀀉been􀀉letting􀀉rot.􀀉

That􀀉was􀀉what􀀉he􀀉was􀀉feeling􀀉now.􀀉The􀀉cool􀀉sheets􀀉had􀀉let􀀉his􀀉back􀀉finally􀀉
lengthen.􀀉The􀀉real􀀉water􀀉had􀀉reached􀀉muscles􀀉that􀀉had􀀉spent􀀉his􀀉whole􀀉life􀀉


half-dry.􀀉The􀀉food􀀉in􀀉his􀀉stomach􀀉was􀀉giving􀀉his􀀉blood􀀉somewhere􀀉new􀀉to􀀉
go.􀀉He􀀉hurt􀀉everywhere􀀉because􀀉he􀀉was,􀀉for􀀉the􀀉first􀀉time􀀉in􀀉his􀀉life,􀀉healing.􀀉

And􀀉now􀀉Gage􀀉was􀀉lying􀀉in􀀉a􀀉stranger’s􀀉facility,􀀉his􀀉body􀀉better􀀉than􀀉it􀀉had􀀉
been􀀉in􀀉years,􀀉wondering􀀉if􀀉he’d􀀉ever􀀉see􀀉the􀀉surface􀀉again.􀀉

Gage􀀉looked􀀉at􀀉his􀀉friends,􀀉still􀀉strapped􀀉to􀀉their􀀉beds,􀀉their􀀉chests􀀉rising􀀉
and􀀉falling􀀉in􀀉the􀀉white􀀉glow.􀀉In􀀉the􀀉silence􀀉of􀀉this􀀉place,􀀉he􀀉felt􀀉a􀀉deep􀀉
hopelessness.􀀉Flitzberg􀀉didn’t􀀉rule􀀉with􀀉violence􀀉or􀀉fear􀀉—􀀉he􀀉ruled􀀉with􀀉
precision.􀀉Every􀀉variable􀀉accounted􀀉for.􀀉Every􀀉outcome􀀉predicted.􀀉Gage􀀉
had􀀉spent􀀉months􀀉building􀀉a􀀉plan􀀉to􀀉strike􀀉at􀀉the􀀉Trinity,􀀉and􀀉now􀀉he􀀉was􀀉a􀀉
data􀀉point􀀉in􀀉someone􀀉else’s􀀉system,􀀉strapped􀀉to􀀉a􀀉bed􀀉in􀀉a􀀉room􀀉he􀀉
couldn’t􀀉escape.􀀉

He􀀉watched􀀉them􀀉breathe.􀀉Camilla,􀀉slow􀀉and􀀉steady.􀀉Otto,􀀉his􀀉breath􀀉
catching􀀉every􀀉few􀀉cycles􀀉as􀀉some􀀉old􀀉reflex􀀉fired􀀉off􀀉and􀀉quieted􀀉again.􀀉Ben,􀀉
his􀀉breath􀀉so􀀉light􀀉it􀀉barely􀀉showed􀀉against􀀉the􀀉room’s􀀉silence.􀀉The􀀉panels􀀉
gave􀀉no􀀉sense􀀉of􀀉how􀀉the􀀉night􀀉was􀀉passing.􀀉Time􀀉in􀀉this􀀉place􀀉was􀀉
something􀀉other􀀉people􀀉kept.􀀉

Camilla’s􀀉bands􀀉released􀀉first􀀉—􀀉a􀀉soft􀀉click􀀉across􀀉the􀀉room,􀀉the􀀉restraints􀀉
withdrawing􀀉as􀀉she􀀉slept􀀉on.􀀉Some􀀉time􀀉later,􀀉Otto’s􀀉released,􀀉his􀀉breathing􀀉
finally􀀉settled.􀀉Ben’s􀀉came􀀉last,􀀉when􀀉the􀀉rise􀀉and􀀉fall􀀉of􀀉his􀀉chest􀀉had􀀉
matched􀀉the􀀉room’s􀀉quiet􀀉long􀀉enough􀀉to􀀉satisfy􀀉whatever􀀉was􀀉measuring􀀉
it.􀀉Each􀀉band􀀉had􀀉been􀀉reading􀀉its􀀉own􀀉host.􀀉Flitzberg’s􀀉machines􀀉didn’t􀀉
restrain.􀀉They􀀉watched,􀀉and􀀉they􀀉decided.􀀉

Nothing􀀉else􀀉changed.􀀉The􀀉panels􀀉stayed􀀉steady􀀉above.􀀉No􀀉guard􀀉appeared.􀀉
Gage􀀉lay􀀉there􀀉with􀀉his􀀉hands􀀉free􀀉and􀀉understood􀀉that􀀉the􀀉bands􀀉had􀀉never􀀉
been􀀉the􀀉cage.􀀉


Chapter􀀉22􀀉—􀀉The􀀉Engine􀀉and􀀉Eyes􀀉

Gage􀀉was􀀉still􀀉awake􀀉when􀀉Otto􀀉fell.􀀉He’d􀀉been􀀉lying􀀉in􀀉the􀀉white􀀉glow􀀉for􀀉
what􀀉felt􀀉like􀀉hours,􀀉watching􀀉his􀀉friends􀀉breathe,􀀉when􀀉Otto’s􀀉body􀀉
started􀀉to􀀉move􀀉—􀀉not􀀉the􀀉random􀀉twitching􀀉of􀀉a􀀉bad􀀉dream,􀀉but􀀉
something􀀉deliberate.􀀉His􀀉limbs􀀉shifted􀀉through􀀉patterns􀀉Gage􀀉recognized􀀉
from􀀉the􀀉mine:􀀉the􀀉slow,􀀉precise􀀉movements􀀉Master􀀉Chan􀀉had􀀉drilled􀀉into􀀉
Otto’s􀀉muscles􀀉until􀀉they􀀉ran􀀉without􀀉permission.􀀉Otto’s􀀉body􀀉was􀀉
rehearsing􀀉in􀀉his􀀉sleep,􀀉the􀀉way􀀉it􀀉always􀀉did􀀉before􀀉something􀀉hard.􀀉

Then􀀉his􀀉knee􀀉cleared􀀉the􀀉edge􀀉of􀀉the􀀉mattress􀀉and􀀉he􀀉hit􀀉the􀀉floor􀀉with􀀉a􀀉
shriek􀀉that􀀉woke􀀉Ben􀀉and􀀉Camilla.􀀉

They􀀉were􀀉on􀀉their􀀉feet􀀉within􀀉seconds,􀀉looking􀀉around􀀉at􀀉the􀀉room􀀉they’d􀀉
been􀀉too􀀉exhausted􀀉to􀀉examine􀀉the􀀉night􀀉before.􀀉The􀀉steady􀀉white􀀉light.􀀉
The􀀉smooth􀀉walls.􀀉The􀀉hum􀀉of􀀉machines􀀉they􀀉couldn’t􀀉see.􀀉Everything􀀉
clean,􀀉precise,􀀉made􀀉in􀀉a􀀉world􀀉that􀀉had􀀉never􀀉touched􀀉coal.􀀉

The􀀉door􀀉slid􀀉open􀀉and􀀉a􀀉guard􀀉in􀀉a􀀉fitted􀀉black􀀉suit􀀉stepped􀀉in.􀀉He􀀉didn’t􀀉
introduce􀀉himself.􀀉“Get􀀉dressed,”􀀉he􀀉said,􀀉nodding􀀉toward􀀉the􀀉wall􀀉to􀀉their􀀉
right.􀀉They􀀉turned.􀀉Where􀀉there􀀉had􀀉been􀀉smooth,􀀉unbroken􀀉surface􀀉a􀀉
heartbeat􀀉before,􀀉four􀀉doorways􀀉now􀀉stood􀀉open􀀉—􀀉no􀀉seam,􀀉no􀀉slide,􀀉no􀀉
hinge􀀉to􀀉mark􀀉where􀀉one􀀉section􀀉ended􀀉and􀀉another􀀉began.􀀉The􀀉wall􀀉had􀀉
simply􀀉decided􀀉to􀀉be􀀉doors.􀀉“You􀀉have􀀉until􀀉I􀀉come􀀉back.”􀀉He􀀉turned􀀉and􀀉
left.􀀉

Inside􀀉each􀀉room,􀀉they􀀉found􀀉clothing􀀉—􀀉fitted􀀉black􀀉material􀀉that􀀉felt􀀉
nothing􀀉like􀀉the􀀉stiff,􀀉patched􀀉fabric􀀉of􀀉Miner􀀉Town.􀀉Gage􀀉fumbled􀀉with􀀉
the􀀉suit,􀀉his􀀉pit-calloused􀀉fingers􀀉clumsy􀀉against􀀉the􀀉smooth,􀀉stretching􀀉
fabric.􀀉They􀀉didn’t􀀉know􀀉how􀀉long􀀉they􀀉had.􀀉They􀀉didn’t􀀉know􀀉what􀀉came􀀉
next.􀀉They􀀉dressed􀀉fast.􀀉Ben􀀉was􀀉the􀀉last􀀉one􀀉out􀀉—􀀉he􀀉had􀀉paused􀀉to􀀉slip􀀉


something􀀉small􀀉from􀀉his􀀉old􀀉trousers􀀉into􀀉the􀀉suit’s􀀉pocket􀀉before􀀉leaving􀀉
his􀀉mining􀀉clothes􀀉on􀀉the􀀉floor.􀀉

When􀀉they􀀉emerged,􀀉the􀀉clothing􀀉fit􀀉like􀀉a􀀉second􀀉skin.􀀉Gage,􀀉Otto,􀀉and􀀉
Ben􀀉looked􀀉like􀀉miners􀀉stuffed􀀉into􀀉costumes􀀉they􀀉didn’t􀀉deserve.􀀉Camilla􀀉
looked􀀉like􀀉she’d􀀉been􀀉wearing􀀉it􀀉her􀀉whole􀀉life.􀀉She􀀉caught􀀉them􀀉staring􀀉
and􀀉shut􀀉it􀀉down􀀉with􀀉a􀀉look􀀉that􀀉could􀀉have􀀉cracked􀀉glass.􀀉

The􀀉door􀀉slid􀀉open.􀀉The􀀉guard􀀉was􀀉back.􀀉He􀀉looked􀀉them􀀉over􀀉once,􀀉said􀀉
nothing􀀉about􀀉how􀀉they􀀉looked,􀀉and􀀉stepped􀀉aside.􀀉Behind􀀉him,􀀉the􀀉main􀀉
door􀀉had􀀉opened􀀉—􀀉dim􀀉red􀀉light􀀉spilling􀀉through.􀀉“Follow􀀉me,”􀀉he􀀉said.􀀉

He􀀉led􀀉them􀀉into􀀉a􀀉corridor􀀉with􀀉four􀀉doors,􀀉each􀀉labeled:􀀉CONTROL.􀀉
PLAN.􀀉ADAPT.􀀉SACRIFICE.􀀉

The􀀉guard􀀉stopped􀀉at􀀉the􀀉entrance􀀉and􀀉turned􀀉to􀀉face􀀉them.􀀉“Four􀀉rooms.􀀉
Four􀀉tests.􀀉You􀀉pass􀀉all􀀉four,􀀉you􀀉live.􀀉You􀀉fail􀀉—”􀀉He􀀉didn’t􀀉finish􀀉the􀀉
sentence.􀀉He􀀉didn’t􀀉need􀀉to.􀀉“You􀀉take􀀉them􀀉in􀀉order.􀀉Left􀀉to􀀉right.”􀀉He􀀉
stepped􀀉back􀀉and􀀉the􀀉corridor􀀉door􀀉sealed􀀉behind􀀉him.􀀉

For􀀉a􀀉long􀀉moment,􀀉none􀀉of􀀉them􀀉moved.􀀉“What􀀉the􀀉hell,”􀀉Ben􀀉said,􀀉voice􀀉
quiet.􀀉“What􀀉the􀀉actual􀀉hell.”􀀉

Gage􀀉tested􀀉the􀀉corridor􀀉door􀀉behind􀀉them.􀀉Smooth.􀀉No􀀉handle.􀀉No􀀉give.􀀉
Camilla􀀉was􀀉already􀀉checking􀀉the􀀉four􀀉labeled􀀉doors,􀀉fingers􀀉running􀀉along􀀉
the􀀉seams.􀀉Nothing􀀉answered.􀀉Otto􀀉rolled􀀉his􀀉shoulders􀀉and􀀉shifted􀀉his􀀉
weight􀀉forward,􀀉settling􀀉into􀀉a􀀉familiar􀀉stance.􀀉

“The􀀉tests􀀉are􀀉the􀀉only􀀉way􀀉through,”􀀉Camilla􀀉said.􀀉

Gage􀀉moved􀀉toward􀀉the􀀉first􀀉door.􀀉“We􀀉split􀀉up.􀀉One􀀉each.􀀉Cover􀀉them􀀉
faster.”􀀉

Camilla􀀉stepped􀀉in􀀉front􀀉of􀀉him.􀀉“No.􀀉We􀀉stand􀀉a􀀉fighting􀀉chance􀀉as􀀉a􀀉
group.”􀀉She􀀉held􀀉his􀀉gaze.􀀉“Who􀀉was􀀉the􀀉first􀀉one􀀉to􀀉free􀀉themselves􀀉when􀀉
we􀀉were􀀉captured?”􀀉


Gage􀀉exhaled􀀉through􀀉his􀀉nose.􀀉“You􀀉were.”􀀉

“Then􀀉we’ve􀀉made􀀉our􀀉decision.􀀉We􀀉take􀀉each􀀉door􀀉together.”􀀉

Before􀀉they􀀉moved,􀀉Gage􀀉caught􀀉her􀀉arm.􀀉The􀀉others􀀉had􀀉already􀀉started􀀉
toward􀀉the􀀉corridor,􀀉but􀀉he􀀉held􀀉her􀀉back.􀀉In􀀉the􀀉red􀀉light,􀀉her􀀉face􀀉looked􀀉
harder􀀉than􀀉he’d􀀉ever􀀉seen􀀉it􀀉—􀀉the􀀉same􀀉face􀀉he’d􀀉once􀀉ignored􀀉across􀀉the􀀉
dinner􀀉table,􀀉the􀀉same􀀉face􀀉he’d􀀉pushed􀀉away􀀉from􀀉every􀀉plan􀀉he’d􀀉ever􀀉
made.􀀉

“Cami,”􀀉he􀀉said.􀀉“Whatever’s􀀉behind􀀉those􀀉doors􀀉—”􀀉

“Don’t.”􀀉Her􀀉voice􀀉was􀀉sharp􀀉but􀀉not􀀉unkind.􀀉“Don’t􀀉make􀀉a􀀉speech.􀀉
Don’t􀀉tell􀀉me􀀉to􀀉hang􀀉back.􀀉Don’t􀀉tell􀀉me􀀉you’ll􀀉protect􀀉me.”􀀉

“I􀀉wasn’t􀀉going􀀉to􀀉say􀀉any􀀉of􀀉that.”􀀉

“Then􀀉what?”􀀉

He􀀉looked􀀉at􀀉her􀀉—􀀉really􀀉looked,􀀉the􀀉way􀀉he􀀉should􀀉have􀀉looked􀀉at􀀉her􀀉
years􀀉ago,􀀉when􀀉she􀀉was􀀉small􀀉and􀀉fierce􀀉and􀀉invisible􀀉to􀀉him.􀀉“I􀀉was􀀉going􀀉
to􀀉say:􀀉call􀀉the􀀉shots.􀀉In􀀉there,􀀉you􀀉lead.􀀉I’ll􀀉follow.”􀀉

She􀀉blinked.􀀉In􀀉her􀀉whole􀀉life􀀉of􀀉knowing􀀉her􀀉brother,􀀉she􀀉had􀀉never􀀉once􀀉
heard􀀉him􀀉volunteer􀀉to􀀉follow􀀉anyone.􀀉Gage􀀉didn’t􀀉follow.􀀉Gage􀀉broke􀀉
things,􀀉moved􀀉things,􀀉carried􀀉things.􀀉He􀀉was􀀉the􀀉Engine􀀉—􀀉and􀀉engines􀀉
don’t􀀉follow.􀀉

But􀀉he􀀉wasn’t􀀉saying􀀉it􀀉out􀀉of􀀉weakness.􀀉She􀀉could􀀉see􀀉that.􀀉He􀀉was􀀉saying􀀉it􀀉
because􀀉somewhere􀀉in􀀉the􀀉last􀀉twenty-four􀀉hours􀀉—􀀉waking􀀉up􀀉restrained􀀉
in􀀉a􀀉facility􀀉he􀀉didn’t􀀉understand,􀀉watching􀀉his􀀉sister􀀉see􀀉through􀀉a􀀉trick􀀉
that􀀉had􀀉fooled􀀉everyone􀀉else􀀉in􀀉the􀀉room􀀉—􀀉he􀀉had􀀉finally􀀉understood􀀉
what􀀉Pappy􀀉had􀀉been􀀉trying􀀉to􀀉tell􀀉them􀀉both􀀉since􀀉they􀀉were􀀉children:􀀉the􀀉
Engine􀀉is􀀉blind􀀉without􀀉the􀀉Eyes.􀀉Not􀀉as􀀉a􀀉saying.􀀉As􀀉fact.􀀉

“Okay,”􀀉she􀀉said.􀀉“Stay􀀉close.􀀉Move􀀉when􀀉I􀀉move.􀀉And􀀉Gage?”􀀉


“Yeah?”􀀉

“If􀀉I􀀉tell􀀉you􀀉to􀀉stop,􀀉you􀀉stop.􀀉Even􀀉if􀀉every􀀉part􀀉of􀀉you􀀉is􀀉screaming􀀉to􀀉
charge.􀀉You􀀉stop.”􀀉

He􀀉nodded.􀀉It􀀉was􀀉the􀀉first􀀉real􀀉promise􀀉he’d􀀉ever􀀉made􀀉to􀀉her􀀉—􀀉not􀀉the􀀉
kind􀀉you􀀉make􀀉to􀀉be􀀉kind,􀀉but􀀉the􀀉kind􀀉you􀀉make􀀉when􀀉you􀀉finally􀀉see􀀉
who’s􀀉been􀀉standing􀀉beside􀀉you􀀉the􀀉whole􀀉time.􀀉

They􀀉marched􀀉to􀀉the􀀉first􀀉door:􀀉CONTROL.􀀉It􀀉slid􀀉open􀀉as􀀉they􀀉
approached􀀉—􀀉no􀀉handle,􀀉no􀀉touch􀀉needed,􀀉the􀀉door􀀉reading􀀉them􀀉like􀀉
everything􀀉else􀀉in􀀉this􀀉place.􀀉Inside,􀀉a􀀉crystal􀀉hung􀀉in􀀉the􀀉air􀀉—􀀉four􀀉feet􀀉
across,􀀉multi-faceted,􀀉rotating􀀉slowly􀀉on􀀉nothing.􀀉It􀀉caught􀀉the􀀉room’s􀀉light􀀉
and􀀉broke􀀉it􀀉into􀀉colors􀀉none􀀉of􀀉them􀀉had􀀉names􀀉for,􀀉like􀀉the􀀉light􀀉itself􀀉
had􀀉been􀀉cut􀀉into􀀉pieces.􀀉Camilla􀀉had􀀉seen􀀉crystals􀀉only􀀉in􀀉Pappy’s􀀉books􀀉

—􀀉none􀀉of􀀉them􀀉had􀀉ever􀀉stood􀀉in􀀉front􀀉of􀀉one.􀀉The􀀉chamber􀀉felt􀀉colder􀀉
near􀀉it.􀀉A􀀉voice􀀉spoke:􀀉“Round􀀉1.􀀉Three􀀉attempts􀀉allowed.”􀀉
“This􀀉is􀀉it,”􀀉Camilla􀀉said􀀉quietly.􀀉“Flitzberg’s􀀉not􀀉testing􀀉us.􀀉He’s􀀉seeing􀀉if􀀉
four􀀉kids􀀉from􀀉Miner􀀉Town􀀉can􀀉break􀀉under􀀉what􀀉he􀀉built.􀀉We􀀉don’t􀀉
break.􀀉We􀀉never􀀉have.􀀉So􀀉we􀀉move􀀉together.􀀉We􀀉don’t􀀉take􀀉what􀀉looks􀀉easy.􀀉
And􀀉we􀀉trust􀀉each􀀉other􀀉more􀀉than􀀉anything􀀉in􀀉these􀀉rooms.”􀀉

The􀀉crystal􀀉stopped􀀉rotating.􀀉From􀀉its􀀉center,􀀉a􀀉fine􀀉beam􀀉of􀀉light􀀉shot􀀉over􀀉
their􀀉heads􀀉to􀀉the􀀉wall􀀉behind􀀉them,􀀉where􀀉it􀀉bloomed􀀉into􀀉a􀀉picture.􀀉They􀀉
turned.􀀉The􀀉wall􀀉was􀀉a􀀉window􀀉now􀀉—􀀉a􀀉river,􀀉a􀀉wooden􀀉bridge,􀀉a􀀉brown􀀉
horse􀀉standing􀀉on􀀉the􀀉near􀀉bank􀀉with􀀉its􀀉head􀀉bent􀀉to􀀉the􀀉grass,􀀉all􀀉in􀀉colors􀀉
that􀀉moved.􀀉

At􀀉the􀀉same􀀉time,􀀉the􀀉floor􀀉beside􀀉the􀀉crystal􀀉opened􀀉and􀀉a􀀉platform􀀉rose􀀉
into􀀉place:􀀉four􀀉panels􀀉along􀀉its􀀉surface,􀀉each􀀉with􀀉buttons􀀉and􀀉a􀀉lever,􀀉one􀀉
for􀀉each􀀉of􀀉them.􀀉

A􀀉voice􀀉spoke:􀀉“Purpose􀀉—􀀉get􀀉the􀀉horse􀀉across􀀉the􀀉river.􀀉Each􀀉subject􀀉to􀀉a􀀉
panel.”􀀉


Four􀀉positions􀀉on􀀉the􀀉platform􀀉showed􀀉themselves􀀉—􀀉a􀀉lever􀀉and􀀉two􀀉
buttons􀀉at􀀉each􀀉—􀀉one􀀉a􀀉faint􀀉blue,􀀉one􀀉a􀀉faint􀀉red􀀉—􀀉the􀀉markings􀀉barely􀀉a􀀉
suggestion.􀀉They􀀉looked􀀉at􀀉each􀀉other.􀀉Camilla􀀉moved􀀉first.􀀉The􀀉others􀀉
followed.􀀉As􀀉each􀀉foot􀀉found􀀉a􀀉position,􀀉the􀀉platform􀀉closed􀀉around􀀉them:􀀉
bands􀀉snaked􀀉up􀀉their􀀉calves􀀉and􀀉clamped􀀉at􀀉the􀀉knees.􀀉Ben􀀉whispered􀀉
something􀀉—􀀉a􀀉prayer􀀉or􀀉a􀀉curse,􀀉no􀀉one􀀉heard􀀉which.􀀉Otto􀀉was􀀉past􀀉fear,􀀉
his􀀉face􀀉the􀀉mask􀀉Master􀀉Chan􀀉had􀀉drilled􀀉into􀀉him􀀉for􀀉fights.􀀉Gage􀀉exhaled􀀉
hard.􀀉“Of􀀉course,”􀀉he􀀉said.􀀉Camilla􀀉watched􀀉the􀀉bands􀀉engage:􀀉magnetic,􀀉
fast,􀀉clean.􀀉From􀀉above,􀀉a􀀉thin􀀉ring􀀉of􀀉metal􀀉lowered􀀉against􀀉the􀀉crown􀀉of􀀉
each􀀉skull􀀉and􀀉held􀀉there,􀀉cold.􀀉None􀀉of􀀉them􀀉had􀀉felt􀀉anything􀀉touch􀀉their􀀉
head􀀉that􀀉way􀀉before􀀉—􀀉close,􀀉exact,􀀉reading􀀉them.􀀉

Camilla􀀉looked􀀉down􀀉at􀀉the􀀉panel􀀉under􀀉her􀀉hands.􀀉The􀀉lever􀀉had􀀉been􀀉
gripped􀀉before􀀉—􀀉worn􀀉smooth􀀉where􀀉palms􀀉had􀀉pressed􀀉against􀀉it.􀀉The􀀉
buttons􀀉showed􀀉faint􀀉depressions􀀉from􀀉earlier􀀉fingers.􀀉She􀀉glanced􀀉at􀀉the􀀉
others’􀀉panels.􀀉The􀀉same􀀉wear,􀀉in􀀉the􀀉same􀀉places,􀀉on􀀉all􀀉four.􀀉They􀀉were􀀉
not􀀉the􀀉first􀀉to􀀉stand􀀉here.􀀉And􀀉none􀀉of􀀉those􀀉who􀀉had􀀉stood􀀉here􀀉before􀀉
was􀀉here􀀉now􀀉to􀀉say􀀉how􀀉it􀀉had􀀉ended.􀀉

The􀀉voice􀀉continued:􀀉“Each􀀉subject􀀉controls􀀉one􀀉limb􀀉of􀀉the􀀉animal.􀀉Two􀀉
minutes.􀀉Begin.”􀀉

A􀀉light􀀉snapped􀀉on􀀉in􀀉a􀀉corner􀀉near􀀉the􀀉wall-picture􀀉—􀀉and􀀉lit,􀀉for􀀉the􀀉first􀀉
time,􀀉an􀀉hourglass􀀉none􀀉of􀀉them􀀉had􀀉noticed􀀉on􀀉the􀀉way􀀉in.􀀉Glass-bright,􀀉
held􀀉in􀀉a􀀉steel􀀉frame.􀀉As􀀉they􀀉watched,􀀉the􀀉frame􀀉rotated,􀀉turning􀀉the􀀉
hourglass􀀉end􀀉over􀀉end,􀀉and􀀉settled􀀉it􀀉with􀀉the􀀉full􀀉chamber􀀉at􀀉the􀀉top.􀀉
Sand􀀉began􀀉to􀀉fall.􀀉Not􀀉much.􀀉Falling􀀉fast.􀀉

Two􀀉minutes􀀉was􀀉a􀀉measure􀀉none􀀉of􀀉them􀀉had􀀉ever􀀉counted􀀉by.􀀉The􀀉
hourglass􀀉needed􀀉no􀀉translation.􀀉They􀀉knew􀀉time􀀉when􀀉they􀀉saw􀀉it􀀉leaving􀀉
them.􀀉

Gage’s􀀉muscles􀀉tensed􀀉against􀀉the􀀉restraints􀀉—􀀉the􀀉old􀀉urge􀀉to􀀉move,􀀉to􀀉
break,􀀉to􀀉do􀀉something.􀀉He􀀉looked􀀉at􀀉Camilla.􀀉


“Wait,”􀀉she􀀉said.􀀉“Don’t􀀉touch􀀉anything􀀉yet.􀀉Not􀀉until􀀉we􀀉understand􀀉what􀀉
we’re􀀉working􀀉with.”􀀉

But􀀉the􀀉urge􀀉ran􀀉ahead􀀉of􀀉his􀀉thinking􀀉—􀀉his􀀉body􀀉deciding􀀉before􀀉his􀀉
mind􀀉could􀀉stop􀀉it.􀀉He􀀉shoved􀀉his􀀉lever􀀉forward􀀉and􀀉mashed􀀉both􀀉buttons􀀉

—􀀉the􀀉horse’s􀀉front-right􀀉leg􀀉kicked􀀉out􀀉violently.􀀉Ben􀀉flinched􀀉and􀀉yanked􀀉
his􀀉lever􀀉back,􀀉locking􀀉the􀀉front-left􀀉leg􀀉rigid.􀀉Otto􀀉tried􀀉to􀀉match􀀉Gage’s􀀉
rhythm􀀉but􀀉hit􀀉his􀀉buttons􀀉a􀀉half-second􀀉late,􀀉sending􀀉the􀀉rear-left􀀉leg􀀉into􀀉
a􀀉stutter.􀀉Camilla􀀉held􀀉still,􀀉watching,􀀉trying􀀉to􀀉read􀀉the􀀉pattern􀀉—􀀉but􀀉
there􀀉was􀀉no􀀉pattern.􀀉Three􀀉people􀀉were􀀉guessing􀀉at􀀉full􀀉speed􀀉while􀀉the􀀉
fourth􀀉tried􀀉to􀀉think.􀀉The􀀉sand􀀉in􀀉the􀀉hourglass􀀉kept􀀉falling.􀀉Half-down.􀀉
More􀀉than􀀉half.􀀉
The􀀉horse􀀉lurched􀀉sideways,􀀉its􀀉legs􀀉pulling􀀉in􀀉four􀀉different􀀉directions􀀉like􀀉
ropes􀀉in􀀉a􀀉tug􀀉of􀀉war􀀉with􀀉no􀀉teams.􀀉It􀀉staggered􀀉two􀀉steps,􀀉buckled􀀉at􀀉the􀀉
knees,􀀉and􀀉collapsed􀀉face-first􀀉into􀀉the􀀉dirt.􀀉The􀀉image􀀉went􀀉dark:􀀉“Round􀀉
1􀀉Failure.􀀉Two􀀉attempts􀀉remain.”􀀉

For􀀉a􀀉moment,􀀉nothing􀀉happened.􀀉The􀀉horse􀀉lay􀀉in􀀉the􀀉dirt.􀀉They􀀉
breathed.􀀉They􀀉waited.􀀉None􀀉of􀀉them􀀉remembered􀀉the􀀉rings􀀉on􀀉their􀀉
skulls.􀀉

The􀀉rings􀀉hadn’t􀀉forgotten.􀀉

A􀀉jolt􀀉lanced􀀉down􀀉through􀀉the􀀉metal􀀉at􀀉their􀀉crowns,􀀉ripped􀀉through􀀉the􀀉
bands􀀉at􀀉their􀀉knees,􀀉and􀀉met􀀉itself􀀉somewhere􀀉in􀀉their􀀉bodies.􀀉Not􀀉enough􀀉
to􀀉injure.􀀉More􀀉than􀀉enough􀀉to􀀉override.􀀉Every􀀉muscle􀀉seized􀀉at􀀉once.􀀉Every􀀉
thought􀀉scattered.􀀉Ben􀀉screamed.􀀉Otto’s􀀉jaw􀀉locked.􀀉Gage’s􀀉vision􀀉went􀀉
white.􀀉All􀀉four􀀉of􀀉them􀀉folded􀀉forward,􀀉collapsing􀀉onto􀀉their􀀉panels,􀀉cheeks􀀉
pressed􀀉against􀀉the􀀉cold􀀉metal,􀀉arms􀀉hanging􀀉limp.􀀉

When􀀉it􀀉passed,􀀉the􀀉restraints􀀉had􀀉not􀀉let􀀉them􀀉go.􀀉They􀀉hung􀀉forward􀀉over􀀉
the􀀉controls,􀀉held􀀉by􀀉the􀀉bands􀀉at􀀉their􀀉knees􀀉and􀀉the􀀉rings􀀉at􀀉their􀀉skulls,􀀉
residual􀀉current􀀉humming􀀉through􀀉their􀀉bones.􀀉None􀀉of􀀉them􀀉could􀀉fall.􀀉


None􀀉of􀀉them􀀉could􀀉move.􀀉It􀀉took􀀉a􀀉full􀀉minute􀀉before􀀉any􀀉of􀀉them􀀉could􀀉
lift􀀉a􀀉head.􀀉

Camilla􀀉pulled􀀉herself􀀉upright,􀀉her􀀉voice􀀉shaking􀀉but􀀉controlled.􀀉“They􀀉say􀀉
we􀀉have􀀉two􀀉more􀀉attempts.􀀉I􀀉don’t􀀉believe􀀉it.􀀉I􀀉don’t􀀉think􀀉we􀀉survive􀀉
another􀀉shock􀀉like􀀉that.􀀉So􀀉we􀀉treat􀀉this􀀉as􀀉the􀀉last􀀉try.􀀉We􀀉get􀀉it􀀉right.”􀀉

She􀀉turned􀀉to􀀉Gage.􀀉“You􀀉moved􀀉before􀀉I􀀉called􀀉it.”􀀉Her􀀉voice􀀉was􀀉sharper􀀉
than􀀉any􀀉of􀀉them􀀉had􀀉ever􀀉heard􀀉it.􀀉“You􀀉moved􀀉before􀀉any􀀉of􀀉us􀀉
understood􀀉what􀀉we􀀉had.􀀉If􀀉you􀀉do􀀉that􀀉again,􀀉we􀀉die􀀉in􀀉this􀀉room.􀀉All􀀉four􀀉
of􀀉us.”􀀉

Gage􀀉said􀀉nothing.􀀉He􀀉looked􀀉at􀀉his􀀉hands􀀉on􀀉the􀀉lever.􀀉The􀀉fury􀀉was􀀉at􀀉
himself.􀀉

She􀀉looked􀀉at􀀉each􀀉of􀀉them􀀉—􀀉Ben􀀉white-faced,􀀉Otto’s􀀉hands􀀉trembling,􀀉
Gage’s􀀉eyes􀀉on􀀉the􀀉floor.􀀉The􀀉shock􀀉had􀀉done􀀉one􀀉thing􀀉no􀀉argument􀀉could􀀉
have:􀀉it􀀉had􀀉made􀀉them􀀉afraid􀀉enough􀀉to􀀉listen.􀀉

“Listen􀀉to􀀉me,”􀀉she􀀉said.􀀉“We􀀉need􀀉to􀀉get􀀉in􀀉control􀀉—􀀉that’s􀀉what􀀉this􀀉
room􀀉is􀀉testing.􀀉We􀀉move􀀉clockwise.􀀉I’m􀀉the􀀉caller.􀀉You􀀉move􀀉only􀀉on􀀉my􀀉
count.􀀉Nobody􀀉improvises.􀀉Nobody􀀉charges.”􀀉

Then􀀉she􀀉noticed􀀉something􀀉she’d􀀉missed􀀉before.􀀉On􀀉the􀀉edge􀀉of􀀉each􀀉
panel,􀀉tucked􀀉beside􀀉the􀀉lever,􀀉was􀀉a􀀉small􀀉square􀀉of􀀉paper􀀉and􀀉a􀀉stub􀀉of􀀉
pencil.􀀉They’d􀀉been􀀉there􀀉the􀀉whole􀀉time􀀉—􀀉part􀀉of􀀉the􀀉test’s􀀉design.􀀉The􀀉
facility􀀉had􀀉given􀀉them􀀉the􀀉tools􀀉to􀀉plan.􀀉On􀀉the􀀉first􀀉attempt,􀀉they’d􀀉been􀀉
too􀀉panicked􀀉to􀀉see􀀉them.􀀉

Camilla􀀉grabbed􀀉hers􀀉and􀀉started􀀉scribbling􀀉—􀀉muttering􀀉the􀀉logic􀀉under􀀉
her􀀉breath.􀀉The􀀉first􀀉attempt􀀉had􀀉been􀀉the􀀉lesson.􀀉Gage’s􀀉leg􀀉kicked􀀉when􀀉
he􀀉mashed􀀉both􀀉buttons.􀀉Ben’s􀀉locked􀀉when􀀉he􀀉yanked􀀉lever􀀉back.􀀉Otto’s􀀉
stuttered􀀉when􀀉his􀀉timing􀀉slipped.􀀉The􀀉pattern􀀉was􀀉in􀀉the􀀉failure.􀀉Lever􀀉for􀀉
the􀀉angle􀀉—􀀉bigger􀀉angle,􀀉longer􀀉stride.􀀉Red􀀉to􀀉drive.􀀉Blue􀀉to􀀉hold.􀀉One􀀉
limb􀀉reaches,􀀉three􀀉limbs􀀉drive.􀀉Slow􀀉rhythm􀀉trots,􀀉fast􀀉rhythm􀀉sprints.􀀉She􀀉


tore􀀉the􀀉page􀀉into􀀉three􀀉strips􀀉and􀀉shoved􀀉them􀀉into􀀉their􀀉hands􀀉—􀀉each􀀉
with􀀉a􀀉precise􀀉sequence􀀉of􀀉lever􀀉positions􀀉and􀀉button􀀉presses􀀉for􀀉each􀀉
phase.􀀉

She􀀉didn’t􀀉make􀀉one􀀉for􀀉herself.􀀉She􀀉didn’t􀀉need􀀉to.􀀉The􀀉pattern􀀉was􀀉
already􀀉running􀀉in􀀉her􀀉head.􀀉

Ben􀀉looked􀀉at􀀉his􀀉strip,􀀉then􀀉at􀀉Camilla.􀀉“We’re􀀉with􀀉you,”􀀉he􀀉said.􀀉Otto􀀉
nodded.􀀉

Gage􀀉said􀀉nothing.􀀉He􀀉took􀀉the􀀉strip􀀉slowly,􀀉jaw􀀉locked,􀀉like􀀉he􀀉wasn’t􀀉sure􀀉
he􀀉could􀀉trust􀀉himself􀀉with􀀉it.􀀉He’d􀀉nearly􀀉killed􀀉them􀀉all.􀀉The􀀉paper􀀉was􀀉
the􀀉only􀀉road􀀉back.􀀉

“On􀀉my􀀉mark,”􀀉Camilla􀀉whispered.􀀉From􀀉the􀀉corner:􀀉click.􀀉Clack.􀀉The􀀉
steel􀀉frame􀀉turned.􀀉The􀀉hourglass􀀉with􀀉it,􀀉end􀀉over􀀉end.􀀉Fresh􀀉sand􀀉at􀀉the􀀉
top,􀀉and􀀉falling.􀀉She􀀉took􀀉a􀀉breath.􀀉“Levers􀀉to􀀉position􀀉two.􀀉Ready􀀉—􀀉set􀀉—􀀉
go.􀀉One.􀀉Two.􀀉One.􀀉Two.”􀀉

“Levers􀀉to􀀉two,”􀀉Camilla􀀉called.􀀉“Trot􀀉count.”􀀉She􀀉counted􀀉out􀀉loud,􀀉
steady􀀉and􀀉clear,􀀉each􀀉beat􀀉landing􀀉like􀀉a􀀉metronome.􀀉On􀀉“one,”􀀉Ben􀀉and􀀉
Otto􀀉pressed􀀉their􀀉buttons.􀀉On􀀉“two,”􀀉Gage􀀉and􀀉Camilla􀀉pressed􀀉theirs.􀀉
The􀀉horse􀀉lifted􀀉a􀀉leg.􀀉Then􀀉another.􀀉Then􀀉a􀀉third.􀀉

It􀀉was􀀉ugly􀀉at􀀉first􀀉—􀀉the􀀉trot􀀉was􀀉stiff,􀀉mechanical,􀀉blue􀀉pressed􀀉a􀀉half-beat􀀉
late􀀉so􀀉each􀀉leg􀀉drove􀀉straight􀀉into􀀉the􀀉dirt.􀀉But􀀉it􀀉was􀀉moving.􀀉Four􀀉limbs,􀀉
four􀀉people,􀀉one􀀉count.􀀉Camilla􀀉kept􀀉her􀀉voice􀀉locked􀀉to􀀉the􀀉trot􀀉count􀀉—􀀉
never􀀉speeding,􀀉never􀀉slowing,􀀉not􀀉yet.􀀉The􀀉rhythm􀀉was􀀉the􀀉pace;􀀉drifting􀀉
now􀀉would􀀉buckle􀀉the􀀉gait.􀀉Even􀀉when􀀉the􀀉horse􀀉wobbled􀀉on􀀉the􀀉
approach,􀀉she􀀉held􀀉the􀀉count.􀀉

“Don’t􀀉look􀀉at􀀉the􀀉horse,”􀀉she􀀉called.􀀉“Listen􀀉to􀀉me.”􀀉

The􀀉horse􀀉reached􀀉the􀀉bridge􀀉and􀀉stepped􀀉onto􀀉the􀀉planks.􀀉The􀀉river􀀉
churned􀀉beneath􀀉them.􀀉The􀀉far􀀉bank􀀉was􀀉still􀀉a􀀉long􀀉way􀀉off.􀀉Less􀀉than􀀉half􀀉
the􀀉sand􀀉remained􀀉at􀀉the􀀉top􀀉—􀀉and􀀉falling.􀀉


“Sprint,”􀀉Camilla􀀉called.􀀉“Levers􀀉to􀀉four.􀀉New􀀉pattern.􀀉Count􀀉of􀀉four􀀉now􀀉
—􀀉one,􀀉two,􀀉three,􀀉four.􀀉One,􀀉two,􀀉three,􀀉four.􀀉Go.”􀀉

They􀀉shifted􀀉together.􀀉The􀀉horse􀀉accelerated􀀉—􀀉legs􀀉pumping􀀉faster,􀀉the􀀉
projected􀀉landscape􀀉streaking􀀉past􀀉behind􀀉it.􀀉Camilla’s􀀉voice􀀉climbed􀀉to􀀉
keep􀀉pace,􀀉each􀀉number􀀉sharp􀀉and􀀉precise:􀀉“One􀀉—􀀉two􀀉—􀀉three􀀉—􀀉four􀀉
—􀀉one􀀉—􀀉two􀀉—􀀉three􀀉—􀀉four􀀉—”􀀉

Then􀀉Ben􀀉sneezed􀀉—􀀉a􀀉full-body􀀉convulsion􀀉that􀀉jerked􀀉his􀀉hand􀀉off􀀉the􀀉
lever.􀀉The􀀉horse􀀉stumbled,􀀉its􀀉front-left􀀉leg􀀉buckling􀀉mid-stride.􀀉Only􀀉a􀀉
thin􀀉layer􀀉of􀀉sand􀀉remained􀀉at􀀉the􀀉top􀀉—􀀉and􀀉falling􀀉fast.􀀉

“Ben’s􀀉out!”􀀉Camilla􀀉shouted.􀀉“Gage,􀀉cover􀀉—􀀉hold􀀉your􀀉blue􀀉through􀀉a􀀉
full􀀉cycle!”􀀉

Before􀀉she’d􀀉finished􀀉the􀀉sentence,􀀉Gage􀀉was􀀉already􀀉moving.􀀉He􀀉pressed􀀉
his􀀉blue􀀉button􀀉through􀀉one􀀉steady􀀉cycle􀀉—􀀉one,􀀉two,􀀉three,􀀉four􀀉—􀀉a􀀉
rhythm􀀉that􀀉held􀀉the􀀉horse’s􀀉weight􀀉on􀀉three􀀉legs􀀉while􀀉Ben􀀉wiped􀀉his􀀉face􀀉
and􀀉grabbed􀀉his􀀉lever􀀉again.􀀉

“Ben,􀀉back􀀉in􀀉on􀀉the􀀉next􀀉one,”􀀉Camilla􀀉called.􀀉“Three􀀉—􀀉four􀀉—􀀉now!”􀀉

Ben􀀉hit􀀉his􀀉button.􀀉The􀀉horse􀀉caught􀀉itself.􀀉Four􀀉legs􀀉again,􀀉four􀀉beats,􀀉four􀀉
people􀀉locked􀀉to􀀉Camilla’s􀀉count.􀀉The􀀉last􀀉of􀀉the􀀉sand􀀉kept􀀉falling.􀀉The􀀉far􀀉
bank􀀉rushed􀀉closer.􀀉

“Almost􀀉out,”􀀉Camilla􀀉breathed.􀀉“Hold􀀉the􀀉count.􀀉Nobody􀀉stops.􀀉One􀀉—􀀉
two􀀉—􀀉three􀀉—􀀉four􀀉—”􀀉

The􀀉horse’s􀀉last􀀉hoof􀀉touched􀀉ground􀀉on􀀉the􀀉far􀀉side􀀉as􀀉the􀀉last􀀉grain􀀉fell.􀀉

The􀀉image􀀉went􀀉green:􀀉“Round􀀉1􀀉Success.”􀀉

The􀀉restraints􀀉released.􀀉The􀀉bands􀀉retracted􀀉into􀀉the􀀉floor.􀀉For􀀉a􀀉long􀀉
moment,􀀉nobody􀀉moved.􀀉They􀀉stood􀀉in􀀉the􀀉green-lit􀀉room,􀀉shaking,􀀉


drenched􀀉in􀀉sweat,􀀉legs􀀉barely􀀉holding􀀉them.􀀉Nobody􀀉celebrated.􀀉Nobody􀀉
spoke.􀀉They􀀉were􀀉alive,􀀉and􀀉there􀀉were􀀉three􀀉doors􀀉left.􀀉

Then􀀉Camilla􀀉looked􀀉at􀀉Gage.􀀉Gage􀀉looked􀀉at􀀉Camilla.􀀉The􀀉Engine􀀉had􀀉
moved􀀉on􀀉the􀀉Eyes’􀀉count.􀀉For􀀉the􀀉first􀀉time.􀀉


Chapter􀀉23􀀉—􀀉The􀀉Blood􀀉Debt􀀉

They􀀉barely􀀉had􀀉time􀀉to􀀉breathe.􀀉The􀀉voice􀀉returned􀀉—􀀉the􀀉same􀀉flat,􀀉
mechanical􀀉tone􀀉as􀀉before:􀀉“Next􀀉challenge􀀉beginning.􀀉Proceed􀀉to􀀉the􀀉
second􀀉room.”􀀉

Otto􀀉snapped.􀀉“Enough􀀉of􀀉this! I’m􀀉not􀀉playing􀀉this􀀉game!”􀀉

Gage􀀉grabbed􀀉his􀀉arm.􀀉“You􀀉think􀀉we􀀉have􀀉a􀀉choice?”􀀉

Before􀀉the􀀉argument􀀉could􀀉go􀀉further,􀀉the􀀉room􀀉went􀀉dark.􀀉A􀀉hiss􀀉came􀀉
from􀀉somewhere􀀉in􀀉the􀀉walls,􀀉and􀀉a􀀉strange-smelling􀀉gas􀀉began􀀉filling􀀉the􀀉
space􀀉—􀀉not􀀉choking,􀀉but􀀉sharp􀀉enough􀀉to􀀉make􀀉their􀀉eyes􀀉water􀀉and􀀉their􀀉
lungs􀀉tighten.􀀉None􀀉of􀀉them􀀉knew􀀉what􀀉it􀀉was.􀀉Finding􀀉out􀀉was􀀉the􀀉only􀀉
way􀀉through.􀀉

They􀀉sprinted􀀉toward􀀉the􀀉second􀀉door:􀀉PLAN.􀀉It􀀉slid􀀉open􀀉as􀀉they􀀉
approached􀀉—􀀉no􀀉handles,􀀉no􀀉touch,􀀉the􀀉way􀀉the􀀉first􀀉door􀀉had.􀀉

The􀀉moment􀀉they􀀉were􀀉inside,􀀉it􀀉sealed􀀉behind􀀉them.􀀉The􀀉crystal􀀉from􀀉the􀀉
first􀀉room􀀉hung􀀉in􀀉the􀀉center􀀉again􀀉—􀀉but􀀉now􀀉one􀀉quarter􀀉of􀀉it􀀉glowed􀀉
green.􀀉Their􀀉first􀀉success,􀀉recorded􀀉and􀀉visible.􀀉

From􀀉its􀀉center,􀀉a􀀉fine􀀉beam􀀉shot􀀉over􀀉their􀀉heads􀀉to􀀉the􀀉wall􀀉behind􀀉them.􀀉
They􀀉turned.􀀉The􀀉wall􀀉lit􀀉up􀀉with􀀉rapidly􀀉scrolling􀀉lines􀀉of􀀉characters􀀉—􀀉
dizzying,􀀉impossible􀀉to􀀉read.􀀉Then􀀉the􀀉characters􀀉slowed,􀀉pulled􀀉together,􀀉
and􀀉formed􀀉the􀀉sign􀀉of􀀉the􀀉Trinity􀀉cities.􀀉The􀀉image􀀉shifted􀀉—􀀉a􀀉vast􀀉
wasteland􀀉that􀀉looked􀀉like􀀉Sector-1,􀀉dotted􀀉with􀀉mining􀀉locations.􀀉A􀀉
message􀀉appeared:􀀉“Do􀀉you􀀉have􀀉a􀀉plan?􀀉—􀀉Challenge􀀉2.”􀀉

Three􀀉glowing􀀉dots􀀉sat􀀉on􀀉the􀀉map􀀉—􀀉spaced􀀉apart,􀀉pulsing􀀉faintly.􀀉No􀀉
labels.􀀉No􀀉explanation.􀀉They􀀉stared􀀉at􀀉the􀀉wall,􀀉trying􀀉to􀀉make􀀉sense􀀉of􀀉
what􀀉they􀀉were􀀉looking􀀉at.􀀉


“What􀀉are􀀉those?”􀀉Otto􀀉asked.􀀉

Before􀀉anyone􀀉could􀀉answer,􀀉the􀀉voice􀀉cut􀀉in:􀀉“Select􀀉one.􀀉Fifty􀀉seconds.”􀀉

They􀀉looked􀀉at􀀉each􀀉other.􀀉Select􀀉one􀀉of􀀉what?􀀉For􀀉what?􀀉The􀀉dots􀀉gave􀀉
nothing􀀉away􀀉—􀀉just􀀉three􀀉points􀀉of􀀉light􀀉on􀀉Sector-1,􀀉a􀀉place􀀉they􀀉knew􀀉by􀀉
foot􀀉but􀀉had􀀉never􀀉seen􀀉from􀀉the􀀉sky.􀀉

Gage􀀉squinted,􀀉trying􀀉to􀀉match􀀉the􀀉map􀀉to􀀉what􀀉he􀀉knew􀀉of􀀉Sector-1.􀀉“If􀀉I􀀉
could􀀉just􀀉get􀀉closer􀀉—”􀀉

The􀀉voice:􀀉“Forty􀀉seconds.”􀀉

Ben􀀉didn’t􀀉wait.􀀉He􀀉stepped􀀉forward,􀀉pressed􀀉his􀀉trembling􀀉finger􀀉against􀀉
the􀀉nearest􀀉dot,􀀉and􀀉held􀀉his􀀉breath.􀀉The􀀉image􀀉zoomed􀀉in􀀉—􀀉and􀀉suddenly􀀉
the􀀉view􀀉made􀀉sense.􀀉Tunnels.􀀉Shafts.􀀉Support􀀉pillars.􀀉It􀀉was􀀉a􀀉mine,􀀉laid􀀉
out􀀉in􀀉fine􀀉detail􀀉from􀀉above.􀀉

Nothing􀀉exploded.􀀉Nothing􀀉shocked􀀉them.􀀉

Gage􀀉stepped􀀉forward,􀀉his􀀉eyes􀀉wide.􀀉“This􀀉is􀀉where􀀉we􀀉work.􀀉I􀀉know􀀉this􀀉
place􀀉like􀀉the􀀉back􀀉of􀀉my􀀉hand.”􀀉

“Also􀀉like􀀉the􀀉splinters􀀉under􀀉your􀀉bed,”􀀉Ben􀀉muttered,􀀉nodding􀀉at􀀉the􀀉
model􀀉Gage􀀉had􀀉spent􀀉months􀀉building􀀉from􀀉pit-prop􀀉scraps.􀀉

Gage􀀉shot􀀉him􀀉a􀀉look,􀀉but􀀉before􀀉they􀀉could􀀉say􀀉another􀀉word,􀀉the􀀉voice􀀉
spoke:􀀉“Challenge􀀉2.􀀉Two􀀉attempts􀀉allowed.􀀉Collapse􀀉the􀀉mine􀀉using􀀉three􀀉
explosive􀀉charges,􀀉each􀀉with􀀉a􀀉blast􀀉radius􀀉of􀀉one􀀉hundred􀀉meters.􀀉Lever􀀉
selects􀀉location.􀀉Red􀀉places􀀉the􀀉charge.􀀉Blue􀀉arms􀀉with􀀉a􀀉five-second􀀉timer.􀀉
Sixty􀀉seconds.􀀉Begin.”􀀉

Two􀀉lives.􀀉The􀀉last􀀉room􀀉had􀀉given􀀉them􀀉three.􀀉Nobody􀀉said􀀉it,􀀉but􀀉the􀀉
same􀀉thought􀀉crossed􀀉all􀀉four􀀉faces:􀀉this􀀉room􀀉was􀀉going􀀉to􀀉be􀀉worse.􀀉

Otto􀀉and􀀉Ben􀀉froze.􀀉They’d􀀉spent􀀉months􀀉hauling􀀉coal,􀀉but􀀉neither􀀉of􀀉
them􀀉had􀀉ever􀀉thought􀀉about􀀉the􀀉mine􀀉as􀀉a􀀉structure􀀉—􀀉which􀀉walls􀀉held􀀉


the􀀉weight,􀀉which􀀉pillars􀀉kept􀀉the􀀉ceiling􀀉from􀀉collapsing.􀀉They􀀉knew􀀉their􀀉
daily􀀉routes,􀀉their􀀉sections,􀀉their􀀉tonnage􀀉quotas.􀀉That􀀉was􀀉it.􀀉

Camilla􀀉didn’t􀀉even􀀉have􀀉that.􀀉She’d􀀉never􀀉set􀀉foot􀀉inside􀀉the􀀉mine.􀀉She􀀉was􀀉
staring􀀉at􀀉a􀀉map􀀉of􀀉a􀀉place􀀉she’d􀀉only􀀉ever􀀉heard􀀉described􀀉—􀀉by􀀉Gage􀀉at􀀉the􀀉
dinner􀀉table,􀀉by􀀉Pappy􀀉in􀀉old􀀉stories,􀀉by􀀉the􀀉coughing􀀉that􀀉came􀀉through􀀉
the􀀉shack􀀉walls􀀉at􀀉night.􀀉

But􀀉Gage􀀉was􀀉already􀀉scanning􀀉the􀀉map,􀀉his􀀉eyes􀀉tracing􀀉lines􀀉the􀀉rest􀀉of􀀉
them􀀉couldn’t􀀉read.􀀉Every􀀉hour􀀉he’d􀀉spent􀀉hunched􀀉over􀀉his􀀉splinter􀀉
model,􀀉every􀀉tunnel􀀉he’d􀀉memorized􀀉by􀀉touch,􀀉every􀀉pillar􀀉he’d􀀉mapped􀀉in􀀉
candlelight􀀉—􀀉it􀀉was􀀉all􀀉here,􀀉spread􀀉across􀀉the􀀉wall􀀉in􀀉front􀀉of􀀉him.􀀉

As􀀉the􀀉voice􀀉finished􀀉its􀀉instructions,􀀉the􀀉floor􀀉in􀀉front􀀉of􀀉the􀀉map􀀉wall􀀉
shifted.􀀉A􀀉single􀀉long􀀉panel􀀉rose􀀉from􀀉the􀀉ground􀀉—􀀉not􀀉four􀀉separate􀀉
stations􀀉like􀀉the􀀉last􀀉room,􀀉but􀀉one􀀉shared􀀉console.􀀉In􀀉the􀀉center,􀀉a􀀉lever􀀉sat􀀉
in􀀉a􀀉track􀀉that􀀉ran􀀉the􀀉width􀀉of􀀉the􀀉panel,􀀉clearly􀀉designed􀀉to􀀉slide􀀉a􀀉
crosshair􀀉across􀀉the􀀉map.􀀉To􀀉the􀀉left,􀀉a􀀉red􀀉button􀀉glowed􀀉faintly.􀀉To􀀉the􀀉
right,􀀉a􀀉blue􀀉one.􀀉Behind􀀉the􀀉panel,􀀉a􀀉metal􀀉railing􀀉ran􀀉along􀀉the􀀉back􀀉wall􀀉

—􀀉nothing􀀉else.􀀉Three􀀉controls.􀀉Four􀀉subjects.􀀉The􀀉asymmetry􀀉was􀀉the􀀉test.􀀉
Before􀀉anyone􀀉could􀀉make􀀉sense􀀉of􀀉it,􀀉a􀀉whirring􀀉sound􀀉filled􀀉the􀀉room.􀀉
Plate-sized􀀉machines􀀉with􀀉blinking􀀉lights􀀉descended􀀉from􀀉the􀀉ceiling􀀉and􀀉
began􀀉circling􀀉above􀀉their􀀉heads.􀀉After􀀉what􀀉happened􀀉in􀀉the􀀉last􀀉room,􀀉
nobody􀀉needed􀀉to􀀉be􀀉told􀀉what􀀉those􀀉machines􀀉could􀀉do.􀀉And􀀉the􀀉drones􀀉
looked􀀉far􀀉worse􀀉than􀀉the􀀉shock􀀉had􀀉been.􀀉

Gage􀀉stepped􀀉in􀀉front􀀉of􀀉the􀀉lever.􀀉“I’ve􀀉studied􀀉this􀀉mine􀀉a􀀉thousand􀀉
times.􀀉I’ve􀀉built􀀉every􀀉tunnel􀀉and􀀉every􀀉pillar􀀉from􀀉memory.􀀉I􀀉know􀀉where􀀉
it’s􀀉strong􀀉and􀀉where􀀉it’s􀀉weak.”􀀉

He􀀉looked􀀉at􀀉Camilla.􀀉She􀀉gave􀀉him􀀉a􀀉single􀀉nod.􀀉She’d􀀉taken􀀉the􀀉last􀀉room􀀉
because􀀉his􀀉nerves􀀉had􀀉got􀀉the􀀉better􀀉of􀀉him.􀀉This􀀉was􀀉his.􀀉


A􀀉light􀀉snapped􀀉on􀀉in􀀉the􀀉corner􀀉—􀀉another􀀉hourglass,􀀉sand􀀉already􀀉falling􀀉
fast.􀀉

Gage􀀉didn’t􀀉waste􀀉time􀀉explaining􀀉why.􀀉“I’m􀀉on􀀉the􀀉lever􀀉—􀀉I􀀉pick􀀉the􀀉
targets.􀀉Ben,􀀉you’re􀀉left,􀀉red􀀉button􀀉—􀀉you􀀉place􀀉the􀀉charge.􀀉Otto,􀀉right,􀀉
blue􀀉button􀀉—􀀉you􀀉arm􀀉it.􀀉Cam,􀀉you􀀉stand􀀉at􀀉the􀀉railing􀀉and􀀉give􀀉us􀀉a􀀉
steady􀀉count,􀀉one􀀉through􀀉five,􀀉one-second􀀉intervals.􀀉You’re􀀉our􀀉heartbeat.􀀉
Nobody􀀉moves􀀉until􀀉I􀀉call􀀉your􀀉name.􀀉Go.”􀀉

Ben􀀉took􀀉the􀀉left􀀉side􀀉of􀀉the􀀉panel,􀀉hand􀀉hovering􀀉over􀀉the􀀉red􀀉button.􀀉Otto􀀉
moved􀀉right,􀀉fingers􀀉curling􀀉around􀀉the􀀉edge􀀉near􀀉the􀀉blue.􀀉Camilla􀀉gripped􀀉
the􀀉railing􀀉behind􀀉them,􀀉steadying􀀉herself.􀀉From􀀉the􀀉railing,􀀉she􀀉could􀀉see􀀉
the􀀉hourglass􀀉—􀀉the􀀉first􀀉band􀀉of􀀉sand􀀉already􀀉cleared􀀉from􀀉the􀀉top.􀀉

Gage􀀉started􀀉barking􀀉directions􀀉—􀀉north-east􀀉wall􀀉first,􀀉the􀀉load-bearing􀀉
pillar.􀀉“Ben􀀉—􀀉plant!”􀀉Ben􀀉hit􀀉red.􀀉The􀀉crosshair􀀉was􀀉nowhere􀀉near􀀉the􀀉
target.􀀉Gage􀀉yanked􀀉the􀀉lever,􀀉overshooting,􀀉dragged􀀉it􀀉back.􀀉“There􀀉—􀀉no,􀀉
wait􀀉—”􀀉He􀀉found􀀉the􀀉spot.􀀉“Otto􀀉—􀀉arm!”􀀉Otto􀀉hit􀀉blue.􀀉Camilla􀀉started􀀉
counting:􀀉“Five􀀉—􀀉four􀀉—”􀀉

But􀀉the􀀉timing􀀉was􀀉wrong.􀀉Gage􀀉was􀀉still􀀉searching􀀉for􀀉the􀀉second􀀉target􀀉
when􀀉the􀀉first􀀉charge􀀉detonated,􀀉fire􀀉and􀀉dust􀀉erupting􀀉across􀀉the􀀉wall.􀀉The􀀉
voice:􀀉“Structural􀀉integrity􀀉—􀀉ninety-one􀀉percent.”􀀉Barely􀀉a􀀉dent.􀀉He􀀉
shoved􀀉the􀀉lever􀀉south,􀀉called􀀉for􀀉the􀀉second􀀉charge,􀀉but􀀉Ben􀀉hit􀀉red􀀉before􀀉
Gage􀀉had􀀉the􀀉crosshair􀀉placed.􀀉The􀀉charge􀀉landed􀀉in􀀉an􀀉empty􀀉tunnel.􀀉
Wasted.􀀉

Camilla’s􀀉count􀀉was􀀉steady􀀉but􀀉the􀀉others􀀉couldn’t􀀉keep􀀉up􀀉—􀀉Otto􀀉armed􀀉
too􀀉early,􀀉Gage􀀉was􀀉still􀀉repositioning,􀀉Ben􀀉was􀀉hitting􀀉buttons􀀉on􀀉instinct􀀉
instead􀀉of􀀉command.􀀉The􀀉third􀀉charge􀀉went􀀉off􀀉in􀀉the􀀉wrong􀀉section􀀉
entirely.􀀉

The􀀉last􀀉grain􀀉fell􀀉through􀀉the􀀉neck.􀀉The􀀉voice:􀀉“Structural􀀉integrity􀀉—􀀉
seventy-eight􀀉percent.”􀀉


The􀀉voice:􀀉“Challenge􀀉2,􀀉attempt􀀉1􀀉failure.􀀉One􀀉attempt􀀉remains.”􀀉

They’d􀀉tried.􀀉They’d􀀉just􀀉been􀀉too􀀉slow,􀀉too􀀉scattered,􀀉and􀀉too􀀉unfamiliar􀀉
with􀀉working􀀉as􀀉a􀀉unit􀀉on􀀉Gage’s􀀉terms􀀉instead􀀉of􀀉Camilla’s.􀀉

For􀀉a􀀉moment,􀀉nothing􀀉happened.􀀉The􀀉voice􀀉had􀀉gone􀀉quiet.􀀉The􀀉
hourglass􀀉sat􀀉empty􀀉in􀀉its􀀉frame.􀀉They􀀉breathed.􀀉They􀀉waited.􀀉None􀀉of􀀉
them􀀉looked􀀉up􀀉at􀀉the􀀉drones􀀉overhead.􀀉The􀀉drones􀀉were􀀉looking􀀉at􀀉them.􀀉

The􀀉drones􀀉moved.􀀉A􀀉high-pitched􀀉whine􀀉filled􀀉the􀀉room,􀀉rising􀀉in􀀉pitch􀀉
until􀀉the􀀉air􀀉itself􀀉seemed􀀉to􀀉vibrate.􀀉Gage􀀉saw􀀉a􀀉red􀀉laser􀀉slash􀀉overhead􀀉and􀀉
screamed􀀉“Down! Get􀀉behind􀀉the􀀉panel!”􀀉

Ben􀀉and􀀉Otto􀀉threw􀀉themselves􀀉behind􀀉the􀀉console􀀉—􀀉red􀀉laser􀀉beams􀀉
sliced􀀉into􀀉the􀀉metal􀀉housing􀀉above􀀉them,􀀉leaving􀀉smoking􀀉gashes􀀉and􀀉
rivulets􀀉of􀀉molten􀀉metal􀀉running􀀉down􀀉its􀀉sides.􀀉The􀀉console􀀉took􀀉the􀀉cuts􀀉
that􀀉should􀀉have􀀉taken􀀉them.􀀉Gage􀀉threw􀀉himself􀀉down􀀉between􀀉them,􀀉
taking􀀉the􀀉same􀀉cover.􀀉

Camilla􀀉wasn’t􀀉at􀀉the􀀉panel.􀀉She􀀉was􀀉at􀀉the􀀉railing,􀀉exposed,􀀉with􀀉nothing􀀉
between􀀉her􀀉and􀀉the􀀉machines.􀀉She􀀉dove,􀀉but􀀉the􀀉drone􀀉was􀀉already􀀉locked􀀉
on.􀀉A􀀉red􀀉laser􀀉beam􀀉cut􀀉through􀀉the􀀉smoke􀀉—􀀉thin,􀀉precise,􀀉and􀀉utterly􀀉
silent.􀀉

Camilla􀀉didn’t􀀉cry􀀉out􀀉at􀀉first.􀀉The􀀉laser􀀉had􀀉sealed􀀉her􀀉nerves􀀉where􀀉it􀀉cut.􀀉
The􀀉pain􀀉hadn’t􀀉reached􀀉her􀀉yet.􀀉She􀀉was􀀉on􀀉the􀀉ground,􀀉staring􀀉at􀀉the􀀉
ceiling,􀀉wondering􀀉why􀀉the􀀉room􀀉felt􀀉tilted􀀉—􀀉wondering􀀉why􀀉one􀀉side􀀉of􀀉
her􀀉body􀀉had􀀉gone􀀉strangely􀀉weightless,􀀉as􀀉if􀀉some􀀉piece􀀉of􀀉her􀀉had􀀉stopped􀀉
being􀀉there.􀀉Then􀀉she􀀉turned􀀉her􀀉head􀀉and􀀉saw􀀉her􀀉own􀀉arm􀀉lying􀀉on􀀉the􀀉
floor􀀉beside􀀉her􀀉—􀀉severed,􀀉motionless,􀀉no􀀉longer􀀉part􀀉of􀀉her􀀉body.􀀉

The􀀉scream􀀉that􀀉tore􀀉out􀀉of􀀉her􀀉was􀀉not􀀉the􀀉sound􀀉of􀀉a􀀉girl.􀀉It􀀉was􀀉raw,􀀉
animal,􀀉pulled􀀉from􀀉somewhere􀀉below􀀉language􀀉—􀀉the􀀉sound􀀉of􀀉a􀀉body􀀉
that􀀉understood􀀉what􀀉had􀀉happened􀀉before􀀉the􀀉mind􀀉could􀀉catch􀀉up.􀀉It􀀉
echoed􀀉off􀀉the􀀉steel􀀉walls􀀉and􀀉came􀀉back􀀉to􀀉her􀀉like􀀉something􀀉alive.􀀉


Then􀀉silence.􀀉Her􀀉own􀀉breathing,􀀉fast􀀉and􀀉shallow.􀀉The􀀉hum􀀉of􀀉the􀀉drones􀀉
overhead,􀀉already􀀉settling􀀉back􀀉into􀀉their􀀉patrol􀀉pattern􀀉—􀀉as􀀉if􀀉whatever􀀉
had􀀉been􀀉controlling􀀉them􀀉had􀀉seen􀀉enough.􀀉The􀀉smell􀀉hit􀀉her􀀉next􀀉—􀀉hot􀀉
metal􀀉from􀀉the􀀉cuts􀀉in􀀉the􀀉console,􀀉and􀀉something􀀉organic􀀉—􀀉something􀀉
that􀀉shouldn’t􀀉be􀀉exposed􀀉to􀀉air.􀀉

She􀀉didn’t􀀉look􀀉at􀀉her􀀉arm􀀉again.􀀉She􀀉couldn’t􀀉afford􀀉to.􀀉If􀀉she􀀉looked,􀀉the􀀉
part􀀉of􀀉her􀀉brain􀀉that􀀉was􀀉still􀀉functioning􀀉would􀀉shut􀀉down,􀀉and􀀉then􀀉
she’d􀀉be􀀉just􀀉a􀀉body􀀉bleeding􀀉out􀀉on􀀉a􀀉steel􀀉floor􀀉while􀀉a􀀉machine􀀉decided􀀉
she’d􀀉failed.􀀉

Instead,􀀉she􀀉moved.􀀉Her􀀉remaining􀀉hand􀀉found􀀉the􀀉edge􀀉of􀀉the􀀉suit􀀉where􀀉
the􀀉laser􀀉had􀀉cut􀀉—􀀉the􀀉fabric􀀉was􀀉melted􀀉and􀀉ragged􀀉at􀀉her􀀉shoulder,􀀉
already􀀉separating􀀉due􀀉to􀀉the􀀉heat.􀀉She􀀉grabbed􀀉the􀀉loose􀀉edge􀀉and􀀉pulled.􀀉
A􀀉long􀀉strip􀀉tore􀀉free􀀉from􀀉around􀀉the􀀉wound.􀀉She􀀉looped􀀉it􀀉around􀀉the􀀉
shoulder,􀀉but􀀉she􀀉couldn’t􀀉pull􀀉it􀀉tight􀀉with􀀉one􀀉hand.􀀉So􀀉she􀀉bit􀀉down􀀉on􀀉
the􀀉fabric,􀀉clamped􀀉her􀀉teeth􀀉until􀀉her􀀉jaw􀀉screamed,􀀉and􀀉pulled􀀉with􀀉her􀀉
neck􀀉and􀀉her􀀉one􀀉good􀀉arm􀀉together.􀀉The􀀉strip􀀉cinched.􀀉The􀀉muscles􀀉in􀀉her􀀉
jaw􀀉stood􀀉out􀀉like􀀉cables.􀀉Something􀀉warm􀀉and􀀉wet􀀉ran􀀉down􀀉her􀀉side,􀀉but􀀉
slower􀀉now.􀀉Slower􀀉was􀀉enough.􀀉

She􀀉got􀀉her􀀉knees􀀉under􀀉her.􀀉Then􀀉one􀀉foot.􀀉Then􀀉the􀀉other.􀀉The􀀉room􀀉
swayed.􀀉The􀀉floor􀀉tilted.􀀉She􀀉locked􀀉her􀀉legs􀀉and􀀉waited􀀉for􀀉the􀀉world􀀉to􀀉
stop􀀉moving.􀀉

By􀀉the􀀉time􀀉the􀀉boys􀀉reached􀀉her,􀀉she􀀉was􀀉standing.􀀉Shaking,􀀉grey-faced,􀀉
soaked􀀉in􀀉her􀀉own􀀉blood􀀉—􀀉but􀀉standing.􀀉They􀀉reached􀀉her􀀉with􀀉hands􀀉
ready􀀉to􀀉staunch,􀀉to􀀉lift,􀀉to􀀉do􀀉what􀀉needed􀀉doing.􀀉They􀀉stopped􀀉short.􀀉
Camilla􀀉had􀀉not􀀉waited􀀉to􀀉be􀀉saved.􀀉

“Lost􀀉an􀀉arm,􀀉not􀀉the􀀉rest􀀉of􀀉me.􀀉Get􀀉back􀀉to􀀉the􀀉panel,”􀀉she􀀉said.􀀉Her􀀉voice􀀉
was􀀉barely􀀉there􀀉—􀀉thin,􀀉cracked,􀀉coming􀀉from􀀉somewhere􀀉far􀀉away.􀀉But􀀉
her􀀉eyes􀀉were􀀉steady.􀀉Burning.􀀉Fixed􀀉on􀀉a􀀉place􀀉past􀀉the􀀉pain,􀀉past􀀉the􀀉room,􀀉
past􀀉the􀀉drones􀀉still􀀉circling􀀉overhead.􀀉


Whatever􀀉had􀀉been􀀉in􀀉Camilla􀀉before􀀉this􀀉room􀀉—􀀉the􀀉engineer,􀀉the􀀉sister,􀀉
the􀀉girl􀀉who􀀉memorized􀀉Pappy’s􀀉books􀀉by􀀉candlelight􀀉—􀀉was􀀉still􀀉there.􀀉But􀀉
someone􀀉new􀀉had􀀉walked􀀉out􀀉of􀀉those􀀉ten􀀉seconds􀀉between􀀉the􀀉laser􀀉and􀀉
the􀀉tourniquet.􀀉Someone􀀉who􀀉would􀀉never􀀉leave.􀀉

The􀀉voice􀀉returned:􀀉“Next􀀉attempt􀀉in􀀉sixty􀀉seconds.”􀀉

Sixty􀀉seconds.􀀉Camilla􀀉was􀀉bleeding􀀉out.􀀉The􀀉drones􀀉were􀀉still􀀉circling.􀀉
And􀀉they􀀉had􀀉one􀀉attempt􀀉left.􀀉

Gage􀀉pulled􀀉himself􀀉together.􀀉He􀀉looked􀀉at􀀉the􀀉others.􀀉“Stations.􀀉Now.􀀉Just􀀉
follow􀀉my􀀉voice.􀀉Ben,􀀉red􀀉when􀀉I􀀉say􀀉plant.􀀉Otto,􀀉blue􀀉when􀀉I􀀉say􀀉arm.􀀉
Cam􀀉—”􀀉He􀀉stopped.􀀉She􀀉was􀀉gripping􀀉the􀀉railing􀀉with􀀉her􀀉one􀀉hand,􀀉
swaying,􀀉her􀀉face􀀉the􀀉color􀀉of􀀉ash.􀀉

“I􀀉can􀀉count,”􀀉she􀀉said.􀀉“Start􀀉the􀀉round.”􀀉

From􀀉the􀀉corner:􀀉click.􀀉Clack.􀀉The􀀉hourglass􀀉turned􀀉end􀀉over􀀉end.􀀉Fresh􀀉
sand􀀉at􀀉the􀀉top,􀀉and􀀉falling.􀀉

Gage􀀉didn’t􀀉look􀀉at􀀉the􀀉hourglass.􀀉He􀀉looked􀀉at􀀉the􀀉map.􀀉The􀀉first􀀉attempt􀀉
had􀀉been􀀉chaos􀀉—􀀉but􀀉it􀀉hadn’t􀀉been􀀉useless.􀀉He’d􀀉learned􀀉the􀀉lever.􀀉He􀀉
knew􀀉now􀀉how􀀉far􀀉to􀀉push􀀉it,􀀉how􀀉fast􀀉the􀀉crosshair􀀉moved,􀀉how􀀉much􀀉
lead􀀉time􀀉he􀀉needed􀀉before􀀉calling􀀉the􀀉command.􀀉He’d􀀉seen􀀉the􀀉north-east􀀉
wall􀀉take􀀉the􀀉first􀀉charge􀀉and􀀉drop􀀉the􀀉structure􀀉to􀀉ninety-one􀀉percent􀀉—􀀉
the􀀉right􀀉target,􀀉just􀀉badly􀀉timed.􀀉The􀀉second􀀉and􀀉third􀀉charges􀀉had􀀉been􀀉
wasted􀀉because􀀉he􀀉hadn’t􀀉yet􀀉known􀀉the􀀉price􀀉of􀀉his􀀉own􀀉thinking.􀀉Ben􀀉and􀀉
Otto􀀉had􀀉fired􀀉when􀀉he􀀉called􀀉—􀀉but􀀉his􀀉calls􀀉had􀀉been􀀉a􀀉beat􀀉late.􀀉

That􀀉wouldn’t􀀉happen􀀉again.􀀉Not􀀉because􀀉of􀀉anything􀀉he􀀉could􀀉say.􀀉
Because􀀉Camilla’s􀀉arm􀀉was􀀉on􀀉the􀀉floor􀀉behind􀀉them,􀀉and􀀉there􀀉was􀀉no􀀉
room􀀉left􀀉in􀀉anyone’s􀀉body􀀉for􀀉panic􀀉or􀀉hesitation.􀀉

He􀀉pre-set􀀉the􀀉lever,􀀉sliding􀀉the􀀉crosshair􀀉to􀀉the􀀉north-east􀀉wall􀀉before􀀉the􀀉
round􀀉began.􀀉This􀀉time,􀀉the􀀉target􀀉would􀀉be􀀉waiting.􀀉


The􀀉voice:􀀉“Attempt􀀉two.􀀉Begin.”􀀉

“Ben􀀉—􀀉plant!”􀀉Ben’s􀀉hand􀀉hit􀀉the􀀉red􀀉button.􀀉The􀀉crosshair􀀉was􀀉already􀀉
there.􀀉No􀀉overshoot.􀀉No􀀉adjustment.􀀉Clean.􀀉
“Otto􀀉—􀀉arm!”􀀉Otto􀀉hit􀀉blue.􀀉Camilla’s􀀉voice􀀉cut􀀉through􀀉the􀀉room:􀀉“Five􀀉

—􀀉four􀀉—”􀀉
She􀀉wasn’t􀀉just􀀉counting.􀀉She􀀉was􀀉the􀀉heartbeat.􀀉Her􀀉voice􀀉held􀀉the􀀉rhythm􀀉
while􀀉everything􀀉else􀀉shook􀀉apart􀀉—􀀉the􀀉thunder􀀉from􀀉the􀀉speakers,􀀉the􀀉
whine􀀉of􀀉the􀀉drones,􀀉the􀀉fire􀀉in􀀉what􀀉was􀀉left􀀉of􀀉her􀀉shoulder.􀀉Every􀀉
number􀀉cost􀀉her􀀉something,􀀉and􀀉she􀀉paid􀀉it.􀀉
“Three􀀉—􀀉two􀀉—􀀉one􀀉—”􀀉
The􀀉first􀀉explosion􀀉bloomed􀀉on􀀉the􀀉wall􀀉—􀀉thunder􀀉through􀀉the􀀉speakers.􀀉

The􀀉voice:􀀉“Structural􀀉integrity􀀉—􀀉seventy-two􀀉percent.”􀀉
“Ben􀀉—􀀉plant!”􀀉Gage􀀉shoved􀀉the􀀉lever􀀉south.􀀉Ben􀀉hit􀀉red.􀀉
“Otto􀀉—􀀉arm!”􀀉Otto􀀉hit􀀉blue.􀀉
Camilla:􀀉“Five􀀉—􀀉four􀀉—􀀉three􀀉—􀀉two􀀉—􀀉one􀀉—”􀀉
Second􀀉explosion.􀀉Voice:􀀉“Structural􀀉integrity􀀉—􀀉fifty-seven􀀉percent.”􀀉
Ben’s􀀉voice,􀀉small􀀉and􀀉terrified:􀀉“That’s􀀉not􀀉enough􀀉—”􀀉
“Watch,”􀀉Gage􀀉said.􀀉He􀀉pushed􀀉the􀀉lever􀀉to􀀉the􀀉final􀀉position􀀉—􀀉the􀀉

junction􀀉where􀀉the􀀉south􀀉wall􀀉met􀀉the􀀉deep􀀉shaft,􀀉the􀀉point􀀉he’d􀀉stared􀀉at􀀉
for􀀉months􀀉in􀀉his􀀉model,􀀉the􀀉place􀀉where􀀉one􀀉well-placed􀀉charge􀀉would􀀉
send􀀉a􀀉cascade􀀉through􀀉every􀀉remaining􀀉pillar.􀀉

“Ben􀀉—􀀉plant!”􀀉Red.􀀉
“Otto􀀉—􀀉arm!”􀀉Blue.􀀉
Camilla:􀀉“Five􀀉—􀀉four􀀉—”􀀉


Gage’s􀀉eyes􀀉flicked􀀉to􀀉the􀀉corner.􀀉The􀀉hourglass􀀉had􀀉thinned􀀉to􀀉a􀀉ribbon􀀉—􀀉
sand􀀉racing􀀉through􀀉the􀀉neck.􀀉
Camilla:􀀉“Three􀀉—􀀉two􀀉—􀀉one􀀉—”􀀉

Gage:􀀉“Boom.”􀀉
The􀀉final􀀉explosion.􀀉The􀀉map􀀉filled􀀉with􀀉spreading􀀉blue􀀉dots􀀉—􀀉fractures􀀉
racing􀀉through􀀉the􀀉mine’s􀀉skeleton,􀀉pillar􀀉after􀀉pillar􀀉giving􀀉way.􀀉

Voice:􀀉“Structural􀀉integrity􀀉—􀀉thirty-two􀀉percent.”􀀉
Voice:􀀉“Twenty-one􀀉percent.”􀀉
Voice:􀀉“Ten􀀉percent.”􀀉
Ben,􀀉Otto,􀀉and􀀉Camilla􀀉stared􀀉at􀀉Gage.􀀉
Voice:􀀉“Four􀀉percent.”􀀉
Voice:􀀉“Zero􀀉percent.􀀉Mine􀀉collapsed.􀀉Challenge􀀉2􀀉—􀀉success.”􀀉
Gage􀀉didn’t􀀉celebrate.􀀉He􀀉was􀀉already􀀉running.􀀉Camilla’s􀀉legs􀀉had􀀉given􀀉out􀀉

mid-count􀀉—􀀉she􀀉was􀀉on􀀉the􀀉floor,􀀉her􀀉face􀀉grey,􀀉her􀀉breathing􀀉shallow.􀀉

The􀀉makeshift􀀉tourniquet􀀉had􀀉soaked􀀉through.􀀉
Gage􀀉knelt􀀉beside􀀉her.􀀉He􀀉had􀀉her􀀉in􀀉his􀀉arms,􀀉and􀀉nothing􀀉else􀀉—􀀉no􀀉
medicine,􀀉no􀀉doctor,􀀉no􀀉clean􀀉cloth.􀀉He􀀉had􀀉spent􀀉his􀀉whole􀀉life􀀉learning􀀉to􀀉
break􀀉rock.􀀉He􀀉had􀀉never􀀉been􀀉taught􀀉how􀀉to􀀉save􀀉anyone.􀀉

A􀀉soft􀀉click􀀉behind􀀉him.􀀉He􀀉turned.􀀉A􀀉small􀀉compartment􀀉had􀀉opened􀀉in􀀉
the􀀉wall􀀉beside􀀉the􀀉door􀀉—􀀉not􀀉waiting􀀉there􀀉since􀀉the􀀉start􀀉the􀀉way􀀉the􀀉
paper􀀉and􀀉pencils􀀉in􀀉Room􀀉1􀀉had􀀉been.􀀉This􀀉had􀀉opened􀀉just􀀉now,􀀉in􀀉
response.􀀉Inside􀀉sat􀀉a􀀉metal􀀉box.􀀉

Gage􀀉grabbed􀀉the􀀉box.􀀉Inside􀀉was􀀉a􀀉can􀀉with􀀉pictures􀀉running􀀉down􀀉its􀀉side􀀉

—􀀉how􀀉to􀀉hold􀀉it,􀀉where􀀉to􀀉press,􀀉what􀀉it􀀉would􀀉do.􀀉He􀀉didn’t􀀉need􀀉words􀀉

to􀀉understand.􀀉He􀀉pressed􀀉the􀀉top􀀉with􀀉his􀀉thumb.􀀉Something􀀉cold􀀉hit􀀉his􀀉
palm.􀀉He􀀉pointed􀀉the􀀉can􀀉at􀀉Camilla’s􀀉shoulder􀀉and􀀉pressed􀀉again.􀀉

A􀀉blue􀀉gel􀀉hit􀀉the􀀉wound􀀉and􀀉spread􀀉across􀀉the􀀉raw􀀉flesh.􀀉Within􀀉seconds,􀀉it􀀉
thickened􀀉and􀀉hardened􀀉—􀀉a􀀉rubbery􀀉seal􀀉that􀀉stopped􀀉the􀀉bleeding􀀉cold.􀀉
Camilla’s􀀉breathing􀀉steadied,􀀉but􀀉she􀀉didn’t􀀉open􀀉her􀀉eyes.􀀉

Gage􀀉looked􀀉back􀀉at􀀉the􀀉open􀀉compartment.􀀉The􀀉facility􀀉had􀀉taken􀀉
Camilla’s􀀉arm􀀉and􀀉then,􀀉watching􀀉him􀀉hold􀀉her􀀉with􀀉nothing􀀉in􀀉his􀀉hands,􀀉
had􀀉provided􀀉the􀀉tools􀀉to􀀉keep􀀉her􀀉alive.􀀉It􀀉wasn’t􀀉mercy.􀀉Whoever􀀉was􀀉
watching􀀉from􀀉the􀀉other􀀉side􀀉of􀀉these􀀉walls􀀉had􀀉seen􀀉what􀀉Camilla􀀉just􀀉did􀀉

—􀀉counted􀀉steady􀀉fives􀀉while􀀉bleeding􀀉out,􀀉held􀀉the􀀉rhythm􀀉while􀀉her􀀉body􀀉
failed􀀉—􀀉and􀀉decided􀀉she􀀉was􀀉worth􀀉repairing.􀀉
Ben􀀉and􀀉Otto􀀉lifted􀀉her􀀉between􀀉them.􀀉The􀀉second􀀉room􀀉went􀀉dark.􀀉The􀀉
door􀀉to􀀉the􀀉third􀀉room􀀉—􀀉ADAPT􀀉—􀀉stood􀀉open,􀀉an􀀉eerie􀀉green􀀉glow􀀉
spilling􀀉into􀀉the􀀉corridor.􀀉

They􀀉carried􀀉Camilla􀀉through􀀉it.􀀉CONTROL􀀉had􀀉cost􀀉them􀀉their􀀉footing.􀀉
PLAN􀀉had􀀉cost􀀉them􀀉her􀀉arm.􀀉Two􀀉doors􀀉waited.􀀉They􀀉had􀀉stopped􀀉
pretending􀀉the􀀉rooms􀀉would􀀉settle􀀉for􀀉less.􀀉


Chapter􀀉24􀀉—􀀉The􀀉Green􀀉Room􀀉

The􀀉spark􀀉was􀀉gone.􀀉The􀀉fight􀀉wasn’t.􀀉A􀀉minute􀀉ago􀀉they’d􀀉been􀀉fighting􀀉
for􀀉their􀀉lives􀀉against􀀉a􀀉clock􀀉and􀀉a􀀉map.􀀉Now􀀉Ben􀀉and􀀉Otto􀀉were􀀉carrying􀀉
an􀀉unconscious􀀉girl􀀉with􀀉one􀀉arm􀀉through􀀉a􀀉doorway􀀉into􀀉a􀀉room􀀉that􀀉
glowed􀀉green,􀀉and􀀉whatever􀀉belief􀀉they’d􀀉had􀀉that􀀉they􀀉would􀀉walk􀀉out􀀉of􀀉
this􀀉whole􀀉had􀀉been􀀉left􀀉behind􀀉in􀀉a􀀉room􀀉called􀀉PLAN􀀉—􀀉where􀀉nothing􀀉
had􀀉gone􀀉according􀀉to􀀉plan.􀀉

The􀀉green􀀉light􀀉wrapped􀀉around􀀉her􀀉as􀀉they􀀉carried􀀉her􀀉in.􀀉It􀀉pooled􀀉in􀀉her􀀉
hair,􀀉on􀀉her􀀉face,􀀉on􀀉every􀀉surface􀀉it􀀉could􀀉find.􀀉The􀀉corridor􀀉had􀀉been􀀉cold􀀉
and􀀉dry.􀀉This􀀉was􀀉not.􀀉The􀀉air􀀉here􀀉was􀀉warm􀀉and􀀉close,􀀉and􀀉it􀀉touched􀀉
their􀀉skin􀀉as􀀉if􀀉it􀀉had􀀉been􀀉waiting􀀉for􀀉them.􀀉

Gage􀀉took􀀉Camilla􀀉from􀀉them􀀉and􀀉lowered􀀉her􀀉against􀀉the􀀉wall.􀀉Her􀀉head􀀉
lolled􀀉sideways,􀀉the􀀉blue􀀉gel􀀉on􀀉her􀀉wound􀀉catching􀀉the􀀉light.􀀉He􀀉looked􀀉
down􀀉at􀀉her􀀉face􀀉—􀀉pale,􀀉slack,􀀉barely􀀉breathing􀀉—􀀉and􀀉felt􀀉something􀀉
crack􀀉open􀀉inside􀀉him􀀉that􀀉had􀀉nothing􀀉to􀀉do􀀉with􀀉the􀀉facility.􀀉His􀀉
childhood􀀉flashed􀀉through􀀉his􀀉mind,􀀉but􀀉not􀀉the􀀉parts􀀉where􀀉he􀀉was􀀉the􀀉
center􀀉of􀀉the􀀉world.􀀉The􀀉parts􀀉where􀀉she􀀉was􀀉standing􀀉behind􀀉him,􀀉holding􀀉
a􀀉tool􀀉he’d􀀉asked􀀉for,􀀉fixing􀀉a􀀉thing􀀉he’d􀀉broken,􀀉memorizing􀀉a􀀉book􀀉he’d􀀉
ignored􀀉—􀀉and􀀉he’d􀀉never􀀉once􀀉turned􀀉around􀀉to􀀉see􀀉her.􀀉She􀀉had􀀉never􀀉
taken􀀉the􀀉time􀀉to􀀉be􀀉a􀀉kid.􀀉

Ben􀀉touched􀀉his􀀉arm.􀀉“She’ll􀀉recover,􀀉Gage.􀀉Let’s􀀉focus􀀉on􀀉getting􀀉out􀀉
alive.”􀀉

Gage􀀉nodded.􀀉He􀀉straightened􀀉up􀀉and􀀉looked􀀉around.􀀉

This􀀉room􀀉was􀀉different.􀀉The􀀉walls􀀉weren’t􀀉steel􀀉—􀀉they􀀉were􀀉coated􀀉in􀀉
something􀀉translucent􀀉and􀀉wet,􀀉a􀀉thick􀀉membrane􀀉that􀀉pulsed􀀉with􀀉a􀀉slow,􀀉
rhythmic􀀉beat.􀀉Only􀀉the􀀉floor􀀉was􀀉still􀀉bare,􀀉cold􀀉metal􀀉under􀀉their􀀉boots.􀀉
Green􀀉light􀀉came􀀉from􀀉inside􀀉the􀀉walls􀀉themselves,􀀉not􀀉from􀀉panels􀀉above.􀀉


So􀀉did􀀉the􀀉warmth.􀀉It􀀉felt􀀉less􀀉like􀀉a􀀉room􀀉and􀀉more􀀉like􀀉the􀀉inside􀀉of􀀉
something􀀉alive.􀀉

The􀀉crystal􀀉shape􀀉rose􀀉from􀀉the􀀉floor.􀀉Two􀀉of􀀉its􀀉four􀀉sections􀀉glowed􀀉green􀀉

—􀀉two􀀉rooms􀀉down.􀀉The􀀉remaining􀀉two􀀉pulsed􀀉cold􀀉blue.􀀉Two􀀉to􀀉go.􀀉
The􀀉voice:􀀉“Challenge􀀉3.􀀉One􀀉attempt􀀉allowed.􀀉Adapt􀀉to􀀉survive.”􀀉

No􀀉controls.􀀉No􀀉panels.􀀉No􀀉lever􀀉or􀀉buttons.􀀉Just􀀉the􀀉pulsing􀀉green􀀉walls􀀉
and􀀉a􀀉command􀀉that􀀉told􀀉them􀀉nothing.􀀉

“What􀀉are􀀉we􀀉supposed􀀉to􀀉—”􀀉Ben􀀉started.􀀉

The􀀉walls􀀉answered.􀀉The􀀉green􀀉membrane􀀉began􀀉to􀀉move􀀉—􀀉not􀀉fast,􀀉but􀀉
steady.􀀉It􀀉crept􀀉across􀀉the􀀉floor􀀉in􀀉thick,􀀉spreading􀀉tendrils,􀀉warm􀀉and􀀉soft,􀀉
wrapping􀀉around􀀉their􀀉boots􀀉like􀀉living􀀉moss.􀀉The􀀉room􀀉had􀀉not􀀉chosen􀀉
violence􀀉—􀀉it􀀉had􀀉chosen􀀉something􀀉stranger.􀀉Then􀀉it􀀉didn’t􀀉stop.􀀉It􀀉
climbed.􀀉

Ben􀀉was􀀉closest􀀉to􀀉the􀀉wall.􀀉The􀀉substance􀀉reached􀀉his􀀉knees􀀉before􀀉anyone􀀉
else’s􀀉ankles.􀀉He􀀉tried􀀉to􀀉step􀀉back,􀀉but􀀉his􀀉legs􀀉wouldn’t􀀉move.􀀉The􀀉green􀀉
mass􀀉had􀀉hardened􀀉around􀀉them􀀉—􀀉soft􀀉a􀀉second􀀉ago,􀀉now􀀉dense􀀉and􀀉
locked,􀀉like􀀉wet􀀉clay􀀉that􀀉had􀀉set􀀉the􀀉moment􀀉he􀀉pulled􀀉against􀀉it.􀀉The􀀉
harder􀀉he􀀉struggled,􀀉the􀀉tighter􀀉it􀀉gripped.􀀉

It􀀉didn’t􀀉stop.􀀉It􀀉rose􀀉past􀀉his􀀉thighs.􀀉It􀀉crossed􀀉his􀀉hips􀀉with􀀉a􀀉soft,􀀉patient􀀉
warmth,􀀉as􀀉if􀀉checking􀀉him􀀉—􀀉every􀀉contour,􀀉every􀀉inch.􀀉It􀀉pressed􀀉up􀀉
under􀀉his􀀉ribs􀀉and􀀉squeezed􀀉the􀀉breath􀀉slowly􀀉out􀀉of􀀉him.􀀉He􀀉tried􀀉to􀀉
wrench􀀉his􀀉arms􀀉free.􀀉The􀀉substance􀀉found􀀉his􀀉elbows,􀀉then􀀉his􀀉shoulders,􀀉
and􀀉folded􀀉them􀀉against􀀉his􀀉sides􀀉as􀀉if􀀉they􀀉had􀀉never􀀉been􀀉arms.􀀉His􀀉eyes􀀉
found􀀉Gage.􀀉His􀀉mouth􀀉opened.􀀉The􀀉substance􀀉climbed􀀉his􀀉chest,􀀉sealed􀀉
around􀀉his􀀉throat,􀀉and􀀉his􀀉voice􀀉dissolved􀀉into􀀉a􀀉strangled􀀉buzz.􀀉

“Ben!”􀀉Gage􀀉lunged􀀉toward􀀉him,􀀉hands􀀉going􀀉for􀀉Ben’s􀀉face􀀉—􀀉for􀀉the􀀉
thick􀀉green􀀉shell􀀉that􀀉had􀀉sealed􀀉his􀀉mouth􀀉and􀀉throat,􀀉climbing􀀉now􀀉
toward􀀉his􀀉eyes.􀀉Gage􀀉clawed􀀉at􀀉it.􀀉The􀀉mass􀀉peeled􀀉under􀀉his􀀉nails􀀉in􀀉wet,􀀉


rubbery􀀉strips.􀀉A􀀉patch􀀉of􀀉his􀀉chin􀀉came􀀉clear.􀀉A􀀉corner􀀉of􀀉his􀀉mouth.􀀉He􀀉
tore􀀉faster.􀀉The􀀉seal􀀉broke􀀉open􀀉over􀀉his􀀉lips􀀉—􀀉parted,􀀉gasping,􀀉his􀀉first􀀉
breath􀀉in􀀉seconds.􀀉

Then􀀉the􀀉substance􀀉lashed􀀉back􀀉with􀀉twice􀀉the􀀉force,􀀉pouring􀀉over􀀉the􀀉hole􀀉
Gage􀀉had􀀉made􀀉—􀀉sealing􀀉his􀀉mouth􀀉tighter,􀀉surging􀀉up􀀉over􀀉his􀀉nose,􀀉over􀀉
his􀀉eyes.􀀉Ben’s􀀉face􀀉disappeared.􀀉

Gage’s􀀉hands􀀉didn’t􀀉come􀀉free􀀉with􀀉it.􀀉The􀀉substance􀀉had􀀉turned􀀉his􀀉
strength􀀉into􀀉its􀀉own􀀉anchor.􀀉It􀀉was􀀉already􀀉moving􀀉up􀀉his􀀉wrists.􀀉The􀀉
Engine􀀉that􀀉could􀀉break􀀉coal􀀉with􀀉his􀀉hands􀀉couldn’t􀀉break􀀉free􀀉of􀀉
something􀀉that􀀉fed􀀉on􀀉force.􀀉

Otto􀀉was􀀉in􀀉the􀀉center􀀉of􀀉the􀀉room,􀀉beneath􀀉the􀀉crystal.􀀉The􀀉green􀀉mass􀀉
had􀀉risen􀀉past􀀉his􀀉knees,􀀉past􀀉his􀀉hips,􀀉but􀀉the􀀉crystal􀀉hung􀀉untouched􀀉
above􀀉him􀀉—􀀉its􀀉slow􀀉rotation􀀉indifferent,􀀉its􀀉facets􀀉catching􀀉the􀀉room’s􀀉
green􀀉light􀀉without􀀉taking􀀉any􀀉of􀀉it􀀉on.􀀉He􀀉felt􀀉the􀀉substance􀀉climbing􀀉his􀀉
torso.􀀉He􀀉tried􀀉to􀀉breathe,􀀉tried􀀉to􀀉find􀀉the􀀉stillness􀀉Master􀀉Chan􀀉had􀀉
taught􀀉him,􀀉but􀀉the􀀉room’s􀀉rhythmic􀀉pulse􀀉was􀀉drowning􀀉him􀀉out􀀉—􀀉a􀀉
beat􀀉so􀀉deep􀀉it􀀉rattled􀀉his􀀉ribs􀀉and􀀉scattered􀀉every􀀉thread􀀉of􀀉focus􀀉before􀀉he􀀉
could􀀉hold􀀉it.􀀉

The􀀉substance􀀉reached􀀉Camilla􀀉where􀀉she􀀉lay􀀉against􀀉the􀀉wall.􀀉She􀀉had􀀉not􀀉
woken􀀉since􀀉they􀀉laid􀀉her􀀉down.􀀉She􀀉didn’t􀀉wake􀀉now.􀀉The􀀉green􀀉mass􀀉
wrapped􀀉around􀀉her􀀉legs,􀀉her􀀉waist,􀀉her􀀉sealed􀀉shoulder,􀀉indifferent􀀉to􀀉the􀀉
wound􀀉it􀀉was􀀉covering.􀀉

Gage􀀉roared.􀀉He􀀉ripped􀀉and􀀉clawed,􀀉but􀀉every􀀉movement􀀉pulled􀀉him􀀉
deeper.􀀉Across􀀉the􀀉room,􀀉Ben’s􀀉shape􀀉was􀀉still􀀉visible􀀉beneath􀀉the􀀉green􀀉
surface􀀉—􀀉frozen􀀉in􀀉the􀀉position􀀉he’d􀀉been􀀉struggling􀀉in,􀀉eyes􀀉open􀀉behind􀀉
the􀀉seal􀀉but􀀉unseeing,􀀉chest􀀉locked􀀉still.􀀉Not􀀉dead􀀉—􀀉the􀀉faint􀀉pulse􀀉of􀀉the􀀉
substance􀀉around􀀉him􀀉kept􀀉rhythm􀀉with􀀉something􀀉that􀀉might􀀉have􀀉been􀀉a􀀉
heartbeat.􀀉If􀀉he􀀉was􀀉breathing􀀉at􀀉all,􀀉the􀀉substance􀀉was􀀉doing􀀉it􀀉for􀀉him.􀀉


The􀀉green􀀉mass􀀉had􀀉sealed􀀉him􀀉in􀀉like􀀉an􀀉insect􀀉in􀀉resin,􀀉alive􀀉but􀀉
suspended,􀀉waiting􀀉for􀀉the􀀉room􀀉to􀀉decide􀀉what􀀉to􀀉do􀀉with􀀉him.􀀉

The􀀉room􀀉was􀀉swallowing􀀉all􀀉of􀀉them􀀉—􀀉Ben􀀉already􀀉gone,􀀉Camilla􀀉nearly􀀉
gone,􀀉Gage􀀉sinking,􀀉and􀀉Otto􀀉losing􀀉his􀀉focus􀀉as􀀉the􀀉substance􀀉reached􀀉his􀀉
neck.􀀉

Then,􀀉in􀀉the􀀉green􀀉dark􀀉closing􀀉over􀀉his􀀉eyes,􀀉Otto􀀉heard􀀉a􀀉voice􀀉he􀀉hadn’t􀀉
heard􀀉in􀀉years.􀀉

It􀀉wasn’t􀀉the􀀉facility.􀀉It􀀉wasn’t􀀉the􀀉room.􀀉It􀀉came􀀉from􀀉somewhere􀀉near,􀀉
and􀀉yet􀀉strangely􀀉further􀀉away􀀉than􀀉words􀀉could􀀉describe.􀀉It􀀉was􀀉Master􀀉
Shu􀀉Chan,􀀉reaching􀀉from􀀉the􀀉far􀀉beyond.􀀉He􀀉was􀀉sitting􀀉cross-legged􀀉in􀀉the􀀉
yard􀀉behind􀀉the􀀉dojo,􀀉a􀀉blue􀀉butterfly􀀉resting􀀉on􀀉his􀀉shoulder􀀉the􀀉way􀀉it􀀉
always􀀉did.􀀉

“Otto.􀀉You􀀉have􀀉wandered􀀉far􀀉from􀀉the􀀉breath.􀀉Come􀀉back􀀉to􀀉me.”􀀉

“The􀀉room􀀉is􀀉too􀀉loud,􀀉master.􀀉I􀀉cannot􀀉find􀀉it.”􀀉

“The􀀉answer􀀉is􀀉in􀀉front􀀉of􀀉you,􀀉like􀀉it􀀉always􀀉was.􀀉Don’t􀀉fight􀀉it.􀀉Embrace􀀉it􀀉
and􀀉channel􀀉its􀀉energy􀀉as􀀉your􀀉own.􀀉Adapt,􀀉son.􀀉Adapt.”􀀉

Otto’s􀀉eyes􀀉opened.􀀉His􀀉body􀀉went􀀉still.􀀉

He􀀉stopped􀀉fighting.􀀉He􀀉stopped􀀉pulling,􀀉pushing,􀀉straining.􀀉He􀀉let􀀉his􀀉
breathing􀀉slow􀀉until􀀉it􀀉matched􀀉the􀀉pulse􀀉of􀀉the􀀉room􀀉—􀀉not􀀉his􀀉rhythm􀀉
against􀀉the􀀉room’s,􀀉but􀀉his􀀉rhythm􀀉becoming􀀉the􀀉room’s.􀀉The􀀉same􀀉way􀀉
Master􀀉Chan􀀉had􀀉taught􀀉him􀀉to􀀉match􀀉the􀀉weight􀀉of􀀉a􀀉stone􀀉before􀀉lifting􀀉
it.􀀉Not􀀉force.􀀉Alignment.􀀉

The􀀉substance􀀉around􀀉his􀀉body􀀉hesitated.􀀉It􀀉had􀀉been􀀉built􀀉to􀀉grip􀀉against􀀉
resistance􀀉—􀀉to􀀉harden􀀉when􀀉something􀀉pushed.􀀉But􀀉Otto􀀉wasn’t􀀉pushing􀀉
anymore.􀀉He􀀉was􀀉still.􀀉Perfectly,􀀉completely􀀉still.􀀉And􀀉the􀀉substance,􀀉
finding􀀉nothing􀀉to􀀉grip,􀀉nothing􀀉to􀀉fight,􀀉began􀀉to􀀉loosen.􀀉Then􀀉to􀀉wither.􀀉
Then􀀉to􀀉crack.􀀉


A􀀉faint􀀉warmth􀀉began􀀉to􀀉radiate􀀉from􀀉Otto’s􀀉skin.􀀉Where􀀉it􀀉touched􀀉the􀀉
green􀀉mass,􀀉the􀀉substance􀀉shattered􀀉like􀀉dried􀀉clay􀀉and􀀉crumbled􀀉to􀀉dust,􀀉
falling􀀉away􀀉from􀀉him􀀉in􀀉soft􀀉cascades.􀀉His􀀉body􀀉rose􀀉—􀀉inches􀀉off􀀉the􀀉
ground,􀀉then􀀉a􀀉foot,􀀉his􀀉legs􀀉folded􀀉beneath􀀉him􀀉in􀀉the􀀉same􀀉cross-legged􀀉
position􀀉Master􀀉Chan􀀉used􀀉to􀀉hold􀀉for􀀉hours.􀀉

The􀀉effect􀀉spread􀀉outward.􀀉The􀀉green􀀉mass􀀉cracked,􀀉dried,􀀉and􀀉fell􀀉away􀀉—􀀉
from􀀉Gage’s􀀉arms􀀉and􀀉chest,􀀉from􀀉Camilla’s􀀉body,􀀉from􀀉Ben’s􀀉sealed􀀉form.􀀉
Across􀀉the􀀉floor,􀀉the􀀉remaining􀀉substance􀀉turned􀀉grey􀀉and􀀉collapsed􀀉into􀀉
powder.􀀉

Ben􀀉gasped􀀉—􀀉a􀀉raw,􀀉desperate􀀉inhale􀀉of􀀉someone􀀉who’d􀀉been􀀉held􀀉
underwater.􀀉The􀀉shell􀀉that􀀉had􀀉held􀀉him􀀉upright􀀉cracked􀀉from􀀉his􀀉face􀀉and􀀉
chest,􀀉and􀀉he􀀉folded􀀉forward􀀉into􀀉the􀀉grey􀀉dust,􀀉hands􀀉at􀀉his􀀉throat􀀉as􀀉if􀀉to􀀉
make􀀉sure􀀉it􀀉was􀀉still􀀉his􀀉own.􀀉

Gage􀀉tore􀀉his􀀉arms􀀉free,􀀉the􀀉powder􀀉caking􀀉his􀀉fingers.􀀉He􀀉staggered􀀉upright􀀉
and􀀉looked􀀉first􀀉to􀀉Ben􀀉—􀀉breathing􀀉—􀀉then􀀉to􀀉Camilla.􀀉She􀀉lay􀀉against􀀉the􀀉
wall􀀉exactly􀀉as􀀉he􀀉had􀀉laid􀀉her􀀉down.􀀉The􀀉substance􀀉fell􀀉away􀀉from􀀉her􀀉in􀀉
slow􀀉drifts,􀀉settling􀀉around􀀉her,􀀉leaving􀀉her􀀉unconscious,􀀉the􀀉blue􀀉gel􀀉still􀀉
glistening􀀉on􀀉the􀀉sealed􀀉shoulder􀀉where􀀉her􀀉arm􀀉had􀀉been.􀀉

Otto􀀉sank􀀉back􀀉to􀀉the􀀉ground,􀀉landing􀀉in􀀉the􀀉same􀀉cross-legged􀀉position􀀉
he’d􀀉risen􀀉in.􀀉He􀀉sat􀀉in􀀉the􀀉grey􀀉powder􀀉that􀀉had􀀉been􀀉the􀀉substance,􀀉
shaking,􀀉drenched􀀉in􀀉sweat,􀀉too􀀉spent􀀉to􀀉stand.􀀉But􀀉there􀀉was􀀉a􀀉peace􀀉on􀀉his􀀉
face􀀉that􀀉did􀀉not􀀉belong􀀉to􀀉this􀀉place􀀉—􀀉as􀀉if􀀉part􀀉of􀀉him􀀉was􀀉still􀀉in􀀉the􀀉yard􀀉
behind􀀉the􀀉dojo.􀀉

Above􀀉him,􀀉the􀀉crystal􀀉still􀀉hung􀀉in􀀉the􀀉air,􀀉untouched.􀀉As􀀉they􀀉watched,􀀉its􀀉
third􀀉section􀀉shifted􀀉from􀀉blue􀀉to􀀉green.􀀉Three􀀉down.􀀉One􀀉to􀀉go.􀀉

The􀀉voice:􀀉“Challenge􀀉3􀀉—􀀉success.”􀀉

For􀀉the􀀉first􀀉time,􀀉the􀀉voice􀀉was􀀉met􀀉with􀀉something􀀉other􀀉than􀀉fear.􀀉Gage􀀉
let􀀉out􀀉a􀀉shout􀀉that􀀉bounced􀀉off􀀉every􀀉wall.􀀉Ben,􀀉still􀀉coughing,􀀉looked􀀉at􀀉


Otto􀀉and􀀉shook􀀉his􀀉head􀀉—􀀉half􀀉disbelief,􀀉half􀀉awe.􀀉Even􀀉Camilla,􀀉barely􀀉
conscious􀀉against􀀉the􀀉wall,􀀉opened􀀉her􀀉eyes􀀉and􀀉looked􀀉at􀀉Otto􀀉with􀀉
something􀀉that􀀉might􀀉have􀀉been􀀉the􀀉beginning􀀉of􀀉a􀀉smile.􀀉

Ben􀀉and􀀉Gage􀀉moved􀀉toward􀀉her.􀀉They􀀉reached􀀉down􀀉to􀀉lift􀀉her.􀀉Camilla􀀉
raised􀀉her􀀉one􀀉hand􀀉—􀀉not􀀉for􀀉them􀀉to􀀉take,􀀉but􀀉to􀀉keep􀀉them􀀉back.􀀉She􀀉
pressed􀀉her􀀉palm􀀉flat􀀉against􀀉the􀀉floor,􀀉into􀀉the􀀉grey􀀉dust.􀀉She􀀉pushed.􀀉The􀀉
arm􀀉shook.􀀉The􀀉arm􀀉held.􀀉She􀀉rose􀀉to􀀉her􀀉knees.􀀉Then􀀉to􀀉her􀀉feet,􀀉slow􀀉and􀀉
trembling,􀀉dust􀀉falling􀀉from􀀉her􀀉clothes.􀀉She􀀉steadied􀀉herself􀀉against􀀉the􀀉
wall.􀀉She􀀉lifted􀀉her􀀉chin.􀀉Whatever􀀉waited􀀉behind􀀉the􀀉next􀀉door􀀉would􀀉not􀀉
find􀀉her􀀉sitting.􀀉

They􀀉stepped􀀉into􀀉the􀀉corridor.􀀉The􀀉label􀀉on􀀉the􀀉final􀀉door􀀉stared􀀉back􀀉at􀀉
them:􀀉SACRIFICE.􀀉

Nobody􀀉moved􀀉toward􀀉it.􀀉Not􀀉yet.􀀉

Camilla􀀉leaned􀀉against􀀉the􀀉corridor􀀉wall,􀀉her􀀉gaze􀀉fixed􀀉on􀀉the􀀉blue􀀉sealant􀀉
where􀀉her􀀉left􀀉arm􀀉used􀀉to􀀉be.􀀉The􀀉word􀀉kept􀀉circling􀀉her􀀉mind􀀉—􀀉
crippled,􀀉crippled,􀀉crippled􀀉—􀀉a􀀉loop􀀉she􀀉couldn’t􀀉shut􀀉off.􀀉She􀀉thought􀀉of􀀉
Andre􀀉and􀀉his􀀉hacksaw.􀀉She􀀉thought􀀉of􀀉Gerald􀀉and􀀉his􀀉missing􀀉leg.􀀉She􀀉
thought􀀉of􀀉every􀀉broken􀀉miner􀀉she’d􀀉ever􀀉seen􀀉dragged􀀉out􀀉of􀀉the􀀉pits􀀉and􀀉
left􀀉to􀀉rot􀀉in􀀉the􀀉shack-rows.􀀉Was􀀉that􀀉her􀀉now?􀀉A􀀉discarded􀀉part?􀀉

But􀀉then􀀉she􀀉watched􀀉Gage􀀉pace.􀀉Back􀀉and􀀉forth,􀀉back􀀉and􀀉forth,􀀉his􀀉face􀀉
twisted􀀉with􀀉a􀀉guilt􀀉that􀀉was􀀉making􀀉him􀀉sloppy􀀉and􀀉erratic.􀀉She􀀉watched􀀉
Ben􀀉sitting􀀉against􀀉the􀀉wall,􀀉eyes􀀉on􀀉the􀀉ground,􀀉working􀀉something􀀉
through􀀉that􀀉wasn’t􀀉moving.􀀉She􀀉watched􀀉Otto􀀉—􀀉still􀀉wearing􀀉that􀀉
strange,􀀉distant􀀉peace,􀀉not􀀉quite􀀉back􀀉from􀀉where􀀉he􀀉had􀀉been.􀀉Three􀀉of􀀉
them.􀀉Each􀀉lost􀀉in􀀉his􀀉own􀀉way.􀀉None􀀉of􀀉them􀀉looking􀀉at􀀉the􀀉next􀀉door.􀀉

Camilla􀀉looked􀀉down􀀉at􀀉her􀀉sealed􀀉shoulder.􀀉Then􀀉she􀀉looked􀀉at􀀉them.􀀉She􀀉
did􀀉not􀀉need􀀉two􀀉arms􀀉to􀀉do􀀉what􀀉she􀀉had􀀉always􀀉done.􀀉The􀀉arm􀀉she􀀉had􀀉
lost􀀉was􀀉for􀀉the􀀉work􀀉of􀀉hands􀀉—􀀉for􀀉holding,􀀉for􀀉handing,􀀉for􀀉fixing.􀀉She􀀉
had􀀉never􀀉needed􀀉it􀀉to􀀉see􀀉what􀀉the􀀉others􀀉couldn’t.􀀉


They􀀉needed􀀉her.􀀉They􀀉had􀀉not􀀉known􀀉how􀀉much,􀀉until􀀉now.􀀉

She􀀉had􀀉paid􀀉an􀀉arm.􀀉The􀀉facility􀀉hadn’t􀀉gotten􀀉anything􀀉else.􀀉It􀀉hadn’t􀀉
touched􀀉her􀀉vision􀀉—􀀉the􀀉part􀀉of􀀉her􀀉that􀀉could􀀉look􀀉at􀀉a􀀉room􀀉full􀀉of􀀉tricks􀀉
and􀀉see􀀉through􀀉every􀀉one.􀀉She􀀉wasn’t􀀉a􀀉discarded􀀉part.􀀉She􀀉was􀀉the􀀉only􀀉
person􀀉in􀀉this􀀉group􀀉who􀀉could􀀉read􀀉what􀀉was􀀉coming.􀀉Gage􀀉had􀀉the􀀉
strength􀀉to􀀉break􀀉stone.􀀉But􀀉she􀀉was􀀉still􀀉the􀀉only􀀉one􀀉who􀀉could􀀉see􀀉exactly􀀉
where􀀉to􀀉strike.􀀉

Gage􀀉came􀀉to􀀉her􀀉side􀀉and􀀉leaned􀀉against􀀉the􀀉wall.􀀉For􀀉a􀀉long􀀉time􀀉he􀀉said􀀉
nothing.􀀉Then,􀀉quietly:􀀉“I􀀉should􀀉have􀀉been􀀉the􀀉one􀀉standing􀀉at􀀉the􀀉
railing.”􀀉

It􀀉wasn’t􀀉a􀀉joke.􀀉It􀀉wasn’t􀀉a􀀉strategy.􀀉It􀀉was􀀉the􀀉rawest􀀉thing􀀉he’d􀀉ever􀀉said􀀉to􀀉
her􀀉—􀀉and􀀉it􀀉broke􀀉something􀀉she’d􀀉been􀀉holding􀀉together􀀉since􀀉the􀀉laser􀀉
hit.􀀉She􀀉reached􀀉for􀀉him􀀉with􀀉her􀀉one􀀉arm,􀀉and􀀉he􀀉folded􀀉around􀀉her,􀀉his􀀉
weight􀀉against􀀉the􀀉wall􀀉holding􀀉them􀀉both􀀉up.􀀉Ben􀀉moved􀀉in􀀉beside􀀉them,􀀉
his􀀉back􀀉to􀀉the􀀉wall􀀉too.􀀉Otto􀀉leaned􀀉against􀀉the􀀉wall􀀉opposite,􀀉still􀀉shaking􀀉
from􀀉the􀀉room.􀀉Nobody􀀉spoke.􀀉The􀀉dam􀀉of􀀉her􀀉solitude􀀉burst􀀉in􀀉the􀀉
corridor􀀉of􀀉a􀀉facility􀀉that􀀉had􀀉taken􀀉everything􀀉from􀀉her􀀉except􀀉the􀀉three􀀉
people􀀉standing􀀉beside􀀉her.􀀉

Indifference􀀉had􀀉never􀀉meant􀀉disconnect.􀀉

The􀀉crystal􀀉had􀀉pulsed􀀉three􀀉sections􀀉green,􀀉one􀀉still􀀉cold􀀉blue.􀀉Above􀀉the􀀉
last􀀉door,􀀉the􀀉word􀀉waited􀀉where􀀉it􀀉had􀀉waited􀀉from􀀉the􀀉start.􀀉
SACRIFICE.􀀉The􀀉other􀀉rooms􀀉had􀀉asked􀀉what􀀉they􀀉could􀀉do.􀀉This􀀉one􀀉
asked􀀉what􀀉they􀀉would􀀉give.􀀉

Gage􀀉looked􀀉at􀀉the􀀉others.􀀉They􀀉looked􀀉back.􀀉No􀀉speeches.􀀉No􀀉debates.􀀉
They􀀉all􀀉nodded􀀉and􀀉stepped􀀉together􀀉toward􀀉it.􀀉


Chapter􀀉25􀀉—􀀉Don’t Do􀀉It􀀉

As􀀉they􀀉stepped􀀉in,􀀉the􀀉door􀀉sealed􀀉behind􀀉them􀀉and􀀉they􀀉stopped.􀀉They􀀉
stood􀀉listening.􀀉They􀀉waited􀀉for􀀉it􀀉to􀀉begin.􀀉Nothing􀀉came.􀀉Every􀀉room􀀉so􀀉
far􀀉had􀀉announced􀀉itself􀀉—􀀉panels􀀉rising􀀉from􀀉the􀀉floor,􀀉walls􀀉lighting􀀉up,􀀉a􀀉
voice􀀉giving􀀉instructions.􀀉This􀀉room􀀉did􀀉nothing.􀀉It􀀉was􀀉silent.􀀉Empty.􀀉Just􀀉
the􀀉crystal􀀉sitting􀀉at􀀉the􀀉center,􀀉and􀀉four􀀉bare􀀉walls􀀉stretching􀀉away􀀉into􀀉
dim􀀉light.􀀉

No􀀉controls.􀀉No􀀉countdown.􀀉No􀀉voice.􀀉Just􀀉space,􀀉and􀀉the􀀉growing􀀉
certainty􀀉that􀀉whatever􀀉was􀀉about􀀉to􀀉happen,􀀉they􀀉wouldn’t􀀉see􀀉it􀀉coming.􀀉

The􀀉crystal􀀉sat􀀉at􀀉the􀀉center,􀀉same􀀉as􀀉before.􀀉Ben􀀉stepped􀀉closer,􀀉squinting.􀀉

“It’s􀀉the􀀉same􀀉crystal,”􀀉he􀀉said.􀀉

“Of􀀉course􀀉it􀀉is,”􀀉Otto􀀉countered.􀀉“It’s􀀉been􀀉in􀀉every􀀉room.”􀀉

“No􀀉—􀀉I􀀉mean􀀉it’s􀀉literally􀀉the􀀉same􀀉one.􀀉The􀀉exact􀀉same􀀉object.”􀀉

Gage􀀉frowned.􀀉“What􀀉do􀀉you􀀉mean?”􀀉

“Remember􀀉the􀀉blood?”􀀉Ben􀀉said.􀀉“When􀀉Camilla􀀉lost􀀉her􀀉arm􀀉—􀀉when􀀉
the􀀉laser􀀉hit􀀉—􀀉blood􀀉sprayed􀀉everywhere.􀀉Some􀀉of􀀉it􀀉landed􀀉on􀀉the􀀉crystal.􀀉
I􀀉saw􀀉it􀀉happen􀀉but􀀉couldn’t􀀉get􀀉a􀀉clear􀀉look􀀉because􀀉of􀀉the􀀉glow􀀉and􀀉
everything􀀉else􀀉going􀀉on.”􀀉He􀀉pointed􀀉to􀀉the􀀉bottom-right􀀉edge􀀉of􀀉the􀀉
green􀀉section.􀀉“Three􀀉parts􀀉are􀀉green􀀉now.􀀉Look􀀉at􀀉the􀀉edge.”􀀉

There􀀉it􀀉was.􀀉Clear􀀉drops􀀉of􀀉dried􀀉blood,􀀉splattered􀀉across􀀉the􀀉green􀀉
surface.􀀉

Their􀀉eyes􀀉widened.􀀉Camilla􀀉stood􀀉up.􀀉“It’s􀀉my􀀉blood.􀀉Same􀀉crystal.􀀉What􀀉
does􀀉that􀀉mean?”􀀉


“This􀀉thing􀀉is􀀉four􀀉feet􀀉across,􀀉and􀀉it􀀉has􀀉to􀀉weigh􀀉hundreds􀀉of􀀉pounds􀀉at􀀉
least,”􀀉Ben􀀉said.􀀉“Solid􀀉crystal.􀀉To􀀉have􀀉a􀀉machine􀀉that􀀉moves􀀉it􀀉silently􀀉into􀀉
every􀀉room􀀉before􀀉we􀀉arrive􀀉—􀀉that’s􀀉beyond􀀉anything􀀉we’ve􀀉seen,􀀉even􀀉in􀀉
this􀀉place.”􀀉He􀀉paused.􀀉“Unless􀀉the􀀉crystal􀀉never􀀉moved􀀉at􀀉all.”􀀉

Silence.􀀉

“We􀀉haven’t􀀉been􀀉moving􀀉from􀀉room􀀉to􀀉room,”􀀉Ben􀀉continued.􀀉“We’ve􀀉
been􀀉entering􀀉and􀀉leaving􀀉the􀀉same􀀉room􀀉the􀀉whole􀀉time.”􀀉

Gage􀀉scoffed.􀀉“That’s􀀉—”􀀉He􀀉stopped.􀀉He􀀉turned􀀉toward􀀉the􀀉door􀀉they’d􀀉
entered􀀉through􀀉and􀀉looked􀀉at􀀉the􀀉window􀀉panes.􀀉Beyond􀀉the􀀉glass:􀀉
nothing.􀀉Not􀀉darkness􀀉the􀀉way􀀉a􀀉mine􀀉shaft􀀉is􀀉dark.􀀉Absolute􀀉black􀀉—􀀉as􀀉if􀀉
nothing􀀉existed􀀉on􀀉the􀀉other􀀉side.􀀉As􀀉if􀀉the􀀉corridor􀀉they’d􀀉just􀀉walked􀀉
through􀀉had􀀉ceased􀀉to􀀉exist􀀉the􀀉moment􀀉the􀀉door􀀉closed.􀀉

Before􀀉they􀀉could􀀉process􀀉it,􀀉the􀀉voice􀀉spoke:􀀉“Welcome􀀉to􀀉the􀀉Unreal􀀉
Reality.􀀉Simulation􀀉begins􀀉in􀀉forty-five􀀉seconds.”􀀉

“Unreal􀀉Reality,”􀀉Camilla􀀉repeated.􀀉“It’s􀀉telling􀀉us􀀉it’s􀀉not􀀉real.􀀉Whatever􀀉
happens􀀉next􀀉—􀀉remember􀀉that.”􀀉

“Thirty􀀉seconds,”􀀉the􀀉voice􀀉said.􀀉

Gage􀀉looked􀀉at􀀉the􀀉others.􀀉“Whatever􀀉it􀀉shows􀀉us􀀉—􀀉we􀀉stay􀀉together.􀀉We􀀉
don’t􀀉react􀀉to􀀉anything􀀉alone.”􀀉

“Fifteen􀀉seconds,”􀀉the􀀉voice􀀉said.􀀉

They􀀉drew􀀉closer􀀉in􀀉the􀀉empty􀀉room,􀀉shoulders􀀉almost􀀉touching.􀀉Gage􀀉set􀀉
his􀀉stance.􀀉Otto􀀉stood􀀉at􀀉his􀀉flank.􀀉Ben􀀉squared􀀉his􀀉shoulders.􀀉Camilla􀀉
planted􀀉her􀀉feet,􀀉her􀀉one􀀉hand􀀉fisted􀀉at􀀉her􀀉side.􀀉They􀀉were􀀉still􀀉shaking􀀉
from􀀉the􀀉last􀀉room􀀉—􀀉all􀀉of􀀉them􀀉—􀀉but􀀉they􀀉stood.􀀉


The􀀉room􀀉exploded􀀉with􀀉light􀀉—􀀉so􀀉bright􀀉they􀀉threw􀀉their􀀉arms􀀉over􀀉their􀀉
faces.􀀉Hot􀀉wind􀀉hit􀀉them􀀉in􀀉waves,􀀉sharp􀀉and􀀉stinging,􀀉like􀀉standing􀀉too􀀉
close􀀉to􀀉a􀀉furnace􀀉vent.􀀉When􀀉they􀀉opened􀀉their􀀉eyes,􀀉the􀀉room􀀉was􀀉gone.􀀉

Or􀀉it􀀉had􀀉stopped􀀉being􀀉there􀀉for􀀉them.􀀉Their􀀉feet􀀉had􀀉not􀀉taken􀀉a􀀉step.􀀉
Their􀀉bodies􀀉had􀀉not􀀉moved.􀀉The􀀉cold􀀉metal􀀉of􀀉the􀀉SACRIFICE􀀉chamber􀀉
was􀀉still􀀉under􀀉their􀀉boots􀀉—􀀉they􀀉could􀀉feel􀀉it,􀀉faintly,􀀉like􀀉a􀀉second􀀉
heartbeat􀀉behind􀀉the􀀉one􀀉in􀀉their􀀉chests.􀀉But􀀉everything􀀉their􀀉eyes􀀉could􀀉
see,􀀉everything􀀉their􀀉skin􀀉could􀀉feel,􀀉told􀀉them􀀉they􀀉were􀀉somewhere􀀉else.􀀉

They􀀉were􀀉standing􀀉at􀀉the􀀉base􀀉of􀀉a􀀉mountain.􀀉Above􀀉them,􀀉a􀀉volcano􀀉was􀀉
erupting􀀉—􀀉molten􀀉rock􀀉fountaining􀀉into􀀉a􀀉sky􀀉that􀀉burned􀀉orange􀀉and􀀉
grey.􀀉Gage,􀀉Ben,􀀉and􀀉Otto􀀉had􀀉worked􀀉in􀀉heat􀀉that􀀉cracked􀀉lips􀀉and􀀉
blistered􀀉skin􀀉every􀀉day􀀉of􀀉their􀀉lives􀀉in􀀉the􀀉pits􀀉—􀀉but􀀉this􀀉was􀀉something􀀉
else􀀉entirely.􀀉This􀀉heat􀀉had􀀉weight.􀀉It􀀉pressed􀀉down􀀉on􀀉them􀀉like􀀉a􀀉hand,􀀉
and􀀉the􀀉air􀀉itself􀀉tasted􀀉like􀀉metal.􀀉Camilla,􀀉who􀀉had􀀉never􀀉been􀀉
underground,􀀉had􀀉nothing􀀉to􀀉compare􀀉it􀀉to.􀀉She􀀉just􀀉knew􀀉her􀀉skin􀀉was􀀉
screaming.􀀉

But􀀉it􀀉wasn’t􀀉just􀀉the􀀉heat􀀉that􀀉shook􀀉them.􀀉The􀀉ground􀀉beneath􀀉Gage’s􀀉
feet􀀉felt􀀉exactly􀀉like􀀉the􀀉slag􀀉ridge􀀉behind􀀉the􀀉shack􀀉—􀀉the􀀉same􀀉crumble,􀀉
the􀀉same􀀉give􀀉under􀀉his􀀉weight,􀀉the􀀉texture􀀉he’d􀀉felt􀀉a􀀉thousand􀀉times􀀉
walking􀀉home􀀉from􀀉the􀀉mine.􀀉Ben􀀉caught􀀉something􀀉that􀀉stopped􀀉him􀀉
cold:􀀉the􀀉smell􀀉his􀀉father􀀉carried􀀉home􀀉every􀀉night􀀉—􀀉coal􀀉dust,􀀉sweat,􀀉
cooling􀀉metal􀀉—􀀉faint􀀉and􀀉impossible,􀀉threading􀀉through􀀉the􀀉sulfur.􀀉Otto􀀉
caught􀀉a􀀉flicker􀀉of􀀉blue􀀉at􀀉the􀀉edge􀀉of􀀉his􀀉sight􀀉—􀀉there,􀀉gone,􀀉the􀀉wing􀀉of􀀉
something􀀉that􀀉should􀀉not􀀉have􀀉followed􀀉him􀀉here.􀀉The􀀉yard􀀉behind􀀉the􀀉
dojo􀀉had􀀉come􀀉too.􀀉Camilla􀀉felt􀀉a􀀉phantom􀀉ache􀀉where􀀉her􀀉missing􀀉arm􀀉
had􀀉been􀀉—􀀉not􀀉pain􀀉exactly,􀀉but􀀉the􀀉memory􀀉of􀀉weight,􀀉as􀀉if􀀉the􀀉
simulation􀀉had􀀉reached􀀉inside􀀉her􀀉and􀀉found􀀉the􀀉part􀀉of􀀉her􀀉brain􀀉that􀀉still􀀉
believed􀀉the􀀉arm􀀉was􀀉there.􀀉

Whatever􀀉this􀀉was,􀀉it􀀉wasn’t􀀉just􀀉a􀀉picture􀀉painted􀀉over􀀉the􀀉walls.􀀉It􀀉was􀀉
pulling􀀉from􀀉inside􀀉them􀀉—􀀉from􀀉memories,􀀉sensations,􀀉the􀀉private􀀉


architecture􀀉of􀀉their􀀉minds.􀀉Camilla’s􀀉warning􀀉echoed􀀉in􀀉all􀀉of􀀉their􀀉heads:􀀉
it’s􀀉not􀀉real.􀀉But􀀉their􀀉bodies􀀉didn’t􀀉believe􀀉it.􀀉

A􀀉molten􀀉boulder􀀉crashed􀀉nearby,􀀉covering􀀉them􀀉in􀀉smoldering􀀉ash.􀀉
Camilla’s􀀉first􀀉instinct􀀉was􀀉to􀀉run􀀉—􀀉away􀀉from􀀉the􀀉eruption,􀀉downhill,􀀉
anywhere􀀉but􀀉closer.􀀉She􀀉turned􀀉and􀀉sprinted􀀉three􀀉steps􀀉before􀀉hitting􀀉
something􀀉solid􀀉she􀀉couldn’t􀀉see.􀀉An􀀉invisible􀀉wall.􀀉She􀀉could􀀉see􀀉the􀀉
landscape􀀉beyond􀀉it,􀀉but􀀉her􀀉body􀀉couldn’t􀀉pass􀀉through.􀀉

“It’s􀀉blocked,”􀀉she􀀉called􀀉back.􀀉“There’s􀀉no􀀉way􀀉out􀀉behind􀀉us.”􀀉

Ben􀀉was􀀉already􀀉climbing􀀉upward.􀀉“Then􀀉we􀀉go􀀉up.􀀉If􀀉there’s􀀉an􀀉objective,􀀉
it’s􀀉at􀀉the􀀉top.”􀀉

As􀀉they􀀉climbed,􀀉the􀀉air􀀉thickened􀀉until􀀉breathing􀀉felt􀀉like􀀉sucking􀀉through􀀉
wet􀀉cloth.􀀉Ben􀀉stopped􀀉and􀀉tore􀀉a􀀉strip􀀉from􀀉the􀀉bottom􀀉of􀀉his􀀉suit􀀉—􀀉the􀀉
same􀀉way􀀉Camilla􀀉had􀀉torn􀀉hers􀀉for􀀉the􀀉tourniquet.􀀉“Everyone,􀀉tear􀀉a􀀉piece􀀉
off.􀀉Now.”􀀉

“And􀀉do􀀉what􀀉with􀀉it?”􀀉Gage􀀉rasped.􀀉

“Trust􀀉me.”􀀉Ben􀀉held􀀉the􀀉strip􀀉of􀀉fabric􀀉over􀀉a􀀉crack􀀉in􀀉the􀀉rocks􀀉where􀀉
steam􀀉was􀀉already􀀉rising,􀀉letting􀀉the􀀉cloth􀀉catch􀀉it􀀉until􀀉it􀀉grew􀀉heavy􀀉with􀀉
moisture.􀀉He􀀉pressed􀀉the􀀉damp􀀉cloth􀀉against􀀉Camilla’s􀀉face.􀀉She􀀉flinched􀀉

—􀀉but􀀉it􀀉was􀀉warm􀀉and􀀉wet,􀀉not􀀉searing.􀀉And􀀉when􀀉she􀀉breathed􀀉through􀀉
it,􀀉the􀀉air􀀉came􀀉through􀀉cleaner,􀀉the􀀉grit􀀉and􀀉ash􀀉caught􀀉in􀀉the􀀉fibers􀀉instead􀀉
of􀀉her􀀉lungs.􀀉
“There’s􀀉moisture􀀉in􀀉the􀀉air,”􀀉Ben􀀉said.􀀉“The􀀉steam􀀉wets􀀉the􀀉fabric.􀀉Wrap􀀉
your􀀉faces􀀉—􀀉it’ll􀀉catch􀀉the􀀉ash􀀉before􀀉it􀀉hits􀀉your􀀉lungs.”􀀉

They􀀉climbed􀀉for􀀉five􀀉minutes,􀀉breathing􀀉easier􀀉through􀀉the􀀉wet􀀉cloth.􀀉
Halfway􀀉up,􀀉Camilla􀀉stopped.􀀉“Where􀀉are􀀉we?􀀉Is􀀉any􀀉of􀀉this􀀉real?”􀀉

A􀀉hot􀀉pebble􀀉struck􀀉Otto’s􀀉foot􀀉and􀀉he􀀉hissed􀀉—􀀉a􀀉blister􀀉the􀀉size􀀉of􀀉a􀀉coin􀀉
rising􀀉instantly􀀉on􀀉his􀀉skin.􀀉


“Real􀀉enough,”􀀉Gage􀀉said.􀀉“Dream􀀉or􀀉not,􀀉we􀀉treat􀀉it􀀉as􀀉real􀀉until􀀉
something􀀉proves􀀉otherwise.”􀀉

Camilla􀀉pressed􀀉her􀀉remaining􀀉hand􀀉against􀀉her􀀉shoulder􀀉as􀀉they􀀉climbed.􀀉
The􀀉blue􀀉sealant􀀉was􀀉softening􀀉in􀀉the􀀉heat􀀉—􀀉turning􀀉sticky,􀀉losing􀀉its􀀉
shape.􀀉She􀀉could􀀉feel􀀉warmth􀀉seeping􀀉through􀀉it􀀉where􀀉the􀀉hard􀀉shell􀀉had􀀉
been.􀀉She􀀉didn’t􀀉say􀀉anything.􀀉There􀀉was􀀉nothing􀀉anyone􀀉could􀀉do􀀉about􀀉it􀀉
up􀀉here.􀀉

They􀀉kept􀀉climbing􀀉until􀀉the􀀉slope􀀉levelled􀀉off􀀉and􀀉the􀀉ridge􀀉opened􀀉up.􀀉
They􀀉were􀀉standing􀀉at􀀉the􀀉mouth􀀉of􀀉the􀀉volcano􀀉—􀀉a􀀉wide,􀀉jagged􀀉rim􀀉
overlooking􀀉a􀀉lake􀀉of􀀉molten􀀉orange􀀉that􀀉churned􀀉and􀀉spat􀀉below􀀉them.􀀉
The􀀉heat􀀉here􀀉was􀀉strangely􀀉bearable􀀉—􀀉less􀀉than􀀉the􀀉climb,􀀉not􀀉more.􀀉As􀀉if􀀉
something􀀉was􀀉controlling􀀉it.􀀉As􀀉if􀀉something􀀉wanted􀀉them􀀉to􀀉reach􀀉the􀀉
top􀀉and􀀉stay􀀉there􀀉long􀀉enough􀀉to􀀉figure􀀉something􀀉out.􀀉But􀀉what?􀀉What􀀉
was􀀉the􀀉puzzle􀀉this􀀉time?􀀉

“That􀀉doesn’t􀀉make􀀉sense,”􀀉Ben􀀉muttered.􀀉“Closer􀀉to􀀉the􀀉lava􀀉should􀀉be􀀉
worse,􀀉not􀀉better.􀀉Someone’s􀀉managing􀀉this.􀀉There’s􀀉an􀀉objective.􀀉We􀀉just􀀉
can’t􀀉see􀀉it􀀉yet.”􀀉

Gage,􀀉frustrated,􀀉picked􀀉up􀀉a􀀉stone􀀉and􀀉hurled􀀉it􀀉into􀀉the􀀉lava.􀀉It􀀉didn’t􀀉

the􀀉flat,􀀉grey,􀀉sterile􀀉floor􀀉of􀀉the􀀉facility.􀀉Then􀀉the􀀉lava􀀉seemed􀀉to􀀉flow􀀉back􀀉

“Did􀀉you􀀉see􀀉that?”􀀉Ben􀀉whispered.􀀉

“Throw􀀉another􀀉one,”􀀉Camilla􀀉said.􀀉

Gage􀀉threw􀀉another􀀉stone.􀀉The􀀉same􀀉thing􀀉—􀀉a􀀉dull􀀉thud􀀉beneath􀀉the􀀉

“The􀀉stone􀀉went􀀉through,”􀀉Ben􀀉said􀀉slowly.􀀉“It􀀉didn’t􀀉melt.􀀉It􀀉passed􀀉
through􀀉the􀀉surface􀀉and􀀉hit􀀉something􀀉solid􀀉underneath.”􀀉He􀀉was􀀉quiet􀀉for􀀉


a􀀉moment,􀀉staring􀀉at􀀉the􀀉lava.􀀉“I􀀉need􀀉to􀀉test􀀉something􀀉that􀀉isn’t􀀉from􀀉
here.􀀉Something􀀉from􀀉the􀀉real􀀉world.”􀀉

He􀀉patted􀀉his􀀉pockets.􀀉Empty.􀀉He􀀉looked􀀉at􀀉the􀀉others.􀀉“Anyone􀀉carrying􀀉
anything?􀀉Anything􀀉from􀀉Miner􀀉Town􀀉—􀀉anything􀀉that􀀉didn’t􀀉come􀀉from􀀉
this􀀉facility?”􀀉

Gage􀀉shook􀀉his􀀉head.􀀉Otto􀀉had􀀉nothing.􀀉Camilla’s􀀉one􀀉hand􀀉was􀀉empty.􀀉
They’d􀀉entered􀀉the􀀉facility􀀉in􀀉mining􀀉clothes􀀉and􀀉been􀀉stripped􀀉down􀀉to􀀉
suits􀀉that􀀉belonged􀀉to􀀉this􀀉place.􀀉

Ben’s􀀉hand􀀉drifted􀀉to􀀉his􀀉pocket􀀉and􀀉stopped.􀀉His􀀉face􀀉changed.􀀉

“What?”􀀉Camilla􀀉asked.􀀉

“Nothing.”􀀉But􀀉his􀀉hand􀀉hadn’t􀀉moved.􀀉His􀀉fingers􀀉were􀀉curled􀀉around􀀉
something􀀉inside􀀉the􀀉fabric,􀀉holding􀀉it􀀉the􀀉way􀀉you􀀉hold􀀉something􀀉you’re􀀉
not􀀉ready􀀉to􀀉let􀀉go􀀉of.􀀉

“Ben,”􀀉Gage􀀉said􀀉quietly.􀀉“What􀀉have􀀉you􀀉got?”􀀉

Ben􀀉pulled􀀉out􀀉a􀀉small􀀉brass􀀉compass.􀀉His􀀉father􀀉had􀀉given􀀉it􀀉to􀀉him􀀉years􀀉
ago􀀉—􀀉the􀀉only􀀉thing􀀉he’d􀀉carried􀀉from􀀉home.􀀉When􀀉they’d􀀉changed􀀉into􀀉
the􀀉facility􀀉suits,􀀉he’d􀀉slipped􀀉it􀀉from􀀉his􀀉old􀀉trousers􀀉before􀀉leaving􀀉them􀀉
on􀀉the􀀉floor.􀀉Through􀀉the􀀉Initiation,􀀉through􀀉the􀀉mine,􀀉through􀀉every􀀉
room􀀉in􀀉this􀀉facility,􀀉it􀀉had􀀉stayed􀀉with􀀉him.􀀉The􀀉glass􀀉face􀀉was􀀉scratched􀀉
but􀀉unbroken.􀀉The􀀉needle􀀉still􀀉moved.􀀉

“There’s􀀉nothing􀀉else,”􀀉Camilla􀀉said.􀀉She􀀉didn’t􀀉tell􀀉him􀀉to􀀉throw􀀉it.􀀉She􀀉
just􀀉stated􀀉the􀀉fact.􀀉

Ben􀀉looked􀀉at􀀉the􀀉compass􀀉in􀀉his􀀉palm.􀀉His􀀉thumb􀀉ran􀀉across􀀉the􀀉glass􀀉face􀀉
the􀀉way􀀉it􀀉must􀀉have􀀉done􀀉a􀀉thousand􀀉times􀀉before􀀉—􀀉a􀀉gesture􀀉so􀀉worn􀀉it􀀉
was􀀉almost􀀉involuntary.􀀉He􀀉turned􀀉it􀀉over.􀀉Turned􀀉it􀀉back.􀀉Held􀀉it􀀉up􀀉to􀀉
the􀀉orange􀀉light􀀉as􀀉if􀀉seeing􀀉it􀀉for􀀉the􀀉last􀀉time.􀀉


“There􀀉has􀀉to􀀉be􀀉something􀀉else,”􀀉Gage􀀉said.􀀉He􀀉was􀀉already􀀉pulling􀀉at􀀉his􀀉
own􀀉suit,􀀉checking􀀉seams,􀀉digging􀀉into􀀉the􀀉fabric.􀀉“A􀀉button.􀀉A􀀉zipper.􀀉
Anything.􀀉Ben,􀀉don’t􀀉—”􀀉

“There’s􀀉nothing,”􀀉Ben􀀉said.􀀉His􀀉voice􀀉was􀀉flat.􀀉He’d􀀉already􀀉looked.􀀉They􀀉
all􀀉knew􀀉he’d􀀉already􀀉looked.􀀉

Otto􀀉stepped􀀉forward.􀀉“Then􀀉we􀀉find􀀉another􀀉way.􀀉We􀀉already􀀉know􀀉the􀀉
stones􀀉go􀀉through.􀀉We􀀉work􀀉with􀀉that.”􀀉

“I’ll􀀉go,”􀀉Gage􀀉said.􀀉He􀀉stepped􀀉toward􀀉the􀀉lava’s􀀉edge,􀀉looking􀀉down􀀉at􀀉the􀀉
cracks􀀉where􀀉the􀀉facility􀀉floor􀀉had􀀉flickered􀀉through.􀀉“If􀀉the􀀉stones􀀉can􀀉pass􀀉
through,􀀉maybe􀀉we􀀉can􀀉too.􀀉I’ll􀀉jump􀀉and􀀉—”􀀉

“And􀀉what?”􀀉Ben􀀉cut􀀉him􀀉off.􀀉“And􀀉melt?􀀉We􀀉don’t􀀉know􀀉the􀀉rule,􀀉Gage.􀀉
We􀀉don’t􀀉know􀀉why􀀉the􀀉stones􀀉passed􀀉through.􀀉Until􀀉we􀀉understand􀀉that,􀀉
the􀀉next􀀉thing􀀉we􀀉throw􀀉in􀀉could􀀉be􀀉destroyed􀀉—􀀉and􀀉that􀀉next􀀉thing􀀉could􀀉
be􀀉you.”􀀉

Nobody􀀉had􀀉an􀀉answer􀀉for􀀉that.􀀉

Ben􀀉held􀀉the􀀉compass􀀉against􀀉his􀀉chest􀀉—􀀉pressed􀀉it􀀉there,􀀉over􀀉his􀀉heart,􀀉
the􀀉way􀀉you􀀉hold􀀉something􀀉when􀀉you’re􀀉trying􀀉to􀀉memorize􀀉its􀀉weight􀀉
before􀀉it’s􀀉gone.􀀉Gage􀀉looked􀀉away.􀀉Otto’s􀀉hand􀀉twitched􀀉at􀀉his􀀉side,􀀉
fighting􀀉the􀀉urge􀀉to􀀉reach􀀉out􀀉and􀀉stop􀀉him.􀀉

“I􀀉need􀀉it􀀉back,”􀀉Ben􀀉whispered􀀉to􀀉no􀀉one􀀉who􀀉could􀀉answer.􀀉He􀀉closed􀀉his􀀉
eyes,􀀉opened􀀉his􀀉hand,􀀉and􀀉let􀀉it􀀉fall.􀀉

It􀀉splashed.􀀉Sizzled.􀀉Melted􀀉into􀀉nothing.􀀉

Ben’s􀀉knees􀀉buckled.􀀉He􀀉caught􀀉himself􀀉with􀀉one􀀉hand􀀉on􀀉the􀀉ground,􀀉the􀀉
other􀀉still􀀉open􀀉over􀀉the􀀉lava􀀉—􀀉reaching􀀉for􀀉something􀀉that􀀉was􀀉already􀀉
gone.􀀉A􀀉sound􀀉came􀀉out􀀉of􀀉him􀀉that􀀉wasn’t􀀉a􀀉word.􀀉Gage􀀉crouched􀀉beside􀀉
him􀀉but􀀉didn’t􀀉touch􀀉him.􀀉There􀀉was􀀉nothing􀀉to􀀉say.􀀉The􀀉compass􀀉had􀀉


taken􀀉thirty􀀉years􀀉of􀀉a􀀉dead􀀉man’s􀀉love􀀉with􀀉it􀀉into􀀉the􀀉orange,􀀉and􀀉no􀀉
amount􀀉of􀀉words􀀉would􀀉bring􀀉any􀀉of􀀉it􀀉back.􀀉

When􀀉Ben􀀉finally􀀉stood,􀀉his􀀉face􀀉was􀀉a􀀉mask.􀀉His􀀉eyes􀀉were􀀉red􀀉but􀀉dry.􀀉His􀀉
jaw􀀉was􀀉set􀀉so􀀉tight􀀉the􀀉muscles􀀉trembled.􀀉He􀀉looked􀀉at􀀉the􀀉lava􀀉surface􀀉and􀀉
spoke􀀉as􀀉if􀀉the􀀉grief􀀉was􀀉a􀀉door􀀉he’d􀀉just􀀉closed􀀉and􀀉locked􀀉behind􀀉him.􀀉

“That’s􀀉different,”􀀉he􀀉said.􀀉“The􀀉compass􀀉melted.􀀉The􀀉stones􀀉didn’t.”􀀉

“The􀀉stone􀀉came􀀉from􀀉here,”􀀉Ben􀀉said,􀀉thinking􀀉out􀀉loud.􀀉“From􀀉this􀀉place􀀉

—􀀉this􀀉simulation,􀀉whatever􀀉it􀀉is.􀀉The􀀉compass􀀉came􀀉from􀀉outside.􀀉From􀀉
the􀀉real􀀉world.”􀀉His􀀉eyes􀀉widened􀀉as􀀉the􀀉logic􀀉assembled􀀉itself.􀀉“The􀀉surface􀀉
only􀀉destroys􀀉things􀀉that􀀉don’t􀀉belong􀀉to􀀉it.􀀉Things􀀉from􀀉inside􀀉the􀀉
simulation􀀉pass􀀉straight􀀉through􀀉—􀀉because􀀉to􀀉the􀀉simulation,􀀉they’re􀀉part􀀉
of􀀉its􀀉own􀀉world.”􀀉
He􀀉looked􀀉at􀀉the􀀉others.􀀉“If􀀉we􀀉can􀀉get􀀉enough􀀉stones􀀉through􀀉at􀀉once,􀀉we􀀉
might􀀉be􀀉able􀀉to􀀉open􀀉a􀀉gap􀀉big􀀉enough􀀉to􀀉fit􀀉through.”􀀉

“How?”􀀉Gage􀀉said.􀀉“I􀀉can􀀉throw􀀉one􀀉at􀀉a􀀉time.􀀉The􀀉crack􀀉closes􀀉before􀀉I􀀉
can􀀉throw􀀉another.”􀀉

They􀀉stood􀀉there,􀀉staring􀀉at􀀉the􀀉lava.􀀉Gage􀀉picked􀀉up􀀉two􀀉stones􀀉and􀀉threw􀀉
them􀀉together.􀀉Two􀀉cracks􀀉—􀀉but􀀉narrow,􀀉uneven,􀀉already􀀉sealing􀀉before􀀉
they􀀉could􀀉see􀀉the􀀉floor􀀉clearly.􀀉

“We􀀉need􀀉them􀀉to􀀉hit􀀉at􀀉the􀀉same􀀉time,”􀀉Camilla􀀉said.􀀉“All􀀉in􀀉one􀀉ring.􀀉Not􀀉
thrown􀀉—􀀉placed.”􀀉

Everyone􀀉looked􀀉at􀀉Otto.􀀉

He􀀉understood􀀉before􀀉anyone􀀉asked.􀀉He􀀉looked􀀉at􀀉his􀀉hands􀀉—􀀉the􀀉same􀀉
hands􀀉that􀀉had􀀉shattered􀀉the􀀉green􀀉substance􀀉in􀀉ADAPT􀀉—􀀉and􀀉shook􀀉his􀀉
head􀀉slowly.􀀉“I􀀉don’t􀀉know􀀉if􀀉I􀀉can􀀉hold􀀉that􀀉many.”􀀉


“You􀀉floated􀀉in􀀉there,”􀀉Gage􀀉said.􀀉“You􀀉shattered􀀉that􀀉green􀀉stuff􀀉with􀀉
nothing􀀉but􀀉stillness.􀀉If􀀉anyone􀀉can􀀉do􀀉this􀀉—”􀀉

“That􀀉was􀀉different.”􀀉Otto’s􀀉voice􀀉was􀀉quiet.􀀉“That􀀉was􀀉letting􀀉go.􀀉
Releasing􀀉everything.􀀉This􀀉is􀀉the􀀉opposite􀀉—􀀉this􀀉is􀀉holding􀀉on.􀀉Holding􀀉
ten􀀉heavy􀀉objects􀀉in􀀉the􀀉air􀀉while􀀉the􀀉room􀀉fights􀀉me.􀀉I’ve􀀉never􀀉done􀀉
anything􀀉like􀀉that.”􀀉

Silence.􀀉The􀀉lava􀀉churned􀀉below􀀉them.􀀉The􀀉heat􀀉pulsed.􀀉

“Try,”􀀉Camilla􀀉said􀀉quietly.􀀉

Then􀀉she􀀉looked􀀉at􀀉Gage􀀉and􀀉Ben.􀀉“While􀀉he􀀉works,􀀉we􀀉get􀀉ready.􀀉If􀀉he􀀉
opens􀀉a􀀉hole,􀀉we􀀉won’t􀀉have􀀉time􀀉to􀀉prepare.”􀀉She􀀉nodded􀀉at􀀉their􀀉suits.􀀉
“Tear􀀉strips.􀀉Make􀀉a􀀉rope.􀀉Now.”􀀉

Gage􀀉and􀀉Ben􀀉tore􀀉long􀀉strips􀀉from􀀉their􀀉suits􀀉and􀀉knotted􀀉them􀀉together􀀉

—􀀉hands􀀉working􀀉fast􀀉while􀀉their􀀉eyes􀀉stayed􀀉on􀀉Otto.􀀉By􀀉the􀀉time􀀉the􀀉rope􀀉
was􀀉done,􀀉Otto􀀉hadn’t􀀉moved.􀀉He􀀉was􀀉still􀀉kneeling,􀀉palms􀀉flat,􀀉eyes􀀉closed.􀀉
Waiting􀀉for􀀉something􀀉inside􀀉himself􀀉to􀀉answer.􀀉
For􀀉a􀀉long,􀀉terrible􀀉moment􀀉—􀀉five􀀉seconds,􀀉ten􀀉—􀀉nothing􀀉happened.􀀉
Gage􀀉shifted􀀉his􀀉weight.􀀉Ben􀀉opened􀀉his􀀉mouth􀀉to􀀉speak.􀀉Camilla􀀉shook􀀉
her􀀉head􀀉at􀀉him:􀀉wait.􀀉

Then􀀉a􀀉faint􀀉glow􀀉appeared􀀉around􀀉Otto’s􀀉fingers.􀀉A􀀉stone􀀉near􀀉his􀀉right􀀉
hand􀀉trembled􀀉against􀀉the􀀉ground,􀀉rattling􀀉like􀀉a􀀉coin􀀉on􀀉a􀀉table.􀀉It􀀉lifted􀀉—􀀉
an􀀉inch,􀀉then􀀉two􀀉—􀀉and􀀉hung􀀉in􀀉the􀀉air,􀀉wobbling.􀀉

Another􀀉stone􀀉rose.􀀉Then􀀉a􀀉third.􀀉Otto’s􀀉breathing􀀉had􀀉changed􀀉—􀀉deep,􀀉
deliberate􀀉pulls􀀉that􀀉came􀀉from􀀉somewhere􀀉below􀀉his􀀉chest.􀀉His􀀉neck􀀉was􀀉
corded􀀉with􀀉effort,􀀉veins􀀉standing􀀉out􀀉against􀀉the􀀉skin.􀀉

Four.􀀉Five.􀀉His􀀉jaw􀀉locked.􀀉Six.􀀉Seven.􀀉His􀀉hands􀀉were􀀉shaking􀀉now,􀀉the􀀉
glow􀀉around􀀉them􀀉flickering􀀉like􀀉a􀀉flame􀀉in􀀉wind.􀀉A􀀉bead􀀉of􀀉sweat􀀉ran􀀉from􀀉
his􀀉temple􀀉to􀀉his􀀉chin􀀉and􀀉dropped􀀉onto􀀉the􀀉rock.􀀉


Eight.􀀉A􀀉sound􀀉escaped􀀉his􀀉throat􀀉—􀀉not􀀉a􀀉word,􀀉something􀀉between􀀉a􀀉
grunt􀀉and􀀉a􀀉hiss.􀀉Nine.􀀉The􀀉stones􀀉were􀀉drifting,􀀉losing􀀉their􀀉formation,􀀉
some􀀉rising􀀉higher􀀉than􀀉others.􀀉Lifting􀀉them􀀉was􀀉one􀀉thing.􀀉Holding􀀉them􀀉
in􀀉a􀀉circle􀀉—􀀉evenly􀀉spaced,􀀉the􀀉same􀀉height,􀀉a􀀉ring􀀉wide􀀉enough􀀉for􀀉a􀀉body􀀉
to􀀉fit􀀉through􀀉—􀀉that􀀉was􀀉the􀀉real􀀉battle.􀀉Every􀀉stone􀀉wanted􀀉to􀀉drift􀀉to􀀉its􀀉
own􀀉orbit.􀀉Otto􀀉had􀀉to􀀉hold􀀉ten􀀉separate􀀉weights􀀉and􀀉force􀀉them􀀉into􀀉one􀀉
shared􀀉shape.􀀉

“One􀀉more,”􀀉Ben􀀉whispered.􀀉

The􀀉tenth􀀉stone􀀉shuddered,􀀉lifted,􀀉and􀀉joined􀀉the􀀉ring􀀉—􀀉unsteady,􀀉uneven,􀀉
but􀀉airborne.􀀉Otto’s􀀉face􀀉twisted􀀉with􀀉effort􀀉as􀀉he􀀉nudged􀀉it􀀉into􀀉position,􀀉
closing􀀉the􀀉gap􀀉in􀀉the􀀉circle.􀀉Ten􀀉stones􀀉arranged􀀉in􀀉a􀀉rough􀀉but􀀉
recognizable􀀉ring,􀀉held􀀉by􀀉a􀀉boy􀀉who􀀉was􀀉shaking􀀉from􀀉his􀀉shoulders􀀉to􀀉his􀀉
ankles.􀀉

“Now􀀉keep􀀉them􀀉in􀀉that􀀉circle􀀉and􀀉lower􀀉them􀀉together,”􀀉Ben􀀉said.􀀉
“Slowly.􀀉All􀀉at􀀉once.􀀉The􀀉shape􀀉is􀀉what􀀉makes􀀉the􀀉hole.”􀀉

Otto’s􀀉face􀀉was􀀉a􀀉mask􀀉of􀀉concentration.􀀉He􀀉couldn’t􀀉just􀀉drop􀀉them􀀉—􀀉
they􀀉had􀀉to􀀉descend􀀉as􀀉a􀀉formation,􀀉maintaining􀀉the􀀉ring,􀀉or􀀉the􀀉hole􀀉would􀀉
be􀀉a􀀉scatter􀀉of􀀉narrow􀀉cracks􀀉instead􀀉of􀀉one􀀉opening.􀀉The􀀉stones􀀉went􀀉
down􀀉—􀀉not􀀉smoothly,􀀉but􀀉in􀀉jerks,􀀉each􀀉one􀀉fighting􀀉to􀀉break􀀉from􀀉the􀀉
circle􀀉as􀀉if􀀉the􀀉simulation􀀉was􀀉pushing􀀉back.􀀉Otto’s􀀉hands􀀉moved􀀉in􀀉tiny􀀉
corrections,􀀉pulling􀀉a􀀉drifting􀀉stone􀀉back􀀉into􀀉line,􀀉nudging􀀉another􀀉one􀀉
lower􀀉to􀀉match􀀉the􀀉rest.􀀉When􀀉they􀀉finally􀀉touched􀀉the􀀉lava􀀉surface􀀉
together,􀀉the􀀉illusion􀀉cracked􀀉open􀀉with􀀉a􀀉sound􀀉like􀀉tearing􀀉fabric􀀉—􀀉a􀀉
jagged􀀉but􀀉continuous􀀉ring􀀉of􀀉real􀀉floor􀀉visible􀀉beneath􀀉the􀀉orange􀀉glow.􀀉

“I􀀉can􀀉see􀀉the􀀉room!”􀀉Gage􀀉shouted.􀀉The􀀉facility􀀉floor􀀉was􀀉there􀀉—􀀉eight􀀉
feet􀀉below􀀉them,􀀉grey􀀉and􀀉solid􀀉and􀀉real.􀀉

“Go,”􀀉Otto􀀉said􀀉through􀀉clenched􀀉teeth.􀀉“Now.􀀉I􀀉don’t􀀉know􀀉how􀀉long􀀉I􀀉
can􀀉hold􀀉the􀀉circle.”􀀉


Gage􀀉grabbed􀀉the􀀉rope􀀉they’d􀀉already􀀉made.􀀉Every􀀉second􀀉of􀀉Camilla’s􀀉
foresight􀀉was􀀉paying􀀉off􀀉now􀀉—􀀉there􀀉was􀀉nothing􀀉to􀀉prepare,􀀉nothing􀀉to􀀉
fumble􀀉with.􀀉Otto’s􀀉arms􀀉were􀀉trembling􀀉violently,􀀉the􀀉stones􀀉drifting􀀉
inward,􀀉the􀀉circle􀀉tightening􀀉by􀀉inches.􀀉

They􀀉lowered􀀉Camilla􀀉first.􀀉She􀀉dropped􀀉through􀀉the􀀉gap􀀉and􀀉landed􀀉on􀀉
the􀀉real􀀉floor􀀉with􀀉a􀀉gasp.􀀉The􀀉hole􀀉tightened􀀉—􀀉Otto􀀉groaned􀀉and􀀉pushed􀀉
it􀀉back􀀉open,􀀉but􀀉it􀀉was􀀉smaller􀀉than􀀉before.􀀉

Ben􀀉went􀀉next,􀀉sliding􀀉through􀀉fast.􀀉The􀀉moment􀀉his􀀉weight􀀉left􀀉the􀀉rope,􀀉
two􀀉of􀀉the􀀉stones􀀉slipped􀀉from􀀉Otto’s􀀉hold􀀉and􀀉dropped􀀉through􀀉the􀀉lava􀀉
surface􀀉—􀀉they􀀉heard􀀉them􀀉crack􀀉against􀀉the􀀉facility􀀉floor􀀉below,􀀉landing􀀉
somewhere􀀉near􀀉Camilla.􀀉The􀀉ring􀀉shrank􀀉to􀀉barely􀀉shoulder􀀉width.􀀉

Gage􀀉turned􀀉to􀀉Otto.􀀉His􀀉face􀀉was􀀉grey.􀀉His􀀉whole􀀉body􀀉was􀀉vibrating􀀉with􀀉
the􀀉effort.􀀉Drops􀀉of􀀉blood􀀉ran􀀉from􀀉his􀀉nose􀀉—􀀉sweat􀀉alone􀀉had􀀉not􀀉been􀀉
cost􀀉enough􀀉for􀀉what􀀉he􀀉was􀀉holding.􀀉

“We’re􀀉the􀀉only􀀉two􀀉left,”􀀉Gage􀀉said.􀀉“I’ll􀀉tie􀀉the􀀉rope􀀉to􀀉a􀀉boulder􀀉and􀀉carry􀀉
you􀀉down􀀉in􀀉my􀀉arms.􀀉You􀀉hold􀀉the􀀉formation,􀀉I’ll􀀉do􀀉the􀀉climbing.”􀀉

Otto􀀉looked􀀉at􀀉him.􀀉He􀀉didn’t􀀉need􀀉to􀀉test􀀉the􀀉idea.􀀉He􀀉was􀀉barely􀀉holding􀀉
his􀀉footing􀀉on􀀉the􀀉rock􀀉—􀀉every􀀉gram􀀉of􀀉his􀀉focus􀀉anchored􀀉where􀀉he􀀉
stood,􀀉his􀀉concentration􀀉thinned􀀉to􀀉a􀀉single􀀉line.􀀉The􀀉smallest􀀉movement,􀀉
even􀀉a􀀉step􀀉into􀀉Gage’s􀀉arms,􀀉would􀀉break􀀉his􀀉hold,􀀉and􀀉the􀀉stones􀀉would􀀉
drop.􀀉

Otto􀀉smiled􀀉—􀀉a􀀉smile􀀉that􀀉cost􀀉him􀀉something,􀀉pulled􀀉from􀀉reserves􀀉that􀀉
were􀀉already􀀉empty.􀀉“Climb􀀉down􀀉first.􀀉I’ll􀀉find􀀉my􀀉way􀀉after.”􀀉

Gage􀀉didn’t􀀉move.􀀉He􀀉looked􀀉at􀀉the􀀉eight􀀉remaining􀀉stones,􀀉at􀀉Otto’s􀀉
shaking􀀉arms,􀀉at􀀉the􀀉blood􀀉on􀀉his􀀉lip.􀀉The􀀉math􀀉was􀀉simple:􀀉Otto􀀉couldn’t􀀉
be􀀉moved.􀀉Every􀀉part􀀉of􀀉Gage’s􀀉body􀀉understood􀀉what􀀉Otto􀀉was􀀉offering.􀀉

“No,”􀀉Gage􀀉said.􀀉“I’m􀀉not􀀉leaving􀀉you􀀉up􀀉here.􀀉We’ll􀀉find􀀉another􀀉way.”􀀉


“There􀀉is􀀉no􀀉other􀀉way,”􀀉Otto􀀉said.􀀉His􀀉voice􀀉was􀀉strained,􀀉each􀀉word􀀉
costing􀀉him􀀉concentration􀀉he􀀉couldn’t􀀉spare.􀀉“Gage.􀀉Go.”􀀉

“I’ll􀀉hold􀀉the􀀉stones.􀀉You􀀉go􀀉through.􀀉I’m􀀉bigger,􀀉I’m􀀉stronger􀀉—”􀀉

“You􀀉can’t􀀉do􀀉what􀀉I􀀉do.”􀀉Otto’s􀀉eyes􀀉opened.􀀉They􀀉were􀀉calm􀀉—􀀉
impossibly􀀉calm􀀉for􀀉a􀀉boy􀀉bleeding􀀉from􀀉his􀀉nose􀀉and􀀉shaking􀀉under􀀉the􀀉
weight􀀉of􀀉ten􀀉stones.􀀉“You􀀉know􀀉that.”􀀉

Gage􀀉stood􀀉rooted.􀀉He􀀉wasn’t􀀉going.􀀉He􀀉would􀀉stand􀀉here􀀉until􀀉the􀀉circle􀀉
collapsed􀀉and􀀉the􀀉hole􀀉sealed􀀉shut􀀉and􀀉they􀀉were􀀉both􀀉trapped􀀉in􀀉this􀀉
simulation􀀉forever,􀀉because􀀉leaving􀀉Otto􀀉alone􀀉was􀀉something􀀉his􀀉body􀀉
physically􀀉refused􀀉to􀀉do.􀀉

From􀀉below,􀀉Ben’s􀀉voice􀀉echoed􀀉up􀀉through􀀉the􀀉hole:􀀉“Gage!􀀉Camilla’s􀀉
sealant􀀉is􀀉gone.􀀉She’s􀀉bleeding􀀉again.􀀉I􀀉need􀀉you􀀉down􀀉here􀀉NOW.”􀀉

Otto􀀉looked􀀉at􀀉Gage.􀀉“Your􀀉sister􀀉is􀀉dying.􀀉And􀀉you’re􀀉the􀀉only􀀉one􀀉strong􀀉
enough􀀉to􀀉carry􀀉her􀀉out􀀉of􀀉whatever􀀉comes􀀉next.”􀀉

The􀀉words􀀉hit􀀉Gage􀀉like􀀉a􀀉fist.􀀉Not􀀉because􀀉they􀀉were􀀉cruel.􀀉Because􀀉they􀀉
were􀀉true.􀀉

“Promise􀀉me,”􀀉Gage􀀉said.􀀉His􀀉voice􀀉broke.􀀉“Promise􀀉me􀀉you’ll􀀉jump􀀉
through􀀉after􀀉me.”􀀉

They􀀉both􀀉knew􀀉it􀀉was􀀉a􀀉lie.􀀉The􀀉circle􀀉was􀀉barely􀀉shoulder􀀉width􀀉and􀀉
shrinking.􀀉But􀀉Otto􀀉looked􀀉at􀀉his􀀉best􀀉friend􀀉and􀀉gave􀀉him􀀉the􀀉only􀀉gift􀀉he􀀉
had􀀉left􀀉—􀀉a􀀉reason􀀉to􀀉move.􀀉

“I􀀉promise,􀀉brother.”􀀉

Gage􀀉climbed􀀉down.􀀉The􀀉moment􀀉his􀀉feet􀀉hit􀀉the􀀉floor,􀀉he􀀉looked􀀉up.􀀉Otto􀀉
was􀀉still􀀉above,􀀉silhouetted􀀉against􀀉the􀀉orange􀀉light,􀀉the􀀉stones􀀉trembling􀀉in􀀉
their􀀉circle􀀉around􀀉him.􀀉His􀀉footing􀀉slipped.􀀉The􀀉ring􀀉of􀀉stones􀀉began􀀉to􀀉
tighten.􀀉


Then􀀉—􀀉something􀀉Gage􀀉couldn’t􀀉explain.􀀉A􀀉blue􀀉butterfly􀀉appeared􀀉near􀀉
Otto’s􀀉head,􀀉hovering􀀉in􀀉the􀀉heat􀀉as􀀉if􀀉the􀀉fire􀀉couldn’t􀀉touch􀀉it.􀀉Where􀀉it􀀉
had􀀉come􀀉from,􀀉what􀀉it􀀉was,􀀉Gage􀀉would􀀉never􀀉know.􀀉But􀀉Otto’s􀀉eyes􀀉
followed􀀉it􀀉for􀀉a􀀉moment,􀀉and􀀉something􀀉in􀀉his􀀉face􀀉changed.􀀉His􀀉body􀀉
steadied.􀀉His􀀉arms􀀉stopped􀀉shaking.􀀉For􀀉one􀀉breath,􀀉he􀀉was􀀉as􀀉solid􀀉as􀀉the􀀉
mountain􀀉beneath􀀉him.􀀉

“Your􀀉turn,􀀉Otto!”􀀉Gage􀀉screamed􀀉from􀀉below.􀀉

Otto􀀉looked􀀉down􀀉at􀀉them􀀉—􀀉Gage􀀉on􀀉the􀀉floor,􀀉Ben􀀉kneeling􀀉over􀀉
Camilla,􀀉the􀀉grey􀀉walls􀀉of􀀉the􀀉facility􀀉stretching􀀉out􀀉behind􀀉them.􀀉He􀀉
looked􀀉at􀀉the􀀉stones.􀀉He􀀉looked􀀉at􀀉the􀀉hole.􀀉

“Move􀀉away􀀉from􀀉the􀀉opening,”􀀉Otto􀀉said.􀀉His􀀉voice􀀉was􀀉calm.􀀉Steady.􀀉
“When􀀉I􀀉let􀀉go,􀀉the􀀉stones􀀉come􀀉down.”􀀉

“No!”􀀉Ben􀀉screamed.􀀉“Get􀀉down􀀉here!”􀀉

“Otto,􀀉don’t􀀉—”􀀉Gage􀀉started.􀀉

Otto􀀉smiled.􀀉The􀀉same􀀉smile􀀉he’d􀀉worn􀀉the􀀉day􀀉they􀀉were􀀉twelve􀀉and􀀉he’d􀀉
fallen􀀉off􀀉the􀀉slag􀀉heap􀀉trying􀀉to􀀉impress􀀉a􀀉girl.􀀉The􀀉same􀀉smile􀀉he’d􀀉used􀀉
during􀀉the􀀉Initiation􀀉when􀀉the􀀉world􀀉was􀀉trying􀀉to􀀉unmake􀀉them.􀀉It􀀉wasn’t􀀉
a􀀉brave􀀉smile􀀉or􀀉a􀀉sad􀀉smile.􀀉It􀀉was􀀉the􀀉smile􀀉of􀀉someone􀀉who􀀉had􀀉already􀀉
decided.􀀉

Then􀀉Otto􀀉waved.􀀉One􀀉hand,􀀉steady􀀉and􀀉open.􀀉The􀀉stones􀀉drifted􀀉inward.􀀉
The􀀉circle􀀉closed.􀀉The􀀉lava􀀉surface􀀉sealed􀀉over􀀉him,􀀉and􀀉the􀀉illusion􀀉became􀀉
whole􀀉again.􀀉

He􀀉was􀀉gone.􀀉

The􀀉volcano􀀉went􀀉with􀀉him.􀀉The􀀉orange􀀉sky􀀉faded􀀉first,􀀉then􀀉the􀀉rock,􀀉then􀀉
the􀀉heat.􀀉The􀀉grey􀀉walls􀀉of􀀉the􀀉SACRIFICE􀀉chamber􀀉bled􀀉back􀀉into􀀉view􀀉
around􀀉the􀀉three􀀉of􀀉them􀀉as􀀉if􀀉they􀀉had􀀉never􀀉left.􀀉


They􀀉hadn’t.􀀉Their􀀉boots􀀉were􀀉on􀀉the􀀉cold􀀉metal􀀉floor.􀀉They􀀉had􀀉never􀀉
taken􀀉a􀀉step.􀀉

Otto􀀉was􀀉standing􀀉right􀀉there.􀀉

Exactly􀀉where􀀉he􀀉had􀀉stood􀀉when􀀉the􀀉simulation􀀉began,􀀉between􀀉Gage􀀉and􀀉
Ben,􀀉his􀀉shoulders􀀉square,􀀉his􀀉feet􀀉planted,􀀉his􀀉arms􀀉at􀀉his􀀉sides.􀀉His􀀉chest􀀉
rose.􀀉His􀀉chest􀀉fell.􀀉His􀀉eyes􀀉were􀀉open.􀀉He􀀉had􀀉not􀀉moved􀀉at􀀉all.􀀉He􀀉had􀀉
not􀀉gone􀀉anywhere.􀀉

Otto’s􀀉eyes􀀉were􀀉glassed􀀉over,􀀉as􀀉if􀀉his􀀉mind􀀉was􀀉still􀀉in􀀉the􀀉simulation􀀉—􀀉
still􀀉watching􀀉the􀀉lava,􀀉still􀀉watching􀀉the􀀉circle􀀉close.􀀉His􀀉body􀀉was􀀉here.􀀉
His􀀉mind􀀉was􀀉not.􀀉

Gage􀀉had􀀉always􀀉thought􀀉of􀀉himself􀀉as􀀉the􀀉one􀀉who􀀉carried􀀉things.􀀉The􀀉
Engine.􀀉The􀀉one􀀉who􀀉bore􀀉the􀀉weight􀀉so􀀉others􀀉didn’t􀀉have􀀉to.􀀉But􀀉Otto􀀉
had􀀉just􀀉carried􀀉all􀀉four􀀉of􀀉them􀀉out􀀉of􀀉a􀀉volcano􀀉on􀀉the􀀉strength􀀉of􀀉his􀀉
stillness,􀀉and􀀉he’d􀀉done􀀉it􀀉without􀀉once􀀉asking􀀉anyone􀀉to􀀉carry􀀉him􀀉back.􀀉

He􀀉had􀀉smiled.􀀉That􀀉was􀀉the􀀉part􀀉that􀀉wouldn’t􀀉leave.􀀉Not􀀉the􀀉stones,􀀉not􀀉
the􀀉lava,􀀉not􀀉the􀀉closing􀀉circle.􀀉The􀀉smile.􀀉He’d􀀉smiled􀀉because􀀉he’d􀀉already􀀉
decided.􀀉He’d􀀉already􀀉done􀀉the􀀉math􀀉—􀀉his􀀉math,􀀉the􀀉kind􀀉that􀀉lived􀀉in􀀉his􀀉
bones􀀉instead􀀉of􀀉his􀀉head􀀉—􀀉and􀀉the􀀉answer􀀉had􀀉come􀀉back􀀉clean􀀉and􀀉
simple:􀀉them,􀀉not􀀉me.􀀉

That􀀈was􀀈supposed􀀈to􀀈be􀀈me,􀀉Gage􀀉thought.􀀉That􀀉was􀀉always􀀉supposed􀀉to􀀉be􀀉
me.􀀉

He􀀉clenched􀀉his􀀉fists􀀉tight,􀀉nails􀀉pressed􀀉hard􀀉into􀀉his􀀉palms.􀀉The􀀉
discomfort􀀉anchored􀀉him􀀉to􀀉the􀀉present􀀉when􀀉every􀀉other􀀉part􀀉of􀀉him􀀉
wanted􀀉to􀀉collapse.􀀉It􀀉told􀀉him:􀀉you􀀉are􀀉here.􀀉You􀀉are􀀉alive.􀀉Someone􀀉paid􀀉
for􀀉that.􀀉Now􀀉earn􀀉it.􀀉

Gage􀀉looked􀀉at􀀉Otto􀀉once.􀀉Still􀀉standing.􀀉Still􀀉breathing.􀀉Still􀀉gone.􀀉He􀀉
couldn’t􀀉hold􀀉the􀀉look.􀀉


Ben􀀉was􀀉kneeling􀀉over􀀉Camilla.􀀉A􀀉small􀀉compartment􀀉had􀀉opened􀀉in􀀉the􀀉
wall􀀉beside􀀉them􀀉—􀀉the􀀉same􀀉quiet􀀉click􀀉they’d􀀉heard􀀉after􀀉PLAN.􀀉Inside,􀀉
another􀀉spray􀀉can.􀀉The􀀉facility􀀉was􀀉keeping􀀉her􀀉alive􀀉again.􀀉Ben􀀉was􀀉
applying􀀉fresh􀀉sealant􀀉to􀀉her􀀉wound.􀀉Her􀀉eyes􀀉were􀀉closed􀀉but􀀉her􀀉chest􀀉was􀀉
moving.􀀉Alive.􀀉Gage􀀉looked􀀉at􀀉Ben􀀉—􀀉hollow-eyed,􀀉shaking,􀀉his􀀉father’s􀀉
compass􀀉gone􀀉and􀀉his􀀉best􀀉friend􀀉with􀀉it.􀀉Still􀀉working.􀀉Still􀀉fixing􀀉what􀀉he􀀉
could.􀀉

Four􀀉bodies.􀀉Three􀀉minds.􀀉In􀀉a􀀉room􀀉that􀀉might􀀉have􀀉been􀀉every􀀉room.􀀉
With􀀉one􀀉quarter􀀉of􀀉a􀀉crystal􀀉still􀀉pulsing􀀉blue.􀀉

The􀀉room􀀉flooded􀀉with􀀉white􀀉light.􀀉

A􀀉figure􀀉stepped􀀉out􀀉of􀀉the􀀉brightness􀀉—􀀉silhouette􀀉first,􀀉then􀀉shape,􀀉then􀀉
face.􀀉Mr.􀀉Flitzberg.􀀉

Gage􀀉crossed􀀉the􀀉room􀀉in􀀉three􀀉strides􀀉and􀀉grabbed􀀉him􀀉by􀀉the􀀉throat.􀀉
“Why?”􀀉His􀀉voice􀀉cracked.􀀉“Otto􀀉is􀀉here􀀉and􀀉still􀀉gone.􀀉Camilla􀀉is􀀉half-
dead.􀀉What􀀉did􀀉we􀀉ever􀀉do􀀉to􀀉you?”􀀉

Flitzberg􀀉didn’t􀀉flinch.􀀉He􀀉didn’t􀀉reach􀀉for􀀉Gage’s􀀉hands.􀀉He􀀉looked􀀉at􀀉the􀀉
boy􀀉who􀀉was􀀉holding􀀉him􀀉by􀀉the􀀉neck􀀉and􀀉spoke􀀉with􀀉the􀀉same􀀉measured􀀉
calm􀀉he’d􀀉used􀀉since􀀉the􀀉beginning.􀀉

“The􀀉question􀀉is􀀉not􀀉what􀀉I􀀉did􀀉to􀀉you.􀀉The􀀉question􀀉is:􀀉what􀀉will􀀉you􀀉do􀀉
to􀀉regain􀀉the􀀉freedom􀀉that􀀉was􀀉once􀀉your􀀉birthright?”􀀉

“What􀀉does􀀉that􀀉mean?”􀀉Ben􀀉shouted􀀉from􀀉the􀀉floor.􀀉

Flitzberg’s􀀉eyes􀀉moved􀀉from􀀉Gage􀀉to􀀉Camilla􀀉to􀀉Ben􀀉to􀀉Otto.􀀉Four􀀉faces.􀀉
Three􀀉looking􀀉back.􀀉

“You􀀉are􀀉ready􀀉now,”􀀉he􀀉said.􀀉“Ready􀀉for􀀉the􀀉journey􀀉ahead.”􀀉

About the􀀉Author􀀉

Ankit􀀉is􀀉the􀀉author􀀉of􀀉the􀀉Close􀀉Encounters􀀉series􀀉—􀀉three􀀉books􀀉of􀀉letters􀀉
written􀀉from􀀉the􀀉perspective􀀉of􀀉a􀀉baby􀀉girl􀀉growing􀀉up􀀉too􀀉fast􀀉for􀀉her􀀉
father􀀉to􀀉keep􀀉up.􀀉He􀀉promised􀀉himself􀀉he’d􀀉stop􀀉writing􀀉those􀀉letters􀀉
when􀀉his􀀉daughter􀀉could􀀉speak􀀉for􀀉herself,􀀉and􀀉somewhere􀀉along􀀉the􀀉way,􀀉
she􀀉did.􀀉This􀀉is􀀉what􀀉he􀀉wrote􀀉next.􀀉

He􀀉has􀀉spent􀀉over􀀉twenty􀀉years􀀉helping􀀉large􀀉organizations􀀉prepare􀀉for􀀉
change􀀉they􀀉didn’t􀀉see􀀉coming.􀀉If􀀉that’s􀀉not􀀉training􀀉for􀀉writing􀀉dystopian􀀉
fiction,􀀉nothing􀀉is.􀀉

He􀀉started􀀉Miner􀀉Town􀀉in􀀉2012􀀉and􀀉abandoned􀀉it􀀉somewhere􀀉around􀀉
chapter􀀉three,􀀉where􀀉most􀀉of􀀉his􀀉good􀀉intentions􀀉go􀀉to􀀉die.􀀉It􀀉stayed􀀉in􀀉a􀀉
folder􀀉for􀀉thirteen􀀉years,􀀉occasionally􀀉surviving􀀉laptop􀀉changes,􀀉until􀀉his􀀉
then-ten-year-old􀀉daughter􀀉—􀀉an􀀉extrovert,􀀉a􀀉battle-of-the-books􀀉veteran,􀀉
and􀀉a􀀉reader􀀉who􀀉has􀀉finished􀀉more􀀉novels􀀉than􀀉her􀀉father􀀉has􀀉started􀀉—􀀉
demanded􀀉to􀀉see􀀉what􀀉he’d􀀉been􀀉hiding.􀀉She􀀉read􀀉the􀀉draft.􀀉She􀀉asked􀀉him􀀉
to􀀉finish􀀉it.􀀉So􀀉he􀀉did.􀀉

Ankit’s􀀉wife􀀉is􀀉the􀀉cornerstone􀀉of􀀉their􀀉household􀀉and􀀉the􀀉quiet􀀉engineer􀀉of􀀉
the􀀉life􀀉that􀀉makes􀀉everything􀀉else􀀉possible􀀉—􀀉including􀀉this􀀉book.􀀉She􀀉
manages􀀉a􀀉ten-year-old􀀉going􀀉on􀀉fifteen􀀉and􀀉a􀀉forty-plus-year-old􀀉man􀀉with􀀉
teenager􀀉rebellion􀀉syndrome,􀀉and􀀉she􀀉does􀀉both􀀉with􀀉more􀀉patience􀀉than􀀉
either􀀉deserves.􀀉She􀀉is􀀉the􀀉reason􀀉any􀀉of􀀉this􀀉exists.􀀉
